A sequence of canonical-centered personalization routes repeatedly exposed the same conflict: increasing structural pressure made outputs more canonical, while increasing personalization could damage identity-defining strokes. The project was therefore reset around real-human character equivalence classes. A multi-writer CASIA candidate pool showed that a USER-compatible realization often already existed among valid human samples. The task consequently changed from synthesis to character-wise matching, followed by cross-writer composition into a virtual writer. The frozen system uses real-ink features, character-specific human population percentiles, top-20 candidate pruning, and greedy hardest-first whole-row selection. On the covered target set, all 197 USER characters had real-human candidates, and the 100-character evaluation subset was covered 100/100. Knowncharacter held-out comparisons included a row judged visually almost indistinguishable from genuine USER handwriting. A 60- episode stability audit placed every episode in a predefined A-like machine-proxy region, but these were not independent human A-level judgments. The final evidence supports stable practical B-level quality, with many outputs approaching A-level under the USER-defined criterion. The report records why generation became unnecessary for this case without claiming unrestricted or universal handwriting synthesis.

</details>


### [127] [FlowDance: Music-Driven Dance Video Generation with Parallel Pose and RGB Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15818)
*Genying Li,Boda Lin,Jiachen Li,Zijian Jia,Haojie Zheng,Yiming Wang,Shuchen Weng,Si Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlowDance是一个音乐驱动的舞蹈视频生成框架，通过并行姿态和RGB双流架构，结合时间步感知的姿态注入和持久身份注入，实现参考人物的舞蹈动画生成；同时构建了高质量的野外舞蹈视频数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音乐驱动的舞蹈视频合成要求模型同时学习音乐到动作的对应关系、保持身份的人体动画、时序一致性和视觉逼真的视频生成，这是一个极具挑战性的多模态生成任务。

Method: 提出FlowDance框架，采用并行姿态流和RGB流的双流架构，显式整合动作建模与参考保持的视觉合成；引入时间步感知的姿态注入以在去噪步骤中自适应结构引导，以及持久身份注入以保持长视频中的参考外观；并构建了流行度筛选的高分辨率野外舞蹈视频数据集。

Result: 大量实验表明FlowDance在舞蹈动作生成和音乐驱动的舞蹈视频合成两方面均取得了优异的性能。

Conclusion: FlowDance通过显式动作建模与参考保持的视觉合成相结合，有效解决了音乐驱动舞蹈视频生成中的多维度挑战，为这一方向提供了新思路和高质量数据集支撑。

Abstract: Music-driven dance video synthesis aims to animate a reference person according to a given music clip. The task is challenging because it requires a model to jointly learn music-to-motion correspondence, identity-preserving human animation, temporal coherence, and visually realistic video generation. We present FlowDance, a music-driven dance video generation framework that integrates explicit motion modeling with reference-preserving visual synthesis through parallel pose and RGB streams. We further introduce timestep-aware pose injection to adapt structural guidance across denoising steps and persistent identity injection to preserve the reference appearance over long video. To support this task, we further build a popularity-curated, high-resolution in-the-wild dance video dataset with synchronized music, RGB videos, 3D body motion, camera parameters, and projected 2D pose annotations. Extensive experiments show that FlowDance achieves strong performance in both dance motion generation and music-driven dance video synthesis.

</details>


### [128] [MITE-Net: SWaP-Optimized 4K Video Tiny Target Perception for Embodied Edge SAR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15830)
*Mingshuo Xu,Mu Hua,Jigen Peng,Qi Wang,Shigang Yue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MITE-Net，一个面向边缘设备（如无人机）上的实时4K微小目标感知框架，包含设计新型级联架构（学习无关的生物启发式微小目标运动区域提议网络TTM-RPN + 少于0.14M参数的R-CNN风格检测头）、构建SAR-Tiny标准数据集（SeaDroneSee-Tiny和UAVID-Tiny），并在NVIDIA Jetson AGX Xavier上进行硬件级基准测试。MITE-Net在4K海事图像上达到100%搜索成功率、30.33 FPS、仅3.19W功耗（9.51 FPS/W），大幅优于YOLO基线，但在密集城市场景（UAVID-Tiny）中暴露了结构性的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备（如无人机）受到严格的SWaP约束，传统图像下采样会导致微小目标严重特征丢失，而基于切片（slice）的处理方式又引入过高延迟。现有方案难以在嵌入式平台上实时处理高分辨率（4K）图像中的微小目标，亟需一个兼顾精度、速度和能耗的高效感知方案。

Method: 提出MITE-Net级联架构：(1) 前端采用生物启发的、无需学习的TTM-RPN（Tiny Target Motion-Based Region Proposal Network），利用运动信息快速产生候选区域，避免下采样带来的特征损失，同时省去学习的计算开销；(2) 后端为参数少于0.14M的R-CNN风格检测头，对候选区域进行分类和精细定位。此外，构建SAR-Tiny数据集（重新标注SeaDroneSee和UAVID两个无人机数据集），并在Jetson AGX Xavier上进行能效与性能基准评测。

Result: 在4K海事图像上，MITE-Net以30.33 FPS和3.19W功耗实现100%搜索成功率（9.51 FPS/W），在目标召回率和能效上大幅超越YOLO基线。但在UAVID-Tiny（密集城市、极微小目标<64像素）上，学习无关的生物启发式前端在复杂背景下失效，超轻量检测头也缺乏足够表征能力，暴露了架构设计的复合局限性。

Conclusion: 本文交付了一个面向边缘设备的高效机上感知范式，并通过专用数据集和硬件基准建立了严格的性能基线，为未来端到端搜索救援（SAR）架构提供了参考方向，同时明确了无学习前端+超轻量头在复杂场景下的能力边界。

Abstract: Real-time tiny target perception in high-resolution imagery is critical for embodied Search-and-Rescue (SAR) missions. However, strict Size, Weight, and Power (SWaP) constraints on edge devices like UAVs create a bottleneck: traditional image downsampling causes severe feature loss, while slice-based processing incurs prohibitive latency. To address this gap, this paper introduces a comprehensive framework encompassing a novel architecture, specialized datasets, and hardware-level benchmarks. First, we propose MITE-Net, a SWaP-optimized cascaded architecture, which couples a bio-inspired, learning-free Tiny Target Motion-Based Region Proposal Network (TTM-RPN) with a sub-0.14M-parameter R-CNN-like head. Second, to standardize 4K tiny target evaluation, we construct the SAR-Tiny Datasets by relabeling two challenging UAV datasets: SeaDroneSee-Tiny (dynamic maritime scenes, tiny targets predominantly of 64-256 pixels ) and UAVID-Tiny (cluttered urban scenes, extremely tiny targets, less than 64 pixels). Third, we benchmark against state-of-the-art YOLO models on an edge device, NVIDIA Jetson AGX Xavier, where MITE-Net directly processes 4K maritime imagery, achieving a 100\% search success rate at 30.33 FPS. Consuming merely 3.19 W (9.51 FPS/W), MITE-Net vastly outperforms YOLO baselines in target recall and energy efficiency. Conversely, UAVID-Tiny evaluations expose a compound structural limitation: the learning-free bionic front-end struggles against urban backgrounds, while the ultra-lightweight head lacks representational capacity for complex features. Ultimately, this work delivers an efficient onboard perception paradigm and a rigorous baseline guiding future end-to-end SAR architectures.

</details>


### [129] [CardiacMamba: Fair and Robust RGB-RF Fusion for Remote Heart Rate Estimation via State Space Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15831)
*Bo Zhao,Zheng Wu,Yiping Xie,Zitong YU*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CardiacMamba是一个基于状态空间模型的RGB-RF融合框架，用于远程光电容积描记法（rPPG）心率监测。它通过时间差分Mamba模块、双向SSM交互机制和通道级FFT模块，在EquiPleth数据集上达到0.96 bpm MAE的最优性能，并将浅色/深色肤色MAE差距缩小到0.26 bpm，同时保持对RGB退化与RF缺失的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯RGB的远程光电容积描记法（rPPG）方法容易受到光照变化、运动伪影和肤色依赖的光学反射影响，导致心率监测不公平且不稳定。作者希望通过引入射频（RF）心脏运动线索并与RGB融合，提升准确性和公平性。

Method: 提出CardiacMamba框架，包含三个核心模块：1) 时间差分Mamba模块（TDMM）增强微弱的RF时间变化；2) 双向SSM交互机制对齐异构RGB-RF动态；3) 通道级快速傅里叶变换（CFFT）模块进行通道域频谱细化。

Result: 在EquiPleth数据集上达到SOTA性能：MAE 0.96 bpm，RMSE 3.06 bpm，Pearson相关系数0.97；浅深肤色MAE差距降至0.26 bpm；在RGB退化与RF缺失条件下保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: RGB-RF多模态融合通过状态空间建模可以有效提升rPPG心率监测的准确性和肤色公平性，射频信号补充了RGB在光照和肤色变化时的不足。

Abstract: Remote photoplethysmography (rPPG) enables non-contact heart rate (HR) monitoring from facial videos, but RGB-only methods are vulnerable to illumination changes, motion artifacts, and skin-tone-dependent optical reflectance. We propose CardiacMamba, a fair and robust RGB-RF fusion framework that integrates optical facial cues and radio-frequency cardiac motion cues through state space modeling. CardiacMamba introduces a Temporal Difference Mamba Module (TDMM) to enhance subtle RF temporal variations, a bidirectional SSM-based interaction mechanism to align heterogeneous RGB-RF dynamics, and a Channel-wise Fast Fourier Transform (CFFT) module for channel-domain spectral refinement. On the EquiPleth dataset, CardiacMamba achieves state-of-the-art performance with 0.96 bpm MAE, 3.06 bpm RMSE, and 0.97 Pearson correlation, while reducing the observed light-dark skin-tone MAE gap to 0.26 bpm and maintaining robustness under RGB degradation and RF-missing conditions

</details>


### [130] [Beyond Visual CoT: Internalized Visual Thinking for Proactive Video Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15869)
*Xiaoyu Zhu,Xinke Deng,Suresh Taddewadikar,Arnab Kumar Mondal,Zhongyu Jiang,Ian Fasel,Joerg Liebelt*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Internalized Visual Thinking (IVT)框架，让多模态大语言模型在训练阶段通过预测未来帧的潜在表征来内化视觉思维，推理时直接生成答案，无需显式生成中间推理图像。相比显式Visual CoT，IVT在保持同等或更好性能的同时，将端到端延迟降低5倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型采用Visual CoT生成中间推理图像来实现视觉前瞻，但带来了显著的推理开销，尤其对主动视频推理不利。作者探究模型能否在训练时学会视觉思考而在推理时直接推理，避免显式像素空间生成的高昂代价。

Method: IVT是一种后训练框架，通过在无标注视频上联合优化文本预测和下一嵌入(next-embedding)预测。给定部分观测视频，模型同时预测未来帧的潜在表征和目标文本答案，从而捕获运动、物体转换、交互和潜在意图。推理时直接生成答案，无需合成或重新编码未来帧。作者还系统研究了目标表征、解码器设计、预测时域、数据混合、训练课程和预测目标等因素。

Result: IVT在所有六个评测设置上均优于直接答案微调，且推理路径保持不变。相比显式Visual CoT，IVT达到相当或更优的性能，同时将平均端到端延迟降低超过5倍。

Conclusion: 显式像素空间的推理时生成（如Visual CoT）对于有效的主动视频推理可能并非必要。预测性世界模型可在训练阶段内化，从而产生更准确且更高效的模态推理器。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models increasingly use visual chain-of-thought (Visual CoT) to reason about spatial, temporal, and embodied environments. By generating intermediate reasoning images, Visual CoT provides an intuitive mechanism for visual foresight but introduces substantial inference overhead, which is particularly problematic for proactive video reasoning. We ask whether models can learn to think visually during training while reasoning directly at inference. We introduce Internalized Visual Thinking (IVT), a post-training framework that jointly optimizes textual prediction and next-embedding prediction over unlabeled videos. Given a partially observed video, IVT predicts latent representations of future frames together with the target textual answer, encouraging the model to capture motion, object transitions, interactions, and latent intent. At inference, IVT generates the answer directly without synthesizing or re-encoding future frames. We conduct controlled studies across target representations, decoder designs, prediction horizons, data mixtures, training curricula, and predictive objectives. IVT improves over direct-answer fine-tuning on all six evaluation settings while retaining the same inference pathway. Compared with explicit Visual CoT, IVT achieves comparable or better performance and reduces average end-to-end latency by more than 5x. Together, our findings suggest that explicit pixel-space generation at inference time, as used in visual chain-of-thought, may not be necessary for effective proactive video reasoning. Predictive world modeling can be internalized during training to produce multimodal reasoners that are both more accurate and substantially more efficient.

</details>


### [131] [CLARA: Clip-Level Multimodal Alignment with VLM-Derived Rationales for Hateful Video Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15905)
*Yuchen Zhang,Shuang Dai,Zeyu Fu,Yunfei Long,Ravi Shekhar,Haralambos Mouratidis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CLARA是一个用于仇恨视频检测的clip级多模态框架，将视频建模为细粒度片段的序列，通过Mixture-of-Experts编码器、局部-全局对比学习和VLM推理门控Transformer来捕捉时间局部化的仇恨信号，在三个数据集上超越SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频社交媒体平台快速增长，仇恨言论对个人和社会构成严重风险。与文本或静态多模态内容相比，仇恨视频检测仍未被充分探索且更具挑战性，因为仇恨含义常源于语音、音频和视觉内容之间复杂的交互，且这些信号往往是短暂的、隐式的和时间依赖的，传统视频级表示难以捕捉。

Method: 提出CLARA框架：(1) 将视频建模为细粒度clip序列而非单个实例；(2) Mixture-of-Experts clip编码器实现自适应多模态对齐；(3) 局部-全局片段对比目标联合建模短期线索和长期时间依赖；(4) 通过门控Transformer集成VLM衍生的推理，提供高层语义指导。

Result: 在三个仇恨视频数据集上，CLARA一致优于现有最先进方法。消融实验和参数分析验证了每个组件的有效性。

Conclusion: CLARA通过clip级细粒度建模和多种创新组件的结合，有效解决了仇恨视频检测中时间局部信号和多模态交互的挑战，为该领域设立了新的SOTA。

Abstract: Hateful video detection has become increasingly important with the rapid growth of video-centric social media platforms, given the serious risks that hate speech poses to both individual well-being and social cohesion. Compared with text or static multimodal content, hateful video detection remains underexplored and significantly more challenging, as hateful meaning often arises from complex interactions among multimodal cues, including speech, audio, and visual content. Moreover, such signals are often brief, implicit, and temporally dependent, making them difficult to capture using conventional video-level representations. In this work, we propose CLARA, a clip-level multimodal framework for hateful video detection. Instead of treating a video as a single instance, CLARA models it as a sequence of fine-grained clips, enabling more precise capture of temporally localized hateful signals. We introduce a Mixture-of-Experts clip encoder for adaptive multimodal alignment, a local-global segment contrastive objective to jointly model short-term cues and long-range temporal dependencies, and VLM-derived rationales integrated via a gated Transformer to provide high-level semantic guidance. Extensive experiments on three hateful video datasets demonstrate that CLARA consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods. Further ablation studies and parameter analyses validate the effectiveness of each component.

</details>


### [132] [Comprehensive Benchmarking of Deep Learning Architectures for Lung Cancer Histopathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15915)
*Hadi Hasan,Safaa Salman,Lama Sleem,Ralph Mouawad,Ali Chehab*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个两阶段深度学习框架用于肺癌组织病理图像的多类组织分类和像素级区域分割，系统比较了六种分类模型和四种分割模型，最终将最优模型整合为端到端框架。YOLO11分类准确率达98.38%，DeepLabV3+分割IoU为0.80，YOLO11-seg以约14倍更少参数达到0.79的IoU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肺癌是全球癌症相关死亡的首要原因，组织病理学诊断受观察者间差异和人工阅片工作量大影响。虽然深度学习在计算病理学中展现出潜力，但缺乏统一的框架同时集成组织分类和区域分割的综合基准研究。

Method: 构建两阶段深度学习框架：第一阶段进行多类组织分类，在LC25000与LungHist700合并的39,000张图像上评估六种模型（自定义CNN、VGG16、DenseNet、MobileNetV3、自定义ViT、YOLO11），区分腺癌、鳞状细胞癌和正常肺组织；第二阶段使用GlaS基准进行区域分割，评估U-Net、ResNet编码器U-Net、DeepLabV3+和YOLO11-seg；最后整合最优模型为端到端框架。

Result: YOLO11在分类任务中表现最佳：准确率98.38%，五折交叉验证准确率98.21±0.35%，宏F1分数0.98。分割任务中DeepLabV3+获得最高IoU 0.80和Dice 0.89，YOLO11-seg以约14倍更少参数达到IoU 0.79。

Conclusion: 整合最优分类和分割模型的端到端框架提供了准确、计算高效且可复现的自动化组织病理图像分析基线，为肺癌组织病理学的自动化诊断辅助提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Lung cancer remains the leading cause of cancer-related mortality worldwide, while histopathological diagnosis is often affected by inter-observer variability and the substantial workload associated with manual slide examination. Although deep learning has shown considerable potential in computational pathology, comprehensive benchmarks that integrate tissue classification and region segmentation within a unified analytical framework remain limited. This study presents a two-stage deep learning framework for multi-class tissue classification and pixel-level histopathological region segmentation, accompanied by a systematic comparison of state-of-the-art architectures at each stage. For tissue classification, six models, a custom convolutional neural network, VGG16, DenseNet, MobileNetV3, a custom Vision Transformer, and YOLO11, are evaluated on a combined dataset of 39,000 images derived from LC25000 and LungHist700. The models distinguish between adenocarcinoma, squamous cell carcinoma, and normal lung tissue. YOLO11 achieves the best classification performance, with an accuracy of 98.38%, a five-fold cross-validation accuracy of 98.21 +/- 0.35%, and a macro F1-score of 0.98. For region segmentation, U-Net, ResNet-encoder U-Net, DeepLabV3+, and YOLO11-seg are evaluated using the GlaS gland segmentation benchmark. DeepLabV3+ obtains the highest Intersection over Union of 0.80 and a Dice score of 0.89, while YOLO11-seg achieves a comparable Intersection over Union of 0.79 using approximately 14x fewer parameters. The best-performing classification and segmentation models are subsequently integrated into an end-to-end framework, providing an accurate, computationally efficient, and reproducible baseline for automated histopathological image analysis.

</details>


### [133] [BagShift: Measuring How Patch Selection Changes the Evidence Seen by Whole-Slide MIL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15970)
*Ruicheng Yuan,Zhenxuan Zhang,Liwei Hu,Anbang Wang,Haijie Xu,Jiawei Luo,Guang Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BagShift 提出了一个配对协议，在相同病例、相同特征和相同预测器的条件下改变 MIL 的补丁选择器，隔离选择器响应对病例混合的影响。实验表明，即使补丁预算相同，不同选择策略（全组织采样 vs 局部集中采样）会导致显著不同的证据暴露和模型性能（PANDA QWK 下降 1.57 和 17.96 点；CAMELYON16 局部视图仅保留 10% 微转移病灶）。论文强调补丁数量说明的是计算量而非观测证据，部署评估应同时报告选择器保留了什么以及重复观测的聚合方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在整张幻灯片多实例学习（MIL）中，部署时的计算限制、组织掩膜或区域化工作流会改变补丁选择器，即使补丁数量不变，模型观测到的证据也可能完全不同。现有评估未将选择器变化与病例混合的影响分离，导致部署性能评估失真。

Method: BagShift 采用配对协议：对同一病例，保持特征和预测器固定，仅改变补丁选择器（如全组织均匀采样 vs 围绕单一坐标局部集中采样），在固定 128 补丁预算下对比不同观测视图。同时提出在可获得重复局部观测时，将补丁联合后再进行一次非线性 MIL 处理，与直接聚合区域预测进行对比。

Result: PANDA 上全组织采样与局部集中采样分别使 QWK 下降 1.57 和 17.96 点；CAMELYON16 上局部视图仅保留 10.0% 的微转移肿瘤观察，匹配曝光无法稳定恢复损失；外部肺亚型分类任务对此压力源响应较小。补丁联合后再做 MIL（7.87 点 QWK 提升）优于平均区域预测。

Conclusion: 补丁数量只规定了计算资源，并不决定模型观察到的证据内容。部署评估必须同时报告选择器保留的证据范围以及重复观测的聚合策略，否则无法有效衡量 MIL 系统在真实部署中的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Whole-slide multiple-instance learning (MIL) observes only the patches admitted by its selector. Deployment can alter this selector through compute limits, tissue masking, or regional workflows, even when the patch count is unchanged. We introduce BagShift, a paired protocol that changes the selector for the same case while holding its features and predictor fixed, thereby isolating selector response from case mix. With equal 128-patch budgets, sampling across the tissue or concentrating around one coordinate exposes markedly different evidence: on PANDA, the two views reduce quadratic weighted kappa by 1.57 and 17.96 points, respectively (QWK reported on the $\times100$ scale). On CAMELYON16, lesion annotations withheld from model development show that localized views retain tumor in only 10.0\% of micrometastatic observations, and matched exposure does not consistently recover the loss. The same fixed-count stressor produces a much smaller response on external lung subtyping, although differences in relative coverage make cross-task severity descriptive. When repeated localized observations are available, unioning their patches before one nonlinear MIL pass improves PANDA QWK by 7.87 points over averaging regional predictions. Patch count specifies computation, not observed evidence; deployment evaluations should report both what a selector preserves and how repeated observations are aggregated.

</details>


### [134] [CM-MAE: A Physics-Guided Cross-Modal Self-Supervised Learning Framework for Vision-Wireless Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15972)
*Yubo Zhang,Yiyao Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CM-MAE是一个自监督视觉-无线跨场景预训练框架，通过软对比对齐损失（基于波束功率相似度而非仅配对样本）和掩码联合解码器，在DeepSense 6G数据上实现跨场景表示迁移，将线性探针平均准确率从24.88%提升至29.49%，融合微调达到77.38% Top-1准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同步摄像头与无线测量通过不同物理通道观察同一场景，但在视角、交通、光照和传播几何变化时，单一部署中学到的表示会失效。现有方法在跨场景迁移中表现不佳，需要更鲁棒的跨模态自监督预训练框架。

Method: 提出CM-MAE框架：(1) 软对比对齐损失——基于测量波束功率剖面间的相似度构建目标分布，避免将相似方向响应的非相同样本强制分离为假负样本；(2) 掩码联合解码器——在模态dropout下重建隐藏视觉块和无线角度簇；(3) 差分率微调规则——新融合头快速适应而编码器缓慢更新。

Result: 在DeepSense 6G序列不相交协议下，软对齐损失将线性探针迁移均值从24.88%提升至29.49%；融合微调在未见场景6-8达到77.38% Top-1准确率，可选的转导归一化适配达78.69%。

Conclusion: 软对比对齐策略有效缓解了跨模态对比学习中的假负样本问题，提升了视觉-无线表示的跨场景迁移能力。但需要明确该结果属于表示迁移诊断，而非主动波束预测或降低扫描开销的主张。

Abstract: Synchronized camera and wireless measurements observe the same scene through different physical channels. The central difficulty is that a representation learned in one deployment can fail when viewpoint, traffic, illumination, and propagation geometry change. This paper presents CM-MAE, a self-supervised vision--wireless pretraining framework for cross-scenario representation transfer. The evaluated real-data model uses only RGB frames and the measured 64-beam received-power vector available in DeepSense 6G; it does not use ray-traced paths, calibrated depth, or beam-index labels during pretraining. Its central pretraining term is a \emph{soft contrastive alignment loss}. Instead of making the synchronized image--wireless pair the only positive pair, this loss builds a target distribution from similarities between measured beam-power profiles, so nonidentical samples with similar directional responses are not forced apart as false negatives. A masked joint decoder provides the complementary local objective by reconstructing hidden visual patches and wireless angular clusters under modality dropout. After pretraining, a differential-rate fine-tuning rule lets a new fusion head adapt quickly while the encoders move slowly. Under a sequence-disjoint DeepSense 6G protocol, adding the soft alignment loss improves a matched linear-probe transfer average from 24.88\% to 29.49\%. Mild fusion fine-tuning reaches 77.38\% Top-1 accuracy on unseen Scenarios 6--8, and optional transductive normalization adaptation reaches 78.69\%. Since the fusion setting uses the contemporaneous 64-beam power vector at inference, these results should be read as representation-transfer diagnostics, not as proactive beam-prediction or reduced-sweeping claims.

</details>


### [135] [A Plug-and-Play 2D Motion Interface for Real-World Motion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15984)
*Kaname Yokoyama,Norimichi Ukita*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种即插即用的2D运动接口，使3D预训练的Motion Language Models (MoLMs)无需微调即可接受2D运动输入，解决了单目视频难以获取精确3D运动的问题，并在真实世界视频评估中验证了2D运动接口的实用性与竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoLMs通常依赖对3D运动进行tokenization再交给语言模型处理，但从单目视频获取精确的3D运动非常困难，严重限制了MoLMs在真实世界中的应用。本文旨在解决这一瓶颈，使MoLMs能够直接利用易于获取的2D运动输入。

Method: 提出一个plug-and-play的2D Motion Interface，将2D关节点运动映射到3D预训练MoLM可接受的输入空间，无需修改或微调原始模型；同时引入适用于单目真实世界视频的real-video adapter，构建了相应的单目真实世界运动评估数据集。

Result: 在公开数据集上，该方法在多个MoLM上取得与3D运动输入相当的性能，且优于从头在2D运动上训练的MoLM；在构建的单目真实世界视频评估集上，2D运动在单目姿态估计设定下优于3D运动。

Conclusion: 2D运动为MoLMs在真实世界运动理解场景中的部署提供了一种实用接口，表明无需精确3D重建也可实现有效的运动理解。

Abstract: Motion Language Models (MoLMs) typically understand human motions by tokenizing 3D motion and processing the resulting tokens using a language model. However, obtaining accurate 3D motions from monocular videos is challenging, limiting their real-world applicability. To address this issue, we introduce a plug-and-play 2D Motion Interface that enables 3D-pretrained MoLMs to accept 2D motion inputs without modifying or fine-tuning the original models.
  Experiments on public datasets show that our method achieves performance comparable to 3D motion inputs across multiple MoLMs and outperforms training MoLMs from scratch on 2D motions. We further construct a monocular real-world video motion evaluation dataset and introduce a real-video adapter, demonstrating the usefulness of 2D motions over 3D motions under the evaluated monocular pose-estimation setting. These results suggest that 2D motion provides a practical interface for deploying MoLMs in real-world motion understanding settings. Code is available at https://github.com/irajisamurai/2D-Motion-Interface.

</details>


### [136] [Spatial Temporal Synergy: Balancing Change and Invariance in Text Driven 3D Human Motion Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16008)
*Shaohui Lin,Zhenwu Shi,Jingyu Gong,Jiao Xie,Yu Zhou,Baochang Zhang,Lizhuang Ma,Chia-Wen Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CIME统一框架用于文本驱动的动作编辑，将"变化"与"不变"解耦到空间姿态和时间节奏两个维度。空间姿态上采用全能监督的正负学习机制，时间节奏上引入黎曼非均匀积分流形映射模块实现高保真物理节拍复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型的文本驱动动作编辑方法难以平衡文本响应的"变化"与运动本身的"不变性"，依赖粗糙空间约束和刚性均匀时间假设，导致编辑时空间运动失真及固有物理节奏被破坏。

Method: 提出CIME统一框架：(1) 空间姿态维度——采用全能监督的正负学习机制，包含层次化回溯特征监督、细微运动保持以及基于三元组的语义对齐；(2) 时间节奏维度——引入黎曼非均匀积分流形映射(RNIMM)模块，通过运动学感知的非均匀时间戳实现编辑文本中物理节拍的高保真复现。

Result: 在MotionFix和STANCE Adjustment数据集上，CIME在编辑对齐和结构保真度方面达到最先进性能。代码和模型已开源。

Conclusion: 通过将变化与不变性在空间和时间两个维度上系统解耦，CIME有效解决了文本驱动动作编辑中"变化-不变"平衡难题，验证了统一架构的有效性。

Abstract: Text-driven human motion editing aims to modify existing motion sequences according to natural language instructions while maintaining the structural consistency of the original motion. Existing diffusion-based approaches struggle to balance text-responsive "change" and inertial "invariance". They often rely on coarse spatial constraints and rigid uniform time assumptions, leading to spatial motion distortions and the destruction of intrinsic physical rhythms during variable-length editing. To handle these challenges, we propose Change and Invariance Motion Editing (CIME), a unified framework that comprehensively decouples change and invariance into spatial pose and temporal rhythm dimensions. For spatial poses, our method integrates an omni-supervised positive-negative learning mechanism comprising hierarchical retrospective feature supervision, subtle motion preservation, and triplet-based semantic alignment. For temporal rhythms, we introduce the Riemannian Non-uniform Integral Manifold Mapping (RNIMM) module, which achieves high-fidelity reproduction of physical beats in the edited text via kinematics-aware non-uniform timestamps. Extensive experiments on the MotionFix and STANCE Adjustment datasets demonstrate that CIME achieves state-of-the-art performance in editing alignment and structural fidelity, validating the effectiveness of our unified architecture. Our source codes and models have been released at: github.com/ZhenwuShi/CIME.git

</details>


### [137] [Depth-guided Multi-view Exposure Bracketing for HDR Robot Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16014)
*Jinnyeong Kim,Juhyung Choi,Woohyeok Kim,Sunghyun Cho,Seung-Hwan Baek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向多传感器机器人系统的单次曝光HDR成像基准数据集（121个真实场景+20个CARLA合成序列），并提出Depth-guided Multi-view Exposure Bracketing (DMEB)方法，通过多视角低比特深度相机配合不同曝光并结合深度引导的置信度感知融合实现单次HDR成像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极端光照条件下的可靠单次HDR成像一直是长期挑战，但缺少用于评估多传感器机器人系统中HDR感知的综合性基准数据集，因此需要填补这一空白。

Method: 构建大型数据集（自定义机器人视觉平台+iPhone 13 Pro采集121个真实场景，含中等和超高动态范围，另加20个CARLA模拟器合成视频序列）；提出DMEB参考管线，在多视角低比特深度相机上分配差异极大的曝光值，通过深度引导的置信度感知融合实现单次HDR重建。

Result: 在数据集上的评估表明DMEB建立了强参考点，并展示该传感器配置对多相机和深度传感器系统实现鲁棒HDR感知的潜力。

Conclusion: 该工作为多传感器机器人HDR感知提供了首个大规模基准和参考方法，验证了多视角曝光括架配合深度引导融合方案的有效性。

Abstract: Achieving reliable single-shot high dynamic range (HDR) imaging under extreme illumination conditions remains a long-standing challenge, yet no comprehensive benchmark exist for evaluating HDR perception in multi-sensor robotic systems. To fill this gap, we introduce a large-scale dataset collected via a custom robotic vision platform and an iPhone 13 Pro: 121 real-world scenes spanning modest and ultra-high dynamic range conditions, alongside 20 synthetic video sequences from the CARLA simulator. As a reference pipeline for this dataset, we propose Depth-guided Multi-view Exposure Bracketing (DMEB), a single-shot HDR method that distributes drastically different exposures across multi-view low-bit-depth cameras and fuses them via depth-guided confidence-aware fusion. Evaluations on our dataset show that DMEB establishes a strong reference point and highlight the promise of this sensor configuration for robust HDR perception in diverse multi-camera and depth sensor system.

</details>


### [138] [Multi-scale Decomposed Convolution Refinement Network for Visible-Infrared Person Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16015)
*Mingsheng Zheng,Zirui Jiang,Bo Liu,Yupeng Chen,Jun Zhang,Kai Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MDCRNet提出一种多尺度分解卷积精炼网络，通过分层学习模块捕获多尺度空间依赖，并结合联合判别度量损失（含粒度判别损失）来增强跨模态特征学习和判别度量学习，在SYSU-MM01和RegDB数据集上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可见光-红外行人重识别（VI-ReID）面临跨模态差异和判别能力有限的问题，现有方法在语义挖掘、跨模态融合和特征约束方面存在不足。

Method: 提出MDCRNet，包含：(1) 分层学习模块（HLM），内含四个分层分解卷积注意力（HDCA）模块，每个模块配备轻量级通道注意力和多尺度空间感知块；(2) 联合判别度量损失（JDML），包含新颖的粒度判别损失（GDL），同时优化类内紧凑性和跨模态类间可分性。

Result: 在SYSU-MM01和RegDB两个公开基准数据集上取得最先进性能，代码已开源。

Conclusion: MDCRNet通过多尺度分解卷积和有效的度量学习显著改善了VI-ReID的跨模态特征学习能力，为可见光-红外行人重识别提供了新方案。

Abstract: Visible-infrared person re-identification (VI-ReID) suffers from cross-modal discrepancies and limited discriminative capabilities, leading to suboptimal recognition performance. Current approaches exhibit limitations in semantic mining, cross-modal fusion and feature constraints. To tackle these challenges, we propose MDCRNet, a Multi-scale Decomposed Convolution Refinement Network that enhances cross-modal feature learning and discriminative metric learning. Specifically, we introduce a Hierarchical Learning Module (HLM) containing four Hierarchical Decomposed Convolution Attention (HDCA) modules, each equipped with lightweight channel attention and multi-scale spatial perception blocks to capture multi-scale spatial dependencies. Moreover, we develop a Joint Discriminative Metric Loss (JDML) incorporating a novel Granularity Discriminative Loss (GDL) that simultaneously optimizes intra-identity compactness and inter-identity separability across modalities. Extensive experiments on SYSU-MM01 and RegDB datasets demonstrate that MDCRNet achieves state-of-the-art performance on both benchmarks. Code is available at https://github.com/Kevin-zms/MDCRNet.

</details>


### [139] [TR-GS: High-Fidelity Sparse-View CT Volumetric Rendering via t-Distribution Gaussian Splatting and Ray-Confidence Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16042)
*Zedong Xiao,Yiren Wang,Zhou Liu,Xiaolin Liu,Zhangji Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TR-GS是一个面向稀疏视角CT体渲染的高斯泼溅框架，用可投影的学生t分布基元替代标准高斯基元，引入射线置信度模型和置信度引导的3D小波正则化，在合成与真实数据集上超越了多数基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏视角CT能减少投影需求和辐射暴露，但有限观测会导致结构伪影与重建不确定性。现有基于标准高斯基元的CT重建方法对稀疏视角下不可靠观测敏感，需要更鲁棒的体渲染表示。

Method: 1) 用可投影的学生t分布基元（Student's t-distribution primitives）替代标准高斯基元，增强对离群观测的鲁棒性；2) 引入射线置信度模型，根据局部射线可观测性调节基元自由度；3) 采用置信度引导的3D小波正则化，平衡高频细节保留与噪声抑制。

Result: 在合成和真实世界数据集上的实验表明，TR-GS在大多数评估场景中优于代表性基线，在其余场景中也保持竞争力。

Conclusion: TR-GS为稀疏视角CT体渲染提供了一种更稳健的高斯泼溅方案，生成的体表示可支持XR可视化和交互式临床渲染等下游医学多媒体应用。

Abstract: High-fidelity 3D medical visualization supports applications such as clinical assessment and surgical planning. Sparse-view computed tomography (CT) can reduce projection requirements and associated radiation exposure, but limited observations may introduce structural artifacts and reconstruction uncertainty. Although 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) provides an efficient explicit representation for volumetric rendering, existing CT methods based on standard Gaussian primitives may be sensitive to unreliable observations under sparse-view acquisition. We present TR-GS, a Gaussian-splatting framework for sparse view CT volumetric rendering. TR-GS replaces standard Gaussian primitives with projectable Student's t-distribution primitives and introduces a ray-confidence model that regulates their degrees of freedom according to local ray observability. Confidence-guided 3D wavelet regularization is further used to balance high-frequency detail preservation and noise suppression. This work is licensed under a Creative Commons Attribution 4.0 International License. Experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets show that TR-GS improves over representative baselines in most evaluated settings and remains competitive in the remaining cases. The resulting volumetric representations may support downstream medical multimedia applications, including XR-based visualization and interactive clinical rendering.

</details>


### [140] [SafeGesture: Evaluating Fine-Grained Hand Gesture Understanding in Vision-Language Models through Scenario-Conditioned Safety Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16081)
*Taegang Kim,Saleh Afroogh,Junfeng Jiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SafeGesture基准，评估视觉语言模型在手势识别与场景安全推理上的解耦表现，发现主要瓶颈是场景条件下的安全推理而非手势识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放权重和前沿VLM在通用图像理解上表现良好，但在安全关键操作场景中解释细粒度手势的能力未被系统检验。需要一个基准来揭示模型在这种任务上的真实能力边界。

Method: 构建SafeGesture基准：将6个HaGRID手势与8个操作场景配对，共4,800个条目。评估5个模型（Qwen2.5-VL-7B, LLaVA-NeXT-7B, InternVL2-8B, Phi-3.5-Vision, GPT-4o）的手势识别准确率和安全推理准确率，并通过消融实验（无视觉输入、文本手势提示）分离瓶颈。

Result: 发现感知-推理解耦：GPT-4o手势准确率98.4%但安全准确率仅53.3%；Qwen2.5-VL为84.9%和39.5%，差距分别达45.0和45.4个百分点。多数模型几乎不使用不确定性标签。场景多数策略（无视觉输入）达58.3%，超过所有被评估模型。视觉输入提升安全准确率11.2-30.2个百分点，但提供真实手势文本仅提升0.4-3.2个百分点，无模型超过56.2%。

Conclusion: 主要瓶颈是场景条件下的安全推理而非手势识别；当前VLM在安全关键的手势-场景关联推理上存在显著不足，且基准准确率掩盖了严重的标签偏差问题。

Abstract: Open-weight and frontier vision-language models (VLMs) perform well on general image understanding, but their ability to interpret fine-grained hand gestures in safety-critical operational contexts remains largely unexamined. We introduce SafeGesture, a benchmark that evaluates whether a model can infer scenario-appropriate safety actions from hand gestures. It pairs six HaGRID gestures with eight operational scenarios for 4,800 items and evaluates Qwen2.5-VL-7B, LLaVA-NeXT-7B, InternVL2-8B, Phi-3.5-Vision, and GPT-4o. Results reveal a perception-reasoning decoupling: GPT-4o achieves 98.4% gesture accuracy but 53.3% safety accuracy, while Qwen2.5-VL reaches 84.9% and 39.5%, yielding gaps of 45.0 and 45.4 percentage points. Four of five models rarely or never use the uncertainty label, and failure directions differ substantially across models. Accuracy also obscures label bias: a scenario-majority policy with no visual input reaches 58.3%, above every evaluated model, while only GPT-4o exceeds this prior under macro-F1. Visual input improves safety accuracy by 11.2 to 30.2 percentage points, but providing the ground-truth gesture as text improves performance by only 0.4 to 3.2 points, and no model exceeds 56.2%. These results indicate that the main bottleneck is scenario-conditioned safety reasoning rather than gesture recognition.

</details>


### [141] [Representation Is Not Enough: Body-Localized Thermal Evidence for Contactless Stress and Craving Sensing in Opioid Use Disorder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16087)
*Sachin Deb,Harshit Sharma,Asif Salekin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FABLE-Therm是一种弱监督接触式热成像压力感知架构，通过融合冻结的foundation-model编码器在embedding层面保留局部化证据（身体区域×时间×编码器特定表示），在阿片类药物使用障碍（OUD）患者的热视频上达到0.938 AUROC，并首次证明从接触式热视频可恢复自我报告的渴求感。论文还提出了模态无关的子人群差距分解方法，区分数据不足与个体异质性对部署公平性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去除可穿戴设备后，生理监测失去了监督信号（压力反应发生在何时何地）。接触式压力感知成为一个弱监督证据定位问题：需要从clip级标签追溯到产生该标签的身体区域和时间点。在OUD（阿片类药物使用障碍）场景中，压力是复发的主要诱因，而早期恢复期间持续佩戴可穿戴设备困难，因此需要无需佩戴设备的生理监测方案。

Method: 提出FABLE-Therm弱监督架构：(1) 在embedding级别融合多个冻结的foundation-model编码器（而非特征拼接或预测平均），保留了局部化证据直到最终决策；(2) 提供理论解释为何局部化融合优于特征拼接和预测平均；(3) 通过保留的身体区域×时间×编码器特定表示，实现证据定位；(4) 提出模态无关的子人群差距分解，区分数据不足贡献与个体异质性贡献。

Result: 在固定热视频上达到0.938 AUROC（held-out参与者），学习的表示可迁移到自我报告渴求感（首次证明可从接触式热视频恢复渴求）。子人群分析显示：仅改进表示不足以实现公平部署，从服务不足群体补充数据只能恢复约一半的群体差距，其余反映人与人之间的异质性。数据为第一个cohort结构化的接触式热OUD基准。

Conclusion: 保留局部化证据既支持准确的感知，也支持对"模型在谁身上失败及为什么"的原则性分析。提出的差距分解方法适用于具有可识别子人群的任何模型，具有超出热感知领域的普适性。

Abstract: Removing wearables from physiological monitoring also removes their supervision: the signal indicating where and when a stress response occurred. Contactless stress sensing therefore becomes a weakly supervised evidence-localization problem, where a clip-level label must be traced to the body regions and moments that produced it. We address this with FABLE-Therm, a weakly supervised architecture that preserves localized evidence across body regions, time, and encoder-specific representations until the final decision. FABLE-Therm fuses frozen foundation-model encoders at the embedding level, with theory explaining why localized fusion can outperform feature concatenation and prediction averaging. We study this problem in opioid use disorder (OUD), where stress is a major relapse trigger and sustained wearable use can be difficult during early recovery. Using fixed thermal video, FABLE-Therm achieves 0.938 AUROC on held-out participants, and its learned representation transfers to self-reported craving, providing, to our knowledge, the first evidence that craving can be recovered from contactless thermal video. Localized evidence also enables participant-level analysis of deployment failure. We find that improving representation alone is insufficient for equitable deployment: additional data from the underserved group would recover only about half of the cohort gap, while the remainder reflects person-to-person heterogeneity. This modality-agnostic decomposition applies to models with identifiable subpopulations. Together with the first cohort-structured contactless thermal OUD benchmark, our results show that preserving localized evidence supports both accurate sensing and principled analysis of who a model fails and why.

</details>


### [142] [TISC: A Text-Driven Image Semantic Communication System for Faithful Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16100)
*Feifan Zhang,Yuyang Du,Xiaoyan Liu,Soung Chang Liew*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TISC框架，通过树形属性语义提取(TSASE)和初始噪声优化(INO)机制改善文本驱动图像语义通信中I2T语义提取和T2I语义重建的保真度问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式图像语义通信虽然带宽成本极低，但面临两个关键瓶颈：一是I2T阶段整体图像描述会遗漏细粒度对象属性和空间位置信息，导致文本偏离原始图像语义；二是T2I阶段即使给定相同的语义忠实文本，不同初始噪声设置会导致重建图像与原始图像的语义一致性差异。这些问题共同限制了图像重建的语义保真度。

Method: TISC框架包含两个核心设计：(1)树形属性语义提取(TSASE)，将语义提取分解为全局场景、背景和对象级属性描述三层结构，覆盖每个检测对象的空间位置、形状/姿态、颜色、材质等物理属性；(2)初始噪声优化(INO)机制，在发射端根据联合考虑视觉和语义一致性的综合相似度分数选择初始噪声种子。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明，TSASE改善了对象位置恢复和语义描述保真度，INO参数研究支持了采用的噪声选择配置。

Conclusion: 通过树形结构化语义提取和发射端噪声优化选择，可以有效提升生成式图像语义通信中重建图像的语义保真度。

Abstract: Generative image semantic communication converts an image into a text description and then performs text-to-image reconstruction at the receiver via diffusion-based generative models. This paradigm has attracted broad attention due to its extremely low bandwidth cost. However, existing methods still face two critical bottlenecks across image-to-text (I2T) semantic extraction at the transmitter and text-to-image (T2I) semantic reconstruction at the receiver: (i) semantic loss and distortion in I2T, where holistic image descriptions may omit fine-grained object attributes and spatial-position information, causing the generated text to deviate from the original image semantics; and (ii) insufficient semantic faithfulness in T2I, where even with the same semantically faithful text description, different initial noise settings may lead diffusion-based reconstruction to produce images with different levels of semantic consistency with the original image. These issues jointly limit the semantic faithfulness of image reconstruction. To address them, we propose TISC, a text-driven image semantic communication framework tailored for faithful reconstruction. TISC incorporates two key designs: (1) Tree-Structured Attribute Semantic Extraction (TSASE), which decomposes semantic extraction into global scene, background, and object-level attribute descriptions, covering spatial position, shape/pose, color, material, and other physical attributes for each detected object; and (2) an Initial Noise Optimization (INO) mechanism, which selects an initial noise seed at the transmitter according to a comprehensive similarity score that jointly considers visual and semantic consistency. Experiments on multiple datasets show that TSASE improves object-position recovery and semantic description faithfulness, while the INO parameter study supports the adopted configuration for noise selection.

</details>


### [143] [Beyond Similarity Matching: Structured Reasoning for Open-Vocabulary Referring Segmentation in 3DGS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16103)
*Yizhao Wang,Xinfa Wang,Jingbo Wang,Jingbo Wang,Guantao Zhang,Yafeng Han,Guohong Gao,Yuhe Xia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了QAGaussian，一个查询自适应的神经推理框架，用于3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）中的开放词汇指代分割。通过学习查询条件化的多尺度高斯槽、关系感知的槽图传播、以及粒度自适应路由结合多个掩码分支，解决了现有方法在属性、参考对象、空间关系和细粒度部件方面的不足。仅用Mosaic3D-5.6M预训练，在不微调的情况下达到47.2 Avg. mIoU和63.2 Avg. F1，优于最强基线2.7 mIoU与2.9 F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS指代分割方法依赖全局文本-区域相似度，对涉及属性、参考物体、空间关系及细粒度部件的查询能力弱，导致目标-参考混淆、粒度不匹配、部分-整体泄漏和关系违反等问题。

Method: 1) 查询条件化多尺度高斯槽：学习由输入表达式塑造感受野的可微候选槽；2) 关系感知槽图：通过语言条件化边权重传播目标-参考、属性、部分-整体和上下文证据；3) 粒度自适应路由器：软性组合区域级、物体级、部件级、属性感知和关系感知的掩码分支；4) 关系约束精炼：保证空间、部分-整体、属性和几何一致性。

Result: 在独立基准上（无目标数据集微调）达到47.2 Avg. mIoU、63.2 Avg. F1，较最强3DGS指代基线提升2.7 mIoU与2.9 F1；Part-mIoU从38.6提升至43.4，Rel-mIoU从44.4提升至50.8，目标-参考混淆率从10.8降至7.4。

Conclusion: 查询条件化槽学习、关系感知图推理与自适应路由为3DGS开放词汇指代分割提供了有效的神经建模策略，代码已开源。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary referring segmentation in 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) requires a neural model to select Gaussian primitives according to free-form language expressions. Existing 3DGS-based methods usually rely on global text-region similarity, which is weak for queries involving attributes, reference objects, spatial relations, and fine-grained parts. This often causes target-reference confusion, granularity mismatch, part-whole leakage, and relation violations. We propose QAGaussian, a query-adaptive neural reasoning framework for language-guided Gaussian primitive selection. QAGaussian first learns query-conditioned multi-scale Gaussian slots as differentiable candidates whose receptive fields are shaped by the input expression. It then builds a relation-aware slot graph with language-conditioned edge weighting to propagate target-reference, attribute, part-whole, and contextual evidence. A granularity-adaptive router softly combines region-level, object-level, part-level, attribute-aware, and relation-aware mask branches, followed by relation-constrained refinement for spatial, part-whole, attribute, and geometric consistency. QAGaussian is pretrained only on Mosaic3D-5.6M for Gaussian-text alignment and evaluated on independent benchmarks without target-dataset fine-tuning. It achieves 47.2 Avg. mIoU and 63.2 Avg. F1, outperforming the strongest 3DGS referring baseline by 2.7 mIoU points and 2.9 F1 points. It also improves Part-mIoU from 38.6 to 43.4, Rel-mIoU from 44.4 to 50.8, and reduces target-reference confusion from 10.8 to 7.4. These results demonstrate that query-conditioned slot learning, relation-aware graph reasoning, and adaptive routing provide an effective neural modeling strategy for open-vocabulary referring segmentation in 3DGS. The code is available at https://github.com/zqeslwyz/QAGaussian.

</details>


### [144] [Nexus: Structured Synergy for Efficient Text-to-Image Generation using Rectified Flow Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16104)
*Yizhao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Nexus是一个集成了稀疏架构（MoE）、线性复杂度注意力（gated DeltaNet）和低比特量化训练的高效文本到图像生成模型，在保持与SDXL/SD3相当生成质量的同时显著提升推理效率，降低计算量和内存占用，适合高分辨率合成和边缘部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型和流匹配模型在文本到图像生成上取得了显著进展，但高计算量、二次复杂度和大显存占用阻碍了高分辨率合成以及边缘设备部署，需要更高效的架构设计。

Method: Nexus将三种技术联合优化以降低计算和内存：1) MoE（混合专家）前馈层实现稀疏激活；2) gated DeltaNet注意力实现线性复杂度；3) 按专家进行低比特量化训练。三者联合优化以在效率和生成质量间取得平衡。

Result: 在COCO和LAION数据集上的实验表明，Nexus在生成质量上与SDXL和SD3等主流模型相当，同时实现了显著更高的推理效率，验证了其在计算效率和生成质量上的有效性。

Conclusion: Nexus通过稀疏架构、线性注意力和低比特量化的联合设计，证明了高效生成模型可以在不牺牲生成质量的前提下大幅降低计算和内存开销，为高分辨率文本到图像生成和边缘部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Diffusion and flow matching models have made significant progress in text-to-image generation, yet high computation, quadratic complexity, and large memory footprint hinder high-resolution synthesis and edge deployment. We propose Nexus, which integrates sparse architecture, linear complexity, and low-bit quantization. It combines MoE feed-forward layers, gated DeltaNet attention, and per-expert low-bit training to reduce computation and memory. Their joint optimization allows Nexus to achieve generation quality comparable to mainstream models such as SDXL and SD3 while delivering markedly higher inference efficiency. Experiments on COCO and LAION validate its effectiveness.

</details>


### [145] [SUGFW+: An Uncertainty-guided Feature Weighting Framework for Cold Start Active Adaptation of SAM in Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16110)
*Xiaochuan Ma,Ning Zhu,Jia Fu,Lanfeng Zhong,Hanyu Jiang,Bin Song,Kang Li,Guotai Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SUGFW+框架，利用SAM的预训练嵌入进行冷启动主动学习（CSAL），通过补丁级特征与不确定性计算（PFUC）、补丁全局判别表示（PGDR）模块以及贪婪选择（GSCU）策略，将样本选择与模型训练通过不确定性提示微调（UPFT）紧密耦合，在四个公开医学数据集上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有CSAL方法依赖数据集特定的自监督学习（SSL）映射特征空间进行样本选择，效率低且泛化差。SAM作为基础模型提供强泛化特征嵌入，但如何系统利用SAM的嵌入进行冷启动样本选择与微调适配仍未被探索。

Method: SUGFW+框架包含三部分：(1) PFUC利用SAM计算补丁级特征与不确定性；(2) PGDR聚合补丁级嵌入为不确定性感知的图像级特征；(3) GSCU结合聚类的多样性与不确定性进行贪婪样本选择。此外提出UPFT将样本选择与SAM微调训练紧密集成，而非将两者解耦。

Result: 在四个公开医学图像分割数据集上，SUGFW+相比现有CSAL方法取得SOTA性能，代码已开源。

Conclusion: 利用SAM预训练嵌入进行冷启动主动学习是有效且高效的替代方案，将样本选择与模型训练耦合可进一步提升分割性能。

Abstract: Cold Start Active Learning (CSAL) is important in improving the performance of a medical image segmentation model with low annotation budget by querying a small subset for annotation from an unlabeled training set. Existing CSAL methods typically rely on inefficient dataset-specific Self-Supervised Learning (SSL) to map the unlabeled images into a feature space for sample selection. Recently, the advent of foundation models such as the Segment Anything Model (SAM) offer a promising alternative as the pre-trained model can provide strong generalizable feature embeddings, and allow high performance in downstream tasks after fine-tuning (adaptation). However, how to systematically exploit SAM's inherent embeddings for cold-start sample selection during adaptation with low annotation budget remains underexplored. To address this, we propose an extended SAM-based Uncertainty-guided Feature Weighting (SUGFW+) framework for CSAL and adaptation of SAM. Specifically, it leverages the SAM for Patch-level Feature and Uncertainty Calculation (PFUC), and introduces a Patch-based Global Distinct Representation (PGDR) module that aggregates patch-level embeddings into highly discriminative, uncertainty-aware image-level features. These features are then utilized by a Greedy Selection with Cluster and Uncertainty (GSCU) strategy to combine diversity and uncertainty during sample selection. Unlike prior CSAL methods that decouple sample selection from model training, SUGFW+ tightly integrates these two stages via an Uncertainty-Prompted Fine-Tuning (UPFT) process of SAM in model training. Extensive experiments on four public datasets demonstrate that SUGFW+ achieves state-of-the-art performance against existing CSAL methods. Code is available at https://github.com/HiLab-git/SUGFW-plus.

</details>


### [146] [TokenSTFormer: A Tokenized Spatial-temporal Attention Model for Holistic Motion Analysis in Adolescent Idiopathic Scoliosis Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16122)
*Dong Chen,Kenneth M. C. Cheung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ScoliGait数据集（1,516个步态视频片段配X光记录）用于青少年特发性脊柱侧弯（AIS）筛查，并引入TokenSTFormer模型对时空语义进行token化以增强特征表示与收敛，在AIS筛查任务上达到SOTA（准确率0.79），优于vanilla Vision Transformer编码器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AIS是青少年中普遍存在的脊柱畸形，若不及时治疗后果严重。传统筛查方法受限于主观判读、依赖专家经验且难以规模化推广，亟需一种可扩展、低成本、客观的筛查方案。

Method: 构建ScoliGait数据集（1,516个步态视频+对应X光标签）；提出TokenSTFormer模型，通过对空间和时间语义进行token化来增强特征表示并改善训练收敛性，利用注意力机制建模整体运动特征。

Result: TokenSTFormer在AIS筛查任务上达到SOTA，准确率0.79，超越vanilla Vision Transformer编码器在多个关键指标上的表现。

Conclusion: 步态视频中的整体运动特征结合注意力模型为AIS筛查提供了可扩展、低成本的解决方案，具有临床应用潜力。

Abstract: Adolescent Idiopathic Scoliosis (AIS) is a prevalent spinal deformity in adolescents that, if left untreated, can result in severe health outcomes. Traditional screening methods are limited by subjective interpretation, reliance on professional expertise and low scalability. To address these challenges, we present ScoliGait dataset, which comprises 1,516 gait video clips paired with corresponding X-ray records. We also introduce TokenSTFormer, a novel model that tokenizes spatial and temporal semantics to enhance feature representation and convergence. Our model achieves state-of-the-art performance, surpassing vanilla Vision Transformer encoder across key metrics, including accuracy of 0.79. This study highlights the potential of leveraging holistic motion features derived from gait video and attention-based models for scalable, cost-effective AIS screening, paving the way for future clinical applications in scoliosis detection.

</details>


### [147] [Graph Neural Assisted Actor-Critic for Latency-Efficient Edge Vision System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16142)
*Alam Noor,Luis Almeida,Kai Li,Jiyan Wu,Miguel Gutiérrez Gaitán,Eduardo Tovar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于图卷积神经网络辅助的A2C深度强化学习系统（GCN-Assisted A2C），用于无人机视频传输中优化可疑目标像素相关区域的选取，从而降低视频帧传输延迟和误检率。该方法结合拉格朗日对偶与梯度下降解决收敛和惩罚约束问题，仅传输帧的子区域而非整帧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机视觉系统在禁飞区监控等场景中需要将视频流传输到地面服务器供操作员辅助，但现有视频传输技术延迟成本高，严重影响操作员辅助的有效性。

Method: 提出GCN-Assisted A2C深度强化学习模型：1）利用拉格朗日对偶形式与梯度下降结合来解决延迟优化中的收敛问题以及过度/不足惩罚约束违反问题；2）仅传输帧中可疑对象的像素相关子区域而非整帧；3）利用GCN探索特征相关像素组的隐藏表示；4）GCN监督A2C模型选择子区域以优化传输延迟并监督UAV动作训练。

Result: 实验结果表明，GCN辅助的A2C相比其他DRL和现有最优模型，降低了无人机视觉系统的视频帧传输延迟和误检率。

Conclusion: GCN-Assisted A2C方法能有效降低无人机视频传输延迟和误检率，为UAV视觉辅助系统提供了更高效的传输策略。

Abstract: UAV on-board vision systems are widely used for different activities, including monitoring in no-fly zones. In this case, the vision-equipped UAV streams a video to a ground server where an operator assists its activities. The latency of video transmission has a profound impact on the effectiveness of the operator assistance. However, most techniques available for video transmission still incur significant latency costs. In this paper, we propose a graph convolutional neural network-assisted (GCN-Assisted A2C) deep reinforcement learning (DRL) system model to find the optimal pixel-correlated area of a suspicious object. We combine the Lagrangian dual form with gradient descent to prevent lack of convergence and over- and under-penalization constraint violation during latency optimization. The proposed system model sends a sub-group pixel-correlated area of the frame from the UAV to the server rather than the transmission of the whole video frame. The proposed framework utilizes the GCN model to explore hidden representations of feature-correlated groups of pixels. Moreover, the GCN supervises the A2C model, which selects a subgroup to enhance transmission latency, thus supervising the training of UAV actions in A2C. Experimental results show that GCN-assisted A2C reduces video frame transmission latency together with false detection rate in UAV vision systems over other DRL and state-of-the-art models.

</details>


### [148] [The Right Prior for the Right Deformation: Rethinking Continuous Deformable Image Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16146)
*Hengjie Liu,Chushu Shen,Dan Ruan,Ke Sheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对连续配准方法（B-Spline和基于INR的方法）进行了验证研究，通过对比四种参数化方法在脑MR（OASIS）和肺CT（DIR-LAB）配准任务上的表现，揭示了隐式形变先验与目标运动模式匹配的重要性。多分辨率B-Spline方法（MR-D-BSCP）在所有测试方法中表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可变形图像配准模型的性能很大程度上取决于其参数化方式隐含的形变先验，但鲜有工作系统地验证这些隐式先验如何影响不同配准任务上的表现。作者希望厘清B-Spline与INR-based方法各自引入的先验，为配准方法设计提供指导原则。

Method: 对比四种连续参数化配准方法：INR-Dense (IDIR，SIREN-INR直接建模稠密位移场)、INR-BSCP (SINR，INR预测B-Spline控制点)、D-BSCP（直接优化单尺度B-Spline控制点）、MR-D-BSCP（增加多分辨率粗到细方案的B-Spline）。在两个数据集上评估：OASIS（脑间配准，形变中等但局部复杂）和DIR-LAB 4DCT（肺呼气到吸气配准，形变更大且更连贯）。

Result: OASIS上D-BSCP匹配或略优于INR-BSCP，说明B-Spline参数化贡献了INR-BSCP大部分有效性；DIR-LAB上单尺度B-Spline方法不适配大而连贯的呼吸运动，而INR-Dense和MR-D-BSCP更有效；MR-D-BSCP在两个任务上均取得最佳性能。

Conclusion: 配准精度高度依赖于将隐式形变先验与目标运动模式相匹配；先验-形变匹配应作为医学图像配准的实用设计原则。代码将开源。

Abstract: Deformable image registration models implicitly encode deformation priors through their parametrization and optimization. In this work, we conduct a validation study on continuous registration methods to examine how these implicit priors affect performance across different registration tasks. Classic B-Spline transformations impose locality, smoothness, and scale through their control-point structure, whereas recent INR-based methods impose different priors through neural parameterization and optimization. We compare INR-Dense (IDIR), which directly models a dense displacement field using a SIREN-based INR; INR-BSCP (SINR), which predicts B-Spline control points with an INR; D-BSCP, which directly optimizes single-scale B-Spline control points; and MR-D-BSCP, which adds a multiresolution coarse-to-fine scheme. Experiments on inter-subject brain MR registration (OASIS) and intra-subject exhale-to-inhale lung CT registration (DIR-LAB 4DCT) reveal different behavior across deformation regimes. On OASIS, where deformations are moderate but locally complex, D-BSCP matches or slightly outperforms INR-BSCP, suggesting that the B-Spline parameterization accounts for much of INR-BSCP's effectiveness. On DIR-LAB 4DCT, where respiratory motion is larger and more coherent, single-scale B-Spline methods (D-BSCP and INR-BSCP) are less suitable, while INR-Dense and MR-D-BSCP are more effective. Across both tasks, MR-D-BSCP achieves the best performance among the tested continuous parameterizations. These findings highlight that registration accuracy depends strongly on matching the induced deformation prior to the target motion pattern, and support prior-deformation matching as a practical design principle for medical image registration. Our code will be available at https://github.com/HengjieLiu/RightPriorDIR.

</details>


### [149] [KeyID: Decoupled Drafting and Keyframe Editing for Identity-Preserving Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16154)
*Jianjie Luo,Yiming Zhong,Haoming Shen,Yupeng Xiao,Zhenguo Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: KeyID是一个无需训练的IPVG框架，将视频动态合成与身份注入解耦，通过参考感知的视频生成（生成与身份无关的视频草稿）和身份保持的关键帧编辑（稀疏关键帧校正+运动插值）实现复杂长序列动作下的身份一致性，在ACM Multimedia 2026 IPVG挑战赛Track 2中获得亚军。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有身份保持视频生成方法要么调优成本高，要么仅停留在输入级增强，难以在复杂长序列动作中维持刚性的身份一致性。作者旨在解决提示遵循与身份保真之间的容量冲突。

Method: KeyID采用模块化设计，包含两个组件：(1) 参考感知视频生成模块，生成与身份无关、对齐多参考的视频草稿；(2) 身份保持关键帧编辑模块，通过稀疏关键帧校正注入目标身份，再通过运动插值完成全视频生成。从密集帧级监督转向稀疏关键帧级细化。

Result: 在官方挑战基准上通过自动和人工评估，KeyID优于先前工作，获得ACM Multimedia 2026 IPVG Grand Challenge Track 2（序列动作）亚军。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 将身份注入从视频动态合成中解耦，以稀疏关键帧级细化替代密集帧级监督，可有效解决身份保真与提示遵循间的冲突，且模块化设计天然支持多主体引用和复杂序列动作生成，无需额外训练。

Abstract: Identity-preserving video generation (IPVG) requires synthesizing videos that are faithful to both reference subjects and text prompts. Existing methods are often hindered by high tuning costs or limited input-level enhancements, struggling to maintain rigid identity consistency during complex, long-sequence actions. To address these limitations, we propose KeyID, a training-free IPVG framework that decouples the synthesis of video dynamics from the injection of identity. Specifically, KeyID comprises two components: (1) Reference-Aware Video Generation, which produces an identity-agnostic video draft aligned with multiple references, and (2) Identity-Preserved Keyframe Editing, which integrates the target identity via sparse keyframe correction and subsequent motion interpolation. By shifting from dense frame-level supervision to sparse keyframe-level refinement, KeyID effectively resolves the capacity conflict between prompt adherence and identity fidelity. Crucially, our modular design allows seamless extension to multi-subject references and complex sequential action generation without additional training. KeyID outperforms prior works and is validated by automatic and human evaluations on the official challenge benchmark, ultimately securing the runner-up position in the Track 2 (Sequential Action) of the ACM Multimedia 2026 IPVG Grand Challenge. Source code is available at https://github.com/WISLab-GDUT/KeyID.

</details>


### [150] [Beyond Clear Skies: Synthetic Seasonal and Weather Variations for Real-World Drone Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16191)
*Tamara R. Lenhard,Andreas Weinmann,Tobias Koch*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SynDroneVision-Weather (SDV-W)，一个面向恶劣天气和季节域偏移的无人机检测合成数据集。SDV-W包含55,187张高分辨率标注图像，覆盖三个城市场景、三种季节配置和多种恶劣天气（雨、雪、雾的多级严重程度），通过保留SDV的场景与轨迹配置实现干净的clean-adverse对比，用于量化条件相关的检测器性能退化。实验表明SDV-W能提升YOLO类检测器在不利外观偏移下的可靠性，减少漏检与误报，并最适合作为通用合成无人机数据的补充。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实部署中的无人机检测需要对恶劣天气和季节性外观变化具有鲁棒性，但获取和标注这类真实数据极其昂贵且难以系统控制天气条件。现有真实数据集覆盖有限，而合成数据提供可控的环境变化和自动标注能力，因此需要构建一个系统性覆盖恶劣天气与季节变化的合成无人机检测数据集。

Method: 基于游戏引擎构建合成数据管线，在保留SynDroneVision (SDV)原有场景和轨迹配置的基础上，系统性扩展出三种季节配置（春/夏/秋等）和多种恶劣天气条件（雨、雪、雾，各自含多个严重等级），在三类城市场景中渲染生成55,187张自动标注的高分辨率图像。数据集设计支持"配对"的清洁-恶劣对比实验，以量化各条件下检测器的具体性能退化。

Result: 在代表性YOLO模型和真实世界数据集上的实验表明：SDV-W能提升检测器在恶劣外观偏移下的可靠性，减少漏检（missed detections）和误报（false alarms），并且作为通用合成无人机检测数据的补充时效果最佳。数据集将在论文接收后公开发布。

Conclusion: SDV-W是一个系统性覆盖恶劣天气与季节变化的合成无人机检测数据集，通过可控的环境渲染和匹配对比设计，有效改善检测器在真实部署中不利外观条件下的表现，并可作为已有合成数据的有力补充。

Abstract: Reliable drone detection under real-world deployment conditions requires training data that spans the full operational design domain, including adverse weather and seasonal appearance variation. However, acquiring and annotating such data at scale remains highly resource-intensive, as adverse-weather conditions are inherently difficult to control, reproduce, and sample systematically. Existing datasets therefore typically provide only limited coverage of such conditions. Conversely, synthetic data offers a scalable alternative: environmental variation becomes controllable, while modern game-engine-based pipelines provide realistic rendering and automatic annotations. Leveraging this potential, we introduce SynDroneVision-Weather (SDV-W), an systematic extension of SynDroneVision (SDV) targeting adverse-weather and seasonal domain shifts in urban drone detection. SDV-W comprises 55,187 annotated high-resolution images from three urban environments, rendered across three seasonal configurations and diverse weather conditions, including rain, snow, and fog at multiple severity levels. By preserving SDV's scene and trajectory configuration, SDV-W enables matched clean-adverse comparisons and quantification of condition-specific detector degradation. Across representative YOLO models and real-world datasets, we show that SDV-W improves detector reliability under adverse appearance shifts, reduces missed detections and false alarms, and is most effective as a complement to general-purpose synthetic drone-detection data. SDV-W will be publicly released upon paper acceptance.

</details>


### [151] [Picking the Right Image to Classify: Reliable-Input Selection in Teledermatology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16198)
*Fabian Gröger,Marco Weishaupt,Philippe Gottfrois,Simone Lionetti,Linda Wermelinger,Nipun Ranasekara,Ludovic Amruthalingam,Alexander A. Navarini,Marc Pouly*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出并基准测试了"可靠输入选择"（reliable-input selection）这一新任务：在远程皮肤病学中，当同一病例有多张临床照片时，如何选择模型最可能正确分类的那张图片。作者用oracle机制证明若总能选对，加权F1可提升约20个百分点；但无需训练数据的四种选择器（嵌入范数、邻域一致性、预测稳定性、模型置信度）均无法大幅缩小这一差距，最佳方案——模型自身置信度及置信度+马氏距离融合——也只恢复了一小部分差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 皮肤病学模型在远程医疗场景中面临分布偏移（光照、角度、距离、对焦、构图等采集条件与训练数据不同），导致模型常误分类。当同一病例有多张照片时，自然想到通过选择"模型最有把握正确分类"的图片来提升准确性，但此前无人系统研究这一任务。

Method: 定义reliable-input selection任务。先建立oracle上界（看到标签，若存在可正确分类的图则选它）。随后基准测试四类无需训练数据的选择器：嵌入范数（embedding norm）、同病例图片间邻域共识（neighborhood consensus）、预测在小扰动下的稳定性（stability under perturbations）、模型自身置信度。此外还测试了带小规模带标签参考集的融合方法（置信度+马氏距离）。实验跨6个皮肤病数据集、9个冻结backbone。

Result: Oracle选择可使加权F1平均提升约20个百分点。但所有training-data-free选择器都只能恢复很小一部分差距；最佳无数据选择器是模型自身置信度。小规模标签参考集的融合（置信度+马氏距离）虽为总体最佳，但仍留下大部分oracle差距无法弥合。

Conclusion: 这是首个提出并基准测试可靠输入选择的研究。该任务对临床重要且尚未解决——现有选择器均无法充分捕捉oracle上界所展示的巨大增益，为后续工作指出明确方向。

Abstract: Dermatology models face distribution shifts in teledermatology settings, where submitted images differ from the training data in lighting, angle, distance, focus, and framing. These test-time images are ordinary clinical photographs, but some fall outside the model's training conditions, leading the model to often misclassify them due to shifts in acquisition between training and deployment. When multiple images of the same case exist (several photos of one patient or lesion), a natural way to improve accuracy is therefore to select the image the model is most likely to classify correctly. We call this task reliable-input selection. An oracle that, for each case, selects a correctly classified image when one exists raises weighted F1 by about 20 percentage points on average across six dermatology datasets and nine frozen backbones. This oracle is an upper bound that sees the labels, whereas a selector must choose blindly. Capturing this gain in practice is hard. A selector that needs no pretraining data applies to any frozen model, including those whose data is not public. It must judge reliability from quantities the model exposes at inference: its embeddings, their norms, and its confidence. We benchmark four such training-data-free selectors: the embedding norm, the neighborhood consensus among a case's images, the stability of the prediction under small perturbations, and the model's own confidence. No training-data-free selector substantially narrows this oracle gap. The best of them is the model's own confidence, but it recovers only a small part of the gap on the clinical datasets. A small labeled reference set does not help either: the best selector overall, a fusion of confidence and Mahalanobis distance, still leaves most of the gap. To our knowledge, this is the first study to introduce and benchmark reliable input selection, a clinically important, unsolved task.

</details>


### [152] [PCT-Prompt: A Prompt-Guided Transformer Framework for Dense Prediction Tasks in Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16225)
*Dejun Zhang,Yanzi Bai,Yiqi Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PCT-Prompt是一个增强标准Transformer在点云密集预测任务中性能的框架，通过引入提示引导的特征分支（包含细粒度特征提取块和提示精炼特征学习块），配合提示丢弃机制平衡局部细节与全局一致性，在ShapeNetPart、S3DIS和DALES数据集上取得优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Transformer在点云物体分类上表现优异，但在复杂场景中的密集预测任务（如语义分割、部件分割）往往因缺乏强的先验假设而性能受限，需要一种方法来增强标准Transformer在密集预测任务中的适应性和表现力。

Method: 提出PCT-Prompt框架，包含双分支结构：(1) 标准Transformer分支利用预训练模型进行点云全局特征提取；(2) 提示引导特征分支包含两个关键组件——细粒度特征提取块（使用几何敏感抽象层捕获多尺度几何特征，结合PnP-3D层整合局部上下文与全局正则化）和提示精炼特征学习块（生成提示token并通过交叉注意力机制精炼）。同时引入提示丢弃机制，在Transformer层间逐步移除提示信息以平衡局部细节与全局一致性。

Result: 在ShapeNetPart、S3DIS和DALES三个基准数据集上的实验表明，PCT-Prompt显著提升了标准Transformer在密集预测任务中的适应性，在真实世界场景中取得了强劲性能。

Conclusion: 通过提示引导的细粒度特征分支与逐步提示丢弃机制，PCT-Prompt有效弥补了标准Transformer在点云密集预测任务中先验假设不足的缺陷，实现了局部细节与全局一致性的良好平衡。

Abstract: Standard Transformers have proven effective in point cloud object classification, but their performance in dense prediction tasks within complex scenes is often hindered by weak prior assumptions. To address this challenge, we propose PCT-Prompt, a novel framework that enhances standard Transformers by introducing a prompt-guided feature branch to improve performance in dense prediction tasks. The standard Transformer branch leverages pre-trained models for global feature extraction from point cloud data, serving as the backbone for processing high-level features. Meanwhile, the prompt-guided feature branch consists of two key components: a fine-grained feature extraction block that captures multi-scale geometric features using geometry-sensitive abstraction layer, along with the PnP-3D layer to integrate local context with global regularization. The second component, the prompt-refined feature learning block generates prompt tokens, which are subsequently refined through cross-attention mechanisms. Additionally, we introduce a prompt drop mechanism that progressively removes prompt information across Transformer layers, balancing local details and global consistency. Experimental results on the ShapeNetPart, S3DIS, and DALES datasets demonstrate that PCT-Prompt significantly improves the adaptability of standard Transformers to dense prediction tasks, achieving strong performance in real-world scenarios.

</details>


### [153] [GaussianDWM++: Language-Grounded 3D Gaussian Driving World Model for Unified Scene Understanding, Editing, and Multi-Modal Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16234)
*Tianchen Deng,Xuefeng Chen,Shuang Wu,Qu Chen,Jiajun Zhu,Bo Dai,Jianfei Yang,Hesheng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于基础特征高斯的驾驶世界模型（GaussianDWM），将Qwen/SigLIP视觉语言特征蒸馏到3D高斯图元中，统一了场景理解、语言推理、可控4D编辑和多模态生成任务，在多个驾驶基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有驾驶世界模型主要关注条件场景生成，缺乏显式3D场景理解、语言接地推理和可控4D编辑能力。点云、占据栅格或BEV等常用表示难以实现文本信息与底层3D场景结构的细粒度对齐。

Method: 1) 提出基础特征高斯分词器（foundation-feature Gaussian tokenizer），直接将Qwen/SigLIP视觉语言特征蒸馏到3D高斯图元中，构建紧凑的开放词汇高斯语义场；2) 设计几何感知高斯适配器（geometry-aware Gaussian adapter），结合重要性感知的层级选择与文本条件的Perceiver式交叉注意力，将稠密高斯图元聚合为紧凑的世界token；3) 引入基于KL的高斯-图像分布对齐目标，将高斯世界token与基础图像token对齐；4) 基于对齐的高斯表示支持指令可控的场景编辑，包括天气条件生成和动态车辆操控。

Result: 在更广泛的驾驶基准上，该方法在场景理解、视觉接地、规划导向推理和可控4D生成任务中均达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: 通过将视觉语言基础模型特征与3D高斯表示深度融合，该框架提供了统一的驾驶世界建模方案，兼具3D场景理解、语言接地推理与可控4D编辑能力，并将开源代码与数据集。

Abstract: Driving World Models (DWMs) have recently advanced rapidly with generative models, yet most existing methods mainly focus on conditional scene generation and lack explicit 3D scene understanding, language-grounded reasoning, and controllable 4D editing capabilities. Moreover, commonly used point cloud, occupancy, or BEV representations make it difficult to achieve fine-grained alignment between textual information and the underlying 3D scene structure. To address these limitations, we propose a foundation-feature Gaussian driving world model that unifies scene understanding, language-grounded reasoning, controllable 4D editing, and multi-modal generation within a single framework. Specifically, we introduce a foundation-feature Gaussian tokenizer that directly distills Qwen/SigLIP visual-language features into 3D Gaussian primitives, building a compact open-vocabulary Gaussian semantic field. We further design a geometry-aware Gaussian adapter that combines importance-aware hierarchical selection with text-conditioned Perceiver-style cross-attention to aggregate dense Gaussian primitives into compact world tokens. To improve representation compatibility, we introduce a KL-based Gaussian--image distribution alignment objective that aligns Gaussian world tokens with foundation image tokens. Based on the aligned Gaussian representation, our framework further supports instruction-controllable scene editing, including weather-conditioned generation and dynamic vehicle manipulation. Extensive experiments on broader driving benchmarks demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance across scene understanding, visual grounding, planning-oriented reasoning, and controllable 4D generation tasks. We will release the code and datasets publicly on Github.

</details>


### [154] [Convolution-Free Holistic Multivariance Decomposition Layer for Efficient Hyperspectral Image Classification Tensor Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16241)
*Süha Tuna,Ülker Başar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为全变分分解（HMD）的新型端到端可微神经网络层，用于高光谱图像分类的特征提取。HMD通过可学习的矩阵值支撑显式分离单模态独立变化与高维协同交互，与下游分类器联合优化，在三个基准HS数据集上优于经典张量分解基线，且以更少的参数达到与2D/3D-CNN相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统张量分解刚性过强，无法捕捉复杂的光谱-空间相互依赖关系；而卷积神经网络参数过多、计算开销大。需要一个既能建模复杂高维交互又参数高效的替代方案。

Method: 引入HMD框架作为可微神经网络层，包含HMD-0、HMD-1、HMD-2三种近似器。通过可学习的矩阵值支撑将单模态独立变化与多模态协作高维交互显式分离，并与下游分类器通过反向传播联合优化。

Result: 在三个基准高光谱数据集上，高层级HMD层（HMD-1、HMD-2）的分类精度优于Tucker、CP、Tensor Train等可学习张量分解基线；泛化能力和训练稳定性与标准2D/3D-CNN相当，但特征提取器参数显著更少。

Conclusion: HMD框架为高光谱图像分类中传统卷积提供了结构稳健的替代方案，兼具高参数效率和训练稳定性，能够显式建模多模态交互。

Abstract: Feature extraction for hyperspectral image classification is conventionally addressed using rigid tensor decompositions that fail to capture complex spatio-spectral interdependencies, or heavily parameterized convolutional neural networks that are computationally expensive. To overcome these limitations, this work introduces the Holistic Multivariance Decomposition (HMD) framework as a novel, end-to-end differentiable neural network layer. By explicitly separating independent single mode variations from cooperative higher dimensional interactions via learnable, matrix valued supports, the proposed HMD-0, HMD-1 and HMD-2 approximants are optimized jointly with a downstream classifier via backpropagation. Comprehensive evaluations across three benchmark HS datasets demonstrate that the higher level HMD layers achieve superior classification accuracy compared to classical learnable tensor baselines, including Tucker, Canonical Polyadic, and Tensor Train decompositions. Furthermore, HMD-1 and HMD-2 achieve a generalization capacity and training stability comparable to standard 2D and 3D-CNNs while requiring significantly fewer feature extractor parameters. These results demonstrate that the HMD framework provides a structurally robust substitute for traditional convolution in multidimensional HS image classification, offering high parameter efficiency and stability throughout the optimization process.

</details>


### [155] [SCOUT: Semantic Concept Discovery for Open-Vocabulary Editing of face Recognition Templates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16251)
*Leon Todorov,Peter Rot,Peter Peer,Vitomir Štruc,Klemen Grm*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SCOUT是一个端到端框架，利用机械可解释性在面部识别模板中发现并直接操纵语义概念，无需耗时的编辑-重编码流水线，支持开放词表语义编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面部识别模板包含丰富的语义信息，但以往方法要么通过图像编辑间接操纵模板，要么依赖手动神经元检查或预定义属性标签进行可解释性分析，缺乏可扩展性和语义灵活性。现有方法无法在模板空间直接进行开放词表语义编辑。

Method: SCOUT学习稀疏模板表示，从自然语言描述中为潜在特征生成语义假设，并验证其稳定性。发现的特征作为可控的语义方向用于模板直接编辑，避免编辑-重编码循环。

Result: 在CNN、ViT和Swin骨干网络的面部识别模型上，SCOUT发现了超越标准属性标签的可解释概念，实现了可控的、保持身份的模板操纵，且对身份匹配影响可忽略。编辑后的模板可用独立反演模型解码用于可视化和评估。

Conclusion: SCOUT证明面部识别模板可以通过机械可解释性实现直接、开放词表的语义编辑，为模板空间的细粒度语义控制提供了新途径。

Abstract: Face recognition templates are compact identity representations, yet they also encode rich semantic information about facial appearance. Prior work has shown that templates can be inverted to images or indirectly manipulated through image-editing pipelines, but direct semantic editing in template space remains largely unexplored. Existing interpretability methods for face recognition often rely on manual neuron inspection or predefined attribute labels, limiting scalability and semantic flexibility. To address this gap, we propose SCOUT (Semantic Concept Discovery for Open-VocabUlary Editing of Face Recognition Templates), an end-to-end framework for discovering and directly manipulating semantic concepts in face recognition templates using mechanistic interpretability. SCOUT learns sparse template representations, generates semantic hypotheses for latent features from natural-language descriptions, and validates their stability. The resulting features act as controllable semantic directions for direct editing, avoiding costly edit--re-encode pipelines. Experiments with face recognition models using CNN, ViT, and Swin backbones show that SCOUT discovers interpretable concepts beyond standard attribute labels and enables controllable, identity-aware template manipulation with negligible impact on identity matching. We further show that edited templates can subsequently be decoded with independent inversion models for visualization and evaluation.

</details>


### [156] [Defake-o3: From Speculative Rationales to Verifiable Evidence for Explainable AIGI Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16259)
*Bowen Deng,Jiahui Zhan,Yikun Ji,Haozhen Yan,Jianfu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Defake-o3是一个可解释的AI生成图像检测器，通过交互式视觉搜索和证据验证器引导的对齐，从推测性解释转向可验证证据，并构建了GroundFake数据集和FakeFrontier基准来评估检测准确性和解释质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM-based AI生成图像检测器虽然能提供自然语言解释，但往往产生推测性理由，依赖模糊或幻觉的人工痕迹，无法捕捉最新生成器的细微局部缺陷，也缺乏可视觉验证的证据。

Method: Defake-o3结合交互式视觉搜索与验证器引导的证据对齐：模型迭代放大可疑区域检查细粒度细节，同时使用基于人类验证注释训练的Evidence Verifier提供强化学习奖励，偏好有根据的证据并惩罚无根据的声明。构建了GroundFake数据集（含边界框证据、人类验证、纠错推理轨迹和有效/无效证据标注）以及FakeFrontier OOD基准。

Result: 在GroundFake、FakeFrontier及其他out-of-distribution基准上的实验表明，Defake-o3同时提升了检测准确率和解释质量，生成了更局部化、可验证且有说服力的证据。

Conclusion: Defake-o3验证了从推测性解释转向可验证证据的可行性，展示了结合视觉搜索、RL对齐和高质量数据标注的可解释检测范式。

Abstract: The rapid progress of image generation models calls for AI-generated image (AIGI) detectors that are not only accurate but also explainable and reliable. While MLLM-based detectors can provide natural language explanations, existing methods often generate speculative rationales: they rely on vague or hallucinated artifacts, miss subtle localized flaws from the latest generators, and fail to provide evidence that can be visually verified. We present Defake-o3, an explainable AIGI detector that moves from speculative rationales to verifiable evidence. It combines interactive visual search with verifier-guided evidence alignment: the model iteratively zooms into suspicious regions to inspect fine-grained details, while an Evidence Verifier, trained from human verification annotations, provides reinforcement learning rewards that favor grounded evidence and penalize baseless claims. To support this objective, we construct GroundFake, a dataset designed for grounded explainable detection, with localized bounding-box evidence, human verification based on visual grounding and artifact specificity, corrected reasoning trajectories, and valid/invalid evidence supervision. We further introduce FakeFrontier, an out-of-distribution benchmark built from real images and outputs of 10 recent generators, together with an MLLM-based protocol for evaluating evidence quality and persuasiveness. Experiments on GroundFake, FakeFrontier, and additional out-of-distribution benchmarks show that Defake-o3 improves both detection accuracy and explanation quality, producing more localized, verifiable, and persuasive evidence.

</details>


### [157] [Seeing Before Answering: Training-Free Visual Layer Profiling for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16263)
*Ruchen Liu,Yi Yang,Yiming Xu,Michael Ying Yang,Monika Sester,Bodo Rosenhahn*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现LLaVA式视觉-语言模型(VLM)固定使用视觉骨干网络的倒数第二层特征这一惯例是脆弱的：在2个VLM、7个图像和视频基准上，默认层在14个模型-任务组合中有13个是次优的。作者提出用表示几何（基于矩阵熵的Visual Dataset Entropy (VDE)和Gromov-Wasserstein距离）来预测哪些视觉层最有用于语言模型，无需穷举推理即可缩小搜索范围。实验发现VDE（投影前计算）能有效追踪逐层准确率并在所有任务上覆盖oracle最优层，而GW距离不适合作为层选择器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLaVA风格的VLM默认从视觉骨干网络倒数第二层提取视觉token传给语言模型，但作者证明这一默认选择在绝大多数情况下是次优的，且最佳层随任务和视觉骨干变化。穷举逐层推理代价过高，又没有更好的固定默认层，因此需要一种无需训练的方法来预测哪些层"有用"。

Method: 作者研究了两种基于表示几何的层选择信号：(1) Visual Dataset Entropy (VDE)，基于矩阵熵在样本级视觉嵌入上计算；(2) Gromov-Wasserstein距离，原本用于VLM模型选择，被重新用作逐层视觉-语言对齐信号。由于视觉塔冻结而多模态投影器被训练，作者分别对投影器两侧进行评估（pre-projector和post-projector），用100个无标注任务样本即可计算。

Result: VDE可以迁移，GW不能。在SigLIP-based LLaVA-Video上，pre-projector VDE的排名靠前层在所有任务上覆盖了oracle最佳层；在CLIP-based Video-LLaVA上提供区域级指导。post-projector分析显示投影器重塑了视觉几何但没有抹去与性能相关的趋势，pre-projector VDE是更强的信号。GW在投影后趋于平坦，更适合作为对齐诊断而非选择器。

Conclusion: VDE提供了一种可解释、免训练的视觉层选择策略，能将搜索范围缩小到少量候选层进行有限的下游验证，无需为每一层做完整推理。

Abstract: LLaVA-style Vision-Language Models (VLMs) pass visual tokens from a fixed late layer of the vision backbone, typically the penultimate one, to the language model. We first show that this hidden convention is fragile: across 2 VLMs and 7 image and video benchmarks, the default layer is sub-optimal in 13 of 14 model-task pairs, and the best layer shifts with both task and visual backbone. Finding that layer by exhaustive layer-wise inference is prohibitively expensive, and no better fixed default exists. We therefore ask whether layer usefulness can instead be predicted from representation geometry. We study matrix-based entropy, introduced for unimodal layer analysis, which we compute over sample-level visual embeddings as Visual Dataset Entropy (VDE); and Gromov-Wasserstein (GW) distance, introduced for encoder-level VLM model selection, which we repurpose as a layer-wise visual--language alignment signal. Transferring these to LLaVA-based models is not obvious a priori: the vision tower is frozen while the multimodal projector is trained, so we profile both sides of the projector. We find that VDE transfers, and GW does not. Computed from 100 unlabeled task samples without downstream inference, pre-projector VDE tracks layer-wise accuracy and its top-ranked layers cover the oracle best layer on every task for the SigLIP-based LLaVA-Video, while giving region-level guidance for the CLIP-based Video-LLaVA. Post-projector profiles show that the projector reshapes visual geometry but does not erase the performance-relevant trend, leaving $\mathrm{VDE}_{\mathrm{pre}}$ the stronger signal. GW instead flattens after projection and is best read as an alignment diagnostic rather than a selector. VDE thus offers an interpretable, training-free policy that narrows the visual-layer search to a handful of candidates for limited downstream verification.

</details>


### [158] [CoM$^3$eT: A foundation model for medical image analysis through federated, multidimensional context integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16268)
*J. Raphael Schäfer,Kai Geissler,Till Nicke,Chiara Tappermann,Karoline Heber,Eike Petersen,Habib Mergan,Lars Ole Schwen,Nick Weiss,Annika Gerken,Jan Hendrik Moltz,Tom Bisson,Isil Dogan O,Tim-Rasmus Kiehl,Norman Zerbe,Sefer Elezkurtaj,Robin S. Mayer,Nadine Flinner,Peter Wild,Isabel Dahm,Felix Peisen,Heinrich von Busch,Robert Grimm,Sebastian Arndt,Lisa Siegler,Matthias Stefan May,Antje Prasse,Natalia Artysh,Fabian Kiessling,Johannes Lotz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CoM³eT是一个统一病理学与放射学的医疗视觉基础模型，通过多维注意力机制同时处理稀疏/稠密预测、二维/三维输入，在开放竞赛中超越其他医疗基础模型，且仅微调2.5%参数即可达到全量微调效果，并支持联邦学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学基础模型局限于单一专科（病理或放射）以及单一输出类型（分类或分割），缺乏能横跨多模态、多维度、多任务的统一医学视觉基础模型。

Method: 提出Co-representation Multidimensional Multitask Medical Transformer（CoM³eT），通过注意力机制建模多维上下文，统一病理与放射影像、稀疏与稠密预测、二维与高维输入，支持分类、分割和报告生成等多种任务。

Result: 在开放竞赛中在5个断层扫描、4个全切片、3个二维数据集上超越其他医学基础模型；微调少于2.5%参数即可达到全量微调性能；在联邦学习场景下通过互联网和消费级硬件达到与集中式数据训练相当的性能。

Conclusion: CoM³eT证明了一个统一的多维、多任务、多专科医学视觉基础模型的可行性，同时其高效参数微调和联邦学习能力使得算力有限的医疗机构也能受益。

Abstract: Medical foundation models improve generalization when training AI models with limited labeled data, but remain confined to a single specialty, such as pathology or radiology, and to either sparse or dense outputs, such as classification or segmentation. Here, we present CoM$^3$eT (Co-representation Multidimensional Multitask Medical Transformer), a medical vision foundation model that unifies pathology and radiology, sparse and dense predictions, and two- and higher-dimensional inputs by modeling multidimensional context with attention. CoM$^3$eT outperformed other medical foundation models in an open competition spanning five tomographic, four whole-specimen, and three two-dimensional datasets, covering sparse and dense prediction tasks as well as report generation. When adapted across diverse clinical applications, training fewer than 2.5% of parameters achieved performance comparable to full fine-tuning, enabling research without access to high-performance GPU clusters. Applied to federated learning across hospitals, this approach achieved performance comparable to pooled-data training over internet connections and with consumer-grade hardware.

</details>


### [159] [TransAnyText: Translating Arbitrary Text in E-commerce Images via Structured Visual Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16284)
*Xiaoan Liu,Lichen Ma,Zipeng Guo,Yu He,Xiaoyan Su,Shaojie Guo,Hao Yang,Jingling Fu,Xiaolong Fu,Zhen Chen,Yu Guo,Fei Wang,Xinyi Liu,Yongjun Zhang,Ke Zhang,Junshi Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TransAnyText提出一种结构化视觉代码框架，将跨境电商图像文字翻译重新定义为从源图像和目标语言生成可渲染的HTML patch。框架分离语义生成与像素渲染：VLM负责视觉理解、跨语言翻译和结构化视觉生成，扩散模型负责背景修复和像素级精修，最后通过确定性渲染合成最终图像。配套三阶段后训练框架（SFT+PWSD+RLVR）以及多语言数据集和基准TransAnyDataset/TransAnyBench。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨境电商图像翻译需要在产品图、横幅和详情页中生成不同语言的文本，现有方法难以同时实现准确翻译、忠实保持视觉身份和易于编辑的输出。

Method: 1) 将图像文本翻译建模为生成可渲染HTML patch，解耦语义生成与像素渲染；2) VLM负责理解与翻译及结构化生成，扩散模型负责背景修复和像素精修，确定性渲染合成最终图像；3) 三阶段后训练：SFT建立图像到代码映射，privilege-gap加权自蒸馏(PWSD)改进风格与布局token学习，可验证奖励强化学习(RLVR)优化任务级性能；4) 构建TransAnyDataset和TransAnyBench多语言数据集与基准。

Result: 在电商图像翻译任务上与级联pipeline、开源端到端模型和闭源图像编辑系统相比均表现出竞争力的性能，提供有效、可控、可编辑的跨境电商图像翻译方案。

Conclusion: 提出结构化代码式图像翻译框架，通过语义生成与像素渲染解耦实现高质量、可控、可编辑的电商图像文字翻译。

Abstract: Cross-border e-commerce image translation is essential for global retail, where product images, banners, and detail pages need to be produced in different languages. Existing methods struggle to achieve accurate translation, faithful visual identity preservation, and easy-to-edit outputs, simultaneously.
