To address these challenges, we introduce TransAnyText, a structured visual code framework that reformulates image text translation as generating renderable HTML patches from source images and target languages. Our framework decouples semantic generation from pixel rendering: a vision-language model (VLM) handles visual understanding, cross-lingual translation, and structured visual generation, while a diffusion model performs background inpainting and pixel-level refinement, followed by deterministic rendering to synthesize the final image. Based on this formulation, we develop a three-stage post-training framework, where supervised fine-tuning (SFT) establishes the image-to-code mapping, privilege-gap weighted self-distillation (PWSD) improves the learning of style and layout tokens, and reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) further optimizes task-level performance. We further introduce TransAnyDataset and TransAnyBench, a multilingual dataset and benchmark for e-commerce image translation. Extensive experiments demonstrate competitive performance against cascaded pipelines, open-source end-to-end models, and closed-source image editing systems, providing an effective, controllable, and editable solution for cross-border e-commerce image translation.

</details>


### [160] [Audio-Visual Segmentation via Depth-Guided Collaborative Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16285)
*Zhaojin Fu,Yuyang Hong,Qi Yang,Zili Wang,Kun Ding,Shiming Xiang,Bin Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DGCM-AVS，一个引入深度估计作为空间结构线索的三模态（音频-视觉-深度）音频-视觉分割框架。通过深度感知动态调制器改善相邻对象分离、深度引导渐进融合进行跨模态对齐，在AVSS数据集上相对提升M_J 10.2%、M_F 8.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AVS方法未显式建模几何线索（如相对距离和遮挡），限制跨模态对齐的鲁棒性。受人类感知中空间结构与视听证据自然整合的启发，作者将估计深度作为空间结构线索引入AVS。

Method: 提出DGCM-AVS三模态框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) Depth-Aware Dynamic Modulator，利用深度信息改善相邻对象分离同时保持对象内特征一致性；(2) Depth-Guided Progressive Fusion，以深度为中间桥梁逐步对齐音频线索与视觉特征。

Result: 在AVSS数据集上，相较最先进方法，M_J相对提升10.2%，M_F相对提升8.7%。

Conclusion: 深度是AVS中一个有前景但尚未充分探索的模态，该研究可能推动AVS领域的进一步研究。

Abstract: Audio-Visual Segmentation (AVS) is a fundamental task in multimodal perception that performs pixel-level segmentation of sounding objects in videos by leveraging both visual and audio cues. It has broad applications in video understanding, human-computer interaction, and autonomous driving. However, most existing AVS methods do not explicitly model geometric cues such as relative distance and occlusion, thereby limiting the robustness of cross-modal alignment. In human perception, spatial structure is naturally integrated with audio-visual evidence to accurately localize sounding objects. Motivated by this, we incorporate estimated depth as a spatial structural cue for AVS and propose DGCM-AVS, a tri-modal framework that jointly models audio, visual, and depth information. Specifically, we design a Depth-Aware Dynamic Modulator to improve the separation of adjacent objects while preserving intra-object feature consistency. Furthermore, we propose Depth-Guided Progressive Fusion, which uses depth as an intermediate bridge to progressively align audio cues with visual features. Compared to state-of-the-art methods, DGCM-AVS achieves relative improvements of 10.2 percent in M_J and 8.7 percent in M_F on the AVSS dataset. We believe our study highlights depth as a promising yet underexplored modality for AVS and may encourage further research in this direction.

</details>


### [161] [PosterText: Towards Unified Visual Text Generation and Editing for E-commerce Poster](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16289)
*Xiaoan Liu,Lichen Ma,Zipeng Guo,Yu He,Xiaoyan Su,Shaojie Guo,Jingling Fu,Xiaolong Fu,Hao Yang,Tongxuan Liu,Yu Guo,Fei Wang,Xinyi Liu,Yongjun Zhang,Junshi Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Text Patch Generation and Editing (TPGE)，一种统一的电商海报生成与编辑任务形式，将文本块作为原子单元，涵盖海报生成、补丁添加、删除和修改四种操作，并支持参考引导的样式控制。作者构建了PosterText统一模型，采用四阶段课程训练（文本渲染预训练、指令跟随训练、强化学习偏好对齐、空间引导自蒸馏），并构建了大规模带补丁级标注的数据集和基准。实验表明PosterText在生成和编辑任务上与现有方法竞争性相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电商海报方法要么只做端到端生成，要么走多阶段设计流水线，缺乏对已有海报进行灵活且精确编辑的能力。作者希望统一生成与编辑任务，以支持更实用的电商海报设计场景。

Method: 1) 提出TPGE统一任务形式，以文本补丁为原子单元覆盖四种操作；2) 构建PosterText统一模型，采用四阶段课程训练：文本渲染预训练、指令跟随训练、RLHF偏好对齐、空间引导自蒸馏；3) 构建大规模带补丁级标注的数据集和评估基准。

Result: 大量实验表明PosterText在生成与编辑任务上与现有方法相比取得了有竞争力的性能，验证了框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 统一任务形式加四阶段课程训练能有效支持电商海报的生成与灵活编辑，提供了一种可扩展的框架。

Abstract: Automated e-commerce poster design requires both high-quality poster generation and flexible editing of existing designs. However, most existing methods either target end-to-end poster generation or follow multi-stage design pipelines, with limited capability for flexible and precise editing of existing posters. To enable unified generation and editing of e-commerce posters, we introduce Text Patch Generation and Editing, a unified task formulation that treats text patches as atomic units and covers four operations: poster generation, patch addition, patch deletion, and patch modification, with optional reference-guided style control. Based on this, we propose PosterText, a unified model trained with a four-stage curriculum, including text rendering pretraining, instruction-following training, reinforcement learning for preference alignment, and spatial guidance self-distillation for execution refinement. We further construct a large-scale dataset with patch-level annotations and a comprehensive benchmark for evaluation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PosterText achieves competitive performance against existing generation and editing approaches, validating the effectiveness of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [162] [Cross-View Urban Sensing: Mapping Subjective Streetscape Perception via AlphaEarth Embeddings and Urban Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16310)
*Peilin Li,Pengfei Chen,Jingyu Wang,Zhifeng Yang,Tiansheng Chen,Mengjie Gong,Xiao Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CVLNet 是一个跨视角学习网络，利用 AlphaEarth 遥感嵌入和城市上下文数据预测街道级景观感知，无需推理时使用街景图像（SVI）。在四个东南亚城市评估，达到中位路段级 Adjusted R² 0.76，并将感知估计从 SVI 覆盖的13-31%道路扩展到全道路网络，结合人口数据量化环境不平等。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 街景图像（SVI）虽然广泛用于城市景观感知评估，但其覆盖不均、更新不规律，限制了大尺度测量。作者希望利用遥感数据作为SVI的可扩展替代方案，实现城市级街道景观感知的全面映射。

Method: CVLNet将AlphaEarth嵌入与多源城市上下文数据结合，采用per-task自适应门控机制联合建模五个感知维度，使用预训练SVI-Percept模型的标签作为ground truth，在推理时无需SVI输入。

Result: 在四个东南亚城市（新加坡、吉隆坡、雅加达、马尼拉）评估，中位路段级Adjusted R²为0.76，五个感知维度上比基线提升5.9-11.3%。消融实验证实AlphaEarth特征和城市上下文特征互补。生成四个城市全路网感知地图，并用Deficit Palma Ratio量化不同人口密度、人口统计和土地利用群体间的暴露不平等。

Conclusion: 遥感可作为SVI的可扩展替代方案用于城市级街道景观感知映射，支持更全面的城市环境不平等评估。

Abstract: Residents' perception of the urban streetscape is an important factor in public health, active mobility, and social wellbeing. Street view imagery (SVI) has emerged as a widely used data source for assessing these perceptual qualities, yet its uneven coverage and irregular updating limit large-scale measurement. Here, we present CVLNet, a Cross-View Learning Network that predicts street-level perception from AlphaEarth embeddings and multi-source urban contextual data without requiring SVI at inference. CVLNet applies per-task adaptive gating to jointly model five perceptual dimensions, using labels from the pretrained SVI-Percept model as ground truth. The proposed method is evaluated across four Southeast Asian cities: Singapore, Kuala Lumpur, Jakarta, and Manila. CVLNet achieves a median road-segment-level Adjusted $R^{2}$ of 0.76 and consistently outperforms the baseline models, with gains ranging from 5.9--11.3% across the five perceptual dimensions. Ablation experiments show that AlphaEarth features and urban contextual features contribute complementary information. We further produce citywide road-level streetscape perception maps for five subjective perceptual dimensions across all four cities, extending perception estimation from the 13--31% of the road network directly covered by available SVI to the complete road network of each city. Integrating these maps with WorldPop gridded population data, we quantify exposure inequality across population-density, demographic, and land-use groups using the Deficit Palma Ratio. These results demonstrate that remote sensing can serve as a scalable alternative to SVI for citywide streetscape perception mapping, enabling a more comprehensive assessment of urban environmental inequality.

</details>


### [163] [Deep Thought Alignment: Trajectory-Level Latent Distillation for Video Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16316)
*Ao Shen,Yongheng Zhang,Yinghui Li,Manning Wang,Di Yin,Xing Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Latent-OPD，在知识蒸馏的 On-Policy Distillation (OPD) 基础上增加轨迹级潜在表示蒸馏，通过在每条轨迹末端的位置对齐隐藏状态（该位置隐状态总结了跨帧视觉证据和推理上下文），并引入渐进式教师前瞻策略将学生中后层对齐到教师更深层，从而提升大模型到小模型视频推理蒸馏的效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频推理的大多模态模型受限于高计算成本，希望将大模型推理能力蒸馏到小模型。现有 OPD 只匹配输出 token 分布，但视频推理依赖跨帧证据积累，输出级监督仅捕捉 token 预测表达的信息，无法直接约束推理过程中形成的潜在表示。

Method: 1) 在 OPD 基础上增加轨迹级潜在蒸馏（Latent distillation），聚焦每个轨迹末端位置，该处隐状态有效总结累积视觉证据和推理上下文。2) 引入渐进式教师前瞻策略（progressive teacher-lookahead），将学生中后层与逐步加深的教师层对齐。

Result: 在六个视频推理基准上，Latent-OPD 一致优于仅输出级 OPD。尤其在有限帧数、长视频或需要复杂证据聚合的任务中提升最为显著。

Conclusion: Latent-OPD 被确立为一种高效的帧高效视频推理方法，通过轨迹级潜在蒸馏弥补输出级监督的不足，提升小模型视频推理能力。

Abstract: Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) for video reasoning have long been hindered by the high computational cost of processing vast amounts of visual information. This dilemma motivates the transfer of the reasoning capabilities of large models to smaller, more efficient ones. On-Policy Distillation (OPD) offers a promising solution by matching output-token distributions along student-generated trajectories. However, video reasoning often depends on evidence accumulated across multiple frames. In this context, output-level supervision only captures information expressed through token predictions and does not directly constrain the latent representations formed during reasoning. To address this limitation, we propose Latent-OPD, which augments OPD with trajectory-level latent distillation. Specifically, our method focuses on the position at the end of each trajectory, where hidden states effectively summarize the accumulated visual evidence and reasoning context. Furthermore, we introduce a progressive teacher-lookahead strategy, which aligns middle-to-late student layers with increasingly deeper teacher layers. Experiments on six video reasoning benchmarks show that Latent-OPD consistently outperforms output-only OPD. Notably, the improvements are particularly pronounced in scenarios with limited frames, long videos, or tasks requiring complex evidence aggregation. These results establish Latent-OPD as a highly effective approach to frame-efficient video reasoning.

</details>


### [164] [StreamOPD: A Post-Training Recipe with Spatio-Temporal Cue Gating for Streaming Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16320)
*Keming Wu,Baoyi Wang,Kaichen Zhang,Xiang An,Zuhao Yang,Sudong Wang,Haowei Zhu,Tingxuan Huang,Hongcheng Gao,Bin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出StreamOPD，一种结合可验证流式视频数据、思考模式在线策略蒸馏（OPD）和指令模式部署的训练后配方便签，用于流式视频理解。它证明了无记忆近窗口基线已匹配现有系统，而纯训练后方法可将StreamingBench从77.9%提升至83.9%，并优于多种先进基线。此外，教师特权扩展ST-CueGate通过聚集cue与无cue的教师似然比进行重加权，进一步提升了OVO-Bench和Video-MME的性能，同时自蒸馏变体保留了大部分增益并提升了HLD子任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式视频理解需要从不断展开的视频的因果前缀直接产生响应。现有系统依赖推理时的记忆、检索和压缩，但作者发现训练免费的滑动窗口基线已能达到相同效果，因此希望探究纯训练后（post-training）方法能做到什么程度，并解决强化学习在该场景下不适配（鼓励长思考生成）以及在线策略蒸馏稳定性问题。

Method: 提出StreamOPD配方：(1) 固定无记忆近窗口协议；(2) 使用可验证奖励的流式视频数据；(3) 在思考模式下进行在线策略蒸馏（OPD）提供密集token级教师监督；(4) 部署时切换为指令模式。作为教师特权扩展，提出Spatio-Temporal CueGate (ST-CueGate)，利用cue与无cue条件下的教师似然比构造组相对响应分数来重加权OPD损失。还探索了用学生初始策略的冻结副本作为教师进行在线策略自蒸馏。

Result: StreamOPD将StreamingBench从77.9%提升至83.9%，与9B教师仅差0.3个点；在OVO-Bench（排除HLD）上提升9.1个点且推理不变。ST-CueGate在OVO-Bench（排除HLD）达71.9%，Video-MME达64.9%，是唯一在所有四个基准上均保持高于基线的变体。自蒸馏变体保留大部分增益，并将HLD提升至57.0%，超过未训练学生和9B教师。

Conclusion: 训练后方法（尤其是思考模式OPD）足以大幅提升流式视频理解性能，无需解码时记忆/检索机制。自蒸馏结果显示弃权（abstention）损失并非配方的固有特性，为开源流式视频研究提供了透明可复现的参考。

Abstract: Streaming video understanding demands direct responses from the causally observed prefix of an unfolding video. Existing systems add inference-time memory, retrieval, and compression, yet a training-free sliding-window baseline already matches them. We therefore fix a memory-free recent-window protocol and ask how far post-training alone can go. Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards fits this regime poorly, encouraging long ``think-then-answer'' generations, while on-policy distillation (OPD) supplies dense token-level teacher supervision on student trajectories but is stable only when both models train in thinking mode. These observations lead to \textsc{StreamOPD}, a recipe combining verifiable streaming-video data, thinking-mode OPD, and instruct-mode deployment. It raises StreamingBench from $77.9\%$ to $83.9\%$---within $0.3$ points of the 9B teacher---and improves OVO-Bench excluding its hallucination-detection subtask (HLD) by $9.1$ points under unchanged inference. As a teacher-privilege extension, \emph{Spatio-Temporal CueGate (ST-CueGate)} aggregates cue-versus-no-cue teacher likelihood ratios into a group-relative response score that reweights OPD. It reaches $71.9\%$ on OVO-Bench (excluding HLD) and $64.9\%$ on Video-MME, and is the only variant that stays above the base model on all four benchmarks. Replacing the teacher with a frozen copy of the student's initial policy---on-policy self-distillation---retains most of these gains and lifts HLD to $57.0\%$, above both the untrained student and the 9B teacher, so abstention loss is not intrinsic to the recipe. We provide a transparent and reproducible reference for open-source streaming-video research.

</details>


### [165] [LaGSplat: Inferring Physics-Governed Interactive Simulation from Monocular Video Using Latent Lagrangian Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16324)
*Louen Pottier*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LaGSplat提出了一种从单目视频（一条或几条）推断可由物理规律驱动的交互式动力学场景的方法。在推理时，用户可以对拍摄的刚体或可变形物体施加外部力（此力在训练中从未见过），系统能实时渲染物体的响应。其核心是让一个低维潜状态q同时充当学习到的耗散拉格朗日量的广义坐标和高斯溅射解码器的条件变量，从而将图像空间中施加的力通过雅可比J(q)^T映射回潜空间的广义力，进入运动方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有从视频学习动态场景的方法（CNN或NeRF类）无法将用户施加的外部力反传为潜空间的广义力，因此无法支持交互式物理仿真。作者希望从单目视频中重建出可被用户随意推动、具有物理一致性响应的3D动态场景，用于交互式重建和仿真应用。

Method: 将低维潜状态q同时用作两类角色：(1) 学习到的耗散欧拉-拉格朗日方程的广义坐标；(2) 高斯溅射解码器的条件输入。该解码器的原语是随物体运动的显式点μ_i(q)，其归纳偏置使得图像空间施加的力f可以按J(q)^T f拉回到潜空间广义力并进入运动方程。采用耗散Euler-Lagrange方程在少数广义坐标上建模，以换取对未知外力的有界、合理解释性响应。

Result: 在从刚性到可变形、从自主运动到受迫真实系统的递进难度测试集上进行了验证，结合了单目视频与传感器测量。展示了交互式应用：可对重建物体施加任意大小和方向的力，实时渲染2D或3D响应。

Conclusion: 通过拉格朗日力学与高斯溅射解码器的耦合，在未见外力下获得有界且合理的物理响应，而无条件约束的预测器会发散。该方法在交互式物理仿真场景中具有潜力，但目前以少量广义坐标为代价，牺牲了通用性。

Abstract: We present LaGSplat (Latent Lagrangian Gaussian Splatting), a framework that infers interactive, physics-governed dynamics from one or a few monocular videos. At inference it lets a user push on the filmed object, rigid or deformable, with an external force that was never measured, annotated, or seen during training. This is possible because a low-dimensional latent state $\mathbf{q} \in \mathbb{R}^d$ plays two roles at once: it is the generalised coordinate of a learned dissipative Lagrangian and the conditioning variable of a Gaussian Splatting decoder. The inductive bias of this decoder, whose primitives are explicit points $μ_i(\mathbf{q})$ that move with the object, is what lets a force $f$ applied in the image pull back into a latent generalised force $J(\mathbf{q})^\top f$ and enter the equations of motion, which pixel-space (CNN) or neural-field (NeRF) decoders cannot do. We validate LaGSplat on test cases of increasing difficulty, from rigid to deformable and from autonomous to forced real systems, combining monocular video and sensor measurements. We further demonstrate interactive use: forces of arbitrary magnitude and direction can be applied to the reconstructed object at any time, its response rendered in real time, in 2D or 3D. Assuming a dissipative Euler-Lagrange equation over a few generalised coordinates trades generality for a bounded, plausible response to unseen forces, where an unconstrained predictor diverges.

</details>


### [166] [GRNEdit: Efficient General Video Editing from a New Binary-Evidence Perspective in Generative Refinement Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16328)
*Feng Xie,Jiagao Hu,Fuhao Li,Zepeng Wang,Yuxuan Chen,Dahua Gao,Fei Wang,Daiguo Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GRNEdit是一个轻量级两阶段视频编辑框架，通过将编辑语义编码为局部保留/翻转决策，仅用不到3%的条件参数和0.6M训练对，实现指令式通用视频编辑，2B/8B模型在OpenVE-Bench上分别取得4.03和4.18分，超越多个14B开源模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有指令式视频编辑方法依赖资源密集型的条件注入方式（重型分支或昂贵的源视频拼接），作者希望探索更高效地建模编辑意图的方法，降低计算与参数开销。

Method: 提出GRNEdit两阶段框架：Stage I用紧凑编码器将离散源编码转换为连续证据信号，由GRN骨干进行二进制细化吸收；借鉴null-prompt训练的 classifier-free guidance思想，将空指令定义为"不编辑"并监督源重建，形成identity pathway。Stage II直接比较编辑状态与其源保持对应状态，利用差异修正未解决的目标位决策。

Result: 在仅0.6M训练对、不到3%条件参数下，GRNEdit-2B和GRNEdit-8B在OpenVE-Bench上分别取得4.03和4.18分。2B模型超越多个14B开源编辑器，8B模型与领先开源编辑器持平。

Conclusion: GRNEdit验证了通过位级保留/翻转决策高效建模编辑意图的可行性，在极低条件参数开销下达到甚至超越大规模模型的性能，为高效视频编辑提供了新范式。

Abstract: Instruction-based general video editing seeks to unify diverse editing operations within a single, intuitive interface. Existing approaches often rely on resource-intensive conditioning, using either heavyweight branches or costly source concatenation. Is there any efficient way to model editing intent? Thus, we introduce GRNEdit, a lightweight two-stage framework. GRN inspires our approach by encoding visual semantics through combinations of bits. Through task-specific fine-tuning, we take this representation further and recast editing semantics as local retain-or-flip decisions over individual bits. Source information is consequently modeled as coordinate-wise evidence supporting the observed binary states, while the GRN backbone remains responsible for resolving their global composition into coherent generative semantics. In Stage I, a compact encoder translates discrete source codes into continuous evidence signals, which GRN assimilates throughout binary refinement. Inspired by null-prompt training for classifier-free guidance, we further assign the null condition an editing-specific meaning: an empty instruction denotes no edit and is supervised through source reconstruction. This identity pathway not only implicitly strengthens evidence utilization and content preservation in Stage I, but also produces a source-preserving state in the same representation space as the edited state. Stage II can therefore directly compare each edited state with its source-preserving counterpart and use their discrepancy to revise unresolved target-bit decisions. Trained on only 0.6M pairs with less than 3\% conditioning parameters, GRNEdit-2B and GRNEdit-8B achieve scores of 4.03 and 4.18 on OpenVE-Bench. The 2B model outperforms multiple 14B open-source editors, while the 8B model performs on par with leading open-source editors.

</details>


### [167] [Unlocking Motion in Expressions: Temporal Calibration for Referring Video Object Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16332)
*Yiwen Jiang,Zhengtong Zhu,Ruixin Zhang,Jiaqing Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向视频指代目标分割（RVOS）的表达式驱动运动校准（EMC）框架。通过运动信号处理（MSP）模块从自然语言表达式中提取可解释的运动控制信号，利用运动影响校准（MIC）模块在时序决策中动态调整运动线索的贡献，并引入语义时序阶段构建（STSC）模块建立与表达式相关的紧凑时序候选空间。在六个标准基准上验证了方法的优越性，代码将开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RVOS方法通常在统一的跨模态时序建模框架中引入运动信息，语言线索仅用于目标定位和分割，但没有显式建模表达式对运动语义的依赖关系，难以根据不同的语义需求自适应调整运动信息的使用，导致运动信息利用不充分或不恰当。

Method: 提出EMC框架，包含三个模块：(1) MSP模块从文本表达式中提取可解释的运动控制信号；(2) MIC模块在时序决策过程中动态校准运动线索的贡献权重；(3) STSC模块构建表达式相关的语义时序阶段，为运动校准提供紧凑的时序候选空间。整体实现"表达式驱动、运动校准"的RVOS范式。

Result: 在Ref-YouTubeVOS、Ref-DAVIS17、MeViS（valid/valid^u）、A2D-Sentences和JHMDB-Sentences共六个标准基准上进行了广泛评估，验证了方法的优越性。代码将开源在GitHub。

Conclusion: EMC框架通过显式建模表达式中的运动语义并据此校准运动信息的贡献，有效解决了RVOS中对运动信息利用不够自适应的问题，在多个基准上取得了优异效果。

Abstract: Referring Video Object Segmentation (RVOS) aims to segment referred objects at the pixel level in video sequences based on natural language descriptions. Existing methods typically introduce motion information within a unified cross-modal temporal modeling framework, where language cues are used for target localization and segmentation. However, the dependency of expressions on motion semantics is not explicitly modeled, making it difficult to adaptively adjust the use of motion information according to different semantic requirements. To address these issues, we propose an Expression-driven Motion Calibration (EMC) framework for RVOS that explicitly unlocks and leverages the motion semantics within expressions. The proposed method extracts interpretable motion control signals from expressions via a Motion Signal Processing (MSP) module, and employs a Motion Influence Calibration (MIC) module to adjust the contribution of motion cues during temporal decision making. In addition, a Semantic Temporal Stage Construction (STSC) module is introduced to build expression-relevant temporal stages, providing a compact temporal candidate space for motion calibration. Through extensive evaluation on six standard benchmarks, including Ref-YouTubeVOS, Ref-DAVIS17, MeViS (valid/valid$^u$), A2D-Sentences, and JHMDB-Sentences, the superiority of our method is validated. We will release the code on https://github.com/Jeven7/EMC.

</details>


### [168] [SIGMA-Lane: Scale-pyramId Gated MAmba for Temporally Consistent Video Lane Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16338)
*Tiancheng Zhang,Mengmeng Wang,Yan Gao,Xiangjie Kong,Guojiang Shen,Jiaxin Du*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SIGMA-Lane通过状态空间模型（SSM）中的遮挡感知门控机制来解决视频车道检测中因车辆遮挡导致的时序状态污染问题，结合SSM-consistent双门控与结构空间检索（SSR）两条互补路径，在VIL-100和OpenLane-V上实现遮挡下的时序稳定性提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频车道检测需要跨帧稳定，但严重车辆遮挡会破坏时序线索。在流式递归模型中，被污染的观测会进入隐藏状态并在后续帧中持续产生错误。现有遮挡感知方法通常仅将障碍物掩码作为辅助输入，对状态更新路径的保护是间接的，因此需要一种直接防护状态污染的方法。

Method: SIGMA-Lane将遮挡导致的失败模式视为SSM时序建模中的状态污染问题。具体地，在SSM写入路径和残差融合路径上放置遮挡感知门控，控制当前观测如何进入时序记忆以及时序传播后如何融合回主路径。经坐标一致性仿射对齐后，模型结合两条互补路径：SSM-consistent双门控用于时序滤波，以及结构空间检索（SSR）从对齐的历史先验中恢复缺失的车道结构。

Result: 在VIL-100和OpenLane-V数据集上的实验表明，该方法在重遮挡条件下提升了时序稳定性，并取得有竞争力的F1和mIoU分数。

Conclusion: SIGMA-Lane通过直接对SSM状态更新路径施加遮挡感知门控，相比仅提供辅助遮挡掩码的现有方法，能更有效地缓解状态污染问题，在视频车道检测的重遮挡场景下兼顾精度与时序稳定性。

Abstract: Video lane detection requires predictions that remain stable across frames, yet severe vehicle occlusions can break temporal cues. In streaming recurrent models, corrupted observations may enter the hidden state and produce errors that persist into later frames. Existing occlusion-aware refinements usually provide obstacle masks as auxiliary inputs, so the state-update path is only indirectly protected. We propose SIGMA-Lane, which treats this failure mode as state contamination in State Space Model (SSM)-based temporal modeling. SIGMA-Lane places occlusion-aware gates on the SSM write and residual-fusion paths, controlling how current observations enter temporal memory and are fused back after temporal propagation. After coordinate-consistent affine alignment, the model combines two complementary paths: SSM-consistent dual-gating for temporal filtering and Structural Spatial Retrieval (SSR) for recovering missing lane structure from aligned historical priors. Experiments on VIL-100 and OpenLane-V show improved temporal stability under heavy occlusion, with competitive F1 and mIoU scores.

</details>


### [169] [Depth-Dominant Skeleton Detection for Natural Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16367)
*Chengkun Rao,Yixuan Deng,Min Li,Yangjun Ou,Ye Li,Ziwei Luo,Zhaojing Wang,Junwei Tang,Bangchao Wang,Xiaoyun Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次提出以深度图像为主导模态、RGB图像为辅助模态的自然场景骨架检测新范式，并据此提出DDSkel模型。该模型采用非对称编码器设计，RGB分支参数仅占深度分支的12%，整体可训练参数仅为当前最优方法的36%，但在最具挑战性的SymPASCAL数据集上超越了所有现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自然场景骨架检测均以RGB图像为唯一输入，在复杂内容图像上性能显著下降。深度图像对颜色和纹理不敏感，能提供清晰的区域轮廓和区域间空间关系，可自然缓解复杂场景中骨架检测的困难。

Method: 提出深度主导的骨架检测范式DDSkel，采用非对称编码器设计，将RGB信息融合进深度特征中；RGB模态分支参数仅为深度分支的12%，整体结构简洁无复杂设计。

Result: 在SymPASCAL（最具挑战性、包含大量复杂图像的基准）上，DDSkel以仅36%的可训练参数超越所有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 深度主导、RGB辅助的新范式能有效提升复杂场景下的骨架检测性能，且参数效率更高，验证了深度图像在骨架检测任务中的主导价值。

Abstract: To date, all natural scene skeleton detection follows the paradigm of taking RGB images as the sole input; despite notable progress, methods under this paradigm suffer significant performance degradation on complex-content images. We observe that depth images are inherently insensitive to color and texture, and can provide clear regional contours and inter-region spatial relationships, which naturally alleviates the difficulty of skeleton detection in complex scenarios. Motivated by this observation, this paper proposes for the first time a novel skeleton detection paradigm where depth images serve as the dominant modality and RGB images act as the auxiliary, and accordingly presents a model DDSkel (short for Depth-Dominant Skeleton Detection) under this paradigm. DDSkel employs an asymmetric encoder design to fuse RGB information into depth features, with the RGB modality branch having only 12% the parameters of the depth modality branch. DDSkel has a simple structure without intricate designs. Nevertheless, with only 36% of the trainable parameters of the current best method, DDSkel outperforms all state-of-the-art approaches on SymPASCAL, the most challenging dataset with a large volume of complex images.

</details>


### [170] [Adaptive Post-Processing Drives Instance-Level Detection in Stroke Lesion Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16377)
*Qinghui Liu,Jon André Ottesen,Atle Bjørnerud,Kyrre Eeg Emblem*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对医学图像实例级病变检测的体素重叠与实例匹配不匹配问题的后处理方法。作者在ISLES'26提交中发现，后处理对缩小这一差距的贡献远大于架构设计。提出的Volume-Conditioned Adaptive Post-Processing (VCAP)方案根据每个病例的预测病变负担调整组件大小阈值，使Lesion-F1提升0.032（约6倍于任何架构改动）。在1,453例5折交叉验证中，后处理双架构集成达到Dice 0.651和Lesion-F1 0.614，而未经处理的单模型基线为0.644和0.573。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实例级病变检测在医学图像分割中日益重要，但大多数流程仍仅针对体素级重叠进行训练和后处理。这种不匹配对小型病变尤为严重：接近命中但略低于实例匹配阈值的预测与完全错过得分相同，导致检测指标被严重低估。作者希望通过后处理而非架构设计来弥合这一差距。

Method: 提出Volume-Conditioned Adaptive Post-Processing (VCAP)方案：根据每个病例的预测病变负担自适应调整组件大小阈值。同时设计了一种名为Viola2Plus的分辨率感知注意力架构，专门针对小病变分割。最终使用两架构集成加VCAP后处理。

Result: 在ISLES'26的1,453例训练集5折交叉验证中：VCAP使Lesion-F1提升0.032（无偏交叉折估计），约6倍于任何架构改动。Viola2Plus使小病变检测率提升3.7%，而小病变Dice不变。后处理双架构集成达到Dice 0.651和Lesion-F1 0.614。

Conclusion: 实例级病变检测的性能提升中，后处理的重要性远超架构设计。体素重叠指标会掩盖真实的检测效果改善，仅依赖Dice评估会低估针对小病变检测改进的价值。

Abstract: Instance-level lesion detection has been an increasingly larger focal point in medical image segmentation besides the more standard voxel-level overlap. Still, most pipelines are trained and post-processed for voxel overlap alone. In particular, the mismatch is most pronounced for small lesions, where a near-miss prediction---substantial overlap that falls just short of the instance-matching threshold---scores the same as a complete miss. In our ISLES'26 submission, we found that closing this gap mattered far more in post-processing than in architecture design. Our Volume-Conditioned Adaptive Post-Processing (VCAP) scheme adjusts component-size thresholds to each case's predicted lesion burden, improving Lesion-F1 by 0.032 (unbiased cross-fold estimate)---approximately 6 times larger than any architectural change we tested. A resolution-aware attention architecture (Viola2Plus), designed for small-lesion segmentation, shows why the distinction matters: it left small-lesion Dice unchanged but raised small-lesion detection rate by 3.7\%, a real effect voxel-overlap metrics alone would have missed. Under 5-fold cross-validation on the 1,453-case training set, our post-processed two-architecture ensemble achieves Dice 0.651 and Lesion-F1 0.614, versus 0.644 and 0.573 for the unprocessed single-model baseline.

</details>


### [171] [Synthetic Data Augmentation for Satellite-Based Analysis of Battle-Damaged Agricultural Fields in Ukraine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16380)
*Marta Sumyk,Oleksandr Kosovan,Iryna Voitsitska*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究利用合成数据增强（GAN和DDPM生成卫星图像）来解决乌克兰农业土地战争损害分类中标注数据稀缺和不平衡的问题，通过DDPM平衡增强将分类准确率从84%提升至88%，平衡准确率从67%提升至81%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监测乌克兰战争对农业用地的破坏对粮食安全、环境稳定和战后恢复至关重要，但卫星图像损伤分析面临标注数据稀缺和不平衡的瓶颈，尤其缺少被炸毁农田的标注样本。

Method: 训练class-conditional GAN和DDPM在真实卫星图像上生成额外的被炸毁和未炸毁农田样本，仅用于训练数据增强；使用Vision Transformer分类器在多种真实+合成数据配置下评估每种生成方法的实际效用。

Result: 基于平衡DDPM增强的最佳配置将准确率从84%提升到88%，平衡准确率从67%提升到81%，宏F1从65%提升到78%，少数类（未炸毁）召回率从41%提升到69%。

Conclusion: 合成卫星图像增强在数据稀缺的战争影响区域地理空间应用中展现出显著潜力，DDPM优于GAN。

Abstract: Monitoring war-induced damage to agricultural land in Ukraine is important for understanding threats to food security, environmental stability, and post-war recovery. However, the development of computer-vision systems for satellite-based damage analysis is limited by the scarcity of labeled imagery, especially for damaged agricultural fields. This work investigates synthetic data augmentation as a method for improving classification under limited and imbalanced training data. We train class-conditional Generative Adversarial Network (GAN) and Denoising Diffusion Probabilistic Model (DDPM) architectures on real satellite images and use them to generate additional bombed and not-bombed agricultural-field samples. The generated images are used only for training augmentation, while all downstream evaluation is performed on an exclusively real test set. A Vision Transformer classifier is trained under multiple real and synthetic data configurations to measure the practical utility of each generative approach. The best configuration, based on balanced DDPM augmentation, improves accuracy from 84\% to 88\%, balanced accuracy from 67\% to 81\%, macro F1 from 65\% to 78\%, and recall for the underrepresented not-bombed class from 41\% to 69\%. These results demonstrate the potential of synthetic satellite imagery for data-scarce geospatial applications in war-affected regions.

</details>


### [172] [Self-Routed Tensor Adapters for Parameter-Efficient Universal Visual Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16384)
*Suraj Yadav*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Self-Routed Tensor Adapters (SRTA)，一种紧凑的多域视觉适配框架。SRTA将每个输入投影到低秩空间，用可学习的域矩阵计算路由权重，并用这些权重混合共享Tucker核的切片，生成样本特定的适应矩阵，无需外部门控网络。引入渐进式深度加权路由目标来监督跨适配层的路由决策。在5个多域视觉分类基准上，SRTA以显著更少的可训练参数达到与MoE式PEFT相当或更强的精度（rank=64时4域设置仅2.77M vs MoLoRA的9.52M）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用视觉表示需要在异构域间高效适配，但不希望将知识碎片化到域专用模块中。标准低秩适配器对所有输入使用固定子空间，限制了域差异的表达；MoE式适配器虽有多专家路径但依赖外部路由器和大型专家库，增加了参数并分离了路由与适配过程。

Method: SRTA将输入投影到低秩空间，通过可学习域矩阵计算路由权重，用这些权重混合共享Tucker核切片来生成样本特定的适配矩阵。无需外部门控网络，使共享视觉因子可重用同时支持域感知专业化。引入渐进式深度加权路由目标以监督适配层间的路由决策。

Result: 在五个异构多域视觉分类基准上，SRTA达到与MoE式PEFT基线相当或略强的平均准确率，同时使用更少的可训练参数。rank=64时，4域设置用2.77M参数（MoLoRA为9.52M），6域设置用3.00M（MoLoRA为14.31M），展示了优异的精度-参数权衡。

Conclusion: SRTA提供了一种无需外部门控的有效多域视觉适配方案，以显著的参数效率实现与MoE方法相当的精度，为面向通用多域表示的视觉基础模型适配提供了有效选择。

Abstract: Universal visual representations require adaptation mechanisms that adapt across heterogeneous domains without fragmenting knowledge into domain-specific modules. Parameter-efficient fine-tuning adapts frozen visual foundation models efficiently, but standard low-rank adapters use a fixed subspace for all inputs, which can be restrictive when domains differ in style, background, and semantic context. MoE-based adapters improve specialization through multiple expert pathways, but often rely on external routers and large expert banks, adding parameters and separating routing from adaptation. We propose \textbf{Self-Routed Tensor Adapters}, a compact framework for multi-domain visual adaptation. SRTA projects each input into a low-rank space, computes routing weights from this representation using a learnable domain matrix, and uses these weights to blend slices of a shared Tucker core. This produces a sample-specific adaptation matrix without an external gating network, allowing shared visual factors to be reused while supporting domain-aware specialization. To strengthen pathway learning, we introduce a progressive depth-weighted routing objective that supervises routing decisions across adapter layers. Across five heterogeneous multi-domain visual classification benchmarks, SRTA achieves competitive or slightly stronger average accuracy than MoE-style PEFT baselines while using substantially fewer trainable parameters. At rank 64, SRTA uses 2.77M parameters in the 4-domain setting compared with 9.52M for MoLoRA, and 3.00M in the 6-domain setting compared with 14.31M. Overall, SRTA offers an effective accuracy-parameter trade-off for adapting visual foundation models toward universal multi-domain representations. \href{https://github.com/surajyadav-research/SRTA}{GitHub}

</details>


### [173] [Joint Flow Matching Enables Continuous Dose-Conditioned Cell Morphing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16424)
*Lea Bogensperger,Manuela Merlo,Martin Baumgartner,Michael Krauthammer,Bernard Ciraulo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种联合流匹配（joint flow matching）方法，通过双时间步长公式同时建模细胞潜变量和药物浓度，实现剂量条件下的单细胞形态变换，并支持从细胞形态估计浓度和泛化到训练中未见过的剂量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型预测化合物处理下的细胞扰动效应时，要么将扰动建模为分布到分布的映射而不显式处理浓度，要么将浓度视为离散类别标签从而无法实现连续剂量控制。作者希望突破这些局限，实现连续的剂量条件控制。

Method: 采用双时间步长（dual-timestep）的联合流匹配框架，同时建模细胞潜变量与药物浓度，利用流匹配的可逆性实现剂量条件下的单细胞形态变化。该联合公式在潜空间中诱导出单调的剂量-响应几何结构，并支持从形态反向估计浓度。

Result: 在两种化合物上取得了与代表性基线相当或更优的每浓度指标，同时具备离散类别方法结构上无法实现的能力（如连续剂量控制、浓度估计、对训练时未见剂量的泛化）。

Conclusion: 联合流匹配方法能够同时建模细胞状态与药物浓度，提供连续剂量控制能力及额外的浓度估计与剂量外推能力，是对传统离散浓度类别方法的有效补充。

Abstract: Generative modeling has shown increasing promise for predicting cellular perturbation effects under chemical compound treatments. Existing approaches either model perturbation as a distribution-to-distribution mapping without explicit concentration handling, or treat concentration as a discrete class label, precluding continuous dose control. We introduce a joint flow matching approach that simultaneously models cell latents and drug concentration via a dual-timestep formulation, enabling dose-conditioned single-cell morphing through the invertibility of flow matching. The joint formulation induces a monotonic dose-response geometry in latent space and additionally supports concentration estimation from cell morphology. As proof of concept, we further demonstrate generalization to an unseen dose held out during training. Empirically, our method achieves competitive or improved per-concentration metrics on two compounds compared with representative baselines, while enabling capabilities structurally unavailable to discrete-class methods.

</details>


### [174] [Contrastive Energy Fields for Inference-Time Procedure Planning in Instructional Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16457)
*Mohamed Afham,Christoph Reich,Oliver Hahn,Daniel Cremers,Stefan Roth*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CEFITO 提出一种程序规划方法，学习动作条件化的表示空间，将程序规划形式化为任务约束优化问题，在推理时显式省略不相关动作，实现高效规划并在两个基准上达到SOTA精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有程序规划方法（前馈神经网络或扩散推理）将所有动作视为同样可能，缺乏强制执行任务特定逻辑约束的能力，导致预测出不相关或不合理的动作序列。

Method: CEFITO 学习一个预测器来构建动作条件化的表示空间，在此基础上将程序规划表述为任务约束优化问题，通过省略不相关动作在推理阶段显式推理动作空间。

Result: 在两个已建立的程序规划基准上达到最先进（SOTA）精度。

Conclusion: 通过将程序规划重新表述为任务约束优化并在推理时省略不相关动作，可以有效提升程序规划的准确性。

Abstract: Procedure planning seeks to estimate a sequence of actions to transition from an observed initial state to a given goal state. Current procedure planning approaches directly predict action sequences from latent representations using feed-forward neural networks or diffusion-based inference. These paradigms treat every action as plausible, lacking the ability to enforce task-specific logical constraints that render certain actions irrelevant or not plausible. We propose CEFITO, a procedure planning approach that learns a predictor to express an action-conditioned representation space. Based on this representation space, we formulate procedure planning as a task-constrained optimization problem. Unlike prior methods, CEFITO explicitly reasons over the action space by omitting irrelevant actions during inference-time planning. This reformulation enables effective procedure planning and achieves state-of-the-art accuracy on two established procedure planning benchmarks.

</details>


### [175] [Shared-Structure 4D Spectral Gaussian Representation for Sparse-View Spectral CT Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16463)
*Jiancheng Fang,Shaoyu Wang,Wenjun Xia,Yang Chen,Qiegen Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出共享结构4D光谱高斯表示（4D-SG），通过共享高斯几何+GSC-Net光谱密度曲线网络，将空间结构与光谱衰减变化解耦，实现稀疏视角光谱CT重建，在50视图下六个数据集上取得最好平均性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏视角光谱CT需同时处理角度欠采样与光谱耦合，现有方法独立优化各通道几何，忽略了光谱通道间的共享空间结构，导致优化效率低、重建质量受限。

Method: 从全光谱结构投影学习共享高斯几何，用GSC-Net预测每个高斯的原始密度变换，将共享空间结构与光谱衰减变化因子化，建立从离散光谱测量到连续4D表示的映射，支持未观测光谱通道查询。

Result: 在六个数据集（合成、模拟投影、真实投影）上50视图条件下取得最佳平均性能。较最强高斯基线，PSNR从35.56提升至36.61 dB，SSIM从0.909提升至0.914，LPIPS从0.208降至0.194。

Conclusion: 共享结构分解与光谱密度曲线网络能有效解耦空间几何与光谱变化，提升稀疏视角光谱CT的重建质量，验证了连续4D光谱高斯表示的有效性。

Abstract: Sparse-view spectral computed tomography (CT) reconstructs energy-resolved attenuation volumes from limited projection views, requiring simultaneous handling of angular undersampling and spectral coupling. We propose a SharedStructure 4D Spectral Gaussian Representation (4D-SG) that learns shared Gaussian geometry from full spectrum structural projections and uses a Gaussian-wise Spectral Density Curve Network (GSC-Net) to predict Gaussian raw density transformations. This factorization separates shared spatial structure from spectral attenuation variation, avoids independent channel geometry optimization, and establishes a continuous 4D-SG representation from discrete spectral measurements for unobserved spectral channel queries. Experiments on six synthesized, simulated projection, and real projection datasets with 50 views demonstrate the best average performance. Compared with the strongest Gaussian baseline, 4D-SG improves PSNR from 35.56 dB to 36.61 dB, increases SSIM from 0.909 to 0.914, and reduces LPIPS from 0.208 to 0.194, demonstrating its effectiveness for sparse-view spectral CT reconstruction.

</details>


### [176] [Sterilizable Scene Graph Generation for Operating Rooms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16469)
*Nick Lemke,Ssharvien Kumar Sivakumar,Antoine P. Sanner,John Kalkhof,Henry John Krumb,Ghazal Ghazaei,Anirban Mukhopadhyay*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SG-NCA是首个基于神经细胞自动机（NCA）的场景图生成框架，用于手术视频理解。它结合NCA多类分割与轻量关系预测器，在保持与基线相当性能的同时将参数量减少55倍，并成功部署于无风扇边缘设备，适用于手术室环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最先进的手术场景图生成方法依赖大规模参数深度学习模型，因硬件占用、卫生约束、延迟和数据隐私问题难以在实际手术室中部署。需要一种轻量、隐私保护且可部署于无风扇设备的方案。

Method: SG-NCA基于NCA框架：1) 使用NCA进行多类分割实现高效目标检测和特征提取；2) 配以轻量级关系预测器生成场景图。这是首个将NCA用于学习结构化表示的框架。

Result: 在白内障手术和胆囊切除术视频上评估，性能与已有基线相当，但参数量减少55倍。成功部署于适合手术室的无风扇边缘设备，并验证了手术视频字幕生成等下游应用。

Conclusion: SG-NCA证明了NCA可以在保持竞争力的同时大幅降低模型规模，为经济实惠、隐私保护和手术室就绪的术中场景理解提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Scene graph generation from surgical video enables a holistic and structured understanding of surgical scenes by modeling objects and their semantic relationships. Despite recent advances, state-of-the-art approaches rely on large, parameter-heavy deep learning models that are impractical for deployment in the operating room (OR) due to hardware footprint, hygiene constraints, latency, and data privacy concerns. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first scene graph generation method built on NCAs and the first NCA framework capable of learning structured representations. We introduce SG-NCA, a lightweight scene graph generation framework based on Neural Cellular Automata (NCA), designed for inference in fanless devices critical for OR hygiene protocols. SG-NCA is the first scene graph generation combining NCA-based multiclass segmentation for efficient object detection and feature extraction with a lightweight relation predictor. We evaluate SG-NCA on videos of cataract surgery and cholecystectomy, demonstrating performance comparable to established baselines while requiring 55x fewer parameters. We showcase deployment on fanless edge devices better suited for the OR and demonstrate downstream applications such as surgical video captioning, highlighting SG-NCA's potential for affordable, privacy-preserving, and OR-ready intraoperative scene understanding.

</details>


### [177] [RISE: Roadside Infrastructure Sequence Understanding across 3D Tracking and Structured Vision-Language Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16480)
*Yanbo Jiang,Haotian Zheng,Jiahao Wang,Hanxiao Ren,Yitao Xu,Yining Xing,Zehong Ke,Hao Cheng,Yiqian Tu,Jinhao Li,Zhiyuan Xuan,Fang Zhang,Jianqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RISE框架，结合度量3D跟踪与结构化视觉语言推理，用于路侧多相机序列理解。图像方法利用SAM3视频身份与标定引导的掩码一致性实现多视角身份关联，无需LiDAR或任务特定3D训练即可恢复持续3D轨迹。同时构建RISE-VQA数据集（33,910个QA对，557个片段，16个交叉路口）和RISE-Bench评估基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 路侧基础设施（如交叉路口多相机系统）的3D跟踪与高层语义理解缺乏统一的、可泛化的框架。现有方法依赖LiDAR或特定布局训练，难以部署到新路口；同时缺乏结构化的视觉语言推理数据来评估模型对路侧动态场景的预测与推理能力。

Method: 1) 度量跟踪：结合SAM3视频身份分割与标定引导的跨视角掩码协议进行多视图身份关联，利用标定条件几何实现不同路口的即插即用，无需重新训练。2) 视觉语言推理：通过人工审核的MLLM管道筛选高价值片段，使用受限全上下文Oracle生成基于bbox的预测性QA对，确保评估模型不会看到未来证据。3) 构建RISE-VQA数据集与交叉路口留出评估的RISE-Bench。

Result: 在6个交叉路口的20个审核片段上，3D跟踪达到66.9 MOTA。RISE-VQA包含33,910个QA对，覆盖16个路口61个路侧视角。实验表明领域适应和时间上下文有一致收益，但空间定位、未来定位和交互推理仍面临持续挑战。

Conclusion: RISE展示了无需LiDAR的、可跨路口泛化的度量3D跟踪方法，并系统评估了路侧场景的视觉语言推理能力。当前方法的局限包括空间grounding、未来定位和交互推理，为后续研究指明方向。

Abstract: We present RISE (Roadside Infrastructure Sequence Understanding and Evaluation), a framework spanning metric 3D tracking and structured vision-language reasoning in roadside sequences. For metric tracking, our image-only method combines SAM3 video identities with calibration-guided mask agreement for multi-view identity association, recovering persistent 3D tracks without LiDAR or task-specific 3D training. Its calibration-conditioned geometry allows the procedure to be instantiated at different calibrated multi-camera intersections without layout-specific retraining. On 20 human-reviewed clips from six intersections, the generated tracks achieve 66.9 MOTA within the defined multi-view evaluation scope. For structured vision-language reasoning, a human-reviewed MLLM pipeline mines high-value clips and uses a constrained full-context Oracle to construct bbox-grounded predictive QA without exposing future evidence to evaluated models. The resulting RISE-VQA dataset contains 33,910 QA pairs from 557 clips across 16 intersections and 61 roadside views. Its intersection-held-out RISE-Bench evaluates semantic choices, coordinates, future boxes, and interaction sets with deterministic task-specific metrics. Experiments show consistent benefits from domain adaptation and generally from temporal context, while revealing persistent challenges in spatial grounding, future localization, and interaction reasoning.

</details>


### [178] [Remote-Sensing City Layout Extraction with MLLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16484)
*Zigan Zhou,Kai Li,Yupeng Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Code-as-City框架，将单张俯视遥感图像的城市布局提取转化为基于多模态大语言模型(MLLM)的约束代码生成任务。图像模型先生成五类语义先验，三个有序MLLM流程依次恢复道路、土地覆盖区域与关系、建筑，确定性归一化将记录转为城市图和受限布局程序，程序执行生成可渲染的3D城市布局和正交语义投影。在CityLayout-100的100个场景上达到41.1% mIoU和48.3% gIoU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥感系统输出检测框、语义掩码或矢量边界，仅能定位类别并支持图像平面评分，不构成可执行布局——缺乏对象身份、类型化关系、拓扑结构和再生规则。作者旨在将视觉观测转化为可检查、可编辑的城市代码，同时保持平面和3D输出耦合。

Method: 1) 图像模型生成五类对齐语义布局先验；2) 三个有序MLLM流程分别恢复道路、土地覆盖区域及关系、建筑；3) 确定性归一化将积累的记录转换为城市图和受限布局程序；4) 执行程序生成可渲染3D城市布局和正交语义投影，支持像素级比较和命名对象编辑同步再生。

Result: 在CityLayout-100的100个场景上，完整框架获得41.1%平均IoU和48.3%全局IoU，定量证明视觉观察可转化为带耦合平面与3D输出的可检查、可编辑城市代码。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了将遥感图像转化为受限代码程序和3D城市布局的可行路径，使输出超越像素级评分，具备对象身份、关系和编辑能力，为城市布局理解开辟了程序化表示的新方向。

Abstract: Remote-sensing systems usually describe urban content with detection boxes, semantic masks, or vector boundaries. Such outputs locate classes and support image-plane scoring, yet they do not by themselves constitute an executable layout that retains object identities, typed relations, topology, and regeneration rules. Code-as-City instead casts urban-layout extraction from a single top-down image as constrained code generation with a multimodal large language model (MLLM). An image model first produces an aligned five-class semantic layout prior. Three ordered MLLM passes use the image and this prior to recover roads, land-cover regions and relations, and buildings. Deterministic normalization converts the accumulated records into a city graph and a restricted layout program. Executing the program creates a renderable 3D city layout and an orthographic semantic projection over shared geometry. The projection admits pixel-level comparison with remote-sensing masks, while named objects, relations, and editing operations remain available for synchronized regeneration of both views. Evaluated on the 100 scenes of CityLayout-100, the complete framework obtains 41.1% mean intersection-over-union and 48.3% global intersection-over-union. This result provides quantitative evidence that visual observations can be translated into inspectable, editable city code with coupled planar and 3D outputs.

</details>


### [179] [HiFi-BRep: High-Fidelity Latent Representation for Robust B-Rep Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16485)
*Junhao Hou,Chenqi Luo,Pufan Wang,Jiaying Lu,Yusheng Liu,Feiwei Qin,Meie Fang,Kun Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HiFi-BRep提出了一种高保真且结构有效的B-Rep（边界表示）生成框架，通过拓扑感知编码器消除padding噪声和特征污染，结合单阶段解码器并行联合预测几何与拓扑，将核心流形约束嵌入为可微学习目标，显著优于现有SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接合成高保真和结构有效的B-Rep是计算机辅助设计中的基础任务但仍具挑战。现有深度生成方法存在两种脆弱性：表示脆弱性（padding噪声和潜在空间特征污染）和生成脆弱性（顺序误差传播及不可微有效性约束导致的训练-推理不匹配）。

Method: HiFi-BRep包含两个协同贡献：(1) 拓扑感知编码器：通过可学习查询消除padding，并用拓扑引导注意力防止特征污染，构建高保真潜在表示；(2) 单阶段解码器：并行联合预测几何与拓扑，将核心流形约束作为可微学习目标嵌入，实现几何与拓扑的相互引导并避免级联误差。

Result: 大量实验表明，HiFi-BRep在结构有效性和几何保真度上均显著优于SOTA方法，代码和模型已公开。

Conclusion: HiFi-BRep通过同时解决表示脆弱性和生成脆弱性，提供了一种鲁棒的高质量B-Rep合成方案。

Abstract: Boundary representation (B-Rep) generation is a fundamental task in computer-aided design, yet the direct synthesis of high-fidelity and structurally valid B-Reps remains a major challenge. Existing deep generative methods suffer from two forms of brittleness: representation brittleness, caused by padding noise and feature contamination in the latent space, and generation brittleness, stemming from sequential error propagation and a train-inference mismatch due to non-differentiable validity enforcement. We propose HiFi-BRep, a novel framework that addresses these limitations through two synergistic contributions. First, a topology-aware encoder constructs a high-fidelity latent representation by eliminating padding via learnable queries and preventing feature contamination with topology-guided attention. Second, a single-stage decoder jointly predicts geometry and topology in parallel, embedding core manifold constraints as a differentiable learning objective. This design ensures mutual guidance between geometry and topology while avoiding cascaded errors. Extensive experiments show that HiFi-BRep significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods in both structural validity and geometric fidelity, providing a robust solution for high-quality B-Rep synthesis. Code and models are publicly available at https://github.com/1nnoh/HiFi-BRep.

</details>


### [180] [Towards Real-Time and Adaptable LiDAR Scene Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16490)
*Azhar Hussian,Martin Vossiek,Vasileios Belagiannis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RapidLiDAR提出一种LiDAR场景补全方法，将初始化本身作为可学习的数据驱动组件，通过自适应初始化模块预测每个部分输入点的空间变化位移，无需手动噪声调参；并用多尺度重建模块通过3D体素和2D BEV特征图细化点位置，实现0.1秒完成全场景补全，比最快的前序方法快2.3倍，适配10Hz车载LiDAR采集速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LiDAR场景补全方法多采用初始化-细化范式。生成式方法通过迭代细化随机高斯噪声较慢；非生成式方法用固定噪声尺度扰动部分场景，限制了对大间隙和遮挡区域的覆盖，且需为每种传感器配置手动重新校准。作者希望消除手动噪声调参并提升推理速度以满足自动驾驶实时性需求。

Method: 1) 自适应初始化模块：对每个部分输入点预测空间变化的位移，将稀疏观测扩展为适应局部几何的粗场景初始化，无需手动噪声调节。2) 多尺度重建模块：通过查询从输入扫描构建的多尺度3D体素和2D BEV特征图来细化点位置。3) 用体素和BEV特征提取替代最远点采样和k近邻等点邻域算子，使架构更快且天然支持不同输入分辨率。

Result: 在SemanticKITTI和KITTI-360上达到与最先进方法相当的补全性能，同时全场景补全仅需0.1秒，比最快的前序方法快2.3倍，匹配典型车载LiDAR的10Hz采集速率。

Conclusion: RapidLiDAR通过将噪声调参替换为可学习的自适应初始化，结合体素/BEV特征提取替代昂贵的点邻域算子，实现了实时LiDAR场景补全，朝实时自动驾驶3D感知迈出重要一步。

Abstract: LiDAR scene completion is a key component of 3D perception in autonomous driving, where the scene must be completed in real time to be usable in downstream tasks. Existing approaches typically follow an initialize-and-refine paradigm, in which a coarse initialization of the scene is first constructed, then refined into complete 3D geometry. Generative models are slower because they iteratively refine random Gaussian noise into the scene, while non-generative methods perturb the partial scene with a fixed noise scale, which limits coverage of large gaps and occluded regions and requires manual recalibration for each new sensor configuration. We present RapidLiDAR, a LiDAR scene completion method that treats the initialization itself as a learned, data-driven component. We propose an adaptive initialization module that predicts a spatially varying displacement for each partial input point, expanding the partial observations into a coarse scene initialization adapted to the local geometry, without requiring manual noise tuning. To refine this coarse initialization into a complete and coherent scene, we additionally propose a multi-scale reconstruction module that further refines point positions by querying multi-scale 3D voxel and 2D BEV feature maps constructed from the input scan. By replacing point-neighborhood operators such as farthest point sampling and $k$-nearest neighbor search with voxel- and BEV-based feature extraction, our architecture is faster and can handle different input resolutions by design. Experiments on SemanticKITTI and KITTI-360 show that our method achieves completion performance on par with the state of the art while completing a full scene in 0.1 seconds, which is 2.3 times faster than the fastest prior method. This matches the 10 Hz acquisition rate of typical automotive LiDAR sensors, taking a step toward real-time LiDAR scene completion.

</details>


### [181] [MLLM-Guided Semantic Correction for Text-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16513)
*Junhao Chen,Zheqi Lv,Keting Yin,Shengyu Zhang,Zhou Zhao,Feiyang Chen,Xinyu Duan,Baoxing Huai,Fei Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种无需训练、可解释的生成中段修正框架，通过将多模态大语言模型（MLLM）反馈注入扩散采样循环，在视频生成过程中实时检测并纠正语义偏差（如丢对象、属性错误、动作不匹配）。包含语义评估监督器和语义修改助手两个核心模块，在推理时通过可控潜伏轨迹干预实现语义修正，不修改模型参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本生成视频模型常出现语义错误（缺对象、属性错误、动作不匹配）。已有方法仅在采样前优化或在采样后精修，缺乏在视频生成过程中实时检测并纠正语义偏差的手段。

Method: 构建训练免费的中间生成修正框架：(1) 语义评估监督器（Semantic Assessment Supervisor）生成中间预览帧进行语义评估与偏差诊断；(2) 语义修改助手（Semantic Modification Assistant）通过可控潜伏轨迹干预在推理时纠正语义漂移。将MLLM语义反馈直接注入扩散采样循环，实现扩散轨迹修正。

Result: 在多个基准上通过大量实验验证了方法在语义对齐、视觉保真度和时间一致性上的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种在视频生成过程中持续自反思的语义修正机制，无需修改模型参数即可改善生成质量。

Abstract: Recent advances in diffusion models and Transformer architectures have led to significant progress in text-to-video generation. However, these models often suffer from semantic errors such as missing objects, incorrect attributes, or mismatched actions. Although some semantic correction methods perform optimization before sampling or refinement after sampling, how to detect and correct semantic deviations during the video generation process remains underexplored. In this paper, we introduce a training-free, interpretable mid-generation correction framework that integrates multimodal large language model (MLLM) feedback directly into the diffusion sampling loop. Our framework achieves diffusion trajectory correction by injecting semantic evaluation signals during video synthesis, enabling the model to optimize the generated content through continuous self-reflection. We propose two key modules: a Semantic Assessment Supervisor that generates intermediate preview frames for semantic evaluations and deviation diagnostics, and a Semantic Modification Assistant that corrects semantic drift during inference via a controllable latent trajectory intervention. Our method improves semantic alignment, visual fidelity, and temporal consistency without modifying model parameters. We validate the effectiveness of our approach through extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks.

</details>


### [182] [Matched Outcomes, Divergent Gaze: How Foveated MLLMs Search Compared to Humans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16514)
*Mohamed Amine Kerkouri,Marouane Tliba,Aladine Chetouani,Ulas Bagci,Alessandro Bruno*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过将三种通用多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在人类匹配的视网膜中央凹（foveated）输入下逐注视点驱动，与人类眼动扫描路径在目标导向搜索（COCO-Search18）上进行对比，发现模型在目标存在决策和获取效率上达到或超过人类，但注视过程本身不符合人类特征——表现为低熵、大幅度和高度自洽的扫描路径。该差异存在于时间过程轴上，是答案对齐和显著性指标所无法测量的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究人类视觉搜索是串行的（注视点构成扫描路径），作者想探究给定相同中央凹输入时，多模态大语言模型是否像人类一样搜索。这关乎MLLMs是否能作为人类视觉模型使用，以及注意力对齐分数是否可靠。

Method: 在COCO-Search18基准上比较三种通用MLLMs与人类眼动扫描路径，通过逐注视点驱动各模型经过相同的、人类匹配的中央凹视图，沿三个轴评估：目标存在性决策、到达目标的效率、注视过程本身。通过三轴分离分析揭示模型与人类在各维度上的差异。

Result: 模型在目标决策和目标获取上匹配或超过人类（接近天花板的目标检测率，首次扫视到达目标频率高于人类），但注视过程不似人类：三者共享低熵、大幅、自洽的扫描路径特征，模型内部一致性远高于两个人类之间的一致性。匹配的视网膜输入可重现人类看的位置，但不能重现看的展开方式，且没有任何退化机制能恢复人类式搜索同时保持人类式成功率。

Conclusion: 差距位于过程轴上，这是答案对齐和显著性指标无法测量的。此类指标不能认证人类级视觉能力，零样本模型适合结局和空间性问题，但不适合时间性、过程级问题。模型行为与单次通过、非串行架构一致，而非受限于视敏度。

Abstract: Human visual search is serial: the fovea must land on a candidate to confirm it, and those landings form a scanpath. Whether multimodal large language models (MLLMs), given the same foveated input, search as humans do bears on their use as models of human vision and on attention-alignment scores. We compare three general-purpose MLLMs with human eye-movement scanpaths on goal-directed search (COCO-Search18), driving each model fixation by fixation through an identical, human-matched foveated view and assessing it along three axes: the decision of target presence, the efficiency of reaching the target, and the gaze process itself. The axes dissociate. On the decision and on target acquisition the models match or exceed humans, detecting present targets near ceiling and reaching them on the first saccade more often than people do. The gaze process is not human. Under the human-matched condition, all three share one signature: low-entropy, large-amplitude, self-consistent scanpaths that agree with themselves far more closely than two humans agree with each other. That is consistent with a single-pass, non-serial architecture rather than a limit of acuity. Matched retinal input reproduces where humans look but not how the looking unfolds in time, and no degradation regime recovers human-like search at human-like success. The gap sits on a process axis that answer-alignment and saliency metrics do not measure. Because they miss it, such metrics cannot certify human-like vision, and zero-shot models suit outcome and spatial questions but not temporal, process-level ones.

</details>


### [183] [FLEET: Token-Based Feature Extraction for Event Camera-based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16523)
*Tristan Gottwald,Maximilian Schier,Melanie Schaller,Bodo Rosenhahn*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FLEET，一种直接处理事件相机异步序列的特征提取器，利用随机傅里叶特征和交叉注意力将可变长度事件流压缩为固定大小潜表示，将特征提取器推理成本与传感器分辨率解耦，无需辅助损失实现端到端学习，在新基准上超越SOTA并展现出对观测频率变化的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机具有异步、高频、低延迟特性，原则上非常适合控制策略设计，但现有强化学习方法未能充分利用传感器特性：CNN方法将事件聚合为稀疏网格，导致计算成本与传感器分辨率耦合且模糊了时间信息；现有生成式基线又依赖轨迹数据预训练。

Method: FLEET（Feature Learning from Events via Efficient Tokenization）直接处理事件序列。利用随机傅里叶特征进行编码，通过交叉注意力机制将可变长度事件流压缩成固定大小的潜表示，从而将特征提取器骨干网络的推理成本与传感器分辨率解耦，支持无辅助损失的端到端学习。

Result: 在一个新的高吞吐量基准上验证，序列化方法FLEET超越了SOTA性能，并在观测频率变化下表现出更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 基于序列直接处理的事件特征提取方法比基于网格聚合的方法更能利用事件相机的时空稀疏属性，兼顾性能与鲁棒性，为事件相机驱动的强化学习控制提供了更优的特征学习方案。

Abstract: Event cameras generate asynchronous, high-frequency data streams offering spatially sparse information at lower latency than traditional cameras.In principle, these properties should be ideal for the design of control policies.However, reinforcement learning research in this field remains limited as existing approaches fail to fully exploit the sensor's properties.CNN-based methods negate the sensors benefits by aggregating events into sparse grids. This couples compute cost to sensor resolution and blurs the temporal information. Meanwhile, existing generative baselines rely on the availability of trajectory data to pretrain the model. We propose FLEET (Feature Learning from Events via Efficient Tokenization), a feature extractor that processes event sequences directly. Leveraging random Fourier features and cross-attention, our architecture compresses variable streams into fixed-size latent representations. This decouples inference cost of the feature extractor's backbone from the sensor's resolution, enabling end-to-end learning without auxiliary losses. We validate FLEET on a new, high-throughput benchmark. The results demonstrate that our sequence-based approach surpasses SOTA performance and exhibits superior robustness to variations in observation frequencies.

</details>


### [184] [Automatic Cephalometric Landmark Localization on CBCT-Derived Digitally Reconstructed Radiographs for Skeletal Malocclusion Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16535)
*Benjamin Hou,Konstantinia Almpani,Janice S. Lee,Zhiyong Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CephViT，一种基于Vision Transformer的自动2D侧位头影测量标志点定位模型，并在下游骨性错颌畸形分类中验证其可行性。模型在公开数据集上达到1.28±1.42 mm的平均径向误差，3.0 mm内成功检出率92.0%。在私有3D CBCT队列中，通过生成DRR作为2D输入，自动定位标志点驱动的骨性错颌分类精度（70.0%）与手动标注管线（68.3%）相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手动头影测量标志点标注是颅面评估的重要环节，但劳动密集且难以规模化，需要自动化方法以提高效率并支持大规模临床应用。

Method: CephViT采用Vision Transformer架构进行2D侧位头影测量标志点定位；对3D CBCT数据通过生成DRR（数字重建放射影像）转换为类侧位片输入；将标志点坐标归一化到统一坐标系，并利用参考管线和DRR管线共享的标志点进行骨性错颌畸形分类。

Result: CephViT在公开数据集上平均径向误差1.28±1.42 mm，3.0 mm阈值下成功检出率92.0%。DRR定位标志点的分类准确率70.0%，与手动标注的68.3%相当。

Conclusion: 基于CBCT派生DRR的自动化头影测量分析用于骨性错颌评估具有可行性，标志点自动定位可替代手动标注支持下游分类任务。

Abstract: Manual cephalometric landmark annotation is important for craniofacial assessment but is labor-intensive and difficult to scale. We introduce CephViT, a Vision Transformer-based model for automated 2D lateral cephalometric landmark localization, and evaluate its use in downstream skeletal malocclusion classification. CephViT was trained and benchmarked on a public lateral cephalogram dataset, achieving a mean radial error of 1.28 +/- 1.42 mm and a successful detection rate of 92.0% at 3.0 mm. Because the private evaluation cohort consisted of 3D CBCT scans, lateral cephalogram-like digitally reconstructed radiographs (DRRs) were generated from each volume and used as 2D inputs to the landmark localization model. Landmark coordinates were normalized into a common coordinate frame, and skeletal malocclusion classification was performed using landmarks shared between the reference and DRR-based pipelines. Classification performance using DRR-localized landmarks was comparable to that obtained using manually annotated reference landmarks, with accuracies of 70.0% and 68.3%, respectively. These results support the feasibility of automated cephalometric analysis on CBCT-derived DRRs for skeletal malocclusion assessment.

</details>


### [185] [Supervising the Path to Fine Scales: GalerkinFlow for Scientific-Field and Image Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16546)
*Zikang Zhan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GalerkinFlow是一个与方程无关的超分辨率重建框架，将每个粗细图像对转化为对整个重建路径的监督。模型在重建路径的中间状态预测粗细残差速度，通过粗锚点定义伪端点，使每个中间状态都为同一精细目标贡献监督信号，并辅以有限差分目标约束局部空间变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有超分辨率模型仅监督最终高分辨率输出，缺乏对预测在降采样观测与精细目标之间演化过程的控制，导致重建过程不可控、中间状态未被有效利用。

Method: 提出GalerkinFlow框架：1) 在重建路径上随机采样中间状态，模型预测粗细残差速度；2) 用粗锚点定义伪端点，推导出中间速度损失与伪端点重建损失之间的时间相关权重关系；3) 监督粗端点用于单步推理；4) 加入有限差分目标约束局部空间变化；5) 结合卷积特征与尺度条件Galerkin算子混合，无需控制方程或物理元数据。

Result: 在Navier-Stokes和Darcy Flow上达到了评估的与方程无关基线中最低的原始空间误差，在DIV2K上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: GalerkinFlow通过将每个中间状态转化为对同一精细目标的有效监督，显著提升超分辨率重建性能，是一种方程无关且无需物理元数据的通用框架，在流体动力学和自然图像任务上均表现良好。

Abstract: Most super-resolution models learn from paired data by supervising only the final high-resolution output. This provides little control over how the prediction should evolve between the downsampled observation and its fine target. We introduce GalerkinFlow, an equation-agnostic framework that turns each coarse--fine pair into supervision along an entire reconstruction path. At a random sample of intermediate states on the reconstruction path, the model predicts the coarse-to-fine residual velocity and uses coarse-anchor point to define a pseudo-endpoint. We show that the reconstruction loss of this pseudo-endpoint is exactly related to the intermediate velocity loss through a known time-dependent weight. Consequently, every intermediate state contributes supervision toward the same fine target, rather than serving only as an internal step toward an endpoint loss. Because intermediate states already reveal part of the missing fine-scale structure, we additionally supervise the coarse endpoint used during one-step inference. A finite-difference objective further constrains local spatial variation. GalerkinFlow combines convolutional features with scale-conditioned Galerkin operator mixing and requires no governing equation or physical metadata. It achieves the lowest raw-space errors among the evaluated equation-agnostic baselines on Navier--Stokes and Darcy Flow, while remaining competitive on DIV2K.

</details>


### [186] [SQuad: Sub-Quadratic Attention Distillation for Efficient Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16585)
*Animesh Karnewar,Denis Korzhenkov,Amirhossein Habibian,Mohsen Ghafoorian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SQuad框架，通过两阶段蒸馏（Flow-Matching SFT + DMD2）将预训练的全softmax Attention视频DiT压缩为次二次复杂度O(n√n)的Attention模型，在Wan 2.2 5B视频生成模型上以约67倍Attention FLOPs缩减和11倍延迟缩减匹配教师模型质量，并将采样步数从100降至6 NFE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频DiT的主要计算开销来自Self-Attention，其O(n²)复杂度随token数量增长，限制了可生成的视频分辨率和时长。现有的线性O(n)和低秩O(nk)注意力替代方案虽然加快计算，但表达力不足，存在顽固的质量差距，亟需一种在效率和表达力之间取得更好平衡的方案。

Method: 提出SQuad-Attention，实现O(n√n)的复杂度以自然平衡效率与表达力。不从头训练模型，而是将预训练的全softmax Self-Attention DiT蒸馏为SQuad-Attention版本，采用两阶段蒸馏：第一阶段为Flow-Matching监督微调（SFT），第二阶段采用改进的分布匹配蒸馏（DMD2）同时提升采样效率。

Result: 在Wan 2.2 5B文本生成视频模型上，SQuad在VBench评分达到83.20 vs 教师的83.08，匹配教师质量；同时per-step per-block Attention FLOPs降低约67倍，Attention延迟降低约11倍，端到端DiT延迟降低2倍，且仅需6个NFE即可生成视频（教师默认需100步）。

Conclusion: SQuad证明了通过精心设计的蒸馏流程，可以在保持全softmax注意力表达力的同时大幅降低视频DiT的计算成本，实现次二次复杂度的注意力并显著加速采样，为高效视频生成提供了一条可行的工程路径。

Abstract: Video Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) spend most of their compute inside the Self-Attention operation, whose cost grows quadratically, $\mathcal{O}(n^2)$, with the number of latent tokens $n$. For the task of video generation, the token count is large, so this term dominates runtime and memory, and thereby caps the resolution and duration we can generate. Linear $\mathcal{O}(n)$ and low-rank $\mathcal{O}(nk)$ surrogates of Self-Attention trade the full softmax $QK^T$ for cheaper kernels, but rarely recover the original's expressivity, leaving a stubborn quality gap. Motivated by this, we propose SQuad, a Sub-Quadratic Attention Distillation framework that achieves a complexity of $\mathcal{O}(n\sqrt{n})$ in the resulting distilled Attention, naturally balancing the efficiency v/s expressivity trade-off. Instead of training our own Video DiT from scratch, which is prohibitively expensive, we fit a pretrained full softmax Self-Attention DiT into our proposed SQuad-Attention one by distilling the former in two stages: Flow-Matching Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT), followed by improved Distribution Matching Distillation (DMD2) which additionally makes the sampling more efficient. On the Wan~2.2 5B text-to-video model, SQuAD matches the quadratic teacher on VBench ($83.20$ v/s $83.08$) while cutting the per-step per-block attention FLOPs by $\sim$$67\times$ and attention latency by $\sim$$11\times$, and end-to-end DiT latency by 2$\times$, all while also generating a video in only $6$ Neural Functional Evaluations (NFEs) instead of the default $100$.

</details>


### [187] [Ultra: Unsupervised Cross-Task Optimization for Reliable Restoration Segmentation Collaboration under Adverse Weather](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16589)
*Shiqin Wang,Zhiqian Li,Haoyuan Du,Junming Chen,Jiayuan Li,Tianrun Xu,Haoyang Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Ultra框架，一个面向恶劣天气下无监督域适应语义分割的新型无监督恢复-分割协同学习框架，通过CTDN（候选方向生成与协作选择）和CMIL（基于因果效应的跨任务信息传递）来解决严重退化下跨任务优化方向不可识别导致的幻觉误差传播问题，在三个基准上取得SOTA分割性能，并推广到无监督恢复和目标检测协同任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有UDA-ASS方法隐式假设恢复和分割能互相提供有益引导，但在严重退化且无目标域监督条件下，跨任务优化方向的有效性根本不可识别，导致基于幻觉的错误传播。

Method: 提出Ultra框架：(1) CTDN利用互补视觉结构和语义信息生成候选优化方向，并在恢复与分割之间进行协作方向选择；(2) CMIL将跨任务信息传递从相关性传播重构为因果效应评估，抑制幻觉传播。

Result: 在三个广泛使用的UDA-ASS基准上取得SOTA语义分割性能；无监督恢复结果优于现有UDA-ASS恢复方法；可泛化到无监督恢复和目标检测协同任务。

Conclusion: 通过将跨任务交互转化为不确定性下的方向选择与因果效应估计，Ultra能实现可靠的恢复-分割协同学习，在恶劣天气UDA语义分割及更广泛的跨任务协同场景中均有效。

Abstract: Unsupervised Domain Adaptation for Adverse Weather Semantic Segmentation (UDA-ASS) aims to transfer semantic knowledge from labeled normal-weather images to unlabeled adverse environments. Existing approaches implicitly assume that restoration and segmentation provide mutually beneficial guidance. However, under severe degradation and without target-domain supervision, the validity of cross-task optimization directions becomes fundamentally unidentifiable, leading to hallucination-driven error propagation. In this work, we propose a novel Unsupervised Restoration-Segmentation Collaborative Learning Framework (Ultra), which reframes cross-task interaction as direction selection under uncertainty and causal effect estimation, enabling reliable collaboration through candidate direction generation and intervention-based filtering. In detail, we propose CTDN and CMIL. The former exploits complementary visual structures and semantic information to generate candidate optimization directions and performs cooperative direction selection between restoration and segmentation. The latter reformulates cross-task information transfer from correlation-based propagation into causal effect assessment, suppressing hallucination propagation. Extensive experiments on three widely used UDA-ASS benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art segmentation performance. Beyond segmentation, our framework achieves better unsupervised restoration results than existing UDA-ASS restoration methods and generalizes to unsupervised restoration and object detection collaboration tasks. Code and models will be available at https://github.com/Wang-Shiqin/Ultra.

</details>


### [188] [Towards Zero-Shot Domain Generalization for ID Cards Presentation Attack Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16591)
*Mario Nieto-Hidalgo,Juan M. Espin,Juan E. Tapia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向国家身份证件的演示攻击检测(PAD)方法，通过原型网络头和情景式训练策略，仅需每类四个真实样本即可构建可靠原型，并在多国数据集和公开DLC-2021基准上达到约9%的平均等错误率，优于softmax和CLIP零样本基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 国家身份证件的PAD受限于缺乏公开真实样本，导致系统难以跨国家泛化。现有方法需要大量真实数据，而隐私法规限制使得真实样本收集困难，需要一种数据高效、保护隐私且能跨域泛化的PAD方案。

Method: (1) 原型网络头：基于EfficientNet-V2-b0骨干网络，每类仅需四张真实样本构建可靠原型；(2) 情景式训练机制：固定PAD类别但变化卡片域，使网络学习通用的攻击线索而非特定域特征。

Result: 在大规模多国数据集和DLC-2021公开基准上，方法达到约9%的平均等错误率(EER)，优于传统softmax和CLIP零样本基线，且仅需来自单一来源国家的数据即可实现良好的跨域性能。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了准确、隐私保护的PAD方案，同时大幅减少数据采集需求，促进可扩展的跨司法辖区远程入职流程。

Abstract: Presentation-Attack Detection (PAD) for national ID cards is limited by the lack of publicly available genuine samples, making it difficult for systems to generalize across countries. This paper introduces two main innovations: (1) a Prototypical Network head using an EfficientNet-V2-b0 backbone that requires only four genuine samples per class to create reliable prototypes; and (2) an episodic training regime that keeps PAD classes fixed while varying the card domain, allowing the network to learn universal attack cues.
  Evaluated on a large multi-country dataset and the public DLC-2021 benchmark, this method achieves an average Equal Error Rate of around 9\%, outperforming conventional softmax and CLIP zero-shot baselines even with data from a single source country. This approach provides accurate, privacy-preserving PAD while minimizing data collection, facilitating scalable cross-jurisdictional remote onboarding.

</details>


### [189] [GeoPose: Patient-agnostic CTA-to-DSA registration through projection-space calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16600)
*Rudolf L. M. van Herten,Robert Graf,Paula Feldman,Johannes C. Paetzold*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoPose 是一个种群训练（population-trained）的框架，用于将术中双平面数字减影血管造影（DSA）与术前CTA进行快速且准确的3D-2D配准。它通过学习获得的规范坐标系中估计C臂位姿，再通过投影空间校准和变换组合将其转移到未见过的CTA的原生坐标系中，无需患者特定训练或显式的体间预配准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 术中双平面DSA与术前CTA的3D-2D配准需要快速且准确。现有基于优化的方法对初始化敏感、需要数百次迭代；而基于学习的方法通常依赖患者特定训练，无法快速部署到新患者。需要一种无需患者特定适配的通用配准方法。

Method: GeoPose使用种群训练的残差网络，在学习的规范坐标系中估计C臂位姿，通过投影空间校准和变换组合将位姿转换到未见CTA的原生坐标系。可选的低预算图像驱动优化进一步精化位姿，全程无需患者特定适应或显式间体积预配准。

Result: 在80个DSA观测（20个留出患者）上，免优化GeoPose达到颈动脉平均投影中心线距离（mPCD）5.8mm、clDice 0.45，优于最佳基线（14.5mm、0.28），且仅需0.15秒。25次优化迭代后达到mPCD 4.6mm、clDice 0.58（约2秒）。同预算下原生初始化的优化仅达14.6mm和0.15。

Conclusion: GeoPose以固定种群级权重提供了快速的原生坐标系配准，实现了足够精确的几何对应关系，支持下游双平面3D血管重建。

Abstract: Aligning intraoperative biplanar digital subtraction angiography (DSA) to pre-procedural computed tomography angiography (CTA) requires rapid and accurate 3D-to-2D registration. Optimization-based methods are sensitive to initialization and may require hundreds of iterations, whereas learning-based approaches commonly rely on patient-specific training. We propose GeoPose, a population-trained framework that estimates the C-arm pose in a learned canonical frame and transfers it to the native frame of an unseen CTA through projection-space calibration and transform composition. A population-trained residual network refines the pose, followed optionally by low-budget image-driven optimization. GeoPose requires neither patient-specific adaptation nor explicit inter-volume preregistration. On 80 DSA observations from 20 held-out patients, optimization-free GeoPose achieved a carotid mean projected centerline distance (mPCD) of 5.8 mm and a clDice of 0.45, compared with 14.5 mm and 0.28 for the best-performing baseline, while requiring only 0.15 s. After 25 optimization iterations, GeoPose reached an mPCD of 4.6 mm and a clDice of 0.58 in approximately two seconds. Under the same budget, native-initialized optimization achieved 14.6 mm and 0.15, respectively. GeoPose thus provides rapid native-frame registration with fixed population-level weights and the geometric correspondence required for downstream biplanar 3D vascular reconstruction.

</details>


### [190] [Interactive Whole Slide Images for RL-based Tumour Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16607)
*Mohamad Mohamad,Francesco Ponzio,Maxime Gassier,Nicolas Pote,Xavier Descombes*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种端到端的强化学习框架，将全切片图像（WSI）建模为分层多分辨率环境，通过智能体使用移动、缩放和肿瘤选择动作进行序列化肿瘤分割，在肺腺癌WSI上实现全切片直接分割，推理时间缩短至每张数秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WSI分析因全切片图像分辨率极大且肿瘤区域稀疏而计算挑战巨大，现有方法依赖预定义候选patch集合，效率低且无法直接处理整张切片。

Method: 将WSI建模为分层多分辨率交互环境，智能体通过移动、缩放、肿瘤选择三类动作导航；在actor-critic架构中联合处理局部观察与全局缩略图表示，使用近端策略优化（PPO）训练。

Result: 在肺腺癌WSI上验证了直接全切片顺序分割的可行性，在相似放大倍数下达到与patch-based方法相当的粗分割质量，同时将推理时间降至每张切片数秒。并分析了环境设计与动作空间粒度的影响。

Conclusion: 将WSI建模为交互式环境为基于强化学习的计算病理学提供了有前景的方向。

Abstract: Whole-slide image (WSI) analysis remains computationally challenging due to the extremely large spatial resolution of slides and the sparse distribution of tumour regions. We propose an end-to-end reinforcement learning framework for sequential tumour segmentation directly on WSIs. Instead of treating the slide as a predefined collection of candidate patches, we formulate the WSI itself as a hierarchical multi-resolution environment through which an agent navigates using movement, zooming, and tumour selection actions. The agent jointly processes local observations and a global thumbnail representation within an actor-critic architecture trained using proximal policy optimization (PPO). Experiments on pulmonary adenocarcinoma WSIs demonstrate the feasibility of direct sequential tumour segmentation on full slides, achieving comparable coarse segmentation quality relative to patch-based approaches operating at similar magnification levels, while reducing inference time to a few seconds per slide. We further analyse the impact of environment design and action-space granularity. Our results suggest that modelling WSIs as interactive environments provides a promising direction for RL-based computational pathology

</details>


### [191] [Beyond Accuracy: Assessing Calibration of Geospatial Foundation Models and Their Sensitivity to Distribution Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16614)
*Nils Lehmann,Jakob Gawlikowski,Burak Ekim,Isaac Corley,Xiao Xiang Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统性地批判了当前地理空间基础模型（GeoFMs）仅以标准基准精度排名作为评估指标的现状，引入校准（模型置信度与正确性的吻合度）作为核心评估维度。作者在16个冻结编码器、4个分类和5个分割数据集、两条正交压力轴上实验发现：所有编码器随扰动增强而性能退化且排名改变；EO预训练与ImageNet预训练在干净精度和校准上无差异，且EO预训练在分布偏移下更不稳定，比ImageNet更过度自信；CKA分析将其归因于表示刚性。温度缩放和深度集成无法缓解退化，高斯过程探针仅在严重云干扰下减半ECE但使干净数据ECE翻三倍。置信度弃权无法回避错误自信预测，故倡导多条件多指标的基准评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GeoFMs的部署承诺（关键EO任务）需要超越单一精度指标，校准（置信度与正确性的匹配）对高风险遥感决策至关重要，但主流评估协议完全忽略了这一维度。作者旨在揭示当前评估协议过于狭隘，并指出EO预训练声称的优势在压力条件下并不成立。

Method: 在16个冻结编码器（EO预训练与ImageNet预训练）上，对4个分类和5个分割数据集施加两条正交压力轴（corruption类型与强度），评估干净准确率、校准（ECE）、分布偏移稳定性。使用CKA分析表示刚性，并测试三种UQ方法（温度缩放、深度集成、高斯过程探针），以及选择性预测（置信度弃权）的有效性。

Result: 所有编码器随扰动增强性能退化且排名变动；EO预训练与ImageNet在干净准确率与校准上无差异；偏移下EO模型更过度自信；CKA显示EO嵌入在偏移下移动更少但丢失同样多的任务信息；温度缩放与深度集成均无法抵消退化，GP探针仅在严重云干扰下减半ECE但使干净ECE翻三倍；置信度弃权无法规避错误自信预测。

Conclusion: 当前以平均排名衡量干净准确率的评估协议过窄，无法反映部署场景的真实可靠性。模型评估应在多条件、多指标下进行，特别要纳入校准鲁棒性，以更全面地评价模型进展并弥合与真实世界部署的差距。

Abstract: Geospatial Foundation Models (GeoFMs) are most commonly ranked and selected by accuracy on standard benchmark conditions via averaged ranks. We show that this protocol is too narrow: the promised deployment in critical EO tasks requires further angles of analysis, mainly calibration, the agreement between a model's confidence and its correctness. Across 16 frozen encoders, four classification and five segmentation datasets, and two orthogonal stress axes, every encoder degrades as corruption intensifies, and the ranking changes as well. Across the four classification benchmarks, EO-pretrained and ImageNet-pretrained encoders are indistinguishable on clean accuracy and clean calibration, and EO pretraining provides no more stability under shift than ImageNet pretraining. Under shift the GeoFMs drift further into overconfidence than the ImageNet-pretrained encoders, at every grade and in every corruption family. A centered kernel alignment (CKA) analysis ties this to representational rigidity: EO-pretrained embeddings move less under corruption while losing just as much task information and remaining overconfident. We apply three commonly explored uncertainty quantification methods and find that temperature scaling and deep ensembles cannot counteract the degradation, while a Gaussian-process probe roughly halves ECE under severe cloud only by tripling it on clean data. In selective prediction experiments, we find that confidence-based abstention cannot defer around confidently wrong predictions, and advocate that benchmark rankings and evaluations should therefore operate across a multitude of conditions and metrics to more holistically evaluate model development progress and close the gap to real world deployment scenarios.

</details>


### [192] [HarmTrace: Anchor-Calibrated Decoupled Optimization for Fine-Grained Target Identification in Harmful Memes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16622)
*Yujia Li,Yiqun Zhang,Zihan Cheng,Yijie Huang,Tenglong Ye,Zihan Wang,Xiaocui Yang,Shi Feng,Yifei Zhang,Daling Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文扩展了多模态有害模因检测任务，引入细粒度目标识别（目标类别、目标实体、文本提及、视觉区域），提出Mem3W数据集和Joint Record Accuracy (JRA)严格指标，并设计了HarmTrace框架（实体感知SFT + 条件目标识别策略优化CTPO + 虚拟正锚VPA），在多个视觉语言骨干上将JRA大幅提升（Qwen3-VL-8B从17.58%到52.51%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有有害模因检测只做图像-文本二分类，模型可能正确判断有害性却错误识别被攻击的目标或证据，缺乏细粒度的目标识别能力，需要更全面的评估与优化框架。

Method: 提出HarmTrace：先通过实体感知的有监督微调（entity-aware SFT）强化目标实体监督，再应用条件目标识别策略优化（CTPO）解耦有害性与目标识别优势，将目标识别优化限定在标签正确的有害样本响应上；使用虚拟正锚（VPA）作为完全正确的参考进行目标识别优势归一化。

Result: 在多个多模态大语言模型骨干上，HarmTrace同时提升了JRA和有害性准确率；Qwen3-VL-8B骨干上的JRA从17.58%提升到52.51%。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 细粒度目标识别对于全面理解有害模因至关重要，解耦有害性与目标识别优化能有效缩小两者之间的性能差距，该框架具有跨骨干的泛化能力。

Abstract: Multimodal harmful meme detection is typically formulated as image--text harmfulness classification. A model may correctly predict harmfulness while misidentifying the attacked target or its supporting evidence. We therefore extend harmful meme detection with fine-grained target identification, asking what type of target is attacked, who is targeted, and where the target appears in the meme. The model predicts harmfulness for every meme and, for harmful memes, outputs the target category, target entity, textual mention, and visual region. To support this task, we introduce Meme3W, which unifies multiple public harmful meme datasets and provides human-verified annotations for harmful instances. We further introduce Joint Record Accuracy (JRA), a strict record-level metric requiring the harmfulness label and all target-identification fields to be jointly correct. Experiments with representative multimodal large language models reveal a substantial gap between harmfulness accuracy and JRA. To narrow this gap, we propose HarmTrace, an anchor-calibrated decoupled optimization framework. HarmTrace strengthens target-entity supervision through entity-aware supervised fine-tuning. It then applies Conditional Target-identification Policy Optimization (CTPO) to decouple harmfulness and target-identification advantages, restricting target-identification optimization to label-correct responses for harmful examples. CTPO uses a Virtual Positive Anchor (VPA) as a fully correct reference for target-identification advantage normalization. HarmTrace improves both JRA and harmfulness accuracy across the evaluated backbones, with JRA on the Qwen3-VL-8B backbone increasing from 17.58\% to 52.51\%. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/llly1234/HarmTrace-for-Harmful-Memes.

</details>


### [193] [DRAFE: Domain-Robust Asymmetric Fusion of Heterogeneous Detection Transformers for Cross-City Fine-Grained Traffic Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16632)
*Divine Yao Agbobli,Geoffery Eyram Agorku,Israel Afriyie,Kwadwo Amankwah-Nkyi,Marvin Osei-Kuffour,Richmond Owusu Duah,Bright Seglah,Kelvin Asamoah Terkper,Kwabena Amoako Adjei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DRAFE集成框架，结合LW-DETR和RF-DETR两个独立训练的检测器，通过两阶段训练策略和多种推理融合机制，在AI City Challenge 2026 Track 6跨城市细粒度交通目标检测任务中取得0.4022 mAP，排名第六。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能交通系统需要同时实现细粒度车辆识别和跨城市域泛化，但现有方法难以兼顾这两点。

Method: 采用两阶段训练：先在多样化公开交通数据集上使用伪标签扩展和人在回路标注精炼预训练互补检测器（LW-DETR和RF-DETR），生成6,049张图像和203,619个标注的语料库；然后在Project Hafnia Track 6数据集上进行符合竞赛要求的微调。推理时使用anchor条件类别一致性匹配、可靠性加权坐标融合、一致性感知置信度重校准和互补假设恢复四种机制。

Result: 在AI City Challenge 2026 Track 6上取得0.4022 mAP，在25个参赛队伍中排名第六，相比初步集成都提升了0.0553 mAP。

Conclusion: DRAFE通过互补检测器集成和两阶段训练策略有效提升了跨城市细粒度交通目标检测性能，验证了域鲁棒性融合方法的有效性。

Abstract: Deep learning-based object detectors are fundamental to intelligent transportation systems, enabling traffic monitoring, vehicle analytics, and infrastructure management. However, achieving both fine-grained vehicle recognition and robust cross-city domain generalization remains challenging. We present the Domain-Robust Asymmetric Fusion Ensemble (DRAFE), which combines independently trained LW-DETR and RF-DETR detectors for cross-city fine-grained traffic object detection.
