DRAFE employs a two-stage training strategy that first pretrains complementary detectors on diverse public traffic datasets using pseudo-label expansion and human-in-the-loop annotation refinement, producing a curated corpus of 6,049 images and 203,619 annotations, before challenge-compliant fine-tuning on the Project Hafnia Track 6 dataset. At inference, DRAFE applies anchor-conditioned class-consistent matching, reliability-weighted coordinate fusion, agreement-aware confidence recalibration, and complementary hypothesis recovery. On AI City Challenge 2026 Track 6, DRAFE achieves 0.4022 mAP, ranks sixth among 25 participating teams, and improves by 0.0553 mAP over a preliminary ensemble evaluated under identical benchmark conditions.

</details>


### [194] [Training-Free Reconstruction-Based AI-Generated Image Detectors Are Inherently Vulnerable to Adversarial Examples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16646)
*Roman Demchenko,Jonas Ricker,Asja Fischer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对基于自编码器重建误差的合成图像检测器，提出了两种对抗攻击方法。通过构造人眼不可察觉的对抗扰动，放大原始图像与重建图像之间的距离，使伪造图像被错误分类为真实图像。实验表明在三种生成器和三种检测器上检测性能显著下降，且攻击具有跨检测器的迁移性，揭示重建型检测器的固有脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI生成图像质量极高且广泛传播，需要可靠鲁棒的检测方法。重建型检测器虽具有可解释性且无需训练的独特优势，但其与基于分类器的方法运行机制根本不同，其对抗鲁棒性尚不明确，需要专门研究其安全性。

Method: 提出两种针对自编码器重建误差检测器的攻击方法：通过构造人眼不可察觉的对抗扰动，人为增大原始图像与重建图像之间的距离（使距离超过检测阈值），从而使伪造图像被误判为真实。

Result: 在三种SOTA生成器和三种检测器上评估，检测性能显著下降，且攻击在被攻击图像经历真实世界退化后仍然有效。对抗样本天然具有跨检测器的迁移性，因为所有重建型检测器共享相同的工作原理。

Conclusion: 重建型检测器存在固有且本质的脆弱性——由于统一基于自编码器重建误差这一原理，单一攻击思路即可同时击破多个检测器，这为设计更鲁棒的重建型检测方法指明了方向。

Abstract: The impressive visual quality and ubiquity of AI-generated images call for reliable and robust detection methods. Reconstruction-based detectors have emerged as a promising direction for transparent and training-free identification of synthetic images. However, due to their fundamentally different mode of operation (compared to standard, classifier-based methods), little is known about their adversarial robustness. In this work, we propose two novel attack methods targeted at detectors that leverage autoencoder reconstruction error. We find that by constructing imperceptible adversarial examples, the distance between original and reconstruction can be artificially increased, causing fake images to be wrongly classified as real. Our evaluation including images from three state-of-the-art generators and three detectors demonstrates that detection performance is significantly decreased, even if attacked images additionally undergo real-world degradations. Critically, our adversarial examples naturally transfer across detectors, as they all share the same principle, pointing towards an inherent vulnerability of reconstruction-based detectors.

</details>


### [195] [Turning spectra into images improves plant trait retrieval with 2D-CNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16661)
*Javier Lopatin,Teja Kattenborn,Eya Cherif,Sebastián Moreno*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究将1D高光谱反射光谱转换为2D图像表示，使用CNN（EfficientNet-B0）进行多性状预测。发现最简单的Reshape变换比复杂的1D深度学习基线表现更好（R²=0.684 vs 0.587），并进一步用2D掩码自编码器（MAE-2D）在139,000个无标签光谱图像上预训练，线性探测达到R²=0.646，超过所有1D自监督方法。跨数据集泛化时所有模型均大幅下降。通过Integrated Gradients和Grad-CAM解释模型，蛋白和叶片水分的波段重要性与PROSAIL辐射传输模型模拟一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习处理高光谱光谱数据时将其视为1D序列，限制了捕捉长距离波段间依赖关系的能力。作者探究将1D光谱转化为2D图像表示是否能够利用CNN的归纳偏置来提升多性状预测性能。

Method: 比较九种光谱到2D图像的变换方法，使用EfficientNet-B0在GreenHyperSpectra数据集（7,897个标记光谱、8个性状、400-2450nm）上训练，与已发表的1D CNN结果对比。随后在139,000个无标签光谱图像上预训练2D掩码自编码器（MAE-2D），并通过线性探测微调。最后用Integrated Gradients和Grad-CAM进行可解释性分析，与PROSAIL辐射传输模型模拟对比。

Result: 最简单的直接Reshape变换表现最佳（R²=0.684±0.001），优于1D基线（R²=0.587，提升0.097）。MAE-2D线性探测达到R²=0.646，超越所有1D自监督方法含微调后的MAE-1D（R²=0.641）。跨数据集评估中所有模型均大幅下降且未超过1D基线。蛋白（r=0.45）和叶水分（r=0.33）的波段重要性与PROSAIL模拟一致，类胡萝卜素和叶面积指数不一致。

Conclusion: 2D光谱表示的优越性来源于其表征优势本身，而非架构复杂性或ImageNet预训练，这推动了对1D方法的性能提升。模型对于具有尖锐吸收特征的传统叶化学性状能正确读取相关波段。

Abstract: Hyperspectral reflectance spectroscopy enables non-destructive estimation of plant functional traits, yet current deep learning approaches process spectra as one-dimensional sequences, which limits how they capture long-range inter-band dependencies. We asked whether transforming 1D spectra into 2D image representations improves multi-trait prediction with convolutional neural networks (CNN). We compared nine transformations using EfficientNet-B0 on the GreenHyperSpectra dataset (7,897 labeled spectra, eight traits, 400-2450 nm), benchmarked against published 1D CNN results on the same split. Trained from scratch, the simplest transformation, a direct Reshape of the spectrum into a 2D grid, performed best ($R^2 = 0.684 \pm 0.001$) and improved on the state-of-the-art 1D baseline ($R^2 = 0.587$, $+0.097$). We then pretrained a 2D masked autoencoder (MAE-2D) on 139,000 unlabeled spectral images. Linear probing, which freezes the encoder and trains only a multilayer perceptron head, reached $R^2 = 0.646$ and exceeded every 1D self-supervised counterpart, including the fine-tuned MAE-1D ($R^2 = 0.641$). Under cross-dataset evaluation all models lost most of their accuracy and none beat the 1D baseline significantly. To identify which wavelengths drive each prediction, we applied Integrated Gradients and Grad-CAM and unfolded band importance back to the spectral axis. Protein ($r = 0.45$) and leaf water ($r = 0.33$) agreed with sensitivities simulated by the PROSAIL radiative-transfer model, while carotenoids ($r = 0.06$) and leaf area index ($r = -0.11$) did not, showing that the model reads established leaf chemistry for traits with sharp absorption features. The representational advantage of 2D spectral images, rather than architectural complexity or ImageNet pretraining, drives the gain over 1D approaches.

</details>


### [196] [Concept-based explanation of gene expression prediction from H&E images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16669)
*Amos Muench,Jonathan Thielmann,Reduan Achtibat,Maximilian Dreyer,Philip Bischoff,Caroline Forsythe,Hamidreza Parand,Thomas Walter,David Horst,Sebastian Lapuschkin,Wojciech Samek,Teresa Gabriela Krieger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个可解释框架，结合ViT感知的层相关传播与松弛原型TopK稀疏自编码器概念发现，将转录程序与组织形态学联系起来，用于从H&E图像虚拟推断空间转录组学，并在结直肠癌数据上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有病理学基础模型的解释方法仅限于局部热图，无法揭示形态学概念如何贡献于空间转录组学预测，需要一种既能提供局部解释又能提供全局洞察的框架。

Method: 采用ViT框架进行虚拟空间转录组学预测，结合ViT感知的层相关传播(LRP)进行局部解释，并用松弛原型TopK稀疏自编码器(RATopK-SAE)进行概念发现，提供局部与全局的解释映射。

Result: 在HEST-1k结直肠癌数据和TCGA COAD上验证，iCMS2/iCMS3分类加权F1分数达0.872和0.819（TCGA为0.770），均能对患者预后分层，并建立了基于相关性的概念图谱。

Conclusion: 该框架建立了基于概念的病理学预测可解释性通用策略，可直接应用于广泛的ViT病理模型，相关性衍生的概念比激活衍生的概念能更直接连接组织形态学与预测。

Abstract: Recent advances in pathology foundation models have enabled accurate prediction of spatial transcriptomics (ST) from routine H&E images. However, existing explainability methods for vision transformer (ViT)-based models are largely limited to local heatmaps and do not reveal how morphological concepts contribute to ST predictions. Here, we introduce an explainable framework that combines relevance propagation and concept discovery to link transcriptional programs to tissue morphology. We developed a ViT-based framework for virtual ST from H&E images that combines ViT-aware layer-wise relevance propagation with relaxed archetypal TopK sparse autoencoder-based concept discovery. This approach provides both local explanations and global insights into the morphological patterns associated with transcriptional programs. We applied the framework to colorectal cancer ST data from the HEST-1k cohort and evaluated its generalizability in TCGA COAD. Our architecture accurately predicts clinically relevant ST signatures and accompanying molecular phenotypes. Measured and predicted gene expression profiles reveal substantial spatial heterogeneity of the colorectal cancer subtypes iCMS2 and iCMS3 across a large number of samples. Spatially resolved and aggregated iCMS classification achieve weighted F1 scores of 0.872 and 0.819 (0.770 in TCGA COAD), respectively, and both stratify patient outcome. Beyond prediction, our framework establishes a relevance-based concept atlas linking molecular phenotypes to histopathological representations. Comparison of activation- with relevance-derived concepts demonstrates that relevances provide a more direct link between tissue morphology and downstream predictions. We establish a general strategy for concept-based explanation of spatial prediction, and our framework is readily applicable to a broad range of ViT-based pathology models.

</details>


### [197] [How Sampling Strategy Affects Imbalance Mitigation in LiDAR Segmentation: A Study of Structured vs. Random Point-Based Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16673)
*Antonis Savva,Christos Kyrkou,Theocharis Theocharides*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对LiDAR点云语义分割中的类别不平衡问题进行了系统基准测试，评估了6种重加权方案和5种不平衡感知损失，在三个数据集（DALES、S3DIS、STPLS3D）和两种架构（KPConv、RandLA-Net）上进行实验。结果表明逆频率加权会严重退化性能（最高12%），而均匀加权在结构化采样下与复杂损失差距仅2%以内，但随机采样下差距可达4.6%。损失景观分析揭示了采样策略、不平衡程度和数据获取特性之间的复杂交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LiDAR点云语义分割中存在严重的类别不平衡问题，虽然2D视觉领域已有大量缓解技术，但它们在3D场景中的有效性尚不明确。作者旨在系统性地分析这些方法在3D点云上的表现，理解哪些方法真正有效以及背后的原因。

Method: 对6种重加权方案（包括逆频率加权、均匀加权等）和5种不平衡感知损失进行系统性基准测试，使用两种代表性架构（KPConv结构化采样、RandLA-Net随机采样）在三个数据集上评估，并进行损失景观（loss landscape）分析以理解不同方法失效或有效的几何原因。

Result: 逆频率加权相比均匀加权性能下降高达12%，在小类别上出现灾难性失败；均匀加权在结构化采样（KPConv）下与复杂损失差距在2%以内，但在随机采样（RandLA-Net）下差距可达4.6%。损失景观显示：结构化采样下不平衡比在真实LiDAR数据上决定景观几何但合成数据上不相关；随机采样下景观对数据集几何高度敏感而与不平衡比无关。

Conclusion: 对于两种点基架构，采样策略（结构化vs随机）、不平衡严重程度和数据获取特性的交互决定了哪些缓解方法有效。这为3D点云语义分割中的类别不平衡处理提供了实证指导，表明简单方法（均匀加权）在特定条件下可能比复杂的不平衡感知损失更有效。

Abstract: Class imbalance in LiDAR point clouds poses challenges for semantic segmentation in autonomous navigation and urban mapping. While 2D vision has numerous mitigation techniques, their effectiveness in 3D remains unclear. We benchmark six reweighting schemes and five imbalance-aware losses across three datasets (DALES, S3DIS, STPLS3D) using two architectures (KPConv, RandLA-Net). Inverse-frequency weighting degrades performance by up to 12% compared to uniform weighting, with catastrophic failures in minority classes. Uniform weighting performs within 2% of complex losses for structured sampling (KPConv) but benefits less for random sampling (RandLA-Net, up to 4.6% gap). Loss landscape analysis reveals a complex interplay: for structured sampling, imbalance ratio determines landscape geometry on real LiDAR data but decouples from it on synthetic data; for random sampling, landscapes show high sensitivity to dataset geometry regardless of imbalance ratio. For the two evaluated point-based architectures, these results suggest that the interaction between sampling strategy (structured vs. random), imbalance severity, and data acquisition characteristics shapes which mitigation approaches are effective.

</details>


### [198] [Bridging the Gap between Labeled and Unlabeled Data via Unified Flow with Feature Memory Bank](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16681)
*Shanwen Wang,Xin Sun,Danfeng Hong,Junyu Dong,Patrick Le Callet*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出UFFM，一种用于遥感半监督语义分割的新方法，通过统一流（UF）结合外部视觉基础模型与RS域教师生成更少偏置的伪标签，并引入特征记忆库（FMB）动态更新类特定特征以减少标记与未标记数据间的特征差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半监督语义分割（S^4）中，标记数据和未标记数据的独立训练会导致标记数据占据主导地位，严重降低伪标签质量，影响分割性能。

Method: UFFM包含两个核心创新：(1) 统一流（UF），一种新的训练流程，结合外部视觉基础模型（VFM）与RS域教师生成偏差更小的伪标签，并在统一训练目标下联合优化标记和伪标记数据；(2) 特征记忆库（FMB），一种新的记忆模块，在训练中动态更新类特定特征，通过类特征对齐减少标记与未标记数据之间的特征差异。

Result: 在遥感数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法优于SOTA半监督语义分割方法，并验证了其在弥合标记与未标记数据之间优化和特征表示差距方面的有效性。代码已开源。

Conclusion: UFFM通过统一训练流程和特征记忆库有效解决了半监督语义分割中标记数据主导和伪标签质量退化的问题，在遥感数据集上取得了SOTA性能。

Abstract: Although semi-supervised semantic segmentation ($\text{S}^4$) utilizes abundant unlabeled data to reduce manual labeling burdens, independent training of labeled and unlabeled data causes the former to dominate, which severely degrades pseudo-label quality. To address this challenges, we propose a novel remote sensing (RS) $\text{S}^4$ method via unified flow with feature memory bank (UFFM). Specifically, UFFM comprises two key innovations: unified flow (UF) and feature memory bank (FMB). The UF is a new training flow that generates less biased pseudo-labels by combining an external visual foundation model (VFM) with an RS domain teacher, and jointly optimizes labeled and pseudo-labeled data under a unified training objective. The FMB is a novel memory module for $\text{S}^4$ that dynamically updates class-specific features during training and reduces the feature discrepancy between labeled and unlabeled data through class-feature alignment. To verify the effectiveness of our model, we conduct extensive experiments on RS datasets. The experimental results show the superiority of our method over SOTA $\text{S}^4$ methods. Moreover, the results demonstrate the effectiveness of our contributions in bridging the optimization and feature representation gap between labeled and unlabeled data. Our code is released at \href{https://github.com/wangshanwen001/RS-UFFM}{https://github.com/wangshanwen001/RS-UFFM}.

</details>


### [199] [AnchorScore: A CLIP-Based Diagnostic of MLLM Annotation Difficulty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16690)
*Yan Ma,Lizhuo Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AnchorScore，一种基于冻结CLIP零样本精度的低成本先验信号，用于在部署前预测MLLM按类标注的难度排序。在SCB5和Stanford40上验证了与MLLM各类别准确率的显著相关性（rho≈0.77-0.82），并展示了混合CLIP/MLLM路由（+23pp，节省44%成本）等三个实际应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM在自动标注中按类别准确率差异巨大（12%-98%），但评估这些准确率成本极高（27B MLLM在5416张验证图上需约14小时，而冻结CLIP仅需3分钟）。需要一个低成本信号预先对类别按MLLM标注难度排序，以决定将昂贵的MLLM评估资源投向最值得的类别。

Method: 系统评估AnchorProxy（每类零样本CLIP准确率）的全帧形式AnchorScore作为先验难度诊断器。在SCB5（13类、6个MLLM）上验证相关性与显著性，对比DINOv2、ResNet-50、SigLIP和MLLM自述不确定性等替代方案，并在Stanford40上独立复现。同时提出三项应用：混合CLIP/MLLM路由策略、困难类提示消歧、人工审核优先级预测。

Result: AnchorScore与MLLM各类准确率显著相关（SCB5：rho=0.769, p=0.002；Stanford40：rho=0.817, p<0.001），在活动识别数据上最强，在医学和卫星影像上衰减。替代难度预测器在n=13下均无显著相关。路由策略最高提升23个百分点并节省约44%的MLLM计算成本。

Conclusion: AnchorScore不估计精确的MLLM准确率，而是提供低成本的类别难度排序信号，引导昂贵的MLLM评估聚焦于信息量最大的类别。跨模型共识控制表明其捕获的是共享的类别难度因子而非CLIP特有信号。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are widely used for automated annotation, yet their per-class accuracy varies widely (e.g., 12%-98% across the 13 classes of three classroom sub-datasets) and is expensive to measure: evaluating one 27B MLLM on 5,416 validation images takes roughly 14 hours, whereas a frozen-CLIP pass over the same images completes in about 3 minutes. A low-cost signal for ranking classes by expected MLLM annotation difficulty a priori remains underexplored. Building on the AnchorProxy construct (per-class zero-shot CLIP accuracy) introduced in the companion study, this paper systematically evaluates its full-frame formulation, termed AnchorScore here, as an a priori diagnostic that flags the classes MLLMs are least likely to annotate reliably.
  On classroom behavior data (SCB5, 13 classes, 6 MLLMs), AnchorScore correlates with per-class MLLM accuracy (Spearman rho = 0.769, p = 0.002, n = 13). None of the alternative difficulty predictors (DINOv2, ResNet-50, SigLIP, or MLLM self-verbalized uncertainty) showed a significant class-level correlation at n = 13. A cross-model consensus control suggests AnchorScore primarily captures a shared class-difficulty factor rather than a CLIP-specific signal. An independent replication on Stanford40 Actions yields a nearly identical effect (rho = 0.817, p < 0.001); the association is strongest on activity-recognition data and attenuates on medical and satellite imagery.
  Three practical applications follow: a deployable hybrid CLIP/MLLM routing strategy (predicted-class routing: up to +23 pp over CLIP-only at roughly 44% MLLM cost savings), prompt disambiguation on hard classes (exploratory), and review-priority prediction for human verification. AnchorScore does not estimate exact MLLM accuracy; it provides a low-cost ranking signal that directs expensive MLLM evaluation to the classes where it is most informative.

</details>


### [200] [MIRROR: Multimodal Intelligent Radiology Reasoning and Observation Reporter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16709)
*Vignesh Nagarajan,Sriram Venkatapathy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MIRROR是一个研究原型系统，通过链式结构（多标签分类器→Grad-CAM定位器→报告生成器）将医学影像发现的可归因性与报告文本生成分离，确保语言层无法看到像素从而不能编造分类器未做出的发现。系统验证了在ChestMNIST上的判别能力（macro AUROC 0.729）但在默认阈值下大量标签无正预测，并指出放射学类别不平衡下聚合指标会粉饰"不做事的模型"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放射科医生阅读模型输出时面临两个问题：模型只返回数字而没有解释；将数字转为文本的任意系统可能悄悄加入模型从未做出的断言。作者希望将这两种失败分离，使报告中的发现可审计、可追溯。

Method: MIRROR链式架构：(1) 多标签分类器输出概率向量；(2) Grad-CAM定位器将每个阳性发现映射为命名解剖区域；(3) 报告编写器仅接收标签、概率和区域信息，不接触图像。一个注册表统一管理胸部X光、脑MRI、头CT的分类学、解剖和措辞，新增模态只需更改数据。

Result: 在ChestMNIST上，分类器达到macro AUROC 0.729，全部14个标签均优于随机排名，精度是随机排名的1.6至6.8倍。但在默认0.5阈值下11个标签无任何正预测；Brier score 0.045与忽略图像的预测器0.047几乎无差别。作者展示了一个声称系统从未测量的心胸比率的句子，说明文本层的编造风险。

Conclusion: 判别能力是真实的，但决策输出并不可靠。在放射学典型的类别不平衡下，聚合指标会粉饰完全不做事的模型，应将这些指标与"什么都不预测的基线"对照报告。

Abstract: A radiologist reading a model's output faces two problems. The model returns a number and no reason, and any system that turns that number into readable prose can quietly add claims the model never made. MIRROR is a research prototype built to separate those failures. It chains a multi-label classifier, a Grad-CAM localizer that turns each positive finding into a named anatomical region, and a report writer that receives the labels, probabilities, and regions but never the image. Because the language layer cannot see pixels, it cannot assert a finding the classifier did not make. We are precise about what that buys: a MIRROR report's findings are auditable against the probability vector, while the sentences framing them are ordinary generated text, and we show one stating a cardiothoracic ratio the system never measured. One registry holds the taxonomy, anatomy, and phrasing for chest X-ray, brain MRI, and head CT, so adding a modality is a data change; all three are routed and tested, one is trained. On ChestMNIST that classifier reaches macro AUROC 0.729 and ranks better than chance on all 14 labels, at 1.6 to 6.8 times the precision a random ranker would get. Yet at the default 0.5 threshold it emits no positive prediction at all for 11 of them, and its excellent-looking Brier score of 0.045 sits beside the 0.047 earned by a predictor that ignores the image. The discrimination is real; the decisions are not. Under the class imbalance normal in radiology, aggregate metrics flatter models that do nothing, and should be reported against that floor.

</details>


### [201] [PersonaShot: Benchmarking Person-Centric Narrative Continuity in Multi-Shot Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16717)
*Yuji Wang,Yuheng Chen,Teng Hu,Ran Yi,Yijia Hong,Han Feng,Weijian Cao,Chengjie Wang,Lizhuang Ma,Jiangning Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PersonaShot 是首个以人物为中心、评估多镜头视频生成中叙事连续性的基准，包含约1000个多镜头片段和16个指标，覆盖物理连续性、情感动态和电影语法，并通过轻量级专用评估器蒸馏大模型推理能力，实现对跨镜头人物一致性的系统性评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成基准主要评估单镜头质量或角色外观，无法衡量跨镜头切换时人物物理状态、情感状态及电影关系的连贯性，且缺乏针对不同证据类型（视觉、时间、关系）的准则化评估方法。

Method: 构建PersonaShot基准：1) 从三个时间层级（镜头内状态、跨镜头转换、序列级轨迹）评估人物连续性；2) 将大多模态教师模型的推理能力蒸馏为轻量级准则专用评估器，每个评估器基于对应指标所需的视觉、时间或关系证据，并与人类专家判断对齐；3) 对SOTA视频生成模型进行系统评估。

Result: 评估揭示不同SOTA模型呈现不同的能力画像，且感知质量与跨镜头叙事连续性之间存在明显差距。即使视觉上令人信服的视频也频繁出现物理状态重置、情感突变和跨镜头电影关系断裂。人类研究证实评估器与专家判断高度一致。

Conclusion: 视频生成在单镜头视觉质量上已较成熟，但跨镜头叙事连续性仍是大规模短板；PersonaShot提供了一套人类对齐的准则化评估工具，为多镜头视频生成的持续改进建立了可测量的标准。

Abstract: Video generation is rapidly evolving from single-shot clips to multi-shot narratives, where the human character serves as the core narrative anchor. However, existing benchmarks mainly assess character appearance or individual-shot quality, without measuring whether physical and emotional states remain coherent across cuts. They also rarely provide criterion-specific evaluation methods, although physical continuity, facial dynamics, and cinematic relations require different visual, temporal, and relational evidence. To address these limitations, we introduce PersonaShot, the first person-centric benchmark for narrative continuity in multi-shot video generation. PersonaShot contains approximately 1,000 multi-shot segments and 16 metrics spanning physical continuity, affective dynamics, and cinematic grammar. \textbf{\textit{1)} Narrative Continuity Benchmark:} We evaluate character coherence across three temporal levels: within-shot states, cross-shot transitions, and sequence-level trajectories. \textbf{\textit{2)} Human-Aligned Specialist Evaluators:} We distill reasoning from a large multimodal teacher into lightweight criterion-specific evaluators, each grounded in the visual, temporal, or relational evidence required by its metric, and align them with expert human judgments. \textbf{\textit{3)} Systematic Evaluation and Insights:} Our evaluation reveals distinct capability profiles across state-of-the-art models and a clear gap between perceptual quality and cross-shot narrative continuity. Even visually compelling videos frequently exhibit physical-state resets, abrupt affective shifts, and broken cinematic relations across shots. Human studies further demonstrate strong agreement between our evaluators and expert judgments.

</details>


### [202] [CytoFormer: A Molecularly Supervised Cell Foundation Model for Histopathology Cell Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16718)
*Jialu Yao,Songhao Li,Alina Yu,Zhi Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CytoFormer，一个利用成像型空间转录组学（Xenium）与H&E组织学配对数据训练的细胞基础模型。通过81张切片、16个器官、1540万个细胞建立形态-分子标签配对，采用多任务、按器官分类头，在空间留出组织上达到0.85准确率和0.78宏F1，表征可迁移且具有标签高效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接从常规H&E组织学中识别细胞类型可规模化实现单细胞分析，但现有模型依赖病理学家人工标注，成本高、速度慢、对许多细胞类型不可靠。作者希望用分子数据替代人工标注来监督形态学特征，打破标注瓶颈。

Method: 收集81张配对Xenium+HE切片，覆盖16个器官；通过聚类、标记基因注释、人工审查和质量控制获得逐细胞标签，映射到23种常见细胞类型；训练CytoFormer——带有多任务、按器官分类头的细胞基础模型。评估包括空间留出验证、特征迁移（冻结编码器+线性头）、以及主动学习标签效率。

Result: 空间留出组织上准确率0.85、宏F1 0.78；冻结编码器的线性头在4个专家标注基准上超越6个病理基础模型，包括未见器官和细胞类型；主动学习中F1达0.82，领先最强基线0.13。

Conclusion: CytoFormer将配对H&E和空间转录组转化为可复用、标签高效的细胞级分析表示，为常规组织学的单细胞分析提供了可扩展的路径。

Abstract: Identifying cell types directly from routine haematoxylin and eosin (H&E) histology would enable single-cell analysis at scale, but training such models has relied on manual pathologist annotations, which are slow, expensive and unreliable for many cell types. We instead supervise morphology with molecules. Imaging-based spatial transcriptomics profiles individual cells in situ on a section that can afterwards be stained with H&E, so that molecular identity and morphology are observed for the same physical cell. We assembled 81 such paired Xenium sections spanning 16 organs, derived per-cell labels by clustering, marker-gene annotation, organ-wise human review and quality control, and mapped them onto the cell types commonly reported in each organ. This yielded 15.4 million cells, each with a paired H&E image patch and one of 23 cell types, on which we trained CytoFormer, a cell foundation model with a multi-task, per-organ classification head. On spatially held-out tissue CytoFormer reached an accuracy of 0.85 and a macro-F1 of 0.78 across all 16 organs, and its predictions reproduced the tissue architecture of an entire held-out section. The representation also transfers: with the encoder frozen, a linear head on CytoFormer features performed better than six pathology foundation models on four expert-annotated benchmarks, including on organs and cell types that were not part of pretraining. Finally, in an interactive active-learning setting, CytoFormer's embeddings are markedly more label-efficient than existing pathology foundation models, detecting normal epithelium amid look-alike tumour with an F1 of 0.82 from only a few annotations and leading the strongest baseline by 0.13 in F1. CytoFormer turns paired H&E and spatial transcriptomics into a reusable, label-efficient representation for cell-level analysis of routine histology.

</details>


### [203] [GenRouter: Unified Workflow Routing for Agentic Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16721)
*Harold Haodong Chen,Zhiyu Hou,Wen-Jie Shu,Weilin Ruan,Yingjie Xu,Litao Guo,Ying-Cong Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GenRouter是首个面向智能体图像生成（agentic image generation）的统一工作流路由框架。它通过标准化的GenCanvas将异构智能体管线抽象为基础原语和可执行模板，并基于需求分析、经验匹配和Pareto过滤将不同提示自适应路由到最优工作流，在保证视觉对齐的同时大幅降低计算开销和延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2I生成模型已解决像素合成的基础问题，社区转向复杂的多步骤智能体式图像生成工作流。然而这些工作流多以固定"一刀切"拓扑孤立运行，导致严重的计算不匹配——简单查询也被迫经过高计算量管线。

Method: 提出统一工作流路由框架GenRouter：1）设计GenCanvas将多样化智能体管线标准化为通用基础原语和可执行模板；2）在此统一空间上通过需求分析（demand profiling）、经验匹配（experience matching）和Pareto过滤（Pareto filtering）三个机制将异构提示自适应路由到最优工作流；3）通过累积经验实现持续自我进化。

Result: 在多种基准上，与重型静态管线相比，GenRouter在保持优越视觉对齐的同时减少超过95%的执行成本和65%的延迟；系统通过累积经验自我进化，实现稳健的零样本泛化，进一步提升性能并将计算开销减半。

Conclusion: GenRouter证明了按需路由在智能体图像生成中的巨大价值，通过统一抽象和自适应路由解决计算不匹配问题，并展示了一种可自我进化的通用工作流路由范式。

Abstract: The rapid evolution of text-to-image (T2I) generation models has effectively solved the foundational challenge of raw pixel synthesis, shifting the community's focus toward fulfilling increasingly intricate user requests. While recent agentic image generation workflows enhance static inference with advanced capabilities like external knowledge retrieval and iterative reasoning, they mostly operate in isolated silos with fixed ``one-size-fits-all" topologies. This inevitably leads to severe compute-mismatch, where simple queries are forced through computationally heavy pipelines. To bridge this gap, we present GenRouter, the first unified workflow routing framework for agentic image generation. We first formulate GenCanvas, standardizing diverse agentic pipelines into a universal set of foundational primitives and executable templates. Operating over this unified space, GenRouter adaptively routes heterogeneous prompts to their optimal workflows via (i) demand profiling, (ii) experience matching, and (iii) Pareto filtering. Extensive experiments across diverse benchmarks demonstrate that GenRouter achieves superior visual alignment while reducing execution costs by over 95% and latency by 65% compared to heavyweight static pipelines. Furthermore, the system continuously self-evolves via accumulated experience, enabling robust zero-shot generalization that boosts performance and halves computational overhead.

</details>


### [204] [Unsupervised Anomaly Detection for Image Dataset Quality Assurance in Multi-Center Breast MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16725)
*Chiara Tappermann,Steffen Renisch,Lars Ole Schwen,Hans Meine,Horst K. Hahn,Eike Petersen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个面向多中心动态增强乳腺MRI的无监督异常检测（AD）与分布外（OOD）检测基准，涵盖17种真实QA相关异常类型，构建了包含近OOD、中远OOD、远OOD及外部正常数据的控制性基准，并评估了四种方法（投影法、3D重建法及两种混合OOD检测法）。结果显示投影法+位置编码取得最高AUROC 0.954，3D重建法在平衡性能（0.936）和跨机构泛化上最优，混合方法存在关键失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗AI中损坏、不一致或异常数据会威胁安全性和可靠性，虽然监管日益认可高风险医疗AI的数据集质量保证（QA），但可扩展的自动化检测方法仍然不足。作者旨在填补这一空白，为医疗AI管道提供可扩展的无监督QA机制。

Method: 构建17种QA相关异常类型的受控AD基准（协议违规、处理错误、解剖区域错误），基于人类视觉感知提出放射学图像异常分类体系，用于细粒度分析AD失败模式。基准包含近/中远/远OOD样本、分布内及外部正常数据。评估四种方法：扩展域特征提取器和新型位置编码的投影法、扩展至全3D体积并带增强训练目标的重建法、以及两种未修改的混合OOD检测方法。

Result: 中远OOD和远OOD样本能被可靠检测；近OOD和外部正常数据暴露方法间差异。3D重建法最佳平衡检测性能（AUROC 0.936）与跨机构泛化；投影法+位置编码取得最高总体检测性能（AUROC 0.954）。两种混合方法存在关键失败模式，证实跨模态/解剖验证的方法需域特定适配。植入物和乳房切除术后仍然是所有方法的开放挑战。

Conclusion: 该研究为医疗AI管道中可扩展的无监督QA建立了基础和实用指南，强调域特定适配的重要性，并指出植入物和乳房切除术后等特定挑战仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: Corrupted, inconsistent, or anomalous data silently threatens the safety and reliability of medical AI. Despite growing regulatory recognition of dataset quality assurance (QA) for high-risk medical AI, scalable automated detection remains underdeveloped. We employ unsupervised anomaly detection (AD) and out-of-distribution (OOD) detection as an automated dataset QA mechanism for multi-center dynamic contrast-enhanced breast MRI.
  We build a controlled AD benchmark of 17 realistic QA-relevant anomaly types from six public datasets (protocol violations, processing errors, incorrect anatomical regions) and propose a taxonomy of radiological image anomalies based on human visual perception, enabling fine-grained analysis of AD failure modes. The benchmark includes near-, medium-far-, far-OOD samples, as well as in-distribution and external normal data. Four methods are evaluated: a projection-based method extended with a domain-specific feature extractor and a novel positional encoding, a reconstruction-based approach extended to full 3D volumes with an augmented training objective, and two unmodified hybrid OOD detection methods.
  Medium-far- and far-OOD samples are detected reliably, whereas near-OOD samples and external normal data from unseen institutions expose method-specific differences. The 3D reconstruction-based approach best balances detection performance (AUROC: 0.936) and generalization to unseen institutions. The projection-based method with positional encoding achieves the highest overall detection performance (AUROC: 0.954). Both hybrid methods exhibit critical failure modes, confirming that methods validated for one modality or anatomy may not generalize without domain-specific adaptation. Implants and mastectomies remain an open challenge for all methods. Our results establish a foundation and practical guidance on scalable unsupervised QA in medical AI pipelines.

</details>


### [205] [VicEdit: Learning to Edit Videos from Visual In-Context Examples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16745)
*Yuji Wang,Teng Hu,Yuheng Chen,Ran Yi,Han Feng,Weijian Cao,Chengjie Wang,Lizhuang Ma,Jiangning Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出视觉上下文编辑（Visual In-context Editing）新范式，将视频编辑从纯文本指令提升到多模态视觉引导（单图、图像对、视频对），构建了首个大规模数据集VicEdit-400K（含10种任务类型、40万高质量样本），并提出统一框架VicEdit，通过Modality-Adaptive Semantic Distillation从异构视觉参考中提取语义令牌，再经Dual-Context Injection与文本指令协同注入生成过程，在VicEditBench上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯文本指令在视频编辑中难以传达细粒度的纹理和复杂的动态信息，存在感知鸿沟。为桥接这一差距，需要引入多模态视觉引导来增强编辑的精确性和可控性。

Method: 1) 构建VicEdit-400K数据集，通过自动化流水线生成40万高质量样本，覆盖10种任务类型，并采用多维过滤确保视觉保真度和语义一致性；2) 提出VicEdit统一框架，包含Modality-Adaptive Semantic Distillation（从异构视觉参考中生成模态特定语义令牌）和Dual-Context Injection（将视觉与文本信号协同注入生成过程）。

Result: 在VicEditBench上的大量评估表明，VicEdit在基础指令编辑和视觉上下文编辑任务中均达到SOTA性能，验证了视觉上下文学习作为视频编辑的强大可控范式的有效性。

Conclusion: 视觉上下文学习是一种有效的视频编辑新范式，多模态视觉引导能够弥补纯文本指令的不足，实现更精细、更可控的视频编辑。

Abstract: Despite progress in instruction-based video editing, unimodal textual instructions inherently struggle to convey fine-grained textures and complex dynamics. To bridge this perceptual gap, we propose Visual In-context Editing, a new paradigm elevating video editing from textual instructions to multi-modal visual guidance encompassing single image, image pair, and video pair. To facilitate this paradigm, we curate VicEdit-400K, the first large-scale dataset for visual in-context video editing. We develop an automated pipeline to generate 400K high-quality samples across ten task types, ensuring superior visual fidelity and semantic consistency through multi-dimensional filtering. Leveraging this foundation, we introduce VicEdit, a unified framework to bridge visual and textual contexts. To adaptively extract editing semantics from heterogeneous references, we design Modality-Adaptive Semantic Distillation, which produces modality-specific semantic tokens from visual references. These tokens are then synergistically integrated with textual instructions through Dual-Context Injection, enabling the generation process to benefit from both visual and textual signals. Extensive evaluations on VicEditBench demonstrate that VicEdit achieves state-of-the-art performance across both basic instruction editing and visual in-context editing tasks, establishing visual in-context learning as a powerful and controllable paradigm for video editing.

</details>


### [206] [Beyond Uncertainty: Generalizable Failure Monitoring for Surgical Segmentation under Acquisition Degradation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16748)
*Hieu D. Pham,Dang P. M. Cao,Thanh Trung Huynh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TCSR-Monitor是一个外科手术分割网络的部署期失败监控框架，结合置信度、形状、时间一致性和图像质量等互补信号，在不依赖真实标签和模型内部信息的情况下检测模型静默失败。实验表明该方法在未知采集退化上优于纯置信度基线，但存在较高的误报率且零样本迁移到SAM2时不及熵基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外科手术分割网络在采集退化下可能静默失败——预测掩码错误但模型置信度仍然很高。现有的部署期监控主要依赖不确定性估计，因此会漏检这类"自信的失败"。需要一种不依赖模型内部结构、无需真实标签的互补监控方案。

Method: TCSR-Monitor采用事后监控框架，在冻结的分割模型外层包裹，融合置信度、可观测形状特征、时间一致性特征和图像质量线索四种信号。使用Mondrian conformal calibration来平衡不同退化严重程度下的漏检率（miss-rate），并设计了验证协议和循环性控制（circularity control）来评估告警在分布偏移下的可信度，确保检测的是分割失败而非单纯图像损坏。

Result: 在EndoVis 2017上的leave-one-corruption-out实验中，TCSR-Monitor能泛化到未见过的采集退化，显著优于置信度基线。循环性控制确认其预测的是分割失败而非图像损坏。但单个全局阈值在中度退化下会对高达40%的正确分割帧产生误报。零样本迁移到SAM2展示了特征可迁移性，但在两个评估阈值下熵基线均优于迁移后的监控器。

Conclusion: 可靠的部署期监控受益于超越置信度之外的互补可观测信号，但误报率和跨模型迁移方面仍存在显著局限。多种信号融合是正确方向，但需要更精细的阈值校准和跨架构泛化策略。

Abstract: Surgical segmentation networks can fail silently under acquisition degradation: predicted masks may be wrong even when model confidence remains high. Existing deployment-time monitors rely primarily on uncertainty estimates and can therefore miss confident failures. We present TCSR-Monitor (Temporal Conformal Surgical Risk Monitor), a post-hoc failure-monitoring framework that combines confidence with observable shape, temporal-consistency, and image-quality cues. TCSR-Monitor wraps a frozen segmentation model, requires no model internals, and operates without ground truth at deployment. We also introduce a validation protocol to assess whether alarms remain credible under distribution shift. On EndoVis 2017, leave-one-corruption-out evaluation shows that TCSR-Monitor generalizes to unseen acquisition degradations and substantially outperforms confidence-based baselines. A circularity control confirms that it predicts segmentation failure rather than simply detecting corrupted images. Mondrian conformal calibration balances miss-rates across degradation severities, but a single global threshold still produces false alarms on up to 40% of correctly segmented frames at moderate corruption. Zero-shot transfer to SAM2 demonstrates feature portability, although entropy outperforms the transferred monitor at both evaluated thresholds. Overall, reliable monitoring under acquisition degradation benefits from complementary observable signals beyond confidence alone, but substantial false-alarm and transfer limitations remain.

</details>


### [207] [Binarized High-Efficiency RAW Video Restoration and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16756)
*Tianyu Zhu,Ying Fu,Hesong Li,Gengchen Zhang,Xin Yuan,Yulun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BinRVR是一个二值化RAW视频修复框架，通过BIIM模块联合建模时空信息、DAB-Conv利用全精度激活统计缓解量化误差，将计算量和参数减少约96%，性能仅下降约4%，支持多比特量化以灵活权衡精度与效率，在低光增强、去噪、去模糊和超分辨率等RAW视频修复任务及下游应用中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAW视频修复是高质量低层感知的基础，但二值神经网络在视频场景下存在时序建模能力不足和激活值分布建模缺陷，导致其在视频修复任务中效果受限。作者旨在解决BNN在RAW视频修复中效率与性能的矛盾。

Method: 提出二值化RAW视频修复框架BinRVR，包含两个核心组件：(1) Binarized Information Interaction Module (BIIM)用于高效统一地联合建模空间与时间信息；(2) Distribution-Aware Binarized Convolution (DAB-Conv)利用全精度激活的统计信息来缓解二值化量化误差。框架支持多比特量化，可实现跨硬件约束的灵活精度-效率权衡。

Result: 在RAW视频修复任务（低光增强、去噪、去模糊、超分辨率）上达到与SOTA二值化方法竞争的性能，计算量和参数减少约96%，性能仅下降约4%。下游任务（目标检测、单目深度估计）也验证了方法的泛化能力。

Conclusion: BinRVR展示了二值化网络在RAW视频修复中的可行性和高效性，为移动端/边缘设备上的视频质量增强提供了高效解决方案，并验证了其在多种下游视觉应用中的实用性。

Abstract: RAW video restoration is fundamental to high-quality low-level perception and serves as the basis for a wide range of downstream vision applications. While binary neural networks (BNNs) enable efficient lightweight deployment for image enhancement, their deficiencies in modeling temporal coherence and activation value distributions hinder their effectiveness when applied to video scenarios. In this paper, we propose BinRVR, a binarized RAW video restoration framework that reduces computation and parameters by approximately 96% while incurring only about 4% performance degradation. Specifically, we present a Binarized Information Interaction Module (BIIM) to jointly model spatial and temporal information in an efficient and unified manner. Moreover, we develop a Distribution-Aware Binarized Convolution (DAB-Conv) that leverages the statistics of full-precision activations to mitigate quantization errors. The proposed framework further supports multi-bit quantization, enabling flexible accuracy-efficiency trade-offs across different hardware constraints. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our BinRVR achieves competitive performance compared with state-of-the-art binarized methods on RAW video restoration tasks, including low-light enhancement, denoising, deblurring, and super-resolution. We further explore the potential of our method on downstream video applications, including object detection and monocular depth estimation.

</details>


### [208] [TRACE-Bench: Decomposing and Diagnosing Multi-Reference Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16765)
*Haoran Wang,Chaofan Ma,Ran Yi,Lizhuang Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TRACE-Bench，一个基于原子操作符（Anchor、Disentangle、Apply、Compose）的面向多参考图像生成的组合式评测基准，包含约1600个评测案例（slot数1-8），通过操作符对齐的评分协议和诊断树分析对9个领先模型进行细粒度评估，发现主要瓶颈在于解耦和属性绑定而非场景级组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对多参考图像生成的统一多模态模型评测基准围绕预定义任务类型组织，无法覆盖组合性设置，导致覆盖碎片化、复杂度不可控、诊断价值低。作者认为多样化的多参考任务共享一组原子操作，需要一种基于能力的评测视角。

Method: 提出四个原子操作符：Anchor（锚定）、Disentangle（解耦）、Apply（施加）、Compose（组合），任意多参考提示可表示为这些操作符的组合公式，结构复杂度由操作符槽位数量量化。据此构建TRACE-Bench，含约1600个评测案例（槽位数1-8）、631个公式模板、约4000张参考图像。公式结构驱动操作符对齐的逐能力评分协议和递归失败定位的诊断树分析。

Result: 评估9个领先模型发现：主要瓶颈在于解耦（g）和属性绑定（⊕）而非场景级组合（C），即便最佳模型在属性保真度上仅得0.74分。这些发现是整体评分所不可见的。

Conclusion: 基于操作符的组合式评测框架能够揭示传统整体评分无法发现的细粒度模型缺陷，为多参考图像生成模型的诊断和改进提供了更具洞察力的评测工具。

Abstract: Despite recent advances in unified multimodal models for multi-reference image generation, existing benchmarks remain organized around predefined task types (e.g., "subject composition"), which are ill-suited to this combinatorial setting and lead to fragmented coverage, uncontrolled complexity, and little diagnostic value. Recognizing that diverse multi-reference tasks share a common set of atomic operations, we adopt a capability-oriented perspective and formalize four operators: Anchor ($f$), Disentangle ($g$), Apply ($\oplus$), and Compose ($C$). Any multi-reference prompt can then be represented as a compositional formula over these operators, whose structural complexity is quantified by the number of operator slots. Building on this formulation, we construct TRACE-Bench, comprising approximately 1,600 evaluation cases across slot counts 1--8, built from 631 formula templates and around 4,000 reference images spanning diverse artistic styles and real-world subjects. The formula structure directly drives an operator-aligned evaluation protocol for per-capability scoring and a diagnostic tree analysis for recursive failure localization. Evaluating 9 leading models reveals insights invisible to holistic scoring: the primary bottleneck lies in disentanglement ($g$) and attribute binding ($\oplus$) rather than scene-level composition ($C$), with even the best model scoring only 0.74 on attribute fidelity. Project page: https://amuseum-whr.github.io/TraceBench

</details>


### [209] [Calibration-Free Vehicle Speed Estimation: A Monocular Keypoint-Template Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16785)
*Gaofeng Su,Keya Li,Raja Sengupta,Kara M. Kockelman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个免标定的单目视频车速估计框架，使用36关键点车辆模板和逐帧更新的单应矩阵将像素位移投影到度量空间，比较关键点跟踪与warped光流两种策略，在VS13和BrnoCompSpeed数据集上验证，MAE分别达15.0%/9.7%（经10%裁剪后降至11.7%/7.6%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉的车速估计方法依赖道路特征、相机标定或参考物体，限制了便携设备（行车记录仪、智能手机）在公民执法场景中的部署。需要一个无需标定、低成本且可靠的车速估计框架。

Method: 1) 使用YOLO-based关键点检测模块，在多样化数据集上训练，提取36关键点车辆模板；2) 提出两种估计策略：纯关键点跟踪与warped光流+密集空间聚合；3) 利用逐帧更新的单应矩阵将像素域位移投影到公制空间计算车速；4) 通过10%裁剪去除边缘异常值提升鲁棒性。

Result: 在VS13和BrnoCompSpeed数据集上，warped光流方法MAE分别为15.0%和9.7%，±20%误差内占比分别为77.9%和93.1%；经10%裁剪后MAE降至11.7%和7.6%，±20%精度提升至85.3%和95.4%。数据集覆盖30-100 mph，超过400个视频片段。

Conclusion: 该框架解决了现有视觉车速估计对标定和道路特征依赖的局限，为行车记录仪和智能手机等便携设备实现低成本高效测速提供了可行方案，支持公民参与式交通执法。

Abstract: This paper proposes a calibration-free framework for reliably and effectively estimating vehicle speeds from monocular videos, without relying on roadway features, camera calibration, or roadway-feature-based reference objects. The proposed framework estimates vehicle speeds using a 36-keypoint vehicle template and a homography matrix updated at each frame. A YOLO-based keypoint detection module is trained on diverse datasets, and two estimation strategies are compared: keypoint-only tracking and warped optical flow with dense spatial aggregation. Speed is estimated by projecting displacements into metric space using the homography, with validation conducted on over 400 video clips from roadside and overhead datasets, covering speeds from 30 to 100 mph. The method achieves reliable speed estimation on the VS13 and BrnoCompSpeed datasets, with the warped optical flow method delivering MAEs of 15.0% and 9.7%, respectively, and 77.9% and 93.1% of estimates falling within +/-20% error. After applying a 10% trim to remove edge-of-frame outliers, performance improves to MAEs of 11.7% and 7.6%, with within-+/-20% accuracy increasing to 85.3% and 95.4%. This work addresses key limitations of existing vision-based approaches and enables low-cost and efficient speed enforcement using portable devices such as dashcams and smartphones, thereby supporting citizen-based enforcement programs for traffic safety.

</details>


### [210] [Revisiting Classifier-Free Guidance Methods in Latent Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16786)
*Artem Sergievskii,Artyom Turevich,Sergey Kastryulin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文重新评估了八种基于Classifier-Free Guidance (CFG)概念的训练free推理时质量增强方法，在两类开权重rectified-flow transformer模型和三个组合对齐基准上进行测试。结果表明没有任何方法在所有标准上持续优于CFG，APG虽有名义最佳但增益常在评估不确定度内，CFG仍是性价比高的基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多推理时增强方法最初在较旧的U-Net扩散模型上提出，且评估指标只关注图像质量本身，忽略了生成的图像与其文本提示之间的组合对齐和语义对应。作者希望在现代rectified-flow transformer架构和组合对齐基准下重新审视这些方法的真实有效性。

Method: 在SD3.5 Medium和FLUX.2 [klein] 4B Base两个开权重rectified-flow transformer上，采用固定的逐模型协议，对八种训练free的CFG类推理时增强方法（包括APG、注意力扰动方法等）在三个组合对齐基准上进行系统比较评估。

Result: 没有任何方法在所有测量标准上一致优于CFG。APG获得若干名义最佳分数但增益多在评估不确定区间内；注意力扰动方法在SD3.5 Medium上有孤立增益，但在FLUX.2上更频繁出现性能退化；CFG仍是具有竞争力的低成本基线。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了许多先前验证有力的推理时增强方法在新型rectified-flow transformer架构和更严格的组合对齐基准下优势有限，提示社区需要更稳健的评估协议与更强的增强方法，同时CFG作为基线依然难以被简单超越。

Abstract: Inference-time quality-enhancement methods are an effective and widely adopted means of improving diffusion models without expensive retraining. We study a family of training-free techniques conceptually rooted in Classifier-Free Guidance (CFG), most of which were originally proposed on older U-Net diffusion models and validated using metrics that assess image quality in isolation, without accounting for compositional alignment or semantic correspondence between the generated image and its associated text prompt. We re-evaluate eight such methods on two open-weight rectified-flow transformers under a fixed per-model protocol and three compositional-alignment benchmarks. No method consistently improves on CFG across the measured criteria. APG obtains several nominal best scores, but the corresponding gains often remain within the estimated evaluation uncertainty. Attention-perturbation methods provide isolated gains on SD3.5 Medium and more frequent degradations on FLUX.2 [klein] 4B Base, while CFG remains a competitive lower-cost baseline.

</details>


### [211] [Steering the Flow: Inverting Face Recognition Models via Gradient-Guided Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16791)
*Ye Lu,Shen Wang,Zhaoyang Zhang,Yihan Yan,Li Liu,Runze Liu,Fanghui Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SFMI，一种两阶段白盒模型反演攻击方法，将人脸识别模型的训练样本重构问题重新表述为轨迹引导任务。第一阶段预训练通用Flow Matching模型作为人脸先验，第二阶段通过渐进式引导调度器（PGS）在采样过程中注入时间相关的目标特定梯度，将生成流从随机噪声逐步引导至目标类别的高密度区域。在CelebA数据集ArcFace目标上达到ACC 0.9248、FID 22.61、LPIPS 0.3874的SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型反演攻击（MIA）方法依赖间接或高度随机的引导信号，难以稳定地将生成轨迹优化到目标人脸图像。作者希望解决生成轨迹不稳定优化的问题，提高攻击成功率和视觉保真度。

Method: 两阶段方法：Step I预训练通用无条件的Flow Matching模型，编码人脸流形作为稳健先验；Step II利用渐进式引导调度器（PGS），通过目标模型反向传播获取中间生成状态的梯度，在采样过程中逐步注入时间相关的自适应引导信号，将流从随机噪声引导至目标类高密度区域。

Result: 在CelebA数据集、身份不相交的交叉评估设置下，SFMI在ArcFace目标上达到ACC 0.9248、FID 22.61、LPIPS 0.3874。在多个目标模型上的大量实验证明其在攻击成功率和视觉保真度方面达到竞争性的SOTA性能。

Conclusion: SFMI通过将模型反演重构为轨迹引导任务，利用Flow Matching先验和渐进式梯度引导，显著提升了白盒模型反演攻击的稳定性和成功率，展示了生成模型在安全攻击场景中的新应用。

Abstract: Model Inversion Attacks (MIAs) aim to reconstruct representative training samples of target identities from face recognition models, exposing critical security vulnerabilities. Existing methods typically rely on indirect guidance or highly stochastic guidance, making it difficult to stably optimize generation trajectories toward target facial images. In this paper, we propose Steering Flow Model Inversion (SFMI), a novel two-stage white-box model inversion method that reformulates inversion as a trajectory-steering task. Specifically, Step I, Learning a Generic Flow Matching Prior, pre-trains a generic unconditional Flow Matching model to encode the manifold of human faces as a robust prior. Step II, Attacking with Progressive Guidance Scheduler (PGS), injects time-dependent target-specific gradients during sampling. By backpropagating through the target model to obtain gradients from intermediate generated states, PGS progressively injects adaptive guidance signals into the vector field. This process effectively steers the current generative flow from random noise toward the high-density regions of the target class. Under an identity-disjoint cross-evaluation setting using the CelebA dataset, SFMI achieves an ACC of 0.9248, an FID of 22.61, and an LPIPS of 0.3874 on the ArcFace target. Extensive experiments on multiple target models demonstrate that SFMI achieves competitive state-of-the-art performance in attack success and visual fidelity under the evaluated white-box protocol.

</details>


### [212] [PixRestore: Unified Image Restoration via Pixel Diffusion Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16793)
*Lingchen Sun,Rongyuan Wu,Xiangtao Kong,Jixin Zhao,Qiaosi Yi,Yujing Sun,Shuaizheng Liu,Zhengqiang Zhang,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PixRestore是一个VAE-free的像素空间扩散Transformer模型，用于统一图像修复（UIR），直接在patchified像素上进行flow matching，通过LQ-HQ DINO特征相似度预测层特征可靠性来实现自适应退化处理，仅约50M参数且单步推理即可在保真度、感知质量和鲁棒性上取得最优效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有UIR方法多依赖大规模预训练T2I潜在扩散模型，但存在两个关键问题：VAE可能丢弃修复敏感的细节信息，且开放式合成先验可能引入内容不一致的伪影。作者旨在解决这些问题，构建一个不依赖T2I预训练的高效统一图像修复框架。

Method: 1) 提出VAE-free的像素空间DiT架构，直接在patchified像素上执行flow matching，保留细粒度细节并控制token序列长度；2) 利用LQ-HQ DINO特征相似度预测各层特征的可靠性，可靠层特征融合为稠密条件，不可靠层则接受更强的HQ特征监督以促进退化去除；3) 在大规模多样退化语料上训练，并使用DINO对抗目标微调为单步生成器。

Result: 在公开基准和真实世界测试集上，仅约50M参数且单步推理即可在保真度、感知质量和退化鲁棒性方面超越现有UIR模型，同时效率更高；更大规模的变体可进一步提升性能，验证了像素空间设计的可扩展性。

Conclusion: PixRestore证明像素空间扩散架构可以在不依赖T2I预训练的情况下实现高效且高质量的统一图像修复，层可靠性自适应机制有效平衡了细节保留与退化去除，为UIR提供了一条轻量、高效、可扩展的新路线。

Abstract: Unified image restoration (UIR) aims to recover high-quality (HQ) content from low-quality (LQ) images with different degradations using a single model. Most recent methods adapt large pretrained text-to-image (T2I) latent diffusion models for their strong capacity and generative priors. However, the variational autoencoder (VAE) in latent T2I models may discard restoration-sensitive details, while the open-ended synthesis prior can introduce content-inconsistent artifacts. We present PixRestore, a VAE-free pixel-space Diffusion Transformer (DiT) for UIR, where the diffusion backbone is trained entirely from scratch, without relying on T2I pretraining. PixRestore performs flow matching directly on patchified pixels, preserving fine-grained details while keeping the token sequence tractable. To adapt to different degradations, PixRestore learns to predict the reliability of layer features using LQ--HQ DINO feature similarity. Features from more reliable layers are fused as dense conditioning, while less reliable layers receive stronger HQ-feature supervision to encourage degradation removal. We train PixRestore on a large-scale corpus of diverse scenes and degradations, and further finetune it into a one-step generator using DINO-based adversarial objectives for efficient inference. Experiments on public benchmarks and real-world test sets show that, with only about 50M parameters and single-step inference, PixRestore achieves the best overall fidelity, perceptual quality, and robustness to degradations among competing UIR models while being far more efficient. Larger PixRestore variants can further boost performance, demonstrating the scalability of our pixel-space design. Code and the curated benchmark can be found at https://github.com/csslc/PixRestore.

</details>


### [213] [Diagnosing Dense Same-Class Attribute Misbinding in Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16805)
*Yuanzhi Xu,Qian Gao,Jun Fan,Guohui Ding,Zhenyu Yang,Yuteng Xiao,Sixue Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文形式化定义了密集同类属性错绑（DSCAM）这一盲区：大型视觉语言模型能识别拥挤场景中的对象和属性，却把属性错误分配给同类的其他实例。作者提出InstaBind-Lite基准，包含524张图像、529组同类实体、1773个框选实例和9580个确定性评估问题，并通过源实例标注将“属性从另一可见实体复制”与其他失败类型区分开来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉问答准确率将错误响应标记为错误，而对象幻觉指标可能同时认为对象和属性都有图像支持，两者都无法揭示属性在同类实例间的错误转移这一系统性缺陷。

Method: 构建InstaBind-Lite基准：524张图像含529组3-6个同类实体、1773个框选实例、有序邻居、可区分颜色类属性、四个互补问题层级，共9580个确定性评估问题。通过源实例注释分离不支持生成、识别失败、以及从其他可见实体复制属性三类错误，并引入绑定特异性指标量化转移频率、邻接性、序数距离和干预效果。

Result: 五个开源模型平均错绑率19.84%，两个API系统平均7.55%；这些错误被聚合准确率掩盖。在可识别的转移中，开源与API模型分别有80.70%和81.51%来自相邻实例。定位和实例优先干预对部分模型有效，但并非通用解决方案。

Conclusion: InstaBind-Lite将以往无差异的错误答案转化为可溯源来源的失败类别，检验了常规基准无法确定的一个可靠性维度：模型不仅要知道看到了什么，还要知道哪个实例拥有该属性。

Abstract: Large vision-language models can recognize the objects and attributes in a crowded scene yet assign an attribute to the wrong same-class instance. Generic visual-question-answering accuracy marks the response as wrong, while object-hallucination metrics may regard both the object and attribute as image-supported; neither reveals the transfer. This study formalizes this blind spot as Dense Same-Class Attribute Misbinding (DSCAM) and presents InstaBind-Lite, a controlled benchmark that makes it directly measurable. Its 524 images contain 529 curated groups of 3-6 same-class entities, 1773 boxed instances, ordered neighbors, distinguishable color-like attributes, and four complementary question levels, yielding 9580 deterministically evaluated questions. Unlike existing protocols, source-instance annotations separate unsupported generation and recognition failure from an attribute copied from another visible entity. Binding-specific metrics further quantify transfer frequency, adjacency, ordinal distance, and intervention effects. Across five open-source and two commercial/API models, the open-source systems average 19.84% Misbinding Rate and the API systems 7.55%; these errors are hidden by aggregate accuracy. Among identifiable transfers, 80.70% and 81.51%, respectively, originate from adjacent instances. Localization and instance-first interventions help selected models but are not universal remedies. InstaBind-Lite therefore turns previously undifferentiated wrong answers into source-identifiable failure categories and tests a reliability dimension that conventional benchmarks cannot determine: whether a model knows not only what is visible, but which instance owns each attribute.

</details>


### [214] [Unsupervised Learning of Cell Instances with Generative Routing Pyramids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16810)
*Ziwen Liu,Martin Weigert*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无监督的细胞实例分割与表型分类方法，基于粗到细路由金字塔（coarse-to-fine routing pyramid）将图像重建为空间稀疏的潜在源，像素到潜在源的关联生成实例掩膜，潜在编码捕获细胞形态。在多种细胞形态和成像模态下展示了有竞争力的实例分割性能，并支持扰动下的细胞表型生成建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的细胞实例分割和表征通常依赖有监督检测/分割流程，需要大量人工标注，且将实例分割与细胞表征视为分离的两阶段。作者希望提出一种无需标注的端到端无监督方法，同时完成实例分割和形态编码。

Method: 基于粗到细路由金字塔的自编码器架构，用空间稀疏的潜在源重建图像。通过像素到潜在源的关联得到实例掩膜，潜在源本身编码细胞形态信息，实现无监督的实例分割与表征学习一体化。

Result: 在多种细胞形态和成像模态下实例分割性能具有竞争力，并能进行细胞表型的生成式建模（如在扰动条件下的细胞形态生成）。代码和模型权重已开源。

Conclusion: 证明了无监督路由金字塔方法可以替代传统的有监督实例分割+表征两阶段流程，在无需标注的情况下达到有竞争力的分割性能，并支持下游表型建模任务。

Abstract: Identifying and representing object instances such as cells or nuclei is a common task in microscopy image analysis. Established machine learning workflows typically use supervised detection or segmentation followed by feature extraction or classification, which requires manual annotations and treats instance segmentation and cell representation as separate stages. We describe a new unsupervised method for cell instance segmentation and phenotypic classification from unlabeled microscopy images. Our method is based on reconstructing each image using a coarse-to-fine routing pyramid that associates pixels with spatially sparse latent sources. The resulting pixel-to-latent associations yield instance masks, while the source latents encode cell morphology. We demonstrate competitive performance in instance segmentation across diverse cell morphologies and imaging modalities, as well as generative modeling of cellular phenotypes under perturbations. Source code and checkpoints are available at https://github.com/weigertlab/routing-pyramids.

</details>


### [215] [Unlocking the Potential of Image Editing via Concept Scaling and Dense Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16812)
*Long Cui,Xiaoqian Liu,Qi Qin,Yi Xin,Tao Lin,Jianguo Li,Linfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向图像编辑的分层精细概念分类体系（超过1000个编辑概念），构建了ConceptEdit-12M大规模数据集（1200万高质量编辑对），并提出密集监督训练策略以提升训练效率和模型性能，同时发布ConceptEdit-Bench细粒度评测基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图像编辑框架沿用文生图扩散模型的训练范式，但存在两个内在缺陷：对编辑概念粒度的关注不足，以及稀疏监督信号导致的训练效率低下。作者希望解决这些问题。

Method: 1) 建立包含超过1000个细粒度编辑概念的分层分类体系；2) 通过改进的合成框架构建ConceptEdit-12M大规模高质量编辑对数据集，纠正生成数据的分布坍塌并保证数据保真度；3) 提出密集监督训练策略，将多个不干扰的概念合成到单一图像对中，提供更丰富的学习信号提升训练效率；4) 发布ConceptEdit-Bench评测套件。

Result: 训练结果验证了所提出策略的有效性，显著优于先前工作。

Conclusion: 通过概念库驱动的数据构建和密集监督训练，有效解决了图像编辑任务中概念粒度不足和训练效率低下的问题，提供了一个数据、训练策略和评测的完整体系。

Abstract: Existing image editing frameworks predominantly follow the training paradigm of text-to-image diffusion models. However, extending this paradigm to image editing highlights two inherent discrepancies, specifically, the insufficient attention to edit concept granularity and the training inefficiency caused by sparse supervision signals. To address these issues, we establish a comprehensive hierarchical taxonomy featuring over 1,000 fine-grained edit concepts and build ConceptEdit-12M, a massive dataset of 12 million high-quality editing pairs via an improved synthesis framework. This library-driven approach effectively rectifies the distribution collapse of generated data while ensuring high data fidelity. Furthermore, we propose a dense supervision training strategy that synthesizes multiple non-interfering concepts into single image pairs. By providing richer learning signals, this strategy significantly enhances both training efficiency and overall model performance. Training results validate our strategy, significantly outperforming prior works. Finally, we present ConceptEdit-Bench, a granular evaluation suite designed to diagnose model capabilities across a vast array of real-world scenarios.

</details>


### [216] [Can Unsupervised Methods Outperform Supervised Deep Learning When Ground Truth Is Sparse? A Case Study of Bronchovascular Bundle Segmentation in Low-Dose CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16855)
*Anna Mrukwa,Marek Socha,Aleksandra Suwalska,Agata Durawa,Malgorzata Jelitto,Katarzyna Dziadziuszko,Edyta Szurowska,Pawel Bozek,Michal Marczyk,Witold Rzyman,Rafal Dziadziuszko,Joanna Polanska*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RONALD，一种用于LDCT影像中支气管血管束分割的pipeline，通过预处理（肺、肺叶、纵隔分割）后分别分割血管和支气管树，生成二元掩膜以提升早期肺癌结节的检测保留率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肺癌是全球死亡率最高的癌症，因早期诊断不足导致治疗延误。早期结节被邻近血管和气道壁遮挡，可见性降低，因此需要任务特异的支气管血管束分析以提升筛查效率。

Method: RONALD管道包括预处理阶段（肺、肺叶、纵隔分割），随后进行独立的血管和支气管树分割，最终输出肺实质内血管和支气管的二元掩膜。

Result: 在DLCS数据集中结节保留率从93.98%和90.36%提升到100%；在Pomeranian数据集中从83.16%和62.36%提升到99.92%。

Conclusion: 所提出的分割结果可改善极早期肺癌的肺结节检测能力。

Abstract: Background
  Lung cancer remains the deadliest cancer worldwide because it is often diagnosed too late. Effective treatment depends on detection at an early screening stage. However, the growing number of patients and the limited number of radiologists lead to prolonged diagnostic waiting times. In very early stage lung cancer, nodule visibility is further reduced by adjacent blood vessels and airway walls, because nodules are often connected to or supplied by these structures. Task-specific analysis of the bronchovascular bundle is therefore important for efficient nodule detection, and its removal can increase the diagnostic potential of lung cancer screening.
  Materials and Methods
  To assess the efficacy of the proposed method, we used series from widely utilized LDCT datasets, including the Duke Lung Cancer Screening (DLCS) dataset and the Pilot Pomeranian Lung Cancer Screening Program. The proposed bronchovascular bundle segmentation pipeline, RONALD, operates on computed tomography images and returns binary masks of vessels and bronchi located in the lung parenchyma. The method includes a preprocessing stage with lung, lobe, and mediastinum segmentation, followed by separate vessel and bronchial tree segmentation.
  Results
  The proposed pipeline segmented the bronchovascular bundle in low-dose computed tomography scans while improving nodule retention compared with other segmentation methods: from 93.98% and 90.36% to 100% in DLCS, and from 83.16% and 62.36% to 99.92% in the Pomeranian dataset.
  Conclusion
  The resulting segmentations can improve lung nodule detection in the very early stages of lung cancer.

</details>


### [217] [HarnessEval-W: Agentifying the Evaluation of Visual Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16859)
*Weiliang Chen,Haowen Sun,Jun Gao,Jiawei Chi,Hanyang Wang,Qiyu Dai,Yihao Li,Hao Li,Jingnan Gao,Yi-Hsin Hung,Xingzhuo Guo,Shangchen Miao,Zhiyuan Shi,Xiang Li,Fengrui Tian,Weihua Du,Ziqi Huang,Shenyuan Gao,Siqiao Huang,Mingyu Liu,Yifei Li,Shizun Wang,Xi Wang,Tianqi Zhang,Xue Luo,Xiyin Ren,Jinshan Ren,Xiaoyang Shen,Xiaobo Hu,Zhiyang Dou,Mingyu Ding,Yichao Yan,Xinchao Wang,Yizhou Wang,Shilong Liu,Wenzhao Zheng,Yueqi Duan,Yuan Gong,Ziwei Liu,Ming-Yu Liu,Jialong Wu,Jiangran Lyu,Fangfu Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了HarnessEval-W，一种代理化的世界模型评测流水线，将LLM生态中的harness范式引入世界模型基准测试。它通过分层代理工作流（父代理拆解子问题、子代理配备专用工具推理、父代理验证汇总证据）生成可验证的证据树，使每个评测结果都有完整的推理链支撑。在18个世界模型、330个评测案例上验证，判断与人类偏好高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型基准仅提供标量分数，缺乏可检验的推理链。人类能自然发现物理/因果/世界状态演变中的违规，但现有基准采用暴力计算指标，无法自动化这种能力，导致评测结果难以验证和信任。

Method: 采用代理化评测流水线：1) 父代理理解每个评测案例的上下文；2) 将评测问题分解为可测量的子问题；3) 生成专门的子代理，每个配备定制上下文和诊断工具独立推理；4) 父代理验证证据并总结出最终判定。整个分层工作流将每个评测转化为透明的证据树。

Result: 应用于18个代表性世界模型、330个评测案例。判断与人类偏好高度一致，同时提供可验证的、细粒度的对每个生成rollout的诊断。完整流水线已开源。

Conclusion: 提供了首个将harness范式（可验证推理链）引入世界模型评测的基准，使评测从黑盒打分变为透明的证据驱动过程，并作为实时社区基准持续演化。

Abstract: A benchmark should deliver more than a scalar score: what makes an evaluation trustworthy is the reasoning that justifies the score. This is especially critical for world models, where judging a rollout requires understanding whether physics, causality, and world state evolve correctly. Humans spot such violations naturally, yet no existing benchmark automates this capability: metrics are computed brute-force, leaving no reasoning chain that can be examined or verified. We introduce HarnessEval-W, an agentified evaluation pipeline that brings the harness paradigm from the LLM ecosystem to world model benchmarking. Rather than applying a fixed rubric, HarnessEval-W interprets the context of each evaluation case, decomposes the evaluation question into measurable subproblems, and spawns specialized sub-agents, each equipped with tailored context and diagnostic tools to reason over its own subproblem. The parent agent then validates the gathered evidence and summarizes it into the final verdict. This hierarchical workflow turns every evaluation into a transparent evidence tree whose complete reasoning chain justifies the result. We apply HarnessEval-W to 18 representative world models over 330 evaluation cases. Its judgments closely align with human preferences while providing verifiable, fine-grained diagnoses of every generated rollout. We open-source the full pipeline as a live benchmark and invite the broad community to contribute to grow new skills and evaluation cases as world models evolve.

</details>


### [218] [SplatGuide: Geometric Priors from 3D Gaussians for Pose-Free Novel View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16863)
*Yejun Zhang,Zihan Wang,Xu Ji,Yihao Wang,Yuxin Hou,Junyuan Fang,Juho-Matti Kilpeläinen,Arno Solin,Hamed Rezazadegan Tavakoli,Esa Rahtu,Juho Kannala*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SplatGuide通过在同一3DGS重建前向传播中复用三个互补信号（像素渲染、逐高斯源视角投票图、重建token特征）来弥合前馈3DGS重建与多视角扩散之间的信息鸿沟，实现尺度感知的遮挡感知参考选择，在多个数据集上达到SOTA的免姿态新视角合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前的前馈3DGS+多视角扩散管线最多只从重建中提取一个信号（像素渲染或学习特征），没有利用逐高斯的可见性进行遮挡感知的参考视角选择。这种信息断开导致了可渲染几何、可见性线索和学习特征未被充分利用，限制了免姿态新视角合成的质量。

Method: 在同一前馈3DGS重建中复用三个互补信号：(1)渲染图像提供像素对齐的几何条件；(2)将逐高斯源视角索引渲染为目标视角投票图，用于遮挡感知参考选择；(3)重建token通过交叉注意力提供特征级引导。所有三个信号来自同一次重建前向传播。

Result: 在RealEstate10K、DL3DV、Tanks-and-Temples和Mip-NeRF 360上达到SOTA的免姿态新视角合成。在RealEstate10K上，使用中等数量的输入视角时，甚至超过了使用真实姿态的基线。

Conclusion: 通过统一复用单个3DGS场景的多种信号，可以有效弥合重建与扩散之间的信息断开，充分利用几何、可见性和特征信息，显著提升免姿态新视角合成性能。

Abstract: Generating photorealistic novel views from unposed images requires both 3D geometric understanding and the ability to synthesize unseen content. A natural strategy combines feed-forward 3DGS reconstruction with multi-view diffusion. Yet prior pipelines extract at most one signal from the reconstruction, either pixel rendering or learned features, while none exploits per-Gaussian visibility for occlusion-aware reference selection. This *information disconnect* leaves renderable geometry, visibility cues, and learned features unused. SplatGuide closes this disconnect by reusing a single 3DGS scene across three complementary roles. Rendered images provide pixel-aligned geometric conditioning. Per-Gaussian source-view indices are rendered into a target-view voting map for occlusion-aware reference selection. Reconstruction tokens supply feature-level guidance via cross-attention. All three signals derive from the same reconstruction forward pass. Across RealEstate10K, DL3DV, Tanks-and-Temples, and Mip-NeRF 360, SplatGuide achieves state-of-the-art pose-free novel view synthesis. On RealEstate10K, with a moderate number of input views, it surpasses the ground-truth-pose baseline.

</details>


### [219] [An Empirical Study of Training Pixel-Space Text-to-Image Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16887)
*Dengyang Jiang,Ruoyi Du,Zhennan Chen,Dongyang Liu,Zanyi Wang,Mingzhe Zheng,Xiangpeng Yang,Huanqia Cai,Aiming Hao,Yuming Jiang,Peng Gao,Harry Yang,Steven Hoi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了像素空间扩散模型的大规模训练方法，提出一种"潜空间预训练+像素空间后训练"的策略，并识别出关键设计选择（权重初始化、数据组成、预测目标、解码器架构、噪声调度），使像素空间模型匹配或超越潜空间模型，同时获得3.18-4.75倍的端到端推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然像素空间扩散模型已被广泛研究，但多数工作局限在小规模或类别条件设置中，缺乏适用于大规模训练的实用配方。作者发现直接在大规模数据上进行像素空间预训练收敛远慢于潜空间，因此需要一个高效的迁移策略。

Method: 提出latent-to-pixel策略：先在潜空间高效获取生成先验，再在后训练阶段迁移到像素空间。系统探究了迁移过程中的关键设计选择，包括权重初始化、数据组成、预测目标、解码器架构和噪声调度，最终确定一套实用配方。

Result: 得到配方的像素空间模型在生成质量上匹配或超越对应的潜空间模型，同时实现3.18至4.75倍的端到端推理加速。

Conclusion: 为像素空间生成模型的大规模训练提供了实证见解和实用指南，证明通过合理的潜到像素后训练设计，像素空间模型可以兼顾质量与效率。

Abstract: This paper investigates an increasingly important topic in generative modeling: pixel-space diffusion models. Although numerous studies have explored this topic, most focus on small-scale or class-conditional settings. Consequently, a practical recipe for training pixel-space models that rival or exceed well-established latent-space counterparts remains elusive. Through a comprehensive empirical study, we first observe that direct large-scale pre-training in pixel space converges substantially more slowly than in latent space. This observation motivates a latent-to-pixel strategy that acquires generative priors efficiently in latent space and transitions to pixel space during post-training. We then systematically investigate the key design choices governing this transition, including weight initialization, data composition, prediction target, decoder architecture, and noise schedule, and identify a practical recipe that makes the resulting pixel-space models match or outperform their latent-space counterparts while delivering 3.18 to 4.75 times end-to-end inference speedups. We hope that our findings provide useful empirical insights and practical guidelines for future research on pixel-space generation.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [220] [SpotlessGS: Relightable 3D Gaussian Splatting under Dynamic Illumination for Robotic Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14713)
*Liang Hong,Jiaxin Wei,Simon Schaefer,Stefan Leutenegger,Jaehyung Jung*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文扩展DarkGS，提出一种无需显式光参校准的可重打光3D重建框架。通过联合优化光源参数、引入基于球谐的低频光照模型捕获空间变化的残余和环境光照，并采用MLP驱动的BRDF模型处理非朗伯反射，有效缓解暗环境下的光照伪影，提升重建质量，并在下游机器人感知任务中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在黑暗或弱光环境中依赖机载光源，但光源往往导致不均匀照明，进而降低下游感知任务的性能。先前的2D图像增强方法缺乏可靠监督且无法保持多视图几何一致性，需要一种可直接在3D空间中建模并联合优化光照的解决方案。

Method: 1) 在Gaussian Splatting框架内联合优化光照参数，消除显式光参校准需求；2) 提出基于球谐(Spherical Harmonics)的低频光照模型，捕获空间变化的残余与环境光照；3) 引入MLP-based BRDF模型建模非朗伯反射，实现可重打光的3D重建。

Result: 在合成和真实世界数据集上的实验表明，该方法有效减轻光照伪影，重建质量和定量指标均优于先前方法，并通过下游机器人感知任务验证了实用性。

Conclusion: 该方法为暗环境下的3D重建提供了更准确、更灵活的框架，可有效缓解不均匀照明引起的伪影，提升重建质量，并改善下游机器人感知性能。

Abstract: Robots operating in dark or poorly lit environments rely on onboard lights, which often produce uneven illumination that degrades downstream perception tasks. Prior approaches based on 2D image enhancement lack reliable supervision and fail to preserve multi-view geometric consistency. To address these limitations, we extend Dark Gaussian Splatting (DarkGS) toward a more accurate and flexible relightable 3D reconstruction framework. First, we eliminate the need for explicit light parameter calibration by jointly optimizing lighting parameters within the Gaussian Splatting framework. Second, we introduce a low-frequency illumination model based on spherical harmonics (SH) to capture spatially varying residual and ambient lighting effects. Third, we incorporate an MLP-based Bidirectional Reflectance Distribution Function (BRDF) to model non-Lambertian reflectance. Experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that our method effectively mitigates illumination artifacts while improving rendering quality and quantitative performance over prior approaches. We further validate its benefits for robotic perception through a downstream task.

</details>


### [221] [MISTac: A Vision-Based Tactile Sensor for Minimally Invasive Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14772)
*Robin Koch,Annabella Mascot,Rayan Younis,Martin Wagner,Stefanie Speidel,Mark Cutkosky,Ingo Sieber,Roberto Calandra*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MISTac是一款专为微创手术(MIS)设计的高分辨率视觉触觉传感器，直径8mm可穿过套管针，采用模块化3D打印外壳支持可更换的传感尖端和商用照明/成像硬件。传感器光学分辨率176.68μm，触觉分辨率250μm，可检测低至24.3mN的力。猪体内实验验证了其在MIS中的可用性，并训练机器学习模型进行组织分类，留一交叉验证准确率约84%。代码已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微创和机器人辅助手术相对于传统开放手术有许多优势，但剥夺了外科医生的触觉反馈和手指触诊组织的能力。为解决这一关键缺失，需要开发能通过套管针的高分辨率触觉传感器来恢复触诊能力。

Method: 设计直径8mm的可更换传感器尖端（适配微创手术套管针），采用模块化3D打印外壳集成商用照明和成像硬件。基于视觉的触觉传感方案，通过光学系统捕捉弹性体变形来感知触觉信息。在猪体内实验中采集触觉数据，训练机器学习模型进行组织分类任务。

Result: 传感器光学分辨率176.68μm，触觉分辨率250μm，可分辨24.3mN微小力。体内实验验证了其实用性，组织分类的留一交叉验证聚合准确率约84%。

Conclusion: MISTac是向微创手术中触觉反馈恢复迈出的一小步，有潜力未来辅助外科医生进行组织分类或术中肿瘤定位等任务。传感器设计开源发布。

Abstract: Minimally invasive and robot-assisted surgery offer many advantages over traditional open surgery, but deprive surgeons of tactile feedback and the ability to palpate tissue with their fingers. To address this lack of tactile feedback, we introduce the MISTac, a high resolution vision-based tactile sensor specifically designed for palpation in MIS. The sensor has a replaceable sensor tip with a diameter of 8 mm which allows it to fit through the trocars used in minimally invasive surgery. Its modular 3D-printed case design allows the use of bulky off-the-shelf illumination and imaging hardware that can easily be exchanged and upgraded. The sensor has an optical resolution of 176.68 $μm$, a tactile resolution of 250 $μm$, and can resolve forces as little as 24.3 mN. An in vivo study with the sensor shows its usability in minimally invasive surgery. We trained a machine learning model with the tactile data collected in the trial on a tissue classification task achieving an aggregate accuracy of ~84% in a leave-one-out cross validation. Tactile sensors have the potential to one day aid surgeons during minimally invasive surgery with tasks such as tissue classification or intra-operative tumor localization; MISTac is a small step towards this vision. We open-source MISTac at https://github.com/lasr-lab/mistac

</details>


### [222] [Imagining Recovery: Inference-Time Counterfactual Realignment for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14822)
*Yanyan Zhang,Disheng Liu,Kai Ye,Chaoda Song,Xinpeng Li,Mohsen Hariri,Vikash Singh,Yu Yin,Vipin Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CoRe（Counterfactual Realignment）是一个免训练的推理时恢复框架，通过合成观测想象策略在近期可行状态下的继续动作，然后最小化真实对齐以重新接入想象轨迹，从而在不依赖失败数据或重训练的情况下恢复冻结的VLA策略，应对任务目标变化与物理扰动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在在线干扰（目标变化、场景变化、机器人状态变化）下易失效，现有恢复方法需要失败数据、策略重训练或外部纠正智能体，带来额外数据需求与执行风险。

Method: CoRe在检测到偏离后，利用合成观测而非物理执行来想象冻结策略从近期可行状态向当前目标继续行动的轨迹；然后最小化调整机器人与场景以重新接入该想象轨迹，再交还控制权给策略。整个过程无需训练，统一处理中途指令变化与物理扰动。

Result: 在多种仿真器、VLA骨干网络及真实环境实验中，CoRe将成功率提升最高85.0个百分点至接近正常水平，同时减少42.2%的物理恢复操作，无需策略微调或失败特殊恢复训练。

Conclusion: CoRe提供了一种无数据、无训练的在线VLA恢复范式，通过反事实想象-重对齐机制兼顾任务进度保持与统一干扰处理，验证了其在仿真与真实环境中的有效性与通用性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have improved the flexibility and generality of robotic manipulation, yet they remain fragile to online disruptions, such as changes in task goal, scene configuration, or robot state. Existing recovery methods often require failure data, policy retraining, or external corrective agents, introducing additional data requirements and execution risks. We propose Counterfactual Realignment (CoRe), a training-free framework that recovers a frozen VLA at inference time without failure data. Upon detecting a deviation, CoRe imagines how the policy would continue toward the current goal from a recent viable state, using synthesized observations in place of physical execution, and then minimally realigns the robot and scene to rejoin this imagined continuation before returning control to the policy. Recovery is therefore planned without physical trial-and-error, preserves completed task progress, and handles both mid-episode instruction changes and physical perturbations in a unified manner. Extensive experiments across multiple simulators, VLA backbones, and real-world settings show that CoRe improves success rates by up to 85.0 percentage points to near-nominal levels while reducing physical restorations by 42.2%, without policy fine-tuning or failure-specific recovery training.

</details>


### [223] [Modeling and Control of an Eel-Inspired Soft Robot for Design Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14860)
*Zhangjingyi Jiang,Mark Campbell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于鳗鲡式游动的仿鳗鱼软体机器人的仿真模型。该模型使用有限元方法（FEM）建立弹性杆模型来捕捉软体材料特性，并与水动力模型耦合，模拟软体细长机器人在水中的行为。模型用于验证控制方法的有效性，并为设计决策提供洞见，包括系统配置的鲁棒性以及材料退化与失效的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鳗鲡式游动是高效的水中推进方式，新型软体材料推动了鳗鱼仿生软体机器人的发展。需要一种仿真模型来辅助设计优化，并支持基于模型的估计、推理与控制系统开发。

Method: 采用有限元方法建立弹性杆模型以捕捉软体材料特性（该模型便于随材料属性随时间变化进行更新），并耦合水动力模型以模拟软体细长机器人在水中的行为。

Result: 模型验证了所提出的控制方法能够实现期望的游泳行为；揭示了略微不对称的设计具有优势，能在相当的游速下提供更高的机动性；还分析了材料退化与失效对系统的影响。

Conclusion: 该仿真模型可用于指导未来软体机器人设计决策，针对特定任务优化性能。

Abstract: Anguilliform locomotion is a highly efficient swimming mode; the advent of new materials for soft robots enables the development of an eel-inspired soft robot. This paper presents a simulation model of an eel-inspired soft robot designed for anguilliform swimming. This model can aid in design optimization and the development of model-based estimation, reasoning, and control systems. A Finite Element Method (FEM) model of an elastic rod is used to capture the soft materials of the robotic fish, which makes it particularly amenable to variation over time as the material properties change. The material model is coupled with a hydrodynamic force model to simulate the behavior of a soft, elongated robot in water. The model is used to demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed control approaches in achieving desired swimming behaviors. It also provides insights into design decisions, including the robustness of different system configurations and the impact of material degradation and failure. The results show that slightly asymmetric designs are advantageous, offering comparable swimming velocities but greater maneuverability. This model can be used to guide future robotic design decisions aimed at optimizing performance for specific tasks.

</details>


### [224] [Real-time Estimator of Actuator Control and Health (REACH) on an Eel-Inspired Soft Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14865)
*Zhangjingyi Jiang,Myungsun Park,Michael T. Tolley,Mark Campbell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出REACH算法，用于软体游泳机器人（鳗鲡式游动）的执行器健康实时估计。该架构结合软体机器人模型、sigma点滤波器和统计假设检验，能捕捉非线性和随时间的变化，并在故障情况下帮助机器人完成任务和返回基地。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下机器人的运行环境恶劣，软体机器人材料和执行器容易退化。准确估计执行器功能状态对于机器人执行任务以及在执行器退化/故障时安全返回基地至关重要。

Method: REACH架构包含三个核心组件：1）软体机器人动力学模型用于预测；2）sigma点滤波器（无迹卡尔曼滤波类方法）处理非线性；3）形式化统计假设检验用于判定执行器健康状态。实验评估了三种传感器（GPS、IMU、弯曲传感器），传感器数量与放置位置，三种游动步态，并通过滤波验证方法确认算法统计一致性。

Result: 1）弯曲传感器和IMU都是合适的传感器选择，GPS不适用；2）IMU需两个传感器即可，弯曲传感器需三个；3）三种游动步态（直线游动、大转弯、紧转弯）下REACH均能成功预测执行器健康，性能差异极小；4）滤波验证确认故障估计算法在统计上一致；5）基于鱼形机器人弯曲传感器数据的实验验证了REACH在噪声数据和制造差异下的有效性。

Conclusion: REACH是一种有效的软体游泳机器人执行器健康实时估计方法，能够在真实噪声和制造变异条件下准确估计执行器退化状态，为软体水下机器人的可靠运行和故障安全返回提供了保障。

Abstract: An actuator health estimation algorithm for a soft swimming robot that can perform anguilliform swimming is developed. Due to harsh operational environments of underwater robots, and the common degradation of soft robot materials and actuators, accurate estimation of actuator functionality is necessary for robots to perform their missions as well as return to base in the event of actuator degradation and failure. Termed REACH (Real-time Estimator of Actuator Control and Health), the architecture employs a soft robot model, sigma point filter, and a formal statistical hypothesis test to adequately capture the nonlinearities and changes over time. The performance of REACH using three sensor types (GPS, IMU, and Bend Sensor) with one sensor on each actuator is compared, demonstrating that both bend sensor and IMU are adequate choices. Sensor quantity and placement are evaluated for IMU and bend sensor, showing two sensors are sufficient for IMU, whereas three sensors are needed for bend sensor. Three swimming gaits (linear swimming, wide turning, tight turning) are compared, demonstrating that REACH can successfully predict actuator health for all three gaits, with minimal differences in performance. A filter validation method shows the fault estimation algorithm is statistically consistent in finding the correct degradation. The approach is experimentally evaluated using bend sensor data collected from a fish robot, demonstrating that REACH can successfully estimate actuator health with noisy data and variations in manufacturing.

</details>


### [225] [Geometry-Aware Online Mapping for 3D Gaussian Splatting SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14902)
*Thai Luu,Quan Tran,Hieu Phan,Tuan Dang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文重新审视3DGS-SLAM系统中的初始化和密度控制启发式方法，提出了三种几何感知策略（透射率保持的稠密化、基于深度和内参的相机感知尺度初始化、误差引导的稠密化），在解耦的3DGS-SLAM设置下显著提升渲染质量且开销可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数3DGS-SLAM系统直接从离线3DGS重建中继承初始化和密度控制启发式方法，但在线SLAM严格的逐关键帧优化预算和增量地图增长会使这些启发式方法变得脆弱，导致有限的算力和迭代被浪费在不合适的位置。

Method: 提出三种在映射线程中运行的几何感知方法：1) 透射率保持的稠密化（transmittance-preserving densification）；2) 从深度和内参推导的相机感知尺度初始化（camera-aware scale initialization）；3) 误差引导的稠密化（error-guided densification），将新高斯原语聚焦到高残差区域。

Result: 在解耦的3DGS-SLAM设置下，三种方法在渲染质量上取得一致提升，且计算开销可忽略不计，并揭示了在线SLAM中光度残差与位姿不确定性之间的耦合关系。

Conclusion: 适用于在线3DGS-SLAM的几何感知初始化与稠密化策略能有效提升映射质量，代替离线启发式方法是可行的且有价值的改进方向，代码将开源以促进社区发展。

Abstract: Recent 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has enabled efficient photorealistic view synthesis and is rapidly being adopted in simultaneous localization and mapping (SLAM) systems for online mapping. In these systems, a Gaussian map must be expanded and refined incrementally while tracking runs in real time, so initialization and density control directly determine where limited computation and iterations are spent. This contrasts with offline 3DGS reconstruction, where such heuristics can be amortized over long optimization schedules. However, most 3DGS-SLAM pipelines inherit initialization and density-control heuristics from offline reconstruction, which can become brittle under the strict per-keyframe optimization budgets and incremental map growth of online SLAM. In this work, we revisit these heuristics in a decoupled 3DGS-SLAM setting and propose three geometry-aware methods that operate in the mapping thread: transmittance-preserving densification, camera-aware scale initialization from depth and intrinsics, and error-guided densification that focuses new primitives on high-residual regions. Our results show consistent improvements in rendering quality with negligible overhead, highlighting the coupling between photometric residuals and pose uncertainty in online SLAM. We will open-source our code to the community to foster growth and validate reproducibility.

</details>


### [226] [From Continuous Design to Delay-Aware Discrete Synthesis: Guaranteed High-Bandwidth Joint Control for PMSM Drives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14937)
*Edmundo Pozo Fortunić,Mehmet C. Yildirim,Sami Haddadin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种考虑任务需求、显式建模通信与计算延迟的离散时间关节模型，用于直接合成为离散PI电流控制器，并能解析确定最小采样频率、控制器增益和DC-link电压需求。仿真与真实自制机器人关节实验均验证了该方法相比传统连续时间设计的优势（更低采样频率与母线电压需求）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器人关节的动态需求日益提高，要求电流控制器在宽运行范围内具备高带宽，但通信、计算和离散时间效应不可忽略。传统PMSM电流控制器在连续时间下设计后离散化，采样频率和实现延迟的影响主要靠启发式选择和反复迭代验证，缺乏系统性方法。

Method: 引入任务感知、延迟扩展的离散时间关节模型，显式纳入物理通信与计算延迟；基于该模型直接合成具有指定带宽和延迟保证的离散PI电流控制器；解析确定满足电机与关节性能所需的最小采样频率、控制器增益和DC-link电压。

Result: 仿真覆盖多种动态需求，验证了方法论的正确性，并展示相比传统连续时间设计方案大幅降低采样频率和DC-link电压需求；在自制定制机器人关节上的嵌入式实现进一步验证了该框架在真实条件下的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的延迟显式离散时间控制设计框架为机器人关节电流控制器提供了一套系统化、可解析的设计流程，减少了启发式迭代，并在真实硬体验证中表现出显著优势。

Abstract: The increasing dynamic demands of modern robotic joints require current controllers to achieve high bandwidth over wide operating ranges of speed, acceleration, and torque, where communication, computation, and discrete-time effects can no longer be neglected. Conventional PMSM current controllers are typically designed in continuous time and subsequently discretized, leaving the sampling frequency and the impact of implementation delays largely to heuristic selection and iterative validation. This paper introduces a task-aware, delay-extended discrete-time joint model that explicitly accounts for physical communication and computation delays and enables direct synthesis of a discrete PI current controller with prescribed bandwidth and delay guarantees throughout the operating envelope. The framework analytically determines the minimum required sampling frequency, controller gains, and DC-link voltage needed to satisfy the specified motor and joint performance. Simulations across a range of dynamic requirements validate the methodology and demonstrate substantially reduced sampling-frequency and DC-link-voltage requirements compared with conventional continuous-time-based design.
