We further demonstrate its zero-shot deployment on unseen compliant and unstructured terrains, including a foam platform and a gravel road. All code and experimental configurations will be released as open-source to facilitate reproducibility.

</details>


### [260] [Reliable Piezoresistive Strain Sensing Through Physical Limits and Uncertainty Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15784)
*Carmen Ballester,Víctor Muñoz,Dorin Copaci,Dolores Blanco*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可靠性框架，将基于物理信息的概率逆模型与风险因子（融合不确定性与应变/应变率限制）结合，为软压阻应变传感器提供三重状态监测器，使其在超出量程或内部失效时能报告可信度并让系统撤回动作，无需标注故障样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软压阻应变传感器的电阻响应是非线性、滞后的，且可能被推出校准工作区或在内部失效，但概率回归器只跟踪置信度却忽略这些限制。一个看起来自信的预测均值并不等同于可信的估计，因此需要一种能够感知传感器工作范围极限并标记异常状态的可靠性框架。

Method: 提出可靠性框架，包含：(1) 基于物理启发输入特征的物理信息概率逆模型（使用高斯过程回归）；(2) 将不确定性估计与应变和应变率物理极限融合的风险因子；(3) 将状态划分为正常、超出量程、异常三种状态的监测器。在Nitinol丝和镀银聚酰胺线上进行测试。

Result: 在Nitinol丝和镀银聚酰胺线上，拟合分数提升至0.90-0.95（RMSE 0.26%-0.15%），经验覆盖率96%对95%目标。监测器捕获了95%的超出量程情况和100%的异常情况，且在标称操作下保持可靠。

Conclusion: 该框架使传感器在报告估计值的同时报告可靠性，系统可在不信任时撤回动作。由于无需标注故障样本（软材料故障样本难以收集），该方法具有实际可行性，为软体机器人/可穿戴传感的决策与控制提供了信任感知机制。

Abstract: Soft piezoresistive strain sensors are one of the most common sensing solutions for wearable and soft robotic applications due to their flexibility and compliance. However, their resistance response is nonlinear and hysteretic, and a sensor can be pushed past its calibrated workspace or misbehave inside it, carrying that error into a decision or control loop. Probabilistic regressors track confidence but ignore those limits. A predictive mean can look unremarkable even when the reading comes from a sensor outside its admissible range or already failing internally, so a confident-looking estimate is not the same as a trustworthy one. This paper proposes a reliability framework pairing a physics-informed probabilistic inverse model, built on physics-guided input features, with a risk factor fusing uncertainty with strain and strain-rate limits into a three-state monitor. Tests on a Nitinol wire and a silver-coated polyamide thread with a Gaussian Process raised fit scores to 0.90-0.95 (RMSE 0.26%-0.15%) and a 96% empirical coverage against the 95% target. The monitor caught 95% of out-of-range and 100% of abnormal conditions while staying reliable under nominal operation. A sensor that reports confidence alongside its estimate lets a system withhold action instead, since it needs no labeled failure examples, which are hard to collect for soft materials.

</details>


### [261] [ViTaR: Visuo-Tactile Residual Adaptation for Foundation VLA Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15816)
*Yi Wang,Renjun Wu,Jinyan Liu,Xuesong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ViTaR将触觉反馈从“动作生成感知输入”重构为“执行调制器”，在冻结VLA之上选择和缩放有界残差修正，保留预训练能力。在UniVTAC七个接触丰富任务上达到61.3%平均成功率，比冻结VLA基线提升30.6个百分点，并超过专用触觉基线，物理机器人实验验证了在真实传感器噪声下的迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型扩展到真实部署时面临关键盲点：编码了宽泛的视觉-语义先验却对局部接触事件不敏感，接触建立、丢失或失稳时输出相同动作。现有方案要么修改VLA内部结构（冒灾难性遗忘风险），要么在近失败接触条件下在线强化学习，两者都赋予触觉对动作生成的无界影响，与VLA的可泛化先验冲突。

Method: ViTaR将触觉适应分解为两阶段：1) Effect-Guided Modeling：通过结果导向的偏好证据判断是否以及哪种修正在局部是合理的；2) Residual Action Modulation：将证据转化为残差选择，并通过实时视觉-触觉观测连续缩放增益。整个结构将触觉反馈作为执行调制器，在冻结VLA之上选择和有界缩放残差修正，按构造保留预训练能力。

Result: 在UniVTAC基准的7个接触丰富任务上达到61.3%平均成功率，相比冻结VLA基线提升30.6个百分点，并且超越专门构建的触觉基线。物理机器人实验证实有界触觉调制能够迁移到真实传感器噪声和动力学环境。

Conclusion: ViTaR证明了将触觉从动作生成输入重构为执行调制器，可以保留VLA的泛化先验的同时显式修复其对接触事件的盲点，为触觉增强VLA提供了不干扰预训练能力的新范式。

Abstract: As Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models scale toward real-world deployment, contact-rich manipulation exposes a critical blind spot: these policies encode broad visual-semantic priors yet remain unaware of local contact events, producing identical actions whether contact is established, lost, or destabilized. Existing remedies either modify VLA internals, risking catastrophic forgetting, or demand online reinforcement under near-failure contact conditions. Both grant tactile unbounded influence over action generation, conflicting with the priors that make VLAs generalizable. We introduce ViTaR, which reframes tactile feedback from an action-generating perceptual input to an execution modulator that selects and scales bounded residual corrections atop a frozen VLA, preserving pretrained capabilities by construction. ViTaR decomposes adaptation into two stages: Effect-Guided Modeling determines whether and which correction is locally justified via outcome-grounded preference evidence, and Residual Action Modulation converts this evidence into a residual choice with continuously scaled gain from real-time visuotactile observations. On the UniVTAC benchmark spanning seven contact-rich tasks, ViTaR achieves 61.3% average success, a 30.6 percentage-point improvement over its frozen VLA base that also surpasses purpose-built tactile baselines. Physical-robot experiments confirm that bounded tactile modulation transfers to real sensor noise and dynamics.

</details>


### [262] [Scaling Manual-Grounded Appliance Manipulation with Data Synthesis and Unified Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15863)
*Yuxing Long,Lei Kang,Ziyan Yu,Yuzheng Gao,Bin Cheng,Jiyao Zhang,Xiaoqi Li,Haolin Yang,Dongjiang Li,Hui Shen,Hao Dong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MAGE数据合成管道，利用层级家电图谱（HAG）从说明书自动生成部件定位、长时序规划与闭环恢复数据，构建首个大规模家电操作规划数据集UseAppliance，并训练出7B参数的AppliancePlan模型，在真实家电Bench和真实机器人实验中大幅超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 操作家用电器需要长时序、状态依赖且抗扰动的规划能力，但现有大模型因缺乏多样化、任务导向的数据集而难以支持此类规划。

Method: 提出可扩展数据合成管道MAGE，引入层级家电图谱（HAG）自动从操作手册生成部件接地、长时序规划和闭环恢复数据；构建UseAppliance数据集（22类家电、89K+部件标注、53K+操作任务、33K+闭环调整步骤）；基于该数据集训练端到端模型AppliancePlan（7B参数）。

Result: AppliancePlan在RealAppliance-Bench上开放环规划超过最佳基线10倍以上，并在所有任务上持续优于SOTA模型；在6种真实家电上的机器人实验验证了有效的sim-to-real迁移。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了利用说明书合成的数据可高效支撑家电操作的长时序规划，朝通用家务机器人迈出重要一步，为缺乏任务数据的长时序操作规划提供了可行数据合成范式。

Abstract: Operating household appliances requires long-horizon planning that is state-dependent and robust to disturbances, yet existing large models fall short, as no sufficiently diverse, task-oriented dataset exists to support such planning. To bridge this gap, we propose MAGE, a scalable data synthesis pipeline that introduces a novel Hierarchical Appliance Graph (HAG) to automatically generate part grounding, long-horizon planning, and closed-loop recovery data from appliance manuals. With MAGE, we build UseAppliance, the first large-scale dataset for manual-grounded appliance manipulation planning, spanning 22 appliance categories with 89K+ part annotations, 53K+ manipulation tasks, and 33K+ closed-loop adjustment steps. Built on UseAppliance, we develop AppliancePlan, an end-to-end model for manual-grounded appliance manipulation planning. On RealAppliance-Bench, AppliancePlan with only 7B parameters achieves over 10x the best baseline on open-loop planning and consistently outperforms state-of-the-art models across all tasks. Real-robot experiments on six household appliances further confirm effective sim-to-real transfer, marking an important step toward general-purpose household robotics.

</details>


### [263] [GigaBrain-0.7: Scaling Embodied Foundation Models to Emergent Capabilities with a Three-System Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15875)
*GigaBrain Team,Angen Ye,Axiang Sun,Can Jin,Chenxi Cheng,Chong Shi,Dengke Shang,Dingqian Zhang,Guan Huang,Guangqiang Wang,Guangqing Ding,Guo Li,Hangcong Li,Hengyu Zhong,Hongtao Lu,Jianbo Qin,Jiming Mao,Jing Zhu,Jindi Lv,Jingzhi Cui,Junjie Xie,Junyi Bao,Kai Liu,Lei Yuan,Limin Long,Lv Feng,Mingming Yu,Peng Li,Pengfei Yi,Qi Li,Qianli Zhang,Qingfang Li,Qitang Hu,Rui Zhang,Shaoyan Sun,Shibo Sun,Shiying Duan,Tenghui Chen,Tianze Liu,Weijie Ke,Wenyao Xue,Xiaofeng Wang,Xiaoyu Tian,Xinyu Liu,Xinze Chen,Yang Wang,Yankai Wang,Yejun Zeng,Yifan Li,Yifei Nie,Yilong Li,Yilong Liu,Yongchao Feng,Yumeng Wang,Yun Ye,Zhichao Liu,Ziheng He,Zonghai Yang,Zheng Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GigaBrain-0.7 是一个统一理解、预测与动作的机器人具身基础模型，采用三系统架构，在超3.7万小时异构具身数据上预训练，通过单阶段对齐训练联合优化视觉-语言理解与多本体动作生成，在零样本能力、指令跟随和后训练任务成功率上大幅超越前代及π0.5，并已在Maker H01等机器人平台上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型虽然在结构化环境中表现良好，但其架构设计有效性、能否扩展到更大规模和更异构的数据、以及能否在跨任务跨本体上实现广泛泛化仍是开放问题。作者旨在构建一个具有显著跨机器人本体泛化能力的具身基础模型。

Method: GigaBrain-0.7采用三系统架构（理解、预测、动作统一），在超过37,000小时异构具身数据上进行大规模预训练，并引入单阶段对齐训练，同时优化视觉-语言理解与多本体动作生成。

Result: 相比前代GigaBrain-0系列和包括π0.5在内的SOTA模型，GigaBrain-0.7在基础零样本能力、语言条件指令跟随和后训练任务成功率上均取得显著提升；在自研Maker H01平台及主流机器人本体上，家庭和工业场景均展现出强任务适应性与完成能力。

Conclusion: GigaBrain-0.7验证了通过更有效的架构设计、更大规模异构数据预训练以及单阶段联合对齐可以有效提升VLA模型的跨本体泛化能力，是具身基础模型领域的重要进展。代码与预训练权重将全部开源。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have become a dominant paradigm for generalist embodied agents, demonstrating strong complex and long-horizon task completion in structured settings. Yet it remains an open question whether current VLA systems can benefit from more effective architectural design, scale to substantially larger and more heterogeneous data regimes, and achieve broader generalization across tasks and embodiments. To this end, we present GigaBrain-0.7, an embodied foundation model with substantially improved generalization across diverse robot embodiments. Specifically, GigaBrain-0.7 unifies understanding, prediction, and action through a three-system architecture, scales pretraining to over 37,000 hours of heterogeneous embodied data, and introduces one-stage alignment training that jointly optimizes vision-language understanding and multi-embodiment action generation. Compared with the preceding GigaBrain-0 series and prior state-of-the-art models including $π_{0.5}$, GigaBrain-0.7 achieves substantial improvements in foundation zero-shot capabilities, language-conditioned instruction following, and post-training task success rates. In particular, on our in-house Maker H01 platform and mainstream robot embodiments, GigaBrain-0.7 demonstrates strong task adaptability and completion ability across both home and industrial scenarios. All training code and pretrained model weights will be released.

</details>


### [264] [Grouping Auction-Consensus Algorithm for Decentralized Task Allocation in Multi-Robot Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15884)
*Jose Rodriguez,Sven Koenig,Wenjie Dong,Qi Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出分组拍卖-共识算法（GACA），一种分布式多机器人任务分配框架。它在CBBA的两阶段拍卖-共识架构基础上，将投标机制重新设计为对空间邻近任务组成的"组"进行推理。通过最近邻预处理将任务划分为空间连贯的组，代理迭代提出组级行动（认领未分配组、获取部分组、竞争他人持有的组）。在4,000个测试场景中，GACA中位最优性约97%，远超CBBA的81-84%，且收敛迭代次数相等或更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CBBA作为广泛采用的分布式MRTA基线，其个体任务级投标机制与最小化总团队行驶距离的min-sum目标不一致，在空间分布式环境中产生次优分配。现有方法缺乏对任务空间结构进行推理的机制，需要一种能显式利用空间邻近性、同时保持分布式可扩展性的分配框架。

Method: GACA采用CBBA的两阶段拍卖-共识架构，但重新设计了投标机制。具体步骤：(1) 最近邻预处理将任务划分为空间连贯的组；(2) 代理迭代提出组级行动，包括认领未分配的组、获取部分组、或竞争其他代理持有的组；(3) 通过共识阶段解决竞争行动。在MT-SR-IA问题类别中，以MILP作为最优性参考基准进行评估。

Result: 在四种群规模和4,000个测试世界中，GACA中位百分最优性约97%，CBBA为81-84%；GACA收敛迭代次数相等或更少。额外的3,280个可扩展性测试实例（5-20个代理、10-50个任务）确认这些增益在不同问题配置中稳健普适。

Conclusion: GACA通过引入组级任务推理，显著改善了分布式MRTA在空间分布式环境下的解质量，且未牺牲收敛速度，是一种实用且可扩展的分布式任务分配改进方案。

Abstract: Decentralized multi-robot task allocation (MRTA) is essential for scalable and resilient autonomous systems. The Consensus-Based Bundle Algorithm (CBBA) is a widely adopted decentralized baseline. However, its individual task-level bidding is poorly aligned with the min-sum objective of minimizing total team travel distance, leading to suboptimal allocations in spatially distributed environments. This paper introduces the Grouping Auction-Consensus Algorithm (GACA). This decentralized MRTA framework adopts the two-phase auction-consensus architecture of CBBA while fundamentally redesigning its bidding mechanism to reason over groups of spatially proximate tasks. A nearest-neighbor preprocessing step partitions tasks into spatially coherent groups before allocation. Agents then iteratively propose structured group-level actions: claiming unassigned groups, acquiring partial groups, or contesting groups held by other agents. Competing actions are resolved through a consensus phase. Operating in the MT-SR-IA problem class, GACA is evaluated against CBBA using a Mixed-Integer Linear Program as the ground-truth optimality reference. Across four swarm sizes and 4,000 test worlds, GACA achieves a median percent optimality of approximately 97% compared to 81--84% for CBBA, while converging in equal or fewer iterations. A scalability evaluation over 3,280 additional problem instances spanning swarm sizes of 5 to 20 agents and task counts of 10 to 50 confirms that these gains generalize robustly across a wide range of problem configurations.

</details>


### [265] [Tactile Sim2Real without Tactile Simulation via Bottlenecked Latent Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15897)
*Fan Yang,Youngsun Wi,Jinhao Yu,Nima Fazeli,Dmitry Berenson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 SBLR（Sim2Real via Bottlenecked Latent Reconstruction）框架，通过完全避免针对特定传感器的仿真建模来实现触觉感知的 sim2real 迁移。策略先在仿真原生的"预言机传感器"（点云+指尖力）上训练，再通过瓶颈潜空间重建适配到真实传感器的信息损失，利用正视流变换网络在最近邻伪对上学习对齐。在三个接触密集任务上逼近预言机性能，硬件实验（GelSight Mini 和 DIGIT）达到 85-97.5% 零样本成功率，优于物理触觉仿真基线 7.5-15%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人传感器（特别是触觉传感器）种类繁多且迭代迅速，为每种传感器建模需要大量领域专业知识，且仿真计算近似会降低信号保真度。现有 sim2real 方法依赖传感器特定的高保真仿真，成本高且泛化差，亟需一种无需传感器特定建模的通用迁移框架。

Method: SBLR 框架包含两个阶段：(1) 策略在仿真原生的预言机传感器（点云+指尖力）潜空间上训练，无需建模任何特定传感器；(2) 在推理时将真实传感器潜表征对齐到预言机传感器的潜空间，通过瓶颈化潜在重建适配真实传感器相对于预言机的信息损失。对齐学习使用从仿真和真实世界无配对随机游走数据中收集的数据，通过基于正视流（rectified-flow）的变换网络在最近邻伪对上训练。

Result: 仿真实验在三个接触密集任务上显示 SBLR 匹配或接近直接访问触觉仿真的预言机性能。硬件实验在 Peg Insertion 和 Gear Meshing 任务上使用 GelSight Mini 和 DIGIT 传感器实现 85-97.5% 零样本成功，无需任何传感器特定建模或标定，比基于物理的触觉仿真基线高出 7.5-15%。

Conclusion: SBLR 证明了绕过传感器特定仿真的可行性，通过潜空间对齐而非信号级仿真逼近实现真实传感器信息的有效利用。该框架显著降低了触觉 sim2real 的工程门槛，为多变传感器生态下的策略迁移提供了新范式。

Abstract: Robot sensor designs, particularly tactile sensors, are highly diverse and evolve rapidly. Modeling each sensor in simulation demands substantial domain expertise and computational approximations can degrade the fidelity of the simulated signals. We propose Sim2Real via Bottlenecked Latent Reconstruction (SBLR), a framework that avoids sensor-specific simulation entirely by (1) training policies on a simulator-native oracle sensor that is easy to construct without modeling any particular sensor (e.g. we use a point-cloud and finger-tip forces as a tactile oracle), and (2) aligning real sensor latent embeddings to those of the oracle sensor at inference time. Policy training proceeds in two-stage: the policy first learns from the oracle sensor latents, then a bottlenecked latent reconstruction adapts it to the information loss expected when using the real sensor instead of the oracle. The alignment between oracle and real sensor is learned from unpaired random-play data collected in both simulation and the real world, using rectified-flow-based transformation networks trained on nearest-neighbor pseudo-pairs. Simulation experiments on three contact-rich tasks show that SBLR matches or approaches the performance of an oracle with direct access to tactile simulation. Hardware experiments on Peg Insertion and Gear Meshing with GelSight Mini and DIGIT sensors demonstrate 85-97.5% zero-shot success without requiring any sensor-specific modeling or calibration, outperforming a physics-based tactile simulation baseline by 7.5-15%.

</details>


### [266] [Pre-training Visual Dexterity in Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15917)
*Sarthak Kamat,Adam Rashid,Satvik Sharma,Aseem Doriwala,Chelsea Finn,Phillip Isola,C. Karen Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出模拟预训练灵巧操作框架（SPD），通过VR头显让人类在仿真环境中操控虚拟物体，收集75小时的多任务灵巧操作轨迹数据，用因果Transformer进行预训练，再在真实56自由度双臂灵巧手系统上用1-2小时物理演示进行微调，显著优于从头训练的行为克隆策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧多指手的机器人策略受限于数据稀缺：真实遥操作成本高难以规模化，而人类手部视频存在embodiment不匹配、位姿估计有损和重定向损失的问题。大规模预训练虽已提升机器人策略微调的数据效率，但主要依赖简单的平行夹爪数据集和embodiment。

Method: SPD框架利用VR头显让人类在仿真中操控虚拟物体，实现on-embodiment轨迹采集且无需真实机器人。5名操作员在1周内收集75小时多任务灵巧操作数据，用因果Transformer在序列建模目标上预训练，然后在真实56-DoF双臂灵巧操作设备上微调1-2小时物理演示。包含消融实验评估历史条件化和短动作块对反应式控制的影响。

Result: 仿真预训练+真机微调在真实灵巧操作任务上优于从零训练的行为克隆策略，证明仿真遥操作可作为真实世界灵巧操作的可行预训练数据来源。消融实验揭示了历史条件化和短动作块对反应式控制的收益。

Conclusion: VR仿真遥操作是一种高效、可扩展的灵巧操作预训练数据采集方式，有效解决了灵巧手数据稀缺的根本问题，为真实世界灵巧操作预训练提供了新范式。

Abstract: Large-scale pre-training has made robot policy fine-tuning increasingly data-efficient, but this progress has largely been driven by datasets and embodiments built around simple parallel-jaw grippers. Dexterous, multi-fingered hands remain comparatively data-starved because real teleoperation is costly to scale, while human hand video is off-embodiment and requires lossy pose estimation and retargeting. We introduce Simulation Pre-training for Dexterity (SPD), a pre-training framework for dexterous manipulation that uses data entirely collected in simulation. In SPD, humans manipulate virtual objects inside a VR headset, enabling on-embodiment trajectories and robot-free collection. With the help of five operators, we collect 75 hours of multi-task dexterous manipulation over one week, and use it to pre-train a causal transformer on a sequence modeling objective. We study the benefits of simulation pre-training on real-world tasks by fine-tuning on 1-2 hours of physical demonstrations on a 56-DoF bimanual dexterous setup. We find that our approach outperforms training behavior cloning policies from scratch, showing that simulation teleoperation is a viable pre-training source for real-world dexterous manipulation. We perform ablation studies, measuring the benefits of history conditioning and short action chunks for reactive control.

</details>


### [267] [RAPAC-DP: Response-Aligned Pending-Action Compensation for Diffusion Policies under Delayed Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15924)
*Tao Wang,Wei Wang,Jianhui Wang,Qi Wang,Weidi Huang,Bing Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RAPAC-DP，一种面向云侧交互式模仿学习策略的响应对齐待执行动作补偿框架，通过待执行动作序列作为条件输入来补偿通信和计算延迟，支持扩散和流式动作生成器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云侧推理为模仿学习策略提供更强计算资源，但通信和计算延迟会降低控制性能，需要有效的延迟补偿机制。

Method: RAPAC-DP将云响应到达前已排定的待执行动作编码为待执行动作序列，作为参数高效补偿通路的条件输入；当延迟可忽略时可绕过该通路精确恢复冻结的基策略；训练时从无延迟演示构造延迟条件样本，无需显式系统动力学或额外延迟演示。

Result: 在Kinetix最大固定延迟下保持了81.4%的无延迟整体性能；在RoboMimic三个任务的延迟环境下平均成功率达0.633。

Conclusion: 待执行动作补偿对云部署模仿学习策略是有效的延迟缓解方案。

Abstract: Cloud-side inference gives imitation-learning policies access to greater computational resources, but communication and computation delays can degrade control performance. To compensate for these delays, we propose RAPAC-DP, a response-aligned pending-action compensation framework designed for both diffusion- and flow-based action generators. RAPAC-DP encodes the actions already scheduled for execution before the cloud response arrives into a pending-action sequence that serves as the conditioning input to a parameter-efficient compensation pathway. When delay effects are negligible, bypassing this pathway exactly recovers the frozen base policy. For training, RAPAC-DP constructs delay-conditioned samples from delay-free demonstrations, requiring neither explicit system dynamics nor additional delayed demonstrations. At the largest fixed delay tested on Kinetix, RAPAC-DP retained 81.4% of its overall delay-free performance. At the largest fixed delay tested on each RoboMimic task, it achieved a mean success rate of 0.633 across the three tasks. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of pending-action compensation for cloud-deployed imitation-learning policies.

</details>


### [268] [Revisiting Open-Loop Execution in Robotics: Toward Reactive, Higher-Performing Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15938)
*Michael Zeng,Abhinav Agarwal,Ajay Bati,Brian Lee,Siddharth Ancha,Russ Tedrake*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了机器人模仿学习中"动作分块"（action chunking，即预测一串动作并开环执行前缀）的性能机制。作者提出核心论点：长开环执行主要帮助短上下文策略模仿"非马尔可夫示范"，而非先前文献普遍认为的"复合误差缓解"。通过4个仿真和2个真实世界任务验证，专家非马尔可夫性对任务成功率与开环执行时长的关系影响显著；当策略拥有足够长的上下文时，开环执行不再有益，最反应式（完全闭环）的策略表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作分块已成为机器人模仿学习近期进展的关键技术，但其性能优势的机制尚不明确——先前的解释（缓解复合误差、吸收推理延迟、平滑动作）缺乏受控实验证据，且长开环执行会降低反应性、限制纠错能力。本文旨在厘清长开环执行到底为何有效，并为如何在保持反应性的同时获得该收益提供指导。

Method: 作者提出"专家非马尔可夫性"假设作为长开环执行有效性的主要解释。通过4个仿真任务和2个真实世界任务进行受控实验，系统比较：(1) 专家非马尔可夫性对成功率与开环执行时长的关系的影响；(2) 复合误差（先前主流解释）的影响强度；(3) 在给予策略足够长上下文时，开环执行与闭环执行的性能对比。

Result: 实验表明专家非马尔可夫性强有力地塑造了任务成功率与开环执行时长的关系；复合误差虽然存在影响，但其作用远弱于专家非马尔可夫性。关键发现是：当策略拥有足够长的上下文时，开环执行不再带来收益，完全闭环的反应式策略表现最佳。

Conclusion: 长开环执行的成功主要归因于帮助短上下文策略模仿非马尔可夫示范，而非缓解复合误差。因此，作者主张长上下文的反应式策略（闭环）是比长开环动作分块更合理且更优越的范式，为机器人模仿学习提供了重要的方向性指导。

Abstract: Action chunking --- the practice of predicting a sequence of actions and executing a prefix open-loop --- has emerged as a key enabler of recent progress in imitation learning for robotic manipulation. However, executing long open-loop prefixes reduces reactivity, limiting policies' ability to correct for errors. Further, the mechanisms underlying these performance benefits remain poorly understood: prior works cite mitigating compounding errors, absorbing inference latency, or smoothing motions, but provide limited controlled evidence or guidance for preserving reactivity. In this work, we argue that long open-loop execution primarily helps short-context policies imitate "non-Markovian demonstrations". Across four simulation and two real-world tasks, we show that expert non-Markovianity strongly shapes the relationship between task success and open-loop execution horizon. Further, we investigate the impact of compounding errors --- the prevailing explanation for long open-loop execution in prior work --- and find that while they matter, expert non-Markovianity has a much stronger impact in our experimental setting. Finally, we show that when policies are provided with a sufficiently long context, open-loop execution is no longer beneficial and the most reactive, closed-loop policies perform best. While imitation learning has seen great success using long open-loop execution, our findings motivate long-context, reactive policies as a more principled and performant paradigm.

</details>


### [269] [Rotate Disks to Reach Farther: Design and Modeling of a Novel Reconfigurable Tendon Driven Manipulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15946)
*Sabyasachi Dash,Yangkun Liu,Will Hunter,John Golden,Girish Krishnan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可重构的腱驱动连续体机械臂（TDCM）设计，允许中间间隔盘独立旋转从而局部重路由肌腱路径，实现非平凡的骨架空间变形。提出了两种设计（MDL和CDR），并开发了基于分段常应变（PCS）假设的预测静态模型，在势能最小化框架内融合了磁盘旋转、离散肌腱路径、间隔盘刚性和肌腱弹性。实验验证平均尖端误差为总长度的1.2%（平行肌腱路径）和约3%（多盘旋转），计算时间较Cosserat杆求解器低一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统腱驱动连续体机械臂中肌腱路径固定，限制了可实现的变形模式。通过引入可独立旋转的中间间隔盘来重新路由肌腱路径，可以显著扩展TDCM的变形能力，但缺乏相应的预测性静力学模型来支持设计和控制。

Method: 提出两种可重构TDCM设计：（a）手动磁盘锁定（MDL）和（b）连续磁盘转子（CDR）机械臂，分别实现操作前和操作中的磁盘旋转。开发了基于分段常应变（PCS）假设的预测静态模型，在势能最小化框架中整合了磁盘旋转、段间离散肌腱路径、间隔盘的刚性厚度以及肌腱弹性。

Result: 模型经实验验证：平行肌腱路径情况下平均尖端误差为机械臂总长度的1.2%，多盘旋转情况下约3%。计算时间比现有最先进的Cosserat杆求解器低一个数量级。

Conclusion: 所提出的PCS-based静态模型能够准确高效地预测可重构TDCM的变形，为这类扩展变形能力的连续体机械臂提供了有效的建模工具，同时显著降低了计算成本。

Abstract: Rerouting the tendon path in tendon driven continuum manipulators (TDCMs) enables a broad range of deformation modes. This work presents a Reconfigurable TDCM design which allows independent rotation of intermediate spacer disks, thereby locally rerouting the tendon and achieving non-trivial backbone spatial deformations. Two such designs, (a) Manual Disk Locked (MDL) and (b) Continuous Disk Rotor (CDR) manipulators are presented to achieve disk rotations before and during operation, respectively. A predictive static model based on the piecewise constant strain (PCS) assumption is developed within a potential energy minimization framework, incorporating (a) disk rotations, (b) discrete tendon paths between disk segments, (c) rigid thickness of spacer disks, and (d) elasticity of the tendons. The model is validated against experimental results, demonstrating an average tip error of $1.2\%$ of the manipulator's total length for parallel tendon routing and around $3\%$ for the case when multiple disks are rotated. The computation time is an order of magnitude lower than the state of the art Cosserat rod solver.

</details>


### [270] [Tabletop Pen Manipulation With a Vision-Guided 4-DoF Arm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15968)
*Anirudh Rangarajan,Bibit Bianchini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文展示了如何利用感知与运动规划，让一个约200美元的四自由度机械臂在固定俯视相机下完成彩笔的颜色分拣任务，尽管其缺少手腕旋转自由度。通过YOLO11n-OBB检测、ArUco位姿估计与颜色分类，结合矫正扫掠策略补偿缺失自由度，在326次运动中成功纠正高达90度的错位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低成本四自由度机械臂是极易获取的机器人平台，但理论上无法执行需要五自由度的任意姿态拾取任务（尤其缺少手腕旋转）。作者希望证明通过感知与运动规划的巧妙设计，可以在不增加硬件自由度的前提下弥补这一缺失。

Method: 采用固定俯视相机；YOLO11n-OBB检测书写工具的有向边界框；利用相机内参与ArUco参考位姿将像素坐标转换为机器人坐标；颜色分类器标注类别。根据检测到的朝向角度决定运动策略：接近手臂固定接近方向的直接抓取，陡峭角度的通过矫正扫掠重新定向直至可抓取，再分拣至对应颜色容器。

Result: 在326次记录的运动中（覆盖7种书写工具），机械臂完成196次直接抓取和130次矫正扫掠通过，成功纠正了高达90度的错位。

Conclusion: 聪明的任务导向工程能够补偿缺失的自由度，使低成本四自由度机械臂在这类颜色分拣任务中表现良好，为低成本平台的应用提供了可能性。

Abstract: Low-cost four-degree-of-freedom (DoF) arms are among the most accessible robotic platforms. But they are, in theory, underactuated for picking up in situations where objects are at arbitrary orientations, a task that appears to require five degrees of freedom: the planar position (x and y), the height (z), a wrist rotation to align the gripper with the object, and gripper actuation, of which a four-DoF arm lacks the wrist rotation. This work shows that perception and motion planning can enable such an arm, a roughly $200 Waveshare RoArm-M2-S, under a fixed overhead camera to detect and color-sort writing utensils without that joint. A YOLO11n-OBB (You Only Look Once, oriented bounding box) detector locates each writing utensil; camera intrinsics and an ArUco reference pose convert its pixel coordinates to robot coordinates; and a color classifier labels it. The detected orientation angle determines the motion strategy: utensils close to the arm's fixed approach direction are picked up directly, and those at steeper angles are reoriented via corrective sweeps until they are graspable, after which they are picked up and sorted into the assigned color bin. Across 326 logged motions on seven writing utensils, the arm made 196 direct grasps and 130 corrective sweep passes, correcting misalignments up to 90 degrees, suggesting that clever task-informed engineering can compensate for a missing degree of freedom on tasks like this one.

</details>


### [271] [Learning Varying Physical Therapist-Patient Interactions for Robot-mediated Upper Limb Task-Specific Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15995)
*Jia Quan Loh,Vincent Crocher,Marlena Klaic,Denny Oetomo,Ying Tan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于任务参数化高斯混合模型（TPGMM）的从示范学习框架，用于学习康复机器人中的个性化物理治疗师-患者交互，通过少量示范将患者关节运动学映射到治疗师施加的扭矩，并泛化到新的任务变体。在14对模拟治疗师-患者组合、3个任务（每个6种变体）上与查找表方法对比，TPGMM略优，且两者在任务复杂度增加时更接近真实交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 康复机器人虽然能增加任务特异性训练（TST）剂量并解放治疗师，但尚未证明比传统治疗有显著优势。作者认为这与机器人无法准确模拟个性化物理治疗师-患者交互以及TST中缺乏练习变异性有关，因此主张保留个性化交互的机器人康复干预。

Method: 提出Learning-from-Demonstration框架：使用Task-Parameterised Gaussian Mixture Models (TPGMM) 从少量示范中学习个性化治疗师-患者物理交互，将患者关节运动学映射到治疗师施加扭矩，并在新任务变体下泛化重建治疗师扭矩。在14对模拟治疗师-患者对、3个复杂度递增任务（各6个变体）上评估，与Look-Up Table进行基准对比。

Result: TPGMM和LUT两种方法均能复现未见任务变体中的交互，与真实交互略有偏差；TPGMM略微优于LUT；两种方法在任务复杂度增加时复现的交互越来越接近真实交互。

Conclusion: TPGMM框架展示了从少数示范中学习个性化治疗师-患者交互并泛化到新任务变体的可行性，为设计保留个性化交互的康复机器人提供了技术基础，但改进空间仍存在。

Abstract: Upper extremity motor function recovery is positively linked to Task-Specific Training (TST) and sufficient therapy dosage. Rehabilitation robots can increase TST dosage via controlled, repetitive treatment and free therapists to simultaneously manage other patients, but it has yet to demonstrate significant benefits over conventional treatment. This is potentially linked to inaccurate robotic representation of personalised physical therapist-patient interaction and lack of practice variability during TST. Hence, we advocate for robotic interventions that preserve the personalised physical therapist-patient interactions when delivering TST for patients across varying practise conditions. We propose a Learning-from-Demonstration framework using Task-Parameterised Gaussian Mixture Models (TPGMM) to learn personalised physical therapist-patient interaction in Task-Specific exercises, mapping patient joint kinematics to therapist-applied torques using few demonstrations. The model is generalised to reconstruct therapist torques in new task variations. The framework was evaluated on physical interactions from 14 mock "therapist-patient" pairs over three tasks of increasing complexity, each with six variations. A benchmark comparison against a Look-Up Table was conducted. The results show both methods reproducing interactions in unseen task variations that deviate slightly from the actual interaction, with TPGMM slightly outperforming LUT. Both methods reproduced interactions that gets increasingly closer to the actual interaction as task complexity increases.

</details>


### [272] [Benchmarking Identity-Sensitive LLM Outputs for Surveillance and Security Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16030)
*Nneka Hyman,Jasmine Khan,Raj Korpan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文评估大语言模型(LLM)在生成监控与安防机器人设计描述时，身份条件化提示(identity-conditioned prompts)是否会产生系统性差异。使用236个不同的人口统计身份标签，在单标签和模型增强提示条件下分析生成文本的可读性，发现不同的提示条件、设计维度和人口身份之间在可读性上存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地用于生成机器人设计规格、交互策略和风险评估，这些输出可能影响监控和安防机器人的概念化与实际部署。需要评估LLM是否在生成内容中对不同身份群体存在系统性偏差，以确保公平性和可访问性。

Method: 使用236个人口统计身份标签，构建单标签和模型增强两种提示条件，让LLM生成监控与安防机器人设计描述。以可读性(readability)作为初始基准指标，分析不同提示条件、设计维度和身份标签之间的差异。该基准框架还计划扩展到词汇、语义、情感、句法和公平性分析。

Result: 研究发现，不同提示条件、设计维度和人口身份之间在生成文本的可读性上存在显著差异。这表明LLM生成的机器人设计描述确实受身份条件化提示的影响。

Conclusion: 可读性本身无法判断输出是否公平或适应当前社会规范，但它为更广泛的基准评估框架提供了可解释的基线。研究为后续词汇、语义、情感、句法和公平性分析奠定了基础。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to generate textual robot design specifications, interaction policies, and risk assessments during early-stage robot development. Such outputs may influence how surveillance and security robots are conceptualized, documented, and ultimately implemented. This paper evaluates whether identity-conditioned prompts produce systematic differences in LLM-generated surveillance and security robot design descriptions. Using 236 demographic identity labels across single-label and model-augmented prompt conditions, we analyze readability as an initial benchmark for evaluating accessibility and identity-conditioned variation in generated robot design descriptions. The results show significant differences in readability across prompt conditions, design dimensions, and demographic identities. Although readability cannot determine whether an output is fair or socially appropriate, it provides an interpretable baseline within a broader benchmarking framework that also includes lexical, semantic, sentiment, syntactic, and fairness-focused analyses.

</details>


### [273] [ScenarioCharacterization: A Modular Toolkit for Characterizing Safety across Trajectory Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16041)
*Ingrid Navarro,Yutong Duan,Jonathan Francis,Jean Oh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍ScenarioCharacterization，一个开源的、数据集无关的驾驶场景分析框架。它采用模块化、配置驱动的三层流水线：数据集适配器（将自定义数据集映射到开放场景表示）、特征提取/行为探测/关键性评分的特征化层、以及可视化与分析层。各层仅通过Pydantic验证的模式通信，新数据集可轻松接入。报告展示了Waymo Open Motion、Argoverse2和nuPlan上的示例输出，并讨论了下游应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 驾驶场景的自动化特征化对于自动驾驶系统的评估与理解至关重要，但现有工具往往绑定特定数据集格式，导致数据适配成本高、难以复用。作者希望提供一个通用、可扩展的框架，使研究人员能以统一方式分析来自不同数据集的驾驶行为。

Method: 框架由三层组成：(1) 数据集适配器层，将自定义数据集映射到开放的Scenario表示；(2) 特征化层，执行特征提取、行为探测（behavior probing）和场景级与智能体级的关键性评分；(3) 分析层，提供场景可视化、特征/评分/探测分析。各层通过Pydantic验证的schema和配置解耦通信，新数据集只需编写适配器即可复用现有分析栈。

Result: 在Waymo Open Motion、Argoverse2和nuPlan三个主流数据集上展示了框架的示例输出，包括特征提取结果、行为探测和关键性评分，验证了其数据集无关性和模块化设计。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 该框架为驾驶场景分析提供了标准化、可复用的基础设施，降低了不同轨迹数据集之间分析的迁移成本，可服务于场景挖掘、行为理解、自动驾驶验证等下游任务。

Abstract: We introduce ScenarioCharacterization, an open-source framework for automated, dataset-agnostic profiling of driving scenarios in trajectory datasets. Our framework is packaged as a modular, configuration-driven pipeline of three layers: a dataset adapter that maps custom datasets onto an open Scenario representation, a characterizer that performs feature extraction, behavior probing, and criticality scoring at scenario and agent levels, and an analysis layer for scenario visualization and feature, score, and probe analyses. Because the layers communicate only through Pydantic-validated schemas composed via configurations, a new dataset can easily plug in without rewriting the characterization and analysis stack.
  This technical report describes the design and APIs, shows example outputs on Waymo Open Motion, Argoverse2, and nuPlan, and discusses downstream uses of the approach. The framework is available at https://github.com/navarrs/ScenarioCharacterization.

</details>


### [274] [SurgVIL: Scaling Surgical Robot Imitation Learning with Open-source Surgical Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16058)
*Xinhao Chen,JuoTung Chen,Nigel Nelson,Antony Goldenberg,Jesse Haworth,Sean D. Huver,Axel Krieger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SurgVIL利用开源手术视频为无动作标签的影像提供弱监督运动估计，结合带运动标签的phantom机器人演示数据，共同训练手术机器人模仿学习策略，在针拾取和胆囊切除两个da Vinci任务上显著提升对真实组织和分布外场景的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于学习的手术机器人自主性需要大量同步视频+机器人动作的示教数据，但临床/真实组织场景中这类数据极为稀少（机器人运动学通常仅在受控研究系统中可用）；phantom数据有精确动作标签却缺乏视觉多样性，两者难以兼得。

Method: SurgVIL框架：将带有运动学标签的phantom机器人示教与开源手术视频数据结合，对无运动标签的视频通过估计近似运动学作为弱监督信号，共同用于策略学习。在ACT、π0、GR00T-H等多个骨干策略上进行评估。

Result: 在两个da Vinci机器人任务（针拾取、胆囊切除切割）上，加入手术视频后，ACT、π0、GR00T-H三种骨干策略对真实组织和分布外场景的泛化均有显著提升。

Conclusion: SurgVIL展示了从phantom训练利用开源手术视频扩展数据从而实现可泛化手术机器人策略的可扩展路径，为缓解手术机器人模仿学习数据瓶颈提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Learning-based surgical robot autonomy requires large-scale demonstrations with synchronized videos and robot actions, but such data are exceedingly rare in clinical or realistic tissue settings because robot kinematics are typically inaccessible outside controlled research systems. In contrast, phantom data collected on research platforms provide accurate action labels but lack the visual diversity of real tissue. We propose SurgVIL, a framework for scaling surgical robot imitation learning using open-source surgical videos. SurgVIL combines kinematically labeled phantom robot demonstrations with surgical videos from open-source datasets and online sources for policy learning. Since these videos lack robot motion labels, we estimate approximate kinematics as weak supervision. We evaluate SurgVIL on two da Vinci robot tasks: needle pick-up and cholecystectomy cutting. Across ACT, $π_0$, and GR00T-H backbones, adding surgical videos substantially improves generalization to real-tissue and out-of-distribution settings, suggesting a scalable path from phantom training toward generalizable surgical robot policies.

</details>


### [275] [US-VLA: An Ultrasound Vision-Language-Action Model for Embodied Abdomina](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16074)
*Cheng Zhang,Xingzheng Wu,Guihao Yan,Xifeng Hu,Zhi Liu,Mei Wu,Qing Cai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出US-VLA，一种用于自动超声扫描的视觉-语言-动作模型，通过超声感知专家融合模块整合临床语义目标和实时超声反馈，基于包含80,000时间步的真实数据集（US-VLA-Data）进行训练，在肝脏和肾脏超声探查任务中取得竞争性表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习和学习辅助超声扫描方法依赖精心设计的奖励函数或大量交互数据，泛化能力和稳定性受限，难以适应不同设备、患者群体和复杂临床场景。需要一种能显式编码临床语义目标并生成顺序探头操作动作的方法。

Method: 提出US-VLA架构，包含：1）超声感知专家融合模块，联合整合超声观测与辅助上下文信息，使语义超声反馈有效引导扫描过程；2）构建US-VLA-Data真实世界数据集，覆盖肝脏和肾脏检查，含5个临床定义标准切面和320条专家扫描轨迹（约80,000同步时间步）。模型生成顺序探头操作动作。

Result: 在超声探头操作任务上取得竞争性表现，在评估的腹部超声场景中展示了有效性和良好的泛化能力。代码已开源。

Conclusion: US-VLA表明将临床语义目标显式编码进VLA框架可以有效提升自动超声扫描的可靠性和泛化能力，为医疗影像中的自主操控提供了新范式。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence-assisted ultrasound scanning enhances diagnostic reliability and efficiency by providing real-time guidance for standardized image acquisition and reducing operator dependence. However, existing reinforcement learning and learning-assisted ultrasound scanning methods typically rely on carefully designed reward functions or extensive interaction data, which limits their generalization ability and stability across different devices, patient populations, and complex clinical scenarios. To address these challenges, we propose an ultrasound vision-language-action model (US-VLA) for automated ultrasound scanning that explicitly encodes clinical semantic goals and generates sequential probe manipulation actions under real-time ultrasound feedback. In particular, we first design an ultrasound-aware expert fusion module to jointly integrate ultrasound observations with auxiliary contextual information, enabling semantic ultrasound feedback to effectively guide the scanning process. Then, we construct US-VLA-Data, a real-world dataset covering liver and kidney examinations, which includes five clinically defined standard planes and comprises 320 expert scanning trajectories with approximately 80,000 synchronized timesteps. Extensive experiments demonstrate that US-VLA achieves competitive performance in ultrasound probe manipulation tasks, indicating its effectiveness and promising generalization within the evaluated abdominal ultrasound setting. The source code is available at https://github.com/VMVLab/US-VLA.

</details>


### [276] [Unified Condition-Action Modeling for Accurate One-Step Action Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16153)
*Xinyu Zhou,Zikun Cai,Kuangji Zuo,Gen Li,Boyu Ma,Yanshuo Lu,Yutong Song,Mingqi Yuan,Jiayu Chen,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了UCA-Flow，一个统一条件-动作建模框架，将观察条件、时间步条件、区间条件和动作token统一到共享的token空间中，通过Unified Condition-Action Transformer进行联合表示学习，实现单步动作生成，显著提升成功率并大幅加速推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作需要既准确又高效的策略，但现有的扩散和流策略通常将条件视为辅助信号，而非与动作轨迹联合演化。这种限制导致了性能瓶颈，作者希望用简洁有效的统一条件-动作建模设计来缓解这个问题。

Method: 将观察条件、时间步条件、区间条件和动作token统一为单一序列，使用Unified Condition-Action Transformer进行联合条件-动作表示学习。条件表示根据当前生成阶段动态重构，并引入改进的双遍监督方案（对u和v）以增强统一条件-动作建模的优化。

Result: 较最强基线平均成功率提升9.3个百分点；相比DP3和Simple DP3分别获得45.6倍和33.4倍加速；相比单步FlowPolicy和MP1分别快4.3倍和2.3倍。

Conclusion: 统一条件-动作建模是一种简单而有效的设计，将条件和动作放在共享token空间中进行联合学习，可以在显著提升推理速度的同时提高成功率，为机器人操作策略提供了一种高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Robot manipulation requires policies that are both accurate and efficient, as robot control must respond to changing observations under tight latency constraints. Recent diffusion and flow policies are promising, but they often treat conditions as auxiliary signals rather than jointly evolving them with action trajectories. We find that this limitation can be effectively mitigated by a \textbf{simple yet effective unified condition-action modeling design} that represents conditions and actions in a shared token space, allowing a compact model to achieve high performance while improving both inference speed and accuracy. Therefore, we propose UCA-Flow, a unified condition-action modeling framework for accurate one-step action generation. Our method unifies observation conditions, timestep conditions, interval conditions, and action tokens into a single sequence, and processes them with a Unified Condition-Action Transformer for joint condition-action representation learning. As a result, condition representations are dynamically reconstructed according to the current generation stage, highlighting information most relevant for action refinement. Furthermore, we introduce an improved dual-pass supervision scheme over $u$ and $v$ for stronger optimization of unified condition-action modeling. UCA-Flow improves the average success rate by 9.3 percentage points over the strongest baseline, while achieving $45.6\times$ and $33.4\times$ speedups over DP3 and Simple DP3, and remaining $4.3\times$ and $2.3\times$ faster than one-step FlowPolicy and MP1, respectively.

</details>


### [277] [SparkVLA: Stop-Aware Hierarchical VLA with Adaptive Action Chunking for Long-Horizon Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16172)
*Xunyao Lei,Renjun Wu,Tianlin Huo,Xuesong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SparkVLA 将层次化 VLA 系统中"何时终止子任务"与"行动块执行多长"这两个相互依赖的决策统一为一个排序问题：Stop 与所有 action-prefix 长度在同一候选集中竞争，选择得分最高的选项，从而消除阈值调参并仅需离线序数偏好。同时引入 Anchor-Conditioned Context Encoding 和 Stop-Aware Action-Prefix Selection head，在 RoboCerebra 上达到 47.12% 成功率，显著超越基线，并在真实机器人多步任务上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有层次化 VLA 架构在每次重观测时需独立决策"何时停止当前子任务"和"行动块执行多长"，但这两个决策互相依赖（最优停止点取决于执行器计划，最优执行长度取决于子任务边界），孤立评估导致不对称性，单一模块无法弥补。

Method: 1) 将停止决策与行动前缀长度决策统一为单次排序：Stop 令牌与每个 action-prefix 长度在同一候选集内竞争，选最高分。2) Anchor-Conditioned Context Encoding 模块：缓存历史感知的子任务锚点编码（起始状态记忆 + 目标语义），引导视觉 token 剪枝到任务相关区域。3) Stop-Aware Action-Prefix Selection head：在 chunk 边界通过全自注意力对所有候选评分。仅需离线序数偏好监督，无需阈值调优。

Result: 在 RoboCerebra 基准上实现 47.12% 成功率，超过官方层次化基线 30.57%，超过最强可复现方法 26.83%；真实机器人多步任务实验进一步验证了物理硬件上的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过将停止与执行长度两个相互依赖的决策统一为单次排序，SparkVLA 解决了层次化 VLA 中界面决策的耦合问题，在基准和真实机器人上均取得显著提升，展示了该统一决策框架的有效性与实用性。

Abstract: At every re-observation point in a hierarchical Vision-Language-Action (VLA) system, two interface decisions must be made: when to terminate the current subtask and how far to execute the proposed action chunk. These decisions are mutually dependent---the optimal stopping point depends on what the executor plans to do, while the optimal execution length depends on where the subtask boundary lies---yet existing architectures evaluate them in isolation, an asymmetry neither module can overcome alone. We present SparkVLA, a stop-aware hierarchical VLA that resolves this mutual dependency by formulating both decisions as a single ranking: Stop competes against every action-prefix length in a unified candidate set, and the system selects the highest-scoring option, eliminating threshold tuning and requiring only offline ordinal preferences. An Anchor-Conditioned Context Encoding module caches a history-aware subtask anchor encoding onset-state memory and goal semantics, guiding visual-token pruning toward task-relevant regions; a Stop-Aware Action-Prefix Selection head scores all candidates via full self bnattention at chunk boundaries for efficiency. On RoboCerebra, SparkVLA achieves 47.12% success rate, surpassing the official hierarchical baseline by 30.57% and the strongest reproducible method by 26.83% Real-robot experiments on multi-step tasks further validate these gains on physical hardware.

</details>


### [278] [RoboStriker: Latent-Space Strategic Games for Autonomous Humanoid Boxing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16195)
*Kangning Yin,Kaige Liu,Zhe Cao,Wentao Dong,Weishuai Zeng,Tianyi Zhang,Qiang Zhang,Jingbo Wang,Jiangmiao Pang,Yang Li,Ming Zhou,Weinan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RoboStriker，一种用于人形机器人拳击任务的分层框架，将高维原始动作空间中的多智能体强化学习问题转化为两玩家潜空间零和马尔可夫博弈，通过预训练运动解码器约束策略探索，并在真实人形机器人上成功部署验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在接触丰富且高度动态的任务（如拳击）中面临战略智能与物理可行性的根本冲突：直接在多智能体强化学习原始运动空间探索会导致关节级物理崩溃，无法涌现出可行的战斗策略。

Method: 提出RoboStriker分层框架：1) 将预定义拳击动作的跟踪能力蒸馏为拓扑有界潜流形作为动作基础；2) 在潜空间中通过两玩家zero-sum马尔可夫博弈建模；3) 使用Latent-Space Neural Fictitious Self-Play驱动多智能体协同进化；4) 理论上证明潜空间公式等价于解码器可达动作流形上的博弈，提供近似Nash解释。

Result: 实验表明在结构化潜空间中博弈显著优于直接在原始动作空间探索，大幅减少灾难性平衡失败，在竞技胜率和打击效率上取得优越战术表现，并成功部署到真实人形机器人上。

Conclusion: 通过预训练运动解码器约束战略探索，可以解决人形机器人在复杂动态对抗任务中策略探索与物理可行性之间的冲突，实现可部署的实战级对抗策略。

Abstract: Achieving human-level competitive intelligence and physical agility in humanoid robots remains a profound challenge, particularly in contact-rich and highly dynamic tasks such as boxing. While Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning offers a principled framework for strategic interaction, its direct application to unstructured raw motor spaces inevitably leads to joint-level physical collapse, preventing the emergence of any viable combat tactics. To resolve this fundamental conflict between strategic exploration and physical feasibility, we formulate the humanoid combat task as a novel two-player latent-space zero-sum Markov game. Under standard regularity and approximate best-response assumptions, we show that the latent formulation induces an equivalent game over the decoder-reachable action manifold, providing an approximate-Nash interpretation of the resulting self-play dynamics. To instantiate this theoretical formulation, we propose RoboStriker, a hierarchical framework that decouples high-level reasoning from low-level execution. It first distills the tracking expertise of predefined boxing motions into a topologically bounded latent manifold. This structured latent foundation subsequently drives multi-agent co-evolution via Latent-Space Neural Fictitious Self-Play. Extensive experimental results demonstrate that gaming within this structured latent space substantially outperforms direct exploration. By constraining strategic exploration through a pretrained motion decoder, RoboStriker substantially reduces the catastrophic balance failures observed in raw action-space methods and achieves superior tactical performance in both competitive win rates and striking efficiency. Finally, we successfully deploy and validate our learned combat policies on real-world humanoid robots. Our code and video and supplementary materials are available at RoboStriker.

</details>


### [279] [Deep Probabilistic Indoor Gas Source Localization via Physical Dependency-Guided Sequential Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16221)
*Seunghwan Kim,Hyungjin Kim,Junhee Lee,Hyondong Oh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个深度概率框架，通过序列条件推理（先推风场与浓度场，再推断源位置后验）解决室内机器人气体源定位问题，在仿真与真实机器人（嵌入式GPU）上均验证了在线可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内气体源定位对工业与城市安全至关重要，但墙壁和障碍物与气流相互作用产生复杂气体弥散。高保真模型（CFD、filament模型）能捕捉这些效应但计算成本过高，难以在线使用。现有端到端模型不利用气体传输的物理依赖关系，在稀疏噪声观测下性能有限。

Method: 提出深度概率框架，将室内气体传输的物理依赖关系（风场和源位置共同决定浓度场）嵌入序列条件推理中：先推断风场与浓度场，再以这些推断为条件引导源位置后验估计。该结构利用稀疏噪声的移动机器人测量数据进行推理。

Result: 在仿真中优于代表性GSL基线方法，支持准确高效的主动气体源定位；真实机器人实验证明了在嵌入式GPU上进行在线运行的可行性。

Conclusion: 通过嵌入物理依赖的序列条件推理结构，可在稀疏噪声观测下显著提升室内气体源定位精度，且具备真实世界在线部署能力。

Abstract: Reliable gas source localization (GSL) is critical to safety in industrial and urban environments, yet remains challenging indoors because walls and obstacles interact with airflow to create complex gas dispersion. High-fidelity models such as computational fluid dynamics and filament models can capture these effects, but their computational cost limits online use. We propose a deep probabilistic framework that infers the source posterior from sparse and noisy measurements collected by a mobile robot. Unlike end-to-end models that directly infer source estimates from measurements, the proposed method incorporates physical dependencies of indoor gas transport, where wind and source location govern the concentration field. These dependencies are embedded through sequential conditional inference, in which inferred wind and concentration fields guide source posterior estimation. This structure improves localization under sparse and noisy observations. Evaluations show that the proposed method outperforms representative GSL baselines and enables accurate and efficient active GSL in simulations. Real-robot experiments demonstrate the feasibility of online operation on an embedded GPU.

</details>


### [280] [HiPHI: A Large-Scale Benchmark for High-Precision Human Motion and Object-Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16222)
*Jiahao Ji,Ji Ma,Runhan Zhang,Runyi Yu,Wenjia Wang,Weiheng Chi,Qianqian Peng,Weichao Yan,Yongfei Gu,Ye Tian,Ting Wu,Longwei Li,Chun Yuan,Ruoli Dai,Lei Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HiPHI是一个600+小时规模的高保真全身人体运动数据集，基于FrameNet语言学框架指导设计，通过光学动作捕捉管线采集，提供亚毫米级标记点精度和网格级物体轨迹，配套基准套件评估运动空间多样性、交互接地、物体一致性与物理AI应用，显著扩展了现有运动数据集的覆盖范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身数据集存在根本性矛盾：互联网规模视频数据缺乏精确物理状态和交互接地，而实验室运动数据集保真度高但行为覆盖狭窄。这种不匹配成为可扩展人形策略学习的关键瓶颈，需要在大规模、高保真和运动/交互流形覆盖之间取得系统性的平衡。

Method: HiPHI采用FrameNet语言学框架作为理论指导来系统组织人体运动原语，使用光学动作捕捉管线采集数据，实现全身运动的全身体捕获和亚毫米级空间标记追踪精度，同时记录网格级物体轨迹以提供物理交互接地。配套设计基准套件评估不同维度的性能。

Result: 分析表明HiPHI相比现有运动数据集显著扩展了运动覆盖范围，同时保持高保真交互质量，为真实世界具身任务中的人形策略训练、评估和泛化建立了可扩展的数据基础，其扩展同样适用于计算机图形学中的运动先验模型。

Conclusion: HiPHI通过理论引导的数据采集策略解决了人形智能学习中数据规模与保真度的矛盾，为物理AI与人形策略学习提供了规模化的数据基础，且具有跨领域（图形学运动先验）的适用性。

Abstract: Humanoid intelligence requires learning over an extremely diverse space of whole-body motions and physically grounded interactions. However, existing embodied datasets remain fundamentally limited: internet-scale video data lack precise physical states and interaction grounding, while laboratory motion datasets provide high fidelity but only narrow behavioral coverage. This mismatch creates a critical bottleneck for scalable humanoid policy learning. We present HiPHI, a 600+ hour scale high-fidelity whole-body human motion dataset designed to systematically maximize coverage of the human motion and interaction manifold. HiPHI is theoretically guided by FrameNet, a linguistic framework organizing human primitives. Created using an optical motion capture pipeline, HiPHI provides sub-millimeter spatial marker tracking accuracy for full-body human motion and mesh-level object trajectories. We further introduce a benchmark suite evaluating motion-space diversity, interaction grounding, object consistency, and physical AI applications. Our analyses demonstrate that HiPHI significantly expands motion coverage compared to existing motion datasets while maintaining high-fidelity interaction quality, and establishes a scalable data foundation for training, evaluating, and generalizing humanoid policies in real-world embodied tasks, where similar extensions are also applicable to motion prior models in computer graphics.

</details>


### [281] [Planner-Conditioned Diffusion for Coordinated Multi-Agent Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16229)
*Marcus Yu Siong Teo,Jeric Lew,Tanishq Duhan,Guillaume Sartoretti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种规划器条件扩散策略（PCDP），用于图结构多智能体探索。通过将规划器身份作为显式条件输入，训练单一共享模型学习多模态轨迹分布，并利用局部重排序在相邻智能体间选择预测重叠最小的轨迹组合来实现协调。在100张留出地图的四智能体仿真中匹配了扩散基线成功率，同时改善多项团队指标，并通过双智能体真实机器人实验验证了智能体间的涌现空间分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体协同探索需要高效个体覆盖与非冗余团队覆盖，但传统方法依赖手工协调规则，端到端多智能体学习方法难以扩展和训练。现有的扩散式规划器（如DARE）虽然能生成长视界轨迹，但训练于狭窄的规划器分布上，限制了行为多样性和推断期可控性，因此需要一种能学习多模态轨迹分布并支持推断期可控性的方法。

Method: PCDP在多种规划器风格的示范上训练，以规划器身份作为显式条件输入，使单一共享模型学习多模态轨迹分布并从同一观测生成多样化、可控的轨迹候选。不端到端学习协调，而是将多模态单智能体策略复用于所有智能体，通过局部重排序引入协调——邻近智能体联合选择预测重叠最小的轨迹组合。评估采用四智能体仿真和双智能体真实机器人实验。

Result: 在100张留出地图的四智能体仿真中，PCDP匹配了扩散基线的完美成功率，同时改善了平均最大智能体行进距离、团队总行进距离和智能体不平衡指标。消融实验表明仅对单规划器基线做重排序只带来边际收益，说明规划器条件多模态是协调改进的主要贡献因素。真实机器人双智能体实验验证了多样性长视界轨迹生成可产生智能体间的涌现空间分离。

Conclusion: 规划器条件化是扩散式多智能体探索的关键设计，通过多模态轨迹分布学习和局部重排序的组合，可以在无需显式排斥机制的情况下实现有效的涌现空间协调。该方法不需要端到端联合训练，具有良好的可扩展性和推断期可控性。

Abstract: Coordinated multi-agent exploration requires not only efficient individual coverage but also non-redundant coverage across agents over extended planning horizons. Conventional approaches rely on hand-crafted coordination rules, while end-to-end multi-agent learning methods are difficult to scale and train. Diffusion-based planners such as DARE offer a promising alternative by generating long-horizon trajectories instead of single-step actions, but existing methods are trained on a narrow planner distribution, limiting behavioral diversity and inference-time controllability. We propose a Planner-Conditioned Diffusion Policy (PCDP) for graph-based multi-agent exploration. PCDP is trained on demonstrations from multiple planner styles with planner identity as an explicit conditioning input, enabling a single shared model to learn a multimodal trajectory distribution and generate diverse, controllable trajectory candidates from the same observation. Rather than learning coordination end-to-end, we reuse this multimodal single-agent policy across all agents and introduce coordination through local reranking, in which nearby agents jointly select the trajectory combination with minimal predicted overlap. We evaluate PCDP against classical and diffusion-based baselines on 100 held-out maps in a four-agent simulation setting. PCDP matches the perfect success rate of the diffusion-based baselines while improving mean max-agent travel, total team travel, and agent imbalance. Crucially, reranking alone over a single-planner baseline yields only marginal gains, indicating that planner-conditioned multimodality is the main contributor to improved coordination. Qualitative simulation results and real-robot experiments with two agents further validate that diverse long-horizon trajectory generation produces emergent spatial separation between agents without any explicit repulsion mechanism.

</details>


### [282] [Cyclops: LiDAR as a Camera That Dreams in Color](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16264)
*Wei Gao,Jian Shu,Mingle Zhao,Maani Ghaffari,David Kong,Chengzhong Xu,Hui Kong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Cyclops框架将稀疏非重复扫描LiDAR强度数据翻译为RGB视频，实现全天候无需相机的感知推理。该方法先用冻结的预训练稠密化模块将稀疏LiDAR强度投影转为稠密表示，再通过Latent Bridge Matching（LBM）学习的速度场在几步ODE积分内将稠密强度潜在表征迁移到RGB目标分布。为减轻帧间闪烁，注入前帧上下文，并将速度场构建为可微终端奖励优化的策略。实验表明在近黑暗条件下合成的RGB也能让标准RGB感知模型在语义分割、车道检测和点云着色上大幅超越LiDAR基线和传统相机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机在低光或高动态范围环境下性能严重退化，而LiDAR虽对照明不敏感但数据为单通道且稀疏，与基于RGB预训练的视觉模型存在巨大模态鸿沟。作者希望实现全天候的"免相机"感知推理，同时充分利用LiDAR固有的照明不变性。

Method: 1) 使用冻结的预训练稠密化模块将稀疏NRS-LiDAR强度投影转为稠密表示作为几何丰富的条件源；2) 通过Latent Bridge Matching（LBM）学习速度场，用少量ODE积分步将稠密强度潜在表征输送到RGB分布；3) 通过时间注意力层注入前帧上下文以缓解闪烁；4) 将速度场形式化为策略，使用可微终端奖励沿ODE轨迹反向传播优化，强化终端保真度。

Result: 合成的RGB图像（包括近黑暗条件下生成的）使标准RGB感知模型在语义分割、车道检测和点云颜色化任务上，在多种光照条件下显著优于LiDAR基线和传统相机方案。

Conclusion: Cyclops证明了从NRS-LiDAR到RGB视频的可学习翻译能够释放预训练RGB感知模型的全部能力，同时避免了对相机硬件及其光照退化问题的依赖，为全天候感知提供了一个有效的替代路径。

Abstract: Conventionally, robotic perception relies heavily on cameras due to the rich semantic texture they provide. However, their performance degrades significantly in low-light or high-dynamic-range environments. Conversely, while Light Detection and Ranging (LiDAR) captures illumination-invariant geometric and intensity properties, the resulting data are typically single-channel and sparse, creating a significant modality gap when applying vision models pre-trained on RGB datasets. In this paper, we propose Cyclops, a framework that translates sparse Non-Repetitive Scanning LiDAR (NRS-LiDAR) intensity into RGB video, enabling camera-free inference for all-day perception tasks. Our approach first converts sparse LiDAR intensity projections into dense representations via a frozen pre-trained densification module, serving as a geometrically rich source condition. The dense intensity latent is then transported toward the target RGB distribution through Latent Bridge Matching (LBM) with a learned velocity field in a few ODE integration steps. To mitigate inter-frame flickering, we inject prior-frame context via temporal attention layers and further formulate the velocity field as a policy optimized by a differentiable terminal reward that encourages terminal fidelity through backpropagation along the ODE trajectory. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the synthesized RGB, including those generated under near-dark conditions, enable standard RGB-based perception models to substantially outperform both LiDAR baselines and conventional cameras on semantic segmentation, lane detection, and point cloud colorization across diverse lighting conditions.

</details>


### [283] [Marker-Constrained Pose-Graph Correction for Cross-Platform Georeferencing in GNSS-Denied Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16281)
*Marco Giberna,Jose Luis Sanchez Lopez,Holger Voos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种在GNSS拒止环境下，使用伪装匹配的胆甾球形反射器（CSR）制成的fiducial markers作为预先勘测的视觉锚点，对轻量级LiDAR-odometry轨迹和RTAB-Map稠密重建进行地理配准的框架。无需操作期间GNSS测量，即可将输出统一表达在LUREF坐标系中。单个iMarker在六个勘测位置间移动，重新访问第一位置进行漂移校正评估，结果表明重访不一致性降低97.9%（UAV模拟）和99.1%（UGV模拟），跨会话稠密重建中位最近邻距离58cm。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在GNSS拒止环境中（如室内、地下、密集城区），自主系统需要异构建图管线维持一致的空间基准。现有方法依赖GNSS进行地理配准，在信号缺失时无法将局部里程计/建图输出统一到全球坐标系，限制了多会话、多平台的协同操作。

Method: 使用胆甾球形反射器构造的伪装匹配fiducial markers作为预制勘测的视觉地标。方法结合粗相似变换对齐与marker约束的位姿图优化：首先利用marker检测进行初步对齐，然后通过位姿图优化将LiDAR-odometry轨迹和RTAB-Map稠密重建同时约束到LUREF框架。使用单个可移动iMarker在六个勘测位置间进行评估。

Result: UAV模拟会话重访不一致性降低97.9%，UGV模拟会话降低99.1%；与一次性对齐相比，marker锚点校正改善了留存锚点预测精度；跨会话稠密重建中位最近邻距离为58cm（无需显式跨会话配准）；marker处理实时运行，轨迹校正每会话<0.25秒。

Conclusion: 证明了使用视觉上不显眼、预先勘测的视觉锚点，在GNSS拒止操作期间对轻量级里程计和稠密重建进行地理配准的概念验证。该方法无需额外传感器或操作期间GNSS信号即可实现跨会话一致性。

Abstract: Autonomous operation in GNSS-denied environments requires heterogeneous mapping pipelines to maintain a consistent spatial reference. This paper presents a framework using camouflage-matched fiducial markers fabricated from Cholesteric Spherical Reflectors (CSRs) as pre-surveyed visual anchors. The anchors georeference both a lightweight LiDAR-odometry trajectory and a dense RTAB-Map reconstruction, allowing their outputs to be expressed in a common LUREF frame (geodetic coordinate reference system used in Luxembourg) without requiring GNSS measurements during operation. The method combines coarse similarity alignment with marker-constrained pose-graph optimization. We evaluate it using two handheld acquisition sessions with ground-level and elevated motion profiles emulating UGV and UAV operation. A single iMarker was relocated among six surveyed positions, with the first position revisited to quantify drift correction. Marker-anchor correction reduced revisit inconsistency by 97.9% and 99.1% for the UAV- and UGV-emulating sessions, respectively, and improved held-out anchor prediction compared with one-time alignment. Separately georeferenced dense reconstructions achieved a median cross-session nearest-neighbour distance of 58 cm without explicit cross-session registration. Marker processing operated in real time, while trajectory correction required less than 0.25 s per session. These results demonstrate a proof of concept for georeferencing lightweight odometry and dense reconstructions using visually unobtrusive, pre-surveyed anchors during GNSS-denied operation.

</details>


### [284] [Readiness Barrier Functions: Forward-Invariant Control Authority for Overactuated Multirotor Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16335)
*Giuseppe Silano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将多旋翼执行器权限(authority)视为前向不变量的控制分配方案，通过控制障碍函数在分配零空间内以二次规划形式施加于阻力感知的权威余度量的对数行列式上，实现了贪心最大化方案的连续性改进，同时保留了基于力最小化方案的权威保证。单一设计不等式使认证集合紧致且严格位于执行器箱内，转子停用的预备成本对对称设计有闭式解；跟踪损失仅通过显式对齐比率体现，力误差有界为O(ρ^{-1/2})，并有鲁棒变体处理电机参数不确定性。在六旋翼与全驱动八旋翼上验证，闭式间隙与仿真达到机器精度，在权威稀缺区间贪心最大化违反认证下限并产生高达80倍的力误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过度驱动多旋翼的分配方案存在根本矛盾：贪心最大化预备度量的方案会在不连通的最优分层间跳变，产生任何电机都无法提供的加速度；而最小化力度的方案虽然连续，却无法保证力矩率权威(wrench-rate authority)保持在任何认证水平之上。作者旨在调和这两种方案，同时获得连续性与权威保证。

Method: 将权威视为前向不变量，利用控制障碍函数(CBF)对阻力感知的执行器权威余度量(drag-aware actuator-authority co-metric)的对数行列式施加约束。该约束通过在分配零空间内的二次规划(QP)以力矩级别强制执行。设计了一个单一设计不等式使认证集合紧致且严格位于执行器箱(actuator box)内部。对对称设计，任意转子停用的预备成本有闭式解ln(n/(n-m))。另提出鲁棒变体，通过独立于机架矩阵的闭式位移下界处理电机参数不确定性。

Result: 在六旋翼和全驱动八旋翼平台上，闭式间隙与仿真结果达到机器精度一致。在权威稀缺状态下，贪心最大化方法违反认证下限并产生高达80倍的力误差，而所提出的过滤器在可忽略的跟踪代价下保持了认证集合的不变性。跟踪损失仅通过显式对齐比率呈现，力误差有界为O(ρ^{-1/2})。

Conclusion: 该工作成功统一了贪心最大化与力度最小化两类分配方案的优点：既具备连续性，又能对力矩率权威提供可认证的保证。通过控制障碍函数框架将权威作为前向不变量处理，并给出闭式的设计分析，为多旋翼安全关键控制提供了理论基础与实用工具。

Abstract: Allocation schemes that greedily maximize a readiness metric over the actuator fiber bundle of an overactuated multirotor produce commands that jump between disconnected optimal strata, demanding actuator rates no motor can deliver; effort-minimizing schemes are continuous but cannot guarantee that wrench-rate authority stays above any certified level. We reconcile the two by treating authority as a forward-invariant quantity: a control barrier function on the log-determinant of the drag-aware actuator-authority co-metric, enforced at torque level by a quadratic program in the allocation null space. A single design inequality renders the certified set compact and strictly interior to the actuator box, with the readiness cost of any rotor deactivation given in closed form as $\ln(n/(n{-}m))$ for symmetric designs. Tracking is sacrificed only through an explicit alignment ratio, with wrench error bounded by $\mathcal{O}(ρ^{-1/2})$ and a robust variant handles motor-parameter uncertainty with a closed-form floor shift independent of the airframe matrix. On a hexarotor and a fully-actuated octorotor the closed-form gap matches simulation to machine precision; in the authority-scarce regime greedy maximization violates the certified floor and commits wrench errors up to eighty times larger than the proposed filter, which holds invariance of the certified set at negligible tracking cost.

</details>


### [285] [Arm-Aware Guided Dexterous Grasp Generation with Arm-Agnostic Grasp Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16351)
*Yongyi Jia,Yongpeng Jiang,Kangchen Lv,Yi Ren,Mingrui Yu,Xiang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种臂感知的灵巧手抓取生成框架，在推理时仅通过联合优化手部姿态和手臂构型，将预先训练好的臂无关抓取扩散模型引导至可行区域，无需重新训练或拒绝采样，即可高效处理手臂环境碰撞、工作空间边界抓取和连续抓取等约束场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手为中心（hand-centric）的抓取模型忽略手臂约束，而传统臂感知方法要么依赖拒绝采样导致低采样效率，要么需要在特定手臂数据上重训练导致泛化性差。实际场景中手臂相关的约束（环境碰撞、工作空间边界、连续抓取）至关重要。

Method: 将臂感知的约束抓取生成建模为手部姿态与手臂构型的联合优化问题，为手臂相关约束推导闭式梯度；假设手部姿态分布由扩散模型表示，证明基于梯度的优化等价于引导扩散采样（guided diffusion sampling），从而利用预训练的臂无关抓取扩散模型，仅在推理时注入手臂与环境信息。

Result: 在6个场景、超过1万个物体上进行了全面评估，证明了该框架在高度约束场景下生成可行抓取的显著更高概率，体现了其在真实应用中的优势。

Conclusion: 通过在推理时注入手臂和环境信息的引导扩散方法，可以高效复用臂无关的预训练抓取模型，同时兼顾可行性与泛化性，为实际机器人抓取部署提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Dexterous grasp generation that considers arm-related constraints is crucial in real-world scenarios involving arm environment collision avoidance, workspace boundary grasps, and consecutive grasping. Existing hand-centric grasp models, which primarily focus on the floating hand's pose, are insufficient for such cases. Conventional arm-aware methods either rely on rejection sampling to discard infeasible samples or require retraining on arm-specific data, leading to low sample efficiency under adverse conditions or limited generalization across different robots and environments. To overcome these limitations, this letter presents an arm-aware dexterous grasp generation framework that leverages pretrained arm-agnostic grasp models while integrating arm and environmental information only at inference time. Specifically, we formulate arm-aware constrained grasp generation as a joint optimization of hand pose and arm configuration, and derive closed-form gradients for arm-related constraints. Assuming the hand pose distribution is represented by a diffusion model, we prove that gradient-based optimization is equivalent to guided diffusion sampling, steering near-feasible samples toward the feasible region. Through comprehensive evaluation involving 10k objects across 6 scenarios, we demonstrate that the proposed framework generates feasible grasps in highly constrained settings with significantly higher probability, highlighting its advantages in real-world applications. Supplementary materials and appendix are available at https://arm-aware-dexgrasp.github.io/.

</details>


### [286] [Robot-Body-Aware Traversal Risk Graph Planning for Wheeled-Legged Robots in Complex Terrain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16433)
*Zhiqiao Guo,Bichi Zhang,Sören Schwertfeger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RB-TRG提出了一种面向轮腿机器人的车身感知TRG规划方法，通过沿图边和偏航扫掠采样定向矩形足迹，度量纵向支撑变化、横向倾角、地形干涉和不可信区域暴露，将边级地形风险搜索提升为航向和转弯感知的车身风险转移，并以A*在有序节点对状态上优化。在四类扫描地形和MuJoCo闭环实验中，RB-TRG减少了三项核心几何车身放置指标，端到端成功率从51.5%提升至68.5%，路径长度仅增加2.3%，并在IEEE ICRA 2026 Legged Robot Challenges中获得最佳自主与最佳机动性奖项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TRG虽然提供了紧凑的地形感知全局导航表示，但其原生成本在圆形节点邻域和边对齐地形区域上计算，而非机器人有朝向的车身足迹。对轮腿机器人而言，这一抽象可能遗漏部分支撑丧失和车身-地形干涉，尤其在转弯时，导致局部支撑结构失效和计划不可行。

Method: 在稀疏TRG表示基础上，沿图边和yaw扫掠采样有朝向的矩形足迹，测量纵向支撑变异性、横向倾角、地形干涉和对不可信地图区域的暴露；将均值和上尾特征纳入转移成本，通过A*对有序节点-对状态进行累积值最小化，保持TRG构建和规划接口不变。

Result: 同图对比研究中，RB-TRG在四项扫描地形环境中减少了核心几何车身放置指标；MuJoCo配对闭环试验中，端到端成功率从51.5%提升到68.5%，平均路径长度仅增加2.3%；Go2-W实机部署配合完整LiDAR导航栈，获得ICRA 2026 Legged Robot Challenges最佳自主与最佳机动性奖项。

Conclusion: 通过将地形风险建模提升到机器人有向车身足迹层面，RB-TRG在几乎不增加路径代价的前提下显著提升了轮腿机器人导航的鲁棒性和端到端成功率，且该方法保留了TRG的构建与接口，具备实际部署价值。

Abstract: Traversal Risk Graphs (TRGs) provide a compact, terrain-aware representation for global navigation, but native TRG costs are computed over circular node neighborhoods and edge-aligned terrain regions rather than the robot's oriented body footprint. For wheeled-legged robots, this abstraction can miss partial support loss and body-terrain interference, especially during turns. We present Robot-Body-Aware TRG planning (RB-TRG), which builds on the sparse TRG representation and lifts edge-wise terrain-risk search to heading- and turn-aware body-risk transitions. An oriented rectangular footprint is sampled along graph edges and yaw sweeps to measure longitudinal support variation, lateral inclination, terrain interference, and exposure to untrusted map regions. Mean-and-upper-tail features are incorporated into transition costs, whose accumulated value is minimized by A* over ordered node-pair states, preserving TRG construction and its planning interface. We evaluate RB-TRG in a same-graph study on four scanned terrain environments and in paired closed-loop MuJoCo trials. RB-TRG reduces the three core geometric body-placement metrics and increases end-to-end success from 51.5% to 68.5%, while increasing mean path length by 2.3%. A Go2-W deployment further demonstrates RB-TRG with a full LiDAR navigation stack, which received the Best Autonomy and Best Mobility awards at the IEEE ICRA 2026 Legged Robot Challenges. The code for RB-TRG is released at https://github.com/ZhiqiaoGuo/RB-TRG.

</details>


### [287] [Observation-Constrained Joint-Space Viewpoint Optimization for Robotic Inspection of Cylindrical Cavities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16442)
*Yuezhong Wang,Rongshen Yin,Bichi Zhang,Sören Schwertfeger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种全自主的圆柱腔体观测约束巡检方法，在机器人关节空间中通过多起点无导数搜索优化位姿，结合RGB感知前端（椭圆拟合估计开口中心与腔体轴）、碰撞感知运动规划和几何/射线验证，实现观测约束下的腔体底部检查。在Isaac Sim中92/100目标配置成功，平均底部可见性91.65%，优于基线（76/100, 84.3%），并在真实桌面和Unitree A2平台上完成验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机器人的观测类任务（如工业设施监测、基础设施维护、搜救）中，检查圆柱腔体底部是代表性挑战：机器人需在满足可见性、运动学、碰撞约束下精确定位相机。现有方法通常指定单一Cartesian相机位姿，会拒绝可达视点或关节极限余量差的配置，限制了任务成功率。

Method: 1) 将检查目标表示为有效观察几何的集合（而非单一位姿），参数化相机轴对准、横向偏移、轴向距离约束；2) RGB感知前端通过语义掩膜、弧支撑椭圆拟合及体/侧母线线索估计开口中心和方向腔体轴；3) 多起点无导数搜索以字典序优先级优化关节构型（先满足约束，再按运动经济性、关节极限余量、视角质量排序）；4) 碰撞感知运动规划器评估候选，执行后通过几何和射线法验证底部可见性。

Result: Isaac Sim中92/100目标配置成功（基线76/100），平均底部可见性91.65%（基线84.3%）；在桌面实验和Unitree A2机械臂平台上完成完整感知-规划-执行流水线验证。

Conclusion: 通过将观测目标建模为有效观察位姿集合而非单一Cartesian位姿，并在关节空间中进行多起点优化，可显著提升圆柱腔体检视任务的成功率与底部可见性，且已在真实硬件上验证可行性。

Abstract: Inspection is a core capability in many mobile robotics applications, including industrial facility monitoring, infrastructure maintenance, agriculture, and search and rescue. Observing the bottom of a cylindrical cavity, as required by ASTM search-task benchmarks for response robots, presents a representative challenge: the robot must position its camera precisely while satisfying visibility, kinematic, and collision constraints. This paper presents a fully autonomous method for observation-constrained inspection of cylindrical cavities in robot joint space. Rather than prescribing a single Cartesian camera pose, the method represents the inspection objective as a set of valid viewing geometries, thereby avoiding the rejection of reachable viewpoints and configurations with poor joint-limit margins. An RGB perception front end estimates the opening center and directed cavity axis from semantic masks using arc-supported ellipse fitting together with body and side-generator cues. These estimates parameterize constraints on camera-axis alignment, lateral offset, and axial standoff. A multistart derivative-free search then optimizes robot joint configurations with lexicographic priority given to constraint satisfaction; feasible configurations are ranked according to motion economy, joint-limit margin, and view quality. The resulting candidates are evaluated by a collision-aware motion planner, and the executed camera pose is verified geometrically and using a ray-based estimate of bottom visibility. In Isaac Sim, the proposed method successfully completes 92 of 100 target configurations and attains 91.65% mean bottom visibility among executed trials, compared with 76 of 100 and 84.3% for a multistart coordinate-search baseline. Tabletop and Unitree A2-mounted experiments demonstrate the complete perception-planning-execution pipeline.

</details>


### [288] [Exposing the Long-tail in Embodied Urban Navigation via Scalable Learning from In-the-Wild Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16476)
*Bingyi Xia,Han Bao,Zhewei Chen,Hanjing Ye,Jingwen Yu,Yuhan Pang,Wenjun Xu,Jiankun Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个可扩展框架，从海量无标签野外第一人称视频中学习点目标城市导航策略，通过自动标注度量轨迹和导航语义训练VLA策略，并基于模型性能与感知-运动模式分布刻画长尾分布，用反思式分析诊断重复性失败模式。实验证明网络视频知识可有效迁移到真实城市导航任务，并揭示聚合性能之外连贯的长尾结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界数据学习具身城市导航策略受限于任务特定数据采集成本高昂，以及罕见但安全关键的场景覆盖率有限。需要利用网络级野外视频的低成本丰富数据来扩展训练分布，同时系统性地暴露和检查长尾分布。

Method: (1) 自动标注框架：对未策划的网络视频自动生成度量轨迹和结构化导航语义标注；(2) 基于标注训练视觉-语言-动作(VLA)策略用于可解释导航规划；(3) 基于模型性能和感知-运动模式分布刻画长尾；(4) 反思式分析诊断重复失败模式。

Result: 在网络视频数据和真实世界城市导航任务上的实验表明，从无约束网络视频到真实导航存在有效的知识迁移，且揭示了聚合导航性能之外的连贯长尾结构。

Conclusion: 该框架证明了利用网络级非结构化视频进行具身导航学习的可行性，并提出了一种系统性刻画和诊断长尾分布的方法，为安全关键场景的覆盖提供了新思路。

Abstract: Learning embodied urban navigation policies from real-world data is constrained by the cost of task-specific data collection and the limited coverage of rare yet safety-critical scenarios. To address these challenges, we present a scalable framework for learning point-goal urban navigation from web-scale in-the-wild egocentric videos while systematically exposing its long tail. The framework automatically annotates uncurated web videos with metric trajectories and structured navigation semantics, which are then used to train a vision-language-action policy for interpretable navigation planning. We characterize the long tail based on model performance and the distribution of perception-motion patterns, and employ reflection-based analysis to diagnose recurring failure modes. Experiments on web-video data and real-world urban navigation tasks demonstrate effective knowledge transfer from unconstrained videos and reveal coherent long-tail structures beyond aggregate navigation performance.

</details>


### [289] [OccamView: Object-Conditioned View Selection for Frame-Budgeted Active 3D Gaussian Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16499)
*Hongbo Gao,Wei Zhang,Zeyu Ni,Dihao Zhu,Ruifeng Li,Yunke Wang,Chang Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出OccamView，一个面向帧数受限的主动三维高斯重建的对象条件化视角选择框架。通过维护在线对象记忆并用保守隐藏区域代理未解析的局部占用，用遮挡感知的代理覆盖率评分评估候选视角，并引入Geo-Floor机制在几何竞争性视角内进行对象条件重排序。在Replica和Matterport3D上统一帧预算协议下，五种帧预算均一致提升Completion并改善Completion Ratio。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动3DGS方法主要基于几何信息增益规划视角，将对象引起的遮挡隐藏区域与普通未探索空间同等对待。在紧帧预算下，几何驱动策略可能优先覆盖全局场景而忽视对部分观测对象的完整重建，导致对象重建不完整。

Method: OccamView框架：(1) 从开放的词汇检测中基于实测RGB-D观测维护在线对象记忆；(2) 将检测对象周围的未解析局部占用表示为保守的隐藏区域代理；(3) 用遮挡感知的代理覆盖率评分评估候选视角；(4) Geo-Floor机制限定对象条件重排在几何竞争性候选中进行，兼顾对象线索与几何驱动探索。

Result: 在Replica和Matterport3D基准上，统一帧预算协议下五种帧预算均一致降低Completion并提升Completion Ratio，尤其在帧预算受限时增益显著。

Conclusion: 轻量级对象条件线索能有效补充几何驱动的主动视角规划，在紧帧预算下显著提升对象重建完整性，无需预测未见对象几何或进行形状补全。

Abstract: Active 3D Gaussian reconstruction fundamentally relies on selecting informative next-best views under limited sensing budgets. Existing active 3DGS methods primarily plan viewpoints according to geometric information gain, treating object-induced hidden regions in the same manner as general unexplored space. Under tight frame budgets, such geometry-driven strategies may prioritize global scene coverage while leaving partially observed objects incompletely reconstructed. To address this limitation, we propose OccamView, an object-conditioned view-selection framework for frame-budgeted active 3D Gaussian reconstruction. Rather than predicting unseen object geometry or performing shape completion, OccamView maintains an online object memory from open-vocabulary detections grounded in measured RGB-D observations and represents unresolved local occupancy around detected objects as conservative hidden-region proxies. Candidate viewpoints are then evaluated using an occlusion-aware proxy-coverage score. Furthermore, we introduce a Geo-Floor mechanism that restricts object-conditioned re-ranking to geometrically competitive candidates, allowing object-conditioned cues to guide complementary observations while preserving the geometry-driven exploration behavior of the underlying planner. Experiments on Replica and Matterport3D under a unified frame-budgeted protocol show that OccamView consistently reduces Completion and improves Completion Ratio across five frame budgets, with particularly pronounced gains under limited frame budgets. These results demonstrate that lightweight object-conditioned cues effectively complement geometry-driven active view planning.

</details>


### [290] [NebulaVLA: A Dual-Frequency Vision-Language-Action Model With Guide Action for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16503)
*Cong Zhao,Shuai Tian,Xu Zhang,Baocheng Ni,Xinguo Song,Xueying Sun,Shu Jiang,Shouchang Yang,Bo Tang,Jin Deng,Ge Zhu,YongCheng Wang,Jin Xu,Ri Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NebulaVLA提出了异步双频架构来解耦高层语义推理与低层动作控制，配合GESTURE-7统一跨具身动作表示和Guide Action平滑约束算法，显著提升VLA模型的效率、泛化与执行平滑度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在实际部署中面临效率-性能权衡、跨具身泛化困难和执行不平滑三大瓶颈，需要解耦推理与控制、统一异构机器人间的语义表示，并保证动作时序连续性。

Method: 1) 异步双频架构：将高层语义推理（低频）与低层动作控制（高频）解耦，优化资源分配与模块化；2) GESTURE-7：统一语言接地动作表示，弥合异构机器人间的语义鸿沟；3) Guide Action算法：基于掩码的平滑约束强制运动学连续性。

Result: 在LIBERO-Plus上平均成功率85.5%，动作生成加速约2.7倍，显著优于同步基线。

Conclusion: 异步解耦设计能够同时提升VLA的效率、响应速度与可部署性，为实际机器人控制提供了高效方案。

Abstract: Real-world deployment of Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models is often bottlenecked by efficiency-performance trade-offs, cross-embodiment generalization, and execution smoothness. We present NebulaVLA, an asynchronous dual-frequency architecture that decouples high-level semantic reasoning from low-level action control, optimizing computational resources and modularity. To bridge semantic gaps across heterogeneous robots, we introduce GESTURE-7, a unified language-grounded action representation. Furthermore, our Guide Action algorithm enforces kinematic continuity via mask-based smoothness constraints. Comprehensive evaluations demonstrate that NebulaVLA significantly outperforms synchronous baselines, achieving an 85.5\% average success rate on LIBERO-Plus and accelerating action generation by \textasciitilde 2.7$\times$. This asynchronous design enables highly efficient and responsive control for practical robotics.

</details>


### [291] [Co-design of Neural and Muscle Network based on Embodied Perceptron Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16555)
*Siyuan Tao,Yoichi Masuda,Hiroyuki Nabae,Masato Ishikawa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出"具身感知器"(Embodied Perceptron)理论框架，将物理身体系统统一建模为神经网络形式：机械参数对应权重，物理非线性对应激活函数，从而在理论上阐明身体可替代部分神经控制的功能。作为应用，作者在肌肉骨骼机器人中联合优化控制策略与肌肉配置，展示了仅使用单神经元控制器也能获得固有稳定性、更高学习效率和大幅缩小的模型规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人设计聚焦于复杂控制策略，而身体设计仍依赖专家直觉，缺乏系统化的理论框架。作者希望建立统一理论解释身体-控制器协同作用的机理，并实现身体与控制的联合优化，以克服当前机器人身体简单、适应性受限的问题。

Method: 提出Embodied Perceptron理论框架，将物理身体建模为神经网络：将物理约束表示为权重，非线性物理属性表示为激活函数，使物理身体可用神经网络形式表示。基于该统一表示，理论上证明身体可以替代部分神经控制功能，并以此为基础进行控制策略与肌肉配置的联合优化。

Result: 在肌肉骨骼机器人中联合优化控制策略与肌肉配置，结果显示该方法能提供固有稳定性、提升学习效率、大幅减少模型规模——即使仅使用单神经元控制器也可实现。

Conclusion: 该工作弥合了信息世界与物理世界的鸿沟，为理解和系统化设计具身AI系统提供了理论路径。

Abstract: Recent advances in AI technologies have enabled the advanced design of complex control policies. In contrast, focusing on the body, many robots still employ simple bodies that can limit adaptability to environments. Studies in embodied robotics have shown that well-designed bodies can partially replace the role of control and computation with physical body-environment interactions, yet such designs still depend heavily on expert intuition. There is a need for a systematic theoretical framework for body design, as well as a method for joint optimization of the body and controller. To address this, we introduce the Embodied Perceptron, a theoretical framework that unifies neural networks and physical body systems. In this view, the body itself acts as a perceptron: mechanical parameters correspond to weights, and physical nonlinearities play the role of activation functions. By representing physical constraints as weights and nonlinear properties as activation functions, a physical body can be modeled in neural-network form. The system representation enables us to explicitly and theoretically explain that the body can substitute for part of the neural control. As an application, we co-optimize control policy and muscle configuration in a musculoskeletal robot and show that the resulting embodied intelligence can provide inherent stability, improve learning efficiency, and drastically reduce model size-even with a single-neuron controller. The results bridge the informational and physical worlds and provide a pathway toward understanding and systematic design of embodied AI systems.

</details>


### [292] [ViHaTeleop: A Low-Cost, Lightweight Visual-Haptic Teleoperation System for Dexterous Manipulation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16572)
*Fucai Zhu,Yanhou Lai,Paul Maestre,Koichi Hashimoto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ViHaTeleop是一个轻量级（0.7kg）、低成本（$550）的视觉-触觉遥操作系统，结合SLAM手腕追踪、相机手部追踪和基于线性谐振执行器（LRA）的指级振动触觉反馈。系统在Franka + LEAP Hand + 9DTact上部署于真实与仿真环境，通过九个参与者在六个接触密集型任务上的对照实验表明，触觉反馈将成功率提升了+2.2至+15.6个百分点，并在仿真与真实环境中均显著改善了接触清晰度和抓取信心。系统还集成了基于深度相机的触觉替代方案于Isaac Sim，实现了从多模态演示采集到视觉-触觉策略训练的完整流程，初步结果表明触觉线索在接触关键子任务（如插针入孔，比纯视觉高+17个百分点）中发挥显著作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从示范中学习的灵巧操作方法前景广阔，但低成本遥操作硬件难以采集高质量的接触关键型演示数据。现有系统往往缺乏有效的触觉反馈通道，导致操作者无法在演示过程中感知接触信息，从而影响后续策略学习的质量。作者旨在构建一个轻量、低成本的视觉-触觉遥操作系统，以提升接触关键型灵巧操作示范数据的采集质量。

Method: 系统由三部分构成：(1) 硬件设计：SLAM腕部追踪、相机手部追踪、指级LRA振动触觉反馈，重量0.7kg、成本$550；(2) 关键设计选择：LED照明优化、鱼眼手部相机、触觉感知重定向约束；(3) 系统集成：在Franka + LEAP Hand + 9DTact上部署，并提供Isaac Sim中的深度相机触觉代理（tactile proxy），支持从多模态演示采集到视觉-触觉策略训练的完整管线。实验设计采用匹配的有/无触觉条件，九个参与者完成六个接触关键任务，并进行主客观评估。

Result: 在六个接触关键任务中，触觉反馈将成功率提升+2.2至+15.6个百分点（所有任务均有提升）；完成时间的影响因任务而异。主观评分显示接触清晰度和抓取信心在仿真和真实环境中均显著提升（Wilcoxon符号秩检验，p<0.05）。下游验证中，视觉-触觉策略在插针入孔任务中比纯视觉策略高+17个百分点。

Conclusion: ViHaTeleop验证了低成本触觉反馈遥操作系统在提升接触关键型灵巧操作示范采集质量方面的有效性。触觉线索不仅改善了操作者的接触感知和抓取信心，还为下游视觉-触觉策略训练提供了更有价值的演示数据。系统开源设计可支持从数据采集到策略训练的全流程。

Abstract: Learning from demonstration is a promising approach for dexterous manipulation, but collecting high-quality contact-critical demonstrations remains difficult with low-cost teleoperation hardware. We present ViHaTeleop, a lightweight (0.7 kg), low-cost (\$550) visual-haptic teleoperation system with SLAM-based wrist tracking, camera-based hand tracking, and finger-wise vibrotactile feedback through Linear Resonant Actuators (LRA). The system includes several design choices (LED illumination, fisheye hand camera, and tactile-aware retargeting constraints) and is deployed on Franka + LEAP Hand + 9DTact in both real and simulated environments. Under matched with/without-haptic conditions with nine participants across six contact-critical tasks, haptics improved success rates across all tasks (+2.2 to +15.6 percentage points), while completion-time effects were task-dependent. Subjective ratings showed significant gains in contact clarity and grasp confidence in both simulation and real-world settings (Wilcoxon signed-rank, $p<0.05$). We also integrate a lightweight depth-camera-based tactile proxy in Isaac Sim, enabling a full pipeline from multi-modal demonstration collection to visual-tactile policy training. Preliminary downstream validation by training visual-tactile policies from collected demonstrations shows tactile cues benefit contact-critical subtasks (peg-in-hole: +17 percentage points over vision-only).

</details>


### [293] [Zetta $ζ$: An Efficient Closed-Loop Embodied Harness for Self-Evolving Physical Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16590)
*Xin Ding,Liang Mi,Mingzhe Huang,Zixuan Wang,Chao Zhang,Zixu Hao,Fu Chen,Xiangyu Li,Yikai Zheng,Yaoyu Guo,Weijun Wang,Kun Li,Hao Wu,Yunxin Liu,Ting Cao*

Main category: cs.RO
