TL;DR: Zetta是一个闭环具身智能体框架，通过三层时间尺度分离的循环（动作频率治理、rollout级评判器-恢复技能提议、验证门控的技能更新）在线演进基于代码的运行时评判器和恢复技能，同时保持基础策略冻结。配合Z-Infra异构执行基础设施，在LIBERO-Pro和RoboCasa上达到90.8%和93.6%的成功率，推理速度提升11.1倍，技能可零样本迁移并涌现"机器人顿悟时刻"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身智能体框架在物理执行中仍为开环学习：rollout遵循固定技能，仅在episode结束后反思。物理交互需要以超过当前大型智能体模型频率的高频决策跟踪机器人与环境状态，事后反思无法在运行中管控执行过程，因此需要闭环在线演化的具身框架。

Method: Zetta通过三层时间尺度分离的闭环循环：(1) 动作频率治理循环提供高频执行管控；(2) rollout级别的评判器-恢复技能提议循环生成恢复建议；(3) 验证门控的技能更新循环确保安全演进。同时提出Z-Infra rollout基础设施，将智能体逻辑与异构执行资源解耦。基础策略冻结不变，仅在线演进代码型运行时评判器和恢复技能。

Result: 在LIBERO-Pro达到90.8%成功率，RoboCasa达到93.6%成功率，推理速度提升11.1倍；成功率先随自探索经验扩展；学习到的技能支持零样本迁移；出现清晰的机器人"Aha Moments"。

Conclusion: 闭环harness的自我演化开启了迈向可靠物理智能的扩展路径，证明在线演进而非事后反思是具身智能体闭环学习的关键方向。

Abstract: Embodied agents are increasingly used to close the gap left by end-to-end policy models. Yet the agentic path has not realized closed-loop learning in physical execution: existing harnesses remain largely open-loop, following fixed skills during rollout and reflecting only after an episode completes. Such post-hoc reflection cannot govern execution as it unfolds, because physical interaction requires decisions to track rapidly changing robot-environment states at a frequency beyond today's large agentic models. We present Zetta, a closed-loop embodied harness that evolves code-based runtime critics and recovery skills online while keeping the base policy frozen. Through three timescale-separated loops, Zetta provides action-frequency governance, rollout-level critic-recovery proposal, and validation-gated skill updates. Together with Z-Infra, a rollout infrastructure decoupling agent logic from heterogeneous execution resources, Zetta achieves state-of-the-art success on LIBERO-Pro and RoboCasa under our current rollout budget, reaching 90.8% and 93.6%, with an 11.1x inference speedup; success continues to scale with self-exploration experience; learned skills transfer zero-shot, and clear robotic "Aha Moments" emerge. These results show that closed-loop harness self-evolution opens a scaling path for reliable physical intelligence.

</details>


### [294] [DPNet: Efficient Dead-End Prediction and Avoidance for Vision-Based UAV Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16640)
*Ruibin Zhang,Lun Pan,Zelong Xia,Jialiang Hou,Fei Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于RGB-D输入的轻量级神经网络，用于预测无人机视野内的潜在死胡同的相对距离和方位，并通过剪枝预定义的轨迹库实现主动避障，在50 Hz频率下进行规划，无需真实世界数据标注即可迁移，仿真与真实实验均验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉驱动的无人机在死胡同中常因传感器精度和范围有限而导航失败，缺乏一种高效、可迁移的死胡同预测与主动规避机制。

Method: 使用轻量级神经网络从RGB-D输入预测潜在死胡同的相对距离和方位角，利用这些预测对预定义的紧凑轨迹库进行剪枝，使规划器在保持轨迹平滑性的同时主动规避死胡同。系统以50 Hz进行高频重规划。

Result: 仿真基准测试在成功率、飞行时间和轨迹长度上表现优越；真实世界实验在复杂场景中验证了系统的有效性，且无需人工标注或真实数据微调即可迁移部署。

Conclusion: 该方案为无人机死胡同问题提供了高效、轻量、可迁移的系统性解决方案，兼顾了导航平滑性与实时性。

Abstract: Vision-based Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) often suffer from navigation failures in dead ends due to limited sensing accuracy and range. To address this challenge, this paper proposes a systematic solution for efficient dead-end prediction and avoidance. The proposed method introduces a lightweight neural network to predict the relative distance and bearing of potential dead ends within the current field of view using RGB-D inputs. These predictions prune a predefined, compact trajectory library, enabling the planner to proactively avoid dead ends while maintaining navigational smoothness. Notably, our approach transfers across real-world scenarios without manual annotation or fine-tuning on real-world data. The system achieves high-frequency replanning at 50 Hz onboard. Extensive simulation benchmarks demonstrate superior performance in success rate, flight time, and trajectory length, and real-world experiments further validate its effectiveness in complex scenarios.

</details>


### [295] [Throwing a Tight Spiral American Football by a Humanoid Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16642)
*Zaid Mahboob,Bowen Weng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种让人形机器人（Unitree G1 + Dex3-1三指夹爪）实现美式橄榄球回旋稳定投掷（tight spiral throw）的控制框架。通过将上半身运动分为投掷阶段（轨迹优化加速球体至目标状态）和跟随阶段（MPC主动控制手腕及接触手指），结合下半身稳定控制，实现了高达93.6%的旋转效率和0.286弧度的鼻尖对齐误差，投掷线速度达5.35 m/s，角速度达14.5 rad/s。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人投掷研究多聚焦于开放式夹爪下生成动态可行的释放速度，但对释放瞬间自旋注入（spin injection）的显式控制研究不足，尤其对于具有气动各向异性的物体（如美式橄榄球）而言。精确控制线动量与角动量的耦合对飞行稳定性和命中精度至关重要。

Method: 提出耦合全身控制策略：下半身执行有信息的稳定控制；上半身分为两阶段——(1) 投掷阶段通过轨迹优化与跟踪将球加速至目标状态，(2) 跟随阶段利用模型预测控制（MPC）主动控制手腕和仍在接触中的手指，处理亚100毫秒释放阶段中球体部分滑过手指形成的欠驱动瞬态接触动力学。

Result: 在29自由度Unitree G1人形机器人配7自由度Dex3-1三指夹爪上实证验证：投掷橄榄球达到93.6%旋转效率、0.286弧度鼻尖对齐误差（理想紧螺旋为100%效率和0误差），线速度最高5.35 m/s，角速度14.5 rad/s。

Conclusion: 该工作首次系统性地解决了人形机器人投掷各向异性气动物体时的自旋注入控制问题，通过分阶段全身控制有效管理了高速运动中的瞬态接触动力学，为更广泛的动态操作任务提供了方法学基础。

Abstract: Accurate throwing of the American football requires precise regulation of release conditions, where coupled linear and angular momentum determine flight stability and targeting accuracy. While prior work on robotic object throwing has largely focused on generating dynamically feasible release velocities using open-gripper paradigms, explicit control of spin injection at detachment remains underexplored, particularly for aerodynamically anisotropic objects like the American football. In this paper, we present the spin-stabilized controlled tight spiral throw of an American football by a humanoid robot. Achieving this requires (i) accurately reaching the desired coupled momentum, which often involves high degrees-of-freedom (DoF) movements completed within approximately half a second, and (ii) managing the complex transient contact dynamics that arise during the sub-100-millisecond release phase, when the football is effectively underactuated as it moves partially across the fingers. To this end, we develop a coupled whole-body control strategy where the lower body is performing informed stabilization while the upper body is further divided into two phases with (i) a throw phase accelerating the football to a target state through trajectory optimization and tracking, and (ii) a follow-through phase utilizing model predictive control to actively control the wrist and remaining in-contact fingers. The proposed framework is empirically validated on a 29-DoF Unitree G1 humanoid equipped with a 7-DoF Dex3-1 three-fingered gripper. The thrown American football reaches up to 93.6% spin efficiency and a 0.286 radians linear-velocity-to-nose-alignment (nose-angle) error (where an ``ideal'' tight spiral corresponds to 100 % spin efficiency and 0 radians nose-angle error) at up to a 5.35 m/s linear velocity and an angular velocity of 14.5 rad/s.

</details>


### [296] [Orbit-Planner: Towards Latent World Models for On-Orbit Obstacle Avoidance of Satellite Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16651)
*Zhijian Li,Chao Ren,Peijin Wang,Xian Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Orbit-Planner，一个用于在轨避障的两阶段潜在世界模型。第一阶段学习以动作为条件的航天器动力学以在潜在空间进行长程未来状态推演，第二阶段引入Physics Probe从想象的潜在轨迹中解码物理状态变化。在Isaac Sim闭环避障导航中达到91.7%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在轨导航任务中，卫星智能体需基于有限的星上观测预测碰撞风险。然而传统规划器依赖预定义地图和固定的环境假设，难以适应动态在轨场景中的突发状况，需要更具适应性的避障规划方法。

Method: 提出两阶段潜在世界模型Orbit-Planner：(1) 学习以动作为条件的航天器动力学，在潜在空间中执行未来状态推演（long-horizon latent rollouts）；(2) 引入Physics Probe模块，从想象的潜在轨迹中解码物理状态变化（如位置、速度等），实现潜在预测与物理可解释性的结合。

Result: 实验表明Orbit-Planner能够执行长程潜在推演，并能从想象轨迹中恢复物理状态。在Isaac Sim仿真环境的闭环避障导航中达到91.7%的成功率。代码已开源。

Conclusion: Orbit-Planner展示了潜在世界模型在卫星在轨避障任务中的有效性，通过潜在空间的动力学学习和物理状态解码，实现了无需固定地图假设的自适应在轨避障规划。

Abstract: Satellite agents for on-orbit navigation tasks need to predict collision risks using limited onboard observations. However, conventional planners often rely on predefined maps and fixed environmental assumptions, limiting their adaptability in dynamic on-orbit scenarios. In this paper, we propose Orbit-Planner, a two-stage latent world model for on-orbit obstacle avoidance. Orbit-Planner learns action-conditioned spacecraft dynamics to perform future-state rollouts in latent space, and introduces a Physics Probe to decode physical state changes from imagined latent trajectories. Experiments demonstrate that Orbit-Planner can perform long-horizon latent rollouts and recover physical states from imagined trajectories. In closed-loop obstacle-avoidance navigation in Isaac Sim, it attains a success rate of 91.7%. Code is available at https://github.com/ZhijianLi2003/Orbit_Planner.

</details>


### [297] [H-PAC Hand: Control-Oriented Modeling and Tendon-Elasticity Compensation for an Underactuated Robotic Hand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16712)
*Teng Yan,Jiongxu Chen,Teng Wang,Yue Yu,Qixiang Hua,Zihang Wang,Yongru Chen,Bingzhuo Zhong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出H-PAC，一个模块化6执行器、15自由度的欠驱动腱驱动机器人手，通过从腱布线几何推导的稀疏解析执行器-关节模型和基于力学的弹性补偿模型，显著提升关节角度预测精度（MAE从1.15°降至0.18°以下），并采用分层控制架构实现在无外部关节/力传感的闭环下进行姿态复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欠驱动腱驱动手具有紧凑作动和被动柔顺的优势，但腱在恢复弹簧载荷下的伸长会引起与构型相关的关节偏差，导致姿态复现精度受限，缺乏控制导向的建模与补偿方法。

Method: 从腱布线几何推导稀疏解析执行器-关节模型，并开发基于力学的补偿模型以考虑腱弹性引起的关节误差；采用分层架构：上位机执行工作空间约束的姿态映射与补偿，ESP32生成六个位置控制伺服电机的同步指令。

Result: 单调伺服扫描实验中，补偿显著改善关节角度预测；食指DIP关节MAE从1.15°降至0.18°，全部九个评估关节MAE均低于0.23°。代表性姿态与抓取构型在无外部关节/力传感的控制回路上成功执行。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了在紧凑欠驱动机械端执行器中提升姿态复现性的实用方法，无需任务特定重调参数即可工作。

Abstract: Underactuated tendon-driven hands offer compact actuation and passive compliance, but tendon elongation under restoring-spring loading introduces configuration-dependent joint deviations. This paper presents H-PAC, a modular 6-actuator, 15-DoF robotic hand with a control-oriented modeling and implementation framework. A sparse analytical actuator-joint model is derived from the tendon-routing geometry, and a mechanics-based compensation model is developed to account for tendon-elasticity-induced joint errors.
  The proposed method is implemented in a hierarchical architecture: a host computer performs workspace-constrained posture mapping and compensation, while an ESP32 generates synchronized commands for six position-controlled servos. The same control parameters and execution strategy are used across all tasks without task-specific retuning.
  Monotonic servo-sweep experiments show that the compensation substantially improves joint-angle prediction. The MAE of the index DIP joint decreases from 1.15 degrees to 0.18 degrees, and all nine evaluated joints achieve an MAE below 0.23 degrees. Representative postures and grasping configurations are further executed using the same control pipeline without external joint or force sensing in the control loop. The results demonstrate a practical approach to improving posture reproducibility in compact underactuated robotic end-effectors.

</details>


### [298] [MatchingPolicy: Correspondence-Aware Policy Enables Cross-Object In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16715)
*Qijin She,Hanyang Yu,Zeming Li,Ping Tan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MatchingPolicy是一个基于对应关系（correspondence）驱动的少样本模仿学习框架，通过将演示到场景的匹配与策略学习解耦，利用对应感知的扩散策略和视觉基础模型实现在未见物体和新场景上的可靠泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的上下文模仿学习（in-context imitation learning）虽然能实现少样本策略泛化，但在未见过物体和新场景上的性能会显著下降。作者指出匹配（demonstration-to-scene matching）与动作适应之间存在内在冲突，需要一个架构上解耦的方案来解决这一问题。

Method: 提出MatchingPolicy框架：（1）显式地将演示到场景匹配从策略学习中解耦；（2）提出对应感知的扩散策略（correspondence-aware diffusion policy），直接以稠密语义对应关系为条件生成机器人动作；（3）集成视觉基础模型与新颖的两阶段匹配算法，动态建立可靠的对应关系。

Result: 在RLBench仿真基准和真实世界操作任务上进行了广泛评估，证明MatchingPolicy在少样本设置下取得优越性能，能可靠地泛化到未见物体实例和语义类别。

Conclusion: 通过架构上将对应关系识别与动作适应分离，可以有效解决上下文模仿学习中的泛化瓶颈，实现跨物体实例和语义类别的鲁棒少样本策略迁移。

Abstract: In-context imitation learning enables few-shot policy generalization but struggles to maintain performance on unseen objects and novel scenarios. To address this, we introduce MatchingPolicy, a correspondence-driven framework that explicitly decouples demonstration-to-scene matching from policy learning. Central to our method is a correspondence-aware diffusion policy that conditions robotic actions directly on dense semantic correspondences. This architectural separation resolves the inherent conflict between correspondence identification and action adaptation, enabling robust out-of-distribution transfer. Our framework integrates vision foundation models with a novel two-stage matching algorithm to dynamically establish reliable correspondences. Extensive evaluations on RLBench and real-world manipulation tasks confirm that MatchingPolicy achieves superior few-shot performance, generalizing reliably across unseen object instances and semantic categories.

</details>


### [299] [Design Optimization for Large High-Force Soft Robot Manipulators Under Gravitational Loads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16728)
*Isara Cholaseuk,Penelope Llibre,Alexa Kyriacou,Audrey Wang,Akua K. Dickson,Ran Jing,Juan C. Pacheco Garcia,Andrew P. Sabelhaus*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在大规模软体机器人肢体设计中优化几何形状的方法，在自重反屈曲约束下最大化阻塞力，并证明在特定假设下存在解析解。通过在三种几何构型的大型软体气动机械臂上进行实验，该方法能正确预测哪些设计满足约束以及哪个产生最大末端力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型软体机器人能够产生高力以进行物理人机交互，但此前的相关研究仅停留在概念验证原型阶段，缺乏系统化的框架来预先判断某种设计范式是否适合目标任务。需要一种方法在设计和制造之前就能确定软机械臂是否适用于大规模物理交互。

Method: 提出一个几何优化方法，以阻塞力为目标函数、自重下的反屈曲为约束条件；在特定假设下推导出该优化问题的闭式解析解；并通过三种不同几何构型的大型软体气动机械臂进行实验验证。

Result: 实验证明该方法能正确预测哪些几何设计满足约束条件，并准确识别出产生最大末端执行器力的设计。方法具有闭式解，设计人员可以在前期确定软体机械臂类别是否适合大规模物理交互。

Conclusion: 该闭式优化方法为大型软体机器人设计提供了系统化的先验评估工具，填补了该领域缺乏设计框架的空白，有助于设计者在构型选择阶段判断软体机械臂是否适用于物理人机交互场景。

Abstract: Designing large soft robots capable of generating high forces for physical human-robot interaction remains a significant challenge in soft robotics. Prior work in large soft robots has focused on proof-of-concept prototypes, and no systematic framework exists for determining the suitability of a design paradigm for a desired task. This manuscript introduces a method for optimizing the geometry of a soft robot limb, maximizing its blocking force subject to an anti-bucking constraint under its own gravitational loading. We demonstrate that an explicit solution exists to the proposed optimization problem under certain assumptions. Experiments with three geometries of a large, soft, pneumatically-actuated manipulator demonstrate that the method correctly predicts which designs meet constraints and which produces the largest end-effector forces. This method, with its closed-form solution, can allow designers to determine a-priori if an intended class of soft manipulators is an appropriate choice for physical interaction at large size scales.

</details>


### [300] [Semantic- and Density-Aware Planning for Accessibility-Preserving Multi-Object Placement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16741)
*Benno Wingender,Nils Dengler,Nicolas Busch,Sicong Pan,Maren Bennewitz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SDPP（Semantic-Dense Placement Planning）方法，用于在线多物体搁架放置任务。该方法通过语义-密度分数对候选位姿排序，并结合 Accessibility Map 预先过滤不可达位姿，兼顾语义组织、空间密集利用与机械臂可达性。仿真实验显著优于基线，室内真实场景实验验证了可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期操作规划需要同时考虑即时任务成功与未来交互影响。家用服务机器人在部分占据的搁架上整理物品时，需在未知未来物品到达的情况下高效利用有限空间并保持后续放置的可达性，现有方法无法同时解决语义组织、空间密集利用和操作器可达性这三个问题。

Method: 提出SDPP框架：1) 设计semantic-density评分，结合物体间语义相似度与空间邻近度来对候选放置位姿排序；2) 构建Accessibility Map，在运动规划前过滤不可达候选位姿，并对降低剩余可达工作空间的放置进行惩罚；两者协同实现语义组织、密集摆放与可达性保持。

Result: 仿真实验表明SDPP在语义放置质量上显著优于最先进基线，并获得最高平均搁架密度；Accessibility Map极大减少了识别可行放置位姿所需时间；定性真实世界实验验证了在家用搁架存储场景中的可行性。

Conclusion: SDPP通过将语义相似度、空间密度与可达性感知统一到一个评分框架中，有效解决了在线多物体搁架放置的联合优化问题，在语义质量和空间利用率上超越现有方法，并具备真实场景应用潜力。

Abstract: Long-term manipulation planning requires robots to reason not only about immediate task success but also about how current decisions affect future interactions with the environment. In this context, household service robots may need to organize groceries in partially occupied shelves while using limited storage space efficiently and preserving access for subsequent placements. In this paper, we consider an online multi-object shelf-placement setting in which future objects arrivals are unknown. Existing approaches do not jointly address semantic organization, dense space utilization, and manipulator accessibility during sequential shelf filling. To address this gap, we propose Semantic-Dense Placement Planning (SDPP), an accessibility-preserving approach that ranks candidate poses using a semantic-density score combining inter-object semantic similarity with spatial proximity. An Accessibility Map (AM) further filters candidates unlikely to be reachable before motion planning and penalizes placements that reduce the remaining accessible workspace. Simulation experiments show that SDPP significantly improves semantic placement quality over state-of-the-art baselines and achieves the highest average shelf density, while the AM substantially reduces the time required to identify feasible placement poses. A qualitative real-world experiment demonstrates the applicability of our pipeline in a domestic shelf-storage scenario.

</details>


### [301] [Neurosymbolic Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16794)
*Mohammad Albinhassan,Yuming Feng,Alessandra Russo,Pranava Madhyastha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种神经符号agent，将长时程家庭任务分解为任务导向的视觉探索与受约束的符号规划。第一阶段用视觉语言模型和探索框架获取目标相关谓词与实例绑定，构建符号初始状态；第二阶段用PDDL转移模型限制解码token为可执行的扩展动作，并用蒙特卡洛树搜索评估可执行续接。在VirtualHome和ALFWorld上，4B-27B开放模型均超过90%成功率，最小agent远超27B直接视觉策略。约束与搜索互补，组合在ALFWorld上解决超95%任务，且生成token和可见图像更少，残差失败集中在状态获取阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言和视觉语言模型虽能生成看似合理的具身计划，但无法保证可执行性——输出可能违反环境动力学或作用于错误grounded实体。现有方法缺乏对计划可执行性的硬性保证，作者旨在构造一种按构造即可执行的神经符号agent。

Method: 两阶段神经符号框架：(1) 视觉语言模型+探索harness从第一视角观测和grounded交互中获取目标相关谓词与实例绑定，生成符号初始状态；(2) PDDL转移模型将解码token限制为可扩展的适用动作，随后用蒙特卡洛树搜索和领域无关规划启发式评估可执行续接。计划在转移模型下按构造可执行，向真实环境的迁移取决于视觉grounding的正确性。

Result: 在VirtualHome和ALFWorld上，4B-27B开放模型均超过90%成功率；最小agent（4B）在两种环境中都大幅优于27B直接视觉策略。ALFWorld中，单独使用约束或搜索各只能解决不到三分之一的任务，二者组合可解决超95%。方法生成的token数比extended thinking少数倍，model-visible图像远少于直接交互，无需专门训练，残差失败定位于状态获取而非计划生成。

Conclusion: 神经符号约束与搜索是互补而非可互换的机制，二者结合能显著提升长时程家庭任务的可执行计划质量。该方法无需专门训练即达高成功率，效率更高，且清晰揭示了剩余瓶颈在于视觉状态获取而非规划本身。

Abstract: Language and vision-language models generate plausible embodied plans but do not guarantee executability, as their outputs can violate environment dynamics or act on incorrectly grounded entities. We present a neurosymbolic agent that factors long-horizon household tasks into task-directed visual exploration and constrained symbolic planning. In the first phase, a vision-language model and exploration harness acquire goal-relevant predicates and instance bindings from egocentric observations and grounded interactions, producing a symbolic initial state. In the second, a PDDL transition model restricts decoding to tokens that extend applicable actions. Monte Carlo tree search then evaluates executable continuations using a domain-independent planning heuristic. The resulting plans are executable by construction under the transition model, with transfer to the environment conditioned on correct visual grounding. On VirtualHome and ALFWorld, open 4B-27B models exceed 90% success in both environments, and our smallest agent substantially outperforms a 27B direct visual policy in each. Constraints and search prove complementary rather than interchangeable: in ALFWorld either alone solves under a third of tasks, whereas their combination solves over 95%. The method also uses several times fewer generated tokens than extended thinking and far fewer model-visible images than direct interaction, and residual failures localize to state acquisition rather than plan generation without any specialized training.

</details>


### [302] [When State Becomes an Attack Surface: State-Semantic Injection in LLM-Driven Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16806)
*Jiawei Liu,Jiacheng Guo,Tian Zhang,Yiwei Xu,Juan Wang,Jinlin Fan,Bowen Xiao,Chi Guo,Keyan Guo,Hongxin Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文是对大型语言模型驱动的具身智能体（LLM-driven embodied agents）的综述性介绍，梳理了从传统LLM Agent到集成机器人系统的演进路径，涵盖SayCan、Code as Policies、ProgPrompt、VoxPoser、PaLM-E、RT-2、GR00T N1等代表性工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在系统梳理LLM从文本生成模型向具身智能体核心演化的历程，说明语言模型如何与机器人系统结合，承担任务理解、高层规划和行为决策等职能，并揭示其中任务接地（task grounding）的关键挑战。

Method: 以文献综述方式，按技术路线分类介绍代表性方法：1) SayCan将语言模型推理与机器人技能affordances结合；2) Code as Policies和ProgPrompt分别通过策略代码和程序化提示生成机器人任务规划；3) VoxPoser利用语言模型和视觉语言模型构建三维价值图指导操控；4) PaLM-E、RT-2、GR00T N1等视觉-语言-动作模型整合语言、视觉感知与机器人动作。同时讨论了具身智能体中任务接地（结合场景状态、物体属性、空间关系、执行反馈）的完整流程。

Result: 论文归纳了LLM-driven embodied agents的三种主要技术范式：语言模型结合技能逻辑（SayCan）、代码/程序化规划（CaP、ProgPrompt）、三维价值图指导（VoxPoser）以及端到端视觉-语言-动作模型（PaLM-E、RT-2、GR00T N1），并指出这类系统需要完成从用户指令到动作计划再到技能库/运动规划器/控制器执行的全链路。

Conclusion: 本文属于背景综述类工作，没有提出新的方法或实验数据。它概述了LLM驱动具身智能体的技术脉络和构成要素，强调任务接地能力是连接语言理解与机器人执行的关键桥梁。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated capabilities in in-context learning, task decomposition, step-by-step reasoning, and code generation, driving their gradual evolution from text generation models into the core of agents capable of perceiving environments, invoking tools, and executing tasks. Traditional LLM Agents typically obtain information through webpages, documents, databases, or external tools and generate corresponding invocation sequences according to user goals; when this technology is further integrated with robotic systems, large language models begin to undertake functions such as task understanding, high-level planning, and behavioral decision-making. SayCan combines the task reasoning capability of language models with the affordances of robotic skills, while Code as Policies and ProgPrompt generate robot task plans through policy code and programmatic prompting, respectively, and VoxPoser uses language models and vision-language models to construct three-dimensional value maps to guide robotic manipulation \cite{6,7,8,9}. Vision-language-action models such as PaLM-E, RT-2, and GR00T N1 further strengthen the connection among language, visual perception, and robotic actions \cite{10,11,12}. In such LLM-driven embodied agents, the model not only needs to understand user instructions, but also needs to combine scene states, object attributes, spatial relations, and execution feedback to complete task grounding, and then hand the generated action plan to skill libraries, motion planners, or controllers for execution.

</details>


### [303] [Adaptive Repulsive Pheromone Clustering for Foraging Robot Swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16822)
*Carlos Pena-Caballero,Constantine Tarawneh,Qi Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出自适应排斥信息素聚类（ARPC），一种受生物启发的集群觅食方法，机器人在巢穴附近沉积排斥性信息素路标以标记已探索区域，并聚类估计低价值搜索区，从而引导机器人转向未访问区域。在ARGoS大规模仿真中，ARPC在多种竞技场大小、资源密度和空间分布下均优于CPFA和GPFA，早期发现效率提升10%，后期收集效率提升达60%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中心场所觅食算法（CPFA）在机器人集群中虽然结合了场地忠诚度、信息素导航和随机搜索实现去中心化资源收集，但经常重访已探索区域而忽视其他区域，导致资源稀缺时效率下降。需要一种方法提高搜索多样性和资源发现效率。

Method: ARPC方法：机器人在巢穴附近沉积排斥性信息素路标标记已探索位置；这些路标围绕巢穴聚类以估计低价值搜索区域；结合已知资源的利用与冗余探索的系统性回避，将机器人重定向到可能未访问的区域。

Result: 在ARGoS仿真中，跨不同竞技场大小、资源密度及聚集/随机/幂律空间分布，ARPC一致优于CPFA和GPFA。早期发现阶段提升10%，后期收集阶段提升高达60%，在传统方法性能下降的场合表现尤为突出。

Conclusion: ARPC为大规模异构集群觅食环境提供了可扩展且鲁棒的策略，通过排斥性信息素聚类有效提升搜索多样性和资源收集效率。

Abstract: The Central Place Foraging Algorithm (CPFA) combines site fidelity, pheromone-guided navigation, and uninformed random search to enable decentralized resource collection in robot swarms. However, CPFA often revisits previously explored regions while leaving other areas insufficiently searched, reducing efficiency as resources become scarce. In this paper, we propose Adaptive Repulsive Pheromone Clustering (ARPC), a bio-inspired method in which robots deposit repulsive pheromone waypoints to mark previously explored locations. These waypoints are clustered around the nest to estimate low-value search regions, allowing robots to be redirected toward likely unvisited areas. By integrating the exploitation of known resources with systematic avoidance of redundant exploration, ARPC improves search diversity and resource discovery efficiency. Extensive simulations in ARGoS across varying arena sizes, resource densities, and clustered, random, and power-law spatial distributions demonstrate that ARPC consistently outperforms CPFA and the Grid-Based CPFA (GPFA). In particular, ARPC yields significant gains during both early discovery (10\%) and late-stage (up to 60\%) collection, where conventional methods typically degrade. These results indicate that ARPC provides a scalable and robust strategy for large-scale heterogeneous swarm foraging environments.

</details>


### [304] [HAF: Adapting Generalist VLAs to Humanoid Whole-Body Loco-manipulation via Hierarchical Action Flow and Spectral Latent RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16837)
*Langzhe Gu,Chengkai Hou,Meng Li,Xinhua Wang,Jiaming Liu,Xinyuan Lv,Bowei Zhang,Shuanghao Bai,Guangrun Li,Jingyang He,Gaole Dai,Ziluo Ding,Zhiyuan Xu,Kuan Cheng,Jian Tang,Zhengping Che,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HAF是一个将通用VLA基础模型迁移到人形机器人全身运动-操作任务的两部分框架，包含层级动作流生成器HAF-VLA和潜空间离线到在线强化学习模块HAF-Steer，在7个真实世界人形机器人任务上超过单阶段VLA基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用VLA基础模型无法直接应用于人形机器人全身运动-操作任务，因为高维度和动作相互依赖性使单阶段VLA架构难以协调 locomotion、腰部姿态和双臂操作；离线行为克隆策略在真实部署中表现次优，而直接在线微调大型VLA主干计算开销大且有安全风险。

Method: HAF由两部分组成：1) HAF-VLA，基于预训练flow-matching VLA的层级动作流生成器，将全身动作去噪分为三个顺序阶段，使用阶段嵌入和跨阶段KV缓存保留运动学依赖；2) HAF-Steer，冻结HAF-VLA之上的潜空间在线RL管道，利用flow-matching可逆性和DCT降维将RL优化限制在紧凑噪声子空间，训练正则化SAC策略，避免更新大型VLA骨干。

Result: 在7个真实世界人形机器人运动-操作任务上，HAF超越了vanilla单阶段VLA基线，改善了全身协调性和任务性能。

Conclusion: 通过层级解耦和潜空间在线优化，可以高效且安全地将通用VLA基础模型迁移到人形机器人全身运动-操作任务，为利用大型VLA模型驱动复杂人形机器人提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Humanoid robots hold great promise as general-purpose agents in human-centered environments, yet generalist vision-language-action (VLA) foundation models are not readily applicable to humanoid whole-body loco-manipulation. The high dimensionality and interdependence of humanoid motions make it challenging for conventional single-stage VLA architectures to coordinate locomotion, waist posture, and dual-arm manipulation effectively. Moreover, policies trained through offline behavior cloning can remain suboptimal during real-world deployment. Although online reinforcement learning can refine policies through real-world interaction, directly tuning large VLA backbones demands excessive computation and may introduce safety risks during real-robot exploration. To address these bottlenecks, we introduce HAF (Humanoid Adaptation Framework), a two-part framework consisting of HAF-VLA and HAF-Steer that transfers off-the-shelf generalist VLA foundation models to humanoid whole-body loco-manipulation. HAF-VLA is a hierarchical action-flow generator built on a pretrained flow-matching VLA. It splits full-body action denoising into three sequential stages with stage embeddings and cross-stage KV caches that retain kinematic dependencies, avoiding incoherent whole-body actions from one-shot generation. On top of the frozen HAF-VLA, HAF-Steer is a latent offline-to-online RL pipeline that leverages flow-matching invertibility and DCT-based dimensionality reduction to restrict RL optimization to a compact noise subspace and train a regularized SAC policy. This avoids updating the large VLA backbone and enables efficient real-world policy refinement. Evaluated on seven real-world humanoid loco-manipulation tasks, HAF surpasses vanilla single-stage VLA baselines and improves whole-body coordination and task performance. Project website: https://grange007.github.io/HAF .

</details>


### [305] [Security of Foundation-Model-Powered Embodied Agents: Attack Surfaces, Attacks, Defenses, and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16843)
*Jiawei Liu,Jiacheng Guo,Tian Zhang,Yiwei Xu,Juan Wang,Jinlin Fan,Bowen Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种以信任边界为中心的具身智能体（由基础模型驱动）安全综述，将攻击面与攻击机制分离，系统划分为5层12个攻击面，基于58条攻击记录和61条防御记录进行量化分析，指出研究集中在多模态感知和动作接口，而上下文/长期记忆、中间件/网络、世界状态完整性和多智能体信任仍属未充分探索领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基础模型驱动的具身智能体安全综述按机制（如越狱、提示注入、后门、投毒、对抗样本）分类威胁，但这些类别无法一致地识别攻击者首先进入具身控制回路的位置。需要从信任边界视角重新组织攻击面与防御研究。

Method: 采用"首次被攻破的信任边界"原则，将系统划分为五层十二个攻击面：模型供应链、用户指令、上下文与记忆、物理语义环境、多模态感知、世界状态、内部推理、任务规划、动作接口、中间件、多智能体通信、执行控制。收集截至2026年8月15日的58条攻击与61条防御记录，进行攻击跨层传播、防御布置和评估实践的量化分析。

Result: 量化分析显示：攻击研究集中于多模态感知和动作接口，防御集中于动作层和运行时保护；上下文/长期记忆、中间件/网络、世界状态完整性、多智能体信任研究相对不足。

Conclusion: 提出开放挑战：状态溯源、组合式防御、长时序攻击传播、物理可实现性、拜占庭多机器人行为、统一闭环评估。

Abstract: Foundation models are increasingly used for perception, reasoning, planning, and action generation in embodied agents, creating security risks that can propagate from digital inputs to physical behavior. Existing surveys often organize threats by mechanisms such as jailbreaks, prompt injection, backdoors, poisoning, or adversarial examples, but these categories do not consistently identify where an adversary first enters the embodied control loop. We present a trust-boundary-centric survey of foundation-model-powered embodied-agent security. Using a first-compromised-trust-boundary principle, we separate attack surface from attack mechanism and organize the system into five layers and twelve attack surfaces spanning the model supply chain, user instructions, context and memory, physical semantic environments, multimodal perception, world state, internal reasoning, task planning, action interfaces, middleware, multi-agent communication, and execution control. Based on 58 attack records and 61 defense records collected through August 15, 2026, we analyze representative attacks, cross-layer propagation, defense placement, and evaluation practices. Our quantitative analysis shows that attack research is concentrated on multimodal perception and action interfaces, while defenses are especially concentrated on action-level and runtime protection. Context and long-term memory, middleware and networking, world-state integrity, and multi-agent trust remain comparatively underexplored. We conclude with open challenges in state provenance, compositional defenses, long-horizon attack propagation, physical realizability, Byzantine multi-robot behavior, and unified closed-loop evaluation.

</details>


### [306] [FlexWorm: Primitive-augmented Hybrid Contact-motion Planning for Suction-based Multi-segment Deformable Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16853)
*Zili Tang,Tiecheng Guo,Qinyue Zhang,Meng Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向串行多段软体机器人的规划框架，采用块式IK混合搜索（IKHS）与基元增强混合搜索（PaHS），实现复杂表面上的全3D导航，并支持离散吸附切换与连续体变形，在仿真和真实气动软体机器人上验证了有效性与在线恢复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多段吸入式软体机器人在受限或脆弱环境中作业时，严重依赖人工设计的步态和环境专属运动脚本，缺乏通用的自主规划方法。

Method: 构建考虑几何、碰撞、准静态可行性约束的规划公式；核心是块式IK混合搜索（IKHS），在可行吸附转移上执行最佳优先搜索，仅对诱导的自由块求解逆运动学；在其上叠加基元增强混合搜索（PaHS），利用学习到的观测-基元嵌入检索短验证运动段以加速局部提议，检索失败时回退到标准IKHS。

Result: 仿真中框架在规划成功率、转移质量和效率上一致优于基线，PaHS在成功率与IKHS持平的同时大幅降低规划时间；在真实气动多段软体机器人上的重复硬件实验验证了可执行性和在驱动与吸附不确定性下的在线恢复能力。

Conclusion: 该框架为串行多段软体机器人在复杂曲面上自主导航提供了通用、高效且可执行的规划方案，并具备实际部署潜力。

Abstract: Multi-segment suction-based soft robots are promising for inspection and maintenance in confined or fragile environments, but existing approaches still depend heavily on manually designed gaits and environment-specific motion scripts. This work presents a planning framework for serial multi-segment soft robots with deformable body segments and boundary suction pads. The formulation targets full 3D navigation on complex surfaces and explicitly handles discrete adhesion switching and continuous body deformation under geometric, collision, and quasi-static feasibility constraints, while remaining agnostic to the specific actuation realization used to produce segment deformation. Its core, block-wise IK hybrid search (IKHS), performs best-first search over feasible adhesion transitions while solving inverse kinematics only on induced free blocks. On top of IKHS, primitive-augmented hybrid search (PaHS) uses a learned observation--primitive embedding to retrieve short validated motion segments for fast local proposal, with fallback to standard IKHS branching when retrieval fails. In simulation, the framework consistently outperforms controlled baselines in planning success, transition quality, and efficiency across diverse terrains. PaHS matches IKHS in success rate while substantially reducing planning time. Repeated hardware experiments on a pneumatic multi-segment soft robot further demonstrate executability and online recovery under actuation and adhesion uncertainty.

</details>


### [307] [$τ_0$-VLA: a Hierarchical Robot Foundation Model with World-Model-Guided Test-Time Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16885)
*Xiaowei Cai,Yunuo Cai,Bingao Chen,Jingxiao Chen,Zhi Chen,Siyuan Feng,Tengyu Hou,Jingshun Huang,Han Jiang,Runkun Ju,Dong Li,Mingxiang Li,Shaowei Li,Xinchen Li,Yifan Li,Yi Liu,Zhongyuan Liu,Jianlan Luo,Junwen Miao,Ruiqi Ni,Buqing Nie,Mingjie Pan,Xinlin Ren,Jianheng Song,Jiaxu Wang,Peiqi Wang,Sen Wang,Xiaoyan Wang,Dafeng Wei,Dongming Wu,Pengwei Xie,Pu Yang,Hangjian Ye,Xiangyu Yue,Jinyu Zhang,Qinglin Zhang,Xueyong Zhao,Pengfei Zhou,Yue Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: τ₀-VLA是一个层级式机器人基础模型，将高层子任务生成形式化为可通过世界模型引导的测试时计算扩展的推理问题。高层策略利用执行记忆生成子任务，必要时搜索替代方案，低层策略则跨多种机器人平台执行子任务，在40,115小时异构真实数据上训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数层级VLA模型每个决策仅通过单次前向传播完成，缺乏为困难或关键决策分配额外计算的机制，导致长时程操作任务中无法处理复杂或高影响的子任务选择。

Method: 构建τ₀-VLA层级基础模型：高层策略进行可扩展测试时计算的子任务生成，通过执行记忆（execution memory）生成候选子任务，并在需要时搜索替代方案再承诺输出；低层策略跨多平台执行子任务。训练数据为40,115小时异构真实世界数据，并采用多模态协同训练。

Result: 在域内和分布偏移设置下，增加测试时计算量显著提升了下一子任务预测准确率，这些提升转化为长时程机器人操作任务的闭环成功率提高。

Conclusion: 测试时计算扩展是改善层级VLA长时程操作性能的有效机制，世界模型引导的搜索能在关键决策中带来实质收益。

Abstract: Long-horizon robot manipulation requires a robot to both execute individual skills reliably and sequence them coherently over extended tasks. Most hierarchical vision-language-action (VLA) models make each such decision with a single forward pass, leaving no mechanism to allocate additional computation to difficult or consequential choices. We introduce $τ_0$-VLA, a hierarchical robot foundation model that formulates high-level subtask generation as a compute-scalable inference problem through world-model-guided test-time computation. At each inference step, the high-level policy uses execution memory to generate a subtask and, when needed, searches over alternatives before committing to its output. A low-level policy then executes the generated subtask across multiple robot embodiments. The policy is trained on 40,115 hours of heterogeneous real-world data with multimodal co-training. Across in-domain and distribution-shifted settings, allocating additional test-time computation substantially improves next-subtask prediction accuracy, and these gains translate into higher closed-loop success on long-horizon robot manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [308] [Don't Drop the BATON: Long-Horizon Robot Manipulation via Agentic Subtask Exploration and Transition-aware Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16889)
*Bingxin Xu,Yuzhang Shang,Emilio Ferrara*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BATON提出将长时程机器人操作任务分解为以子任务为单位的探索，结合迁移感知记忆（verifier、handoff、lookahead三种过渡机制），在不更新任何参数的前提下，将VLA冻结并交由LLM智能体编排，使探索成本从乘法级降为加法级（T*K），并在RoboMemArena基准上取得11.6%任务成功率和14.9%累计成功率的SoTA提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程操作链条中，VLA虽擅长单技能但无法处理错误累积和子任务间隐式约束。现有方案（冻结VLA+LLM编排）面临两大核心问题：一是整任务探索成本随阶段数呈指数增长（T^K）且失败无法归因到具体阶段；二是VLA原语只携带退出条件而无进入条件，导致子任务输出形式无法被后继任务使用。

Method: BATON以子任务为探索单位，在廉价短时程条件下逐个子任务探索并存储解决方案，长时程轨迹由这些解组合而成。配备三种迁移感知记忆机制：(1) verifier代理控制调用迁移，仅在手腕视角确认场景就绪后才调用VLA；(2) handoff过渡恢复被前驱残留扰动的入口状态；(3) lookahead过渡选择后继任务可继承其结果的策略。全程零参数更新。

Result: 在长时程基准RoboMemArena上，BATON相比SoTA将任务成功率提升11.6%，累计成功率提升14.9%。将探索成本从乘法级T^K降至加法级T*K，并实现失败到单一阶段的精确归因。

Conclusion: BATON表明：通过将探索粒度从整个长任务细化为子任务，并引入过渡条件感知的记忆机制，可以显著提升VLA在长时程操作中的组合成功率，无需更新任何模型参数，是一种实用且高效的编排范式。

Abstract: Long-horizon robot manipulation chains many contact-rich skills into one multi-stage task. Vision-language-action (VLA) models increasingly master the individual skills, yet the chain still fails: errors compound beyond the policy's ability to correct, and one subtask silently constrains the next. A promising recipe freezes the VLA and puts an LLM agent in charge: it plans in language, moves in free space with analytic primitives, invokes the VLA only for contact-rich segments, and writes adaptation into language memory. Applied to long horizons, it breaks twice. (1) Competence comes from whole-task exploration at test time, whose cost is multiplicative in stages: if one stage needs T episodes, a K-stage task needs about T^K, and a failure does not reveal which stage caused it. (2) It has no representation of transitions: the VLA primitive carries an exit but no entry condition, so a subtask can succeed in a form its successor cannot use. We present BATON. Against (1), BATON makes the subtask the unit of exploration: each is explored in the cheap short-horizon regime and its solution stored in memory; a long-horizon trajectory is then composed from these solutions rather than discovered whole. Cost becomes additive (T*K) and every failure is attributed to a single stage. Against (2), BATON equips exploration with a transition-aware memory. Within a subtask, a verifier agent governs the invocation transition: the VLA is called only after the wrist view confirms the scene is ready. Across subtasks, a handoff transition restores an entry state disturbed by the predecessor's residue, and a lookahead transition selects the strategy whose outcome the successor can inherit. No parameters are updated. On the long-horizon benchmark RoboMemArena, BATON improves task success by 11.6% and cumulative success by 14.9% over the SoTA.

</details>
